### [1] [Stagewise and interpretable emergence of inside-outside computation in Transformer-based language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14221)
*Jack T. Parley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过合成上下文无关文法（context-free grammar）中可解析的学习动态，揭示了Transformer分阶段习得inside-outside解析计算的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有"事后"（post-mortem）研究表明训练完成的Transformer近似规范解析算法，但关于这种能力如何被实际学习到的理论认识仍然有限，因此需要可控的理论起点。

Method: 研究在具有可变解析树拓扑的合成上下文无关文法上分析学习动态，所有损失阶段均可解析计算，并可与隐藏潜在树变量（latent tree variable）的表示相关联；同时通过对表示进行探测（probing）来可视化计算的出现过程。

Result: 学习曲线呈现完全可解释的分阶段结构；训练早期仅少数初始层使用局部（bigram/trigram）信息，随后Transformer逐步发展出编码器-解码器（encoder-decoder）结构，最抽象的潜在信息编码于中部，后半部分网络逐步精炼最终词元预测。

Conclusion: 该计算组织方式与真实LLM中的发现相似，且由于合成模型的数据统计完全可控，为理论进展打开了大门。

Abstract: Variability in syntactic structure and parsing ambiguity are fundamental aspects of natural language which modern LLMs have to learn to deal with proficiently. Previous "post-mortem" studies have shown that fully trained Transformers indeed approximate canonical parsing algorithms such as inside-outside, but little is established theoretically about how this is actually learned. Here, we make a first step towards addressing this by studying the learning dynamics in synthetic context-free grammars with variable parse tree topology. The stagewise learning curves are fully interpretable, in the sense that all of the loss stages a) can be computed analytically, and b) can be clearly associated to the representation of a hidden latent tree variable, which allows to lift ambiguity and utilize information from wider contexts. By probing representations, we visualize the emergence of inside-outside computation across depth and during training: while during early stages local (bigram/trigram) information is used and involves only a few initial layers, gradually the Transformer develops an encoder-decoder structure where the most abstract latent information is encoded in the middle, and the latter half of the network is used to progressively refine the final token prediction. This organization of computation is reminiscent of findings in real-world LLMs, and importantly applies here to a synthetic model which allows full analytical control of the data statistics, thus opening the door to theoretical progress.

</details>

### [2] [Mean Curvature Flow with Thermal Fluctuations in Dimensions Two and Higher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13703)
*Zhengyan Wu,Ao Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究空间正则化的 Kawasaki–Ohta 方程（Kawasaki–Ohta equation），在周期图（periodic graphs）上对任意维数 \(d\geq2\)、初值属于 \(H^1(\mathbb T^d)\) 的情形，证明了能量鞅解（energy martingale solutions）的整体存在性；其核心在于处理由 \((1+|\nabla u|^2)^{1/4}\) 型噪声系数所产生的非规则 Stratonovich–Itô 修正项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面张力（surface tension）驱动界面收缩，而热运动（thermal motion）通过涨落–耗散关系（fluctuation–dissipation relation）产生与界面迁移率（interfacial mobility）相联系的形状涨落，二者共同导出带热噪声（thermal noise）的平均曲率流（mean-curvature flow），其图形式即 Kawasaki–Ohta 方程，需建立其适定性。

Method: 建立空间正则化 Kawasaki–Ohta 方程能量鞅解的全局存在性。主要创新为针对 Stratonovich–Itô 修正项 \(\nabla u\cdot D^2u\nabla u/(1+|\nabla u|^2)^{3/2}\) 结构设计的紧性论证（compactness argument）：结合该修正项的因子分解、\(H^1(\mathbb T^d)\) 与面积估计，证明归一化梯度（normalized gradients）的强收敛，并重构加权 Hessian（weighted Hessian）。

Result: 对任意 \(d\geq2\) 与初值 \(u_0\in H^1(\mathbb T^d)\)，证明能量鞅解的全局存在性；\(d=2\) 时无需对噪声强度（noise intensity）施加限制，\(d\geq3\) 时需一个小性条件（smallness condition）。由此可在能量正则性（energy regularity）下定义该修正项并确定其逼近极限（approximation limit）。

Conclusion: 带热噪声的平均曲率流之图形式方程在空间正则化后具有整体能量鞅解，噪声强度在二维无约束、三维及以上受小性限制；所提出的紧性框架可处理非规则 Itô 修正项，为分析此类奇异加权项提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Surface tension drives interfaces towards smaller area, while thermal motion produces shape fluctuations whose strength is linked to the interfacial mobility through the fluctuation--dissipation relation. These mechanisms lead to mean-curvature flow with thermal noise, whose graph formulation is known as the Kawasaki--Ohta equation. We establish global-in-time existence of energy martingale solutions to the spatially regularized Kawasaki--Ohta equation for periodic graphs in every dimension $d\geq2$, with initial data in $H^1(\mathbb T^d)$. No restriction on the noise intensity is needed for $d=2$, while a smallness condition is required for $d\geq3$. The key new challenges arise from the thermal noise, which has a $(1+|\nabla u|^2)^{1/4}$-type coefficient. This coefficient gives rise to an irregular Stratonovich-to-Itô correction term of the form $\nabla u\cdot D^2u\nabla u/(1+|\nabla u|^2)^{3/2}$. Our main innovation is a compactness argument tailored to the structure of the correction. By combining a factorization of this term with $H^1(\mathbb T^d)$ and area estimates, we establish strong convergence of the normalized gradients and reconstruct the weighted Hessian, which allows us to define the correction at energy regularity and identify its approximation limit.

</details>


### [3] [Existence and Splitting of Dirac Points in Honeycomb Gyromagnetic Photonic Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13853)
*Liuyi Bao,Wei Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文在二维圆形钇铁石榴石（yttrium iron garnet, YIG）柱蜂窝阵列中，针对横向磁（transverse magnetic）Maxwell 系统，严格证明了 Dirac 锥（Dirac cone）的存在性以及旋磁偏置（gyrotropic bias）诱导的局域带隙（local band gap）打开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Dirac 色散与磁致带隙多依赖有效离散模型（effective discrete model）或近似假设，缺乏直接由完整 Maxwell 透射问题（Maxwell transmission problem）出发的严格推导；本文旨在填补这一严格性缺口。

Method: 采用准周期层势（quasi-periodic layer-potential）表述，结合蜂窝对称性（honeycomb symmetry）与严格可控的有限维约化（finite-dimensional reductions）；在固定材料参数与非零磁导率反差下，对足够小的柱半径进行分析。

Result: 对非磁情形（κ_g=0），证明小柱阵列在双本征频率处两面 Bloch 色散面（Bloch dispersion surfaces）相交并形成非退化锥；对每个足够小的非零旋磁偏置 κ_g，证明该简并发生劈裂并在原 Dirac 点附近打开局域带隙。

Conclusion: 研究在不假设有效离散模型的前提下，统一导出了锥形交叉与磁致带隙打开，确立了固定材料反差、小夹杂（small-inclusion）区域下从 Dirac 色散到局域带隙谱的转变。

Abstract: We establish the existence of Dirac cones and magnetically induced local band gaps for the transverse magnetic Maxwell system in a two-dimensional honeycomb array of circular yttrium iron garnet (YIG) cylinders. For fixed positive material parameters with nonzero permeability contrast, we prove that sufficiently small cylinders support a Dirac point in the nonmagnetic case $κ_g=0$: two Bloch dispersion surfaces meet at a double eigenfrequency and form a nondegenerate cone. For each fixed sufficiently small nonzero gyrotropic bias $κ_g$, we show that this degeneracy splits and a local band gap opens near the original Dirac point. Our approach combines a quasi-periodic layer-potential formulation with honeycomb symmetry and rigorously controlled finite-dimensional reductions. The central contribution is a unified derivation of the conical crossing and magnetic gap opening directly from the full Maxwell transmission problem, without assuming an effective discrete model. This establishes the transition from Dirac dispersion to a locally gapped spectrum in a small-inclusion regime with fixed material contrast.

</details>


### [4] [Phase transitions and sharp localisation in anomalous localised resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13936)
*Konstantinos Alexopoulos*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究二维准静态包覆结构中失谐与吸收对反常局域共振强度及空间范围的影响，并在小形变条件下对耗散功率给出完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 壳层介电常数取 -1+cℓ^p+iℓ 时，失谐可使材料路径穿过狭窄共振，导致功率呈间歇性而非持续增长；需明确失谐指数、损耗与源位置如何共同决定共振行为。

Method: 针对一族显式非圆壳层与固定的光滑外部偶极源，在形变的定量小性条件下，按失谐指数与共形源距离对耗散功率（dissipated power）进行分类，并分析归一化场的局部收敛性。

Result: 耗散功率沿整条路径发散、呈带消失间隙的发散峰、或衰减，含阈值情形；并给出尖锐的共形边界（sharp conformal boundary），越过该边界后以功率平方根归一化的场沿任意发散功率序列局部收敛到零。

Conclusion: 失谐与吸收共同决定局域共振的强度与空间范围；对共振源，切向参考穿越在临界半径处给出外部梯度能量与耗散功率之比的显式非零极限。

Abstract: We study how detuning and absorption determine the strength and spatial extent of anomalous localised resonance in a two-dimensional quasistatic coated structure with shell permittivity $-1+c\ell^p+i\ell$, where $\ell>0$ is the loss and $c\ne0$, $p>0$ are fixed. Detuning can carry the material path through narrow resonances, producing intermittent rather than sustained power growth. For an explicit family of noncircular shells and a smooth exterior dipole source, both fixed, we classify the dissipated power under a quantitative smallness condition on the deformation. Depending on the detuning exponent and conformal source distance, the power diverges along the entire path, exhibits divergent peaks with vanishing gaps, or decays, including the threshold cases. We also identify the sharp conformal boundary beyond which the field, normalized by the square root of the dissipated power, converges locally to zero along every divergent-power sequence. For resonant sources, tangential reference crossings yield an explicit nonzero limit for the exterior gradient energy divided by the dissipated power at the critical radius.

</details>


### [5] [A Kato-Type Criterion for Vanishing Viscosity and Magnetic Diffusivity Limits for Compressible Magnetohydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14096)
*Siran Li,Tong Tang,Yuanjing Tang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中光滑有界区域上可压缩磁流体力学（MHD）方程在粘性与磁扩散系数趋于零时的极限行为，建立了一个 Kato 型准则，用以保证耗散 MHD 系统的有限能量弱解收敛到理想 MHD 系统的弱解，并排除了极限过程中的反常能量耗散（anomalous energy dissipation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘性与磁扩散系数趋于零的奇性极限（vanishing viscosity and magnetic diffusivity limits）是流体与 MHD 理论的核心问题，其关键困难在于极限过程可能出现反常能量耗散（anomalous energy dissipation），从而破坏能量守恒。作者旨在为可压缩 MHD 在此极限下给出一个可验证的收敛准则。

Method: 在有界光滑区域（smooth bounded domain）$\Omega\subset\mathbb{R}^3$ 上，于内部正则性（interior regularity）与边界附近的局部一致有界性（uniform local boundedness near the boundary）假设下，建立 Kato 型准则（Kato-type criterion），据此分析耗散 MHD 有限能量弱解（finite energy weak solutions）当粘性和磁阻系数趋于零时向理想 MHD 弱解的收敛性。

Result: 在所建立的 Kato 型准则成立时，耗散可压缩 MHD 系统的有限能量弱解随粘性系数与磁阻系数趋于零而收敛到理想 MHD 系统的弱解；特别地，该准则下 MHD 在零粘性与零扩散极限中的反常能量耗散被排除。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Kato 型收敛准则推广至可压缩 MHD 的粘性—扩散消失极限，为判定零耗散极限下能量是否守恒提供了充分条件，并表明在相应正则性与有界性假设下反常能量耗散不会发生。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the vanishing viscosity and magnetic diffusivity limits for the compressible magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) equations on a smooth bounded domain in $\mathbb{R}^3$. Under the assumptions of interior regularity and uniform local boundedness near the boundary, we establish a Kato-type criterion under which finite energy weak solutions to the dissipative MHD system converge to weak solutions to the ideal MHD system as the viscosity and magnetic resistivity coefficients decrease to zero. In particular, under such criterion, the anomalous energy dissipation in the vanishing viscosity and diffusivity limits for MHD is precluded.

</details>


### [6] [Dynamic linear equations in the presence of small perturbations of an interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14494)
*Christian Daveau,Abdessatar Khelifi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究二维热方程与波方程的初边值问题中，由 $C^2$ 边界非均匀体形状小扰动引起的边界电流扰动渐近展开的高阶项，采用场展开法推导、层位势技术证明，将小体积非均匀体的已有结果推广至形状反演算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有渐近展开结果主要针对小体积非均匀体（small-volume inhomogeneities），而对由边界形状小扰动引起的高阶项刻画不足，限制了基于边界测量反演非均匀体形状性质的高效算法开发。

Method: 针对二维热方程与波方程的初边值问题，使用场展开法（field expansion, FE method）进行形式推导，并通过层位势技术（layer potential techniques）给出严格证明。

Result: 严格导出并证明了由 $C^2$ 边界非均匀体形状小扰动所致边界电流扰动渐近展开中的高阶项。

Conclusion: 研究将小体积非均匀体的渐近展开结果推广到形状小扰动情形，为基于边界测量确定非均匀体形状性质的算法提供理论基础。

Abstract: We consider solutions to the initial boundary value problems for both heat and wave equations in two dimensions. In this paper, we aim to advance the development of high-order terms in the asymptotic expansions of current boundary perturbations caused by small perturbations of the shape of a $C^2$-boundary inhomogeneity. Our derivations are rigorously derived by the field expansion (FE) method (formal derivation) and proved by layer potential techniques. It extends those already derived for small-volume inhomogeneities to develop efficient algorithms for determining certain properties of the shape of an inhomogeneity based on boundary measurements.

</details>

### [7] [Nucleon sigma terms in lattice QCD at physical pion mass and in the continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14103)
*Constantia Alexandrou,Christos Iona,Giannis Koutsou,Yan Li,Srijit Paul,Ferenc Pittler*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 利用四个物理π介子质量的 $N_f=2+1+1$ 扭质量格点规范组态，在连续极限下确定核子的轻、奇、粲 σ-项（σ-terms），得到 $\\sigma_{\\pi N}=53.0(7.4)(1.6)$ MeV、$\\sigma_s=58.2(17.0)(2.1)$ MeV、$\\sigma_c=112(28)(25)$ MeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核子 σ-项（σ-term）是刻画手征对称性破缺与标量夸克含量的关键量，需在格点 QCD（lattice QCD）中于物理π介子质量与连续极限下可靠确定。先前结果的系统误差主要来自格距效应与激发态污染，故需多格距、物理质量的系统研究。

Method: 采用四组 $N_f=2+1+1$ 扭质量（twisted-mass）规范组态，π介子质量取物理值且空间体积近似相同，格距由 0.080 fm 至 0.049 fm，所用作用量具 O(a) 改进以支持连续极限外推（continuum extrapolation）。分别分析价夸克（valence）与海夸克（sea）贡献，并处理低能多强子态（multihadron states）引起的激发态污染（excited-state contamination），最后对所有组态作全局拟合（global fits）。

Result: 全局拟合给出 $\\sigma_{\\pi N}=53.0(7.4)(1.6)$ MeV、$\\sigma_s=58.2(17.0)(2.1)$ MeV、$\\sigma_c=112(28)(25)$ MeV，括号内依次为统计与系统不确定度；轻 σ-项精度最高，粲 σ-项不确定度显著较大。

Conclusion: 在物理π介子质量、多格距及 O(a) 改进条件下，可获得核子轻、奇、粲 σ-项在连续极限中的一致估计，并给出去除激发态污染后的统计与系统误差分解。

Abstract: We determine the light, strange and charm $σ$-terms of the nucleon in lattice QCD using four $N_f=2+1+1$ twisted-mass gauge ensembles simulated at physical pion masses and with approximately the same spatial volumes. The four lattice spacings ranging from $0.080$ fm to $0.049$ fm, with an action that has O(a) improvement, enable extrapolating the results to the continuum limit. Both valence and sea quark contributions are analysed accounting for excited-state contamination from low-lying multihadron states. We perform global fits to all ensembles, that yield $σ_{πN}=53.0(7.4)(1.6)$ MeV, $σ_s=58.2(17.0)(2.1)$ MeV and $σ_c=112(28)(25)$ MeV, with statistical and systematic uncertainties, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [Assessing the expressive power of a gauge-invariant PEPS ansatz across a topological phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13852)
*Etsuko Itou,Tsuyoshi Okubo*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该工作将带规范的高斯费米子投影纠缠对态（GGFPEPS）的计算重构为格拉斯曼张量网络（Grassmann TN），结合 HOTRG 与 CTMRG 实现确定性收缩，证明仅含八个复变分参数的拟设可精确刻画纯 Z2 规范理论及其拓扑相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）研究受统计误差限制，难以精确确定变分参数，从而无法可靠评估 GGFPEPS 拟设的表达能力（expressive power）。因此需要一种无统计噪声的确定性计算方案来检验该拟设。

Method: 将 GGFPEPS 的计算重新表述为格拉斯曼张量网络（Grassmann tensor network），对整个网络进行确定性收缩；采用高阶张量重整化群（HOTRG）将计算扩展到 L=34 的大体积，并用角转移矩阵重整化群（CTMRG）评估无限体积系统。

Result: 仅含八个复变分参数的拟设在能量与拓扑纠缠熵（topological entanglement entropy）两方面均准确描述纯 Z2 规范理论，包括拓扑相变区域；由磁化率峰值得到的临界耦合与连续时间量子蒙特卡洛（continuous-time quantum Monte Carlo）预测定量一致。此外，在转变点附近，该拟设所表示的状态位于 plaquette 乘积族之外。

Conclusion: GGFPEPS 拟设以极少参数即可高精度捕捉 (2+1) 维 Z2 规范理论的拓扑相变，且超越了构造时所假设的强弱耦合插值族，表明其具有较强的表达能力；张量网络确定性收缩方法为精确检验此类拟设提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We investigate the expressive power of the gauged Gaussian fermionic projected entangled pair state (GGFPEPS) ansatz, which is a manifestly gauge-invariant tensor network ansatz for $(2+1)$-dimensional lattice gauge theories. In the GGFPEPS ansatz, the gauge field on each link is represented by coupling its configuration to the fermion number of virtual fermions placed at both ends of the link. This ansatz has been studied by variational Monte Carlo (VMC) methods in previous work~\cite{Emonts:2022yom}. However, the statistical error of VMC makes a precise determination of the variational parameters difficult, so that the expressive power of the ansatz could not be examined accurately. In this work, we reformulate the computation as a Grassmann tensor network (TN), in which the whole network is contracted deterministically, and introduce the higher-order tensor renormalization group (HOTRG), which allows us to perform calculations in large volumes up to $L=34$. We also evaluate the infinite volume system using the corner transfer matrix renormalization group (CTMRG). We find that the ansatz with only eight complex variational parameters accurately describes the pure $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory including the region of the topological phase transition, not only in the energy but also in the topological entanglement entropy. The critical coupling obtained from the peaks of their susceptibilities agrees quantitatively with the one predicted by continuous-time quantum Monte Carlo simulations~\cite{wu2012phase}. Furthermore, near the transition point, we show that the ansatz represents a state outside the plaquette product family, which is a one-parameter family of states assumed in the construction of the ansatz as a simple interpolation between the strong and weak coupling limits.

</details>

<details>

Method: 采用已固定规范（gauge-fixed）的格点哈密顿量：边界规范场作为物理模式并使高斯定律（Gauss's law）精确成立；体（bulk）规范场由边界场与一个整数标签决定，该标签对应边界生成、体局域存储的瞬子（instanton）拓扑荷；体等效为电介质（dielectric）。

Result: 对背景规范场，相差一个体电荷单位的扇区在泡利-维拉尔斯减除（Pauli-Villars subtraction）后简并，镜像费米子（mirror fermions）退耦；边界理论不存在真空角。

Conclusion: 哈密顿量中每一项均由边界规范变量、整数标签与费米子模式定义，为量子线路（quantum circuit）构造提供起始点。

Abstract: We present a lattice Hamiltonian for chiral gauge theories on the boundary of a slab. The formulation is gauge-fixed, so the gauge variables are the physical modes of the boundary field, and Gauss's law holds exactly. The bulk gauge field is determined by the boundary field and an integer label, which is the topological charge that the boundary generates and the bulk stores as a localized instanton. The bulk enters as a dielectric, and both gauge anomaly conditions become the question of whether the boundary satisfies Gauss's law on its own. For background gauge fields, we show that sectors that differ by a unit of bulk charge are degenerate after Pauli-Villars subtraction and the mirror fermions decouple. The boundary theory has no vacuum angle. Every term in the Hamiltonian is defined in terms of the boundary gauge variables, the integer label and the fermion modes, a starting point for quantum circuit construction.

</details>

Abstract: We present a constrained mixed variational formulation for incremental irreversible thermodynamics. A single augmented action combines a linearized contact-increment penalty, equilibrium and dissipative Fenchel--Young defects, a storage--transport functional, and multipliers enforcing the constitutive increments. Under frozen-coefficient and boundary assumptions, stationarity yields the linearized contact condition, the Tisza relation, the Onsager flux--force law, and the implicit balance equation. An exact-elimination theorem shows that the contact and multiplier fields can be reconstructed algebraically from a solution of the reduced weak balance problem. We also prove off-shell invariance of the equilibrium--storage--multiplier sector under an equilibrium-sector partial Legendre transformation; invariance of the full augmented action is not claimed. For Fourier heat conduction, we derive a weighted drift identity and a per-step energy-drift bound for the energy-representation three-term incremental functional. In one spatial dimension, a small-time-step estimate yields a uniform $H^1$ bound and a discrete $L^2(0,T_f;H^2)$ bound, leading to cumulative $O(δt)$ drift over fixed time intervals. A conservative mixed discontinuous Galerkin (DG0)--Raviart--Thomas (RT0) discretization with fully implicit conductivity $κ(T)=1+T^2$ preserves discrete energy to roundoff. A separate lumped-mass piecewise-linear benchmark reproduces the predicted finite-step drift.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用半经典描述分析三阶像差（cubic aberration）导致的折叠型焦散（fold caustic）及其Airy正则积分（Airy canonical integral）波场，并结合三维Gross-Pitaevskii方程数值模拟；利用经典Fisher信息（classical Fisher information）与最大似然估计（maximum-likelihood estimation）量化一维分布所携带的相互作用强度信息。

Result: 模拟显示平均场相互作用改变近焦区密度与纵向动量分布，产生非相互作用演化中没有的次级动量空间特征；在沿z方向10^5个探测点样本下，有效相互作用强度的相对不确定度为2.7×10^-3。

Conclusion: 结果确立了研究相互作用驱动的单粒子焦散普适性偏离的框架，并表明物质波透镜（matter-wave lens）可作为平均场相互作用的灵敏探针。

Abstract: Caustics provide a universal connection between classical ray focusing and structured wave patterns. We theoretically investigate this connection in non-linear matter waves focused by a short-duration anharmonic lens acting on a Bose-Einstein condensate. A semi-classical description shows how the leading cubic aberration transforms the harmonic focal point into a fold caustic, whose local wave field is described by an Airy canonical integral. Three-dimensional Gross-Pitaevskii simulations show that mean-field interactions modify the near-focus density and longitudinal momentum distribution, producing a secondary momentum-space feature absent from the non-interacting evolution. We quantify the information about the effective interaction strength carried by the $1D$ distribution using classical Fisher information and maximum-likelihood estimation. We obtain a relative uncertainty of $2.7\times10^{-3}$ for a sample of $10^5$ detected points along $z$. These results establish a framework for studying interaction-driven departures from single-particle caustic universality and promote matter-wave lenses as a sensitive probe of mean-field interactions.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在一维准晶（quasicrystal）中，两粒子跃迁振幅不相等时相互作用束缚对的局域化行为，以理解跃迁不平衡作为调控量子输运的手段。

Method: 采用两体格林函数（two-body Green's function）方法，确定吸引与排斥束缚对的局域化相图，并在强跃迁不平衡和强相互作用极限下给出有效解析描述；同时分析初始在位对的动力学。

Conclusion: 不等跃迁振幅可作为调节准周期晶格中相互作用对输运性质的手段，结果与光晶格（optical lattice）中的超冷原子直接相关。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum localization of two interacting particles moving in a one-dimensional quasicrystal with unequal hopping amplitudes. Using a two-body Green's function approach, we determine the localization phase diagram of attractively and repulsively bound pairs, showing that increasing the hopping amplitude of one constituent while keeping the other fixed raises the critical quasiperiodic strength, promoting pair delocalization. The localization threshold nevertheless remains bounded above by the Aubry-André critical point of the slower constituent. Effective analytical descriptions capture pair localization in the limits of strong hopping imbalance and strong interactions. The dynamics of an initially on-site pair provides signatures of delocalization induced by unequal hopping amplitudes. In particular, the effective spreading exponent of the root-mean-square width of a pair distribution reveals a progression from localized to subdiffusive and eventually superdiffusive behavior as the hopping ratio increases. Our results establish unequal hopping amplitudes as a means of tuning the transport properties of interacting pairs in quasiperiodic lattices, with direct relevance to ultracold atoms in optical lattices.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究环形玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚中由平衡增益/损耗与两个移动光势垒构成的原子电子超导量子干涉仪（atomtronic SQUID, AQUID）中的非厄米约瑟夫森动力学，揭示宇称-时间对称破缺为不可逆输运提供新调控手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约瑟夫森输运研究多集中于厄米体系，缺乏对非厄米、平衡增益损耗条件下不可逆约瑟夫森输运的有效调控机制。本文旨在利用宇称-时间对称（parity-time symmetry）势在破缺空间反演对称时提供的要素，探索非互易（nonreciprocal）约瑟夫森输运与集体激发的可控性。

Result: 增强局域增益/损耗强度可使宇称-时间对称集体模式软化至奇异点，并在腔输出谱中产生额外特征劈裂；对线性平移势垒，非厄米性成为调节临界电流的附加参数；势垒同时平移与旋转时，旋转诱导相位改变反向电流干涉；周期调制下出现更显著的Shapiro台阶。另提出倾斜光晶格（tilted optical lattice）的厄米实现方案。

Conclusion: 宇称-时间对称原子电子SQUID被确立为调控不可逆约瑟夫森输运与驱动量子流体中集体激发的通用平台，并提供实验可行的偏置输运与布居转移工程路径。

Abstract: We investigate non-Hermitian Josephson dynamics in an atomtronic quantum interference device (AQUID), formed by a ring Bose Einstein condensate with balanced atomic gain and loss regions and two moving optical barriers. The balanced gain and loss profile establishes a parity time symmetric potential while breaking spatial inversion symmetry, providing the essential ingredients for nonreciprocal Josephson transport. The dynamics are monitored nondestructively through the phase quadrature spectrum of an optomechanical cavity driven by Laguerre Gaussian beams coupled to the condensate. We demonstrate that increasing the local gain and loss strength softens the parity time symmetric collective mode towards an exceptional point, as verified by Bogoliubov de Gennes analysis. The associated coherent population exchange produces an additional characteristic splitting in the cavity output spectrum. For linearly translating barriers, non-Hermiticity provides an additional control parameter for tuning the critical current. When the barriers undergo simultaneous translation and rotation, the rotation induced phase modifies the interference between counter propagating currents, offering further control over the nonreciprocal response. Under periodic barrier modulation, we find more pronounced Shapiro steps in the current voltage characteristics due to the intrinsic parity time symmetric collective mode. Finally, we propose an alternative Hermitian realization using a tilted optical lattice, providing an experimentally accessible route to engineering biased transport and population transfer. Our results establish parity time symmetric atomtronic SQUIDs as a versatile platform for controlling nonreciprocal Josephson transport and collective excitations in driven quantum fluids.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 复相图重构表明，稳定的径向平衡对应正则化动力学中的相对平衡（relative equilibrium）；而碰撞动力学（impact dynamics）的重构需取奇异极限（singular limit）。

Abstract: We develop a planar polynomial system based on a complex regularization of free rocking and analyze the equilibrium states and phase portraits via an effective radial potential. The conflict between the centrifugal effect, which is introduced by means of regularization, and gravity yields a potential well and an escape barrier for 0 < beta < 27/256; both vanish at a saddle-node bifurcation point. The exact energy conservation law allows us to separate dissipative relaxation, conservative periodic motions along the radial direction, and energy creating trajectories. The node-focus boundary of finite equilibria is different from the degeneracy condition for compactification at infinity. As the last step of analysis, we reconstruct the complex phase portrait and discover that a stable radial equilibrium corresponds to a relative equilibrium in the regularized dynamics, whereas the reconstruction of an impact dynamics requires taking a singular limit.

</details>


### [15] [Thermodynamic Formalism for Non-Invariant Probabilities: Entropy, Divergence, Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13904)
*Artur O. Lopes,Elismar R. Oliveira*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文在不可数符号空间上将 Hölder Jacobian 对应的对偶 Ruelle 算子视为动力系统信道，为未必 σ-不变的概率测度引入 ni-熵与 ni-压力。ni-熵从 Kolmogorov-Sinai 熵经 $\mathcal{M}_σ$ 延拓到全测度空间 $\mathcal{M}$，且具凹性与上半连续性。基于此建立不依赖绝对连续的 ni-Kullback-Leibler 散度及相应信息论变分原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶 Ruelle 算子 $\mathcal{L}_J^*$ 作用在概率测度上，一般会破坏 σ-不变性（σ-invariance），因此经典 Kolmogorov-Sinai 熵不再适用于像测度；而测度本身通常不相对某控制测度（dominating measure）具有密度，经典 Kullback-Leibler 散度依赖绝对连续（absolute continuity）的假设也不适用。论文由此需要构造能处理非不变测度的熵、压力与散度概念。

Method: 以 $Ω=\{1,\dots,d\}^\mathbb{N}$ 上 Borel 概率测度 $\mathcal{M}$ 与 σ-不变概率 $\mathcal{M}_σ$ 为对象，将 $\mathcal{L}_J^*$ 视为“动力系统信道（dynamical channel）”，推广随机矩阵对概率向量的作用。利用长度为一的柱集上的期望值函数（expected value function on cylinders of size one）定义 ni-熵 $h_{ni}$，进而定义势函数 $A$ 的 ni-压力 $\mathcal{P}(A)$，并在不作绝对连续假设下定义 ni-Kullback-Leibler 散度 $D_{ni}(\rho\|\nu)$。随后研究最小输出熵（Minimal Output Entropy）、Donsker-Varadhan 变分原理、$h_{ni}$ 的变分公式、散度界与信息投影（information projections）。

Result: ni-熵 $h_{ni}(\mu)$ 区别于 Shannon 熵，但把 Kolmogorov-Sinai 熵从 $\mathcal{M}_σ$ 延拓到 $\mathcal{M}$，并具有凹性（concavity）与上半连续性（upper semicontinuity）。据此得到 ni-压力 $\mathcal{P}(A)$ 及不要求绝对连续的 ni-散度 $D_{ni}$，并建立散度界、信息投影以及与 ni-压力相关的变分刻画。

Conclusion: 论文表明，通过“柱集期望值”构造的 ni-熵可作为非不变测度上的熵概念，并支撑一整套信息论对象（ni-压力、ni-散度、变分原理、信息投影）的建立，从而把 Ruelle 算子的动力系统框架与信息论变分方法衔接起来。这些结论均在测度不一定 σ-不变、且不一定存在密度的情形下成立。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{M}$ and $\mathcal{M}_σ$ denote, respectively, the sets of Borel and $σ$-invariant probabilities on the uncountable set $Ω=\{1,2,...,d\}^\mathbb{N}$. Given a Hölder Jacobian $J$, the dual Ruelle operator $μ\in \mathcal{M} \mapsto \mathcal{L}_{J}^* (μ)$ will play the role of a dynamical channel; generalizes the action of a stochastic matrix on probability vectors. Since $\mathcal{L}_{J}^*$ generally breaks $σ$-invariance, we introduce the \emph{ni-entropy} $h_{ni}(μ)$ for non-invariant measures, employing the concept of the expected value function on cylinders of size one. $h_{ni}(μ)$ differs from Shannon entropy but extends the Kolmogorov-Sinai entropy from $\mathcal{M}_σ$ to $\mathcal{M}$; $h_{ni}$ is concave and u.s.c. This leads us to the concept of ni-pressure $\mathcal{P}(A)$ of a potential $A:Ω\to \mathbb{R}$. $μ\in \mathcal{M}$ is not a density with respect to a dominating measure. We define an {\it ni-Kullback-Leibler divergence} $D_{ni}(ρ|| ν)$ not assuming absolute continuity. We investigate concepts of Information Theory such as Minimal Output Entropy, Donsker-Varadhan variational principle, a variational formula for $h_{ni}$, bounds for divergence, information projections, and their connections to the ni-pressure $\mathcal{P}$.

</details>

### [16] [Memory-gated transient anomalous transport of quasiparticles out of equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14261)
*Pranay Venkatesh,Tianchu Li,Srijan Bhattacharyya,Andrés Montoya-Castillo*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文以弥散Holstein模型（dispersive Holstein model）为代表，揭示带符号的浴记忆时间（signed bath memory time）决定瞬态输运偏离扩散的方向与强度，其为零时扩散无渐近延迟地出现；调节耗散环境可从亚扩散（subdiffusion）切换至超扩散（superdiffusion）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反常输运（anomalous transport）在凝聚态物理、材料科学与生物物理中普遍存在，但在远离平衡条件下，何种因素决定输运何时、以何种程度、朝何方向偏离扩散，仍是未解决的基本问题。

Method: 采用小极化子（small polaron）的弥散Holstein模型，作为一大类耗散系统的代表，考察带符号的浴记忆时间对瞬态输运行为的调控作用，并将耗散环境相对载流子态密度（density of states）从共振调至非共振。

Result: 记忆时间的符号变化使输运由亚扩散转为超扩散；当记忆时间消失时，扩散无需渐近延迟即可出现。微观上，该记忆效应源于极化子形成（polaron formation）与相干极化子运动（coherent polaron motion）之间的竞争。

Conclusion: 研究建立了基于记忆的瞬态反常输运统一描述，为通过环境调节控制非平衡准粒子（quasiparticle）流动提供了途径。

Abstract: Anomalous transport is ubiquitous across condensed matter, materials science, and biophysics. Yet, in far-from-equilibrium settings, what determines when, how much, and in which direction transport departs from diffusion remains a fundamental open question. By adopting the dispersive Holstein model of small polarons as representative of a wide class of dissipative systems, we show that a signed bath memory time governs the direction and the strength of the transient departure, and that when it vanishes, diffusion emerges without an asymptotic delay. Tuning the dissipative environment from resonant to off-resonant with the carrier density of states, transport turns from subdiffusion to superdiffusion as the memory time changes sign. Microscopically, we trace this memory effect to a competition between polaron formation and coherent polaron motion. These findings establish a unified memory-based description of transient anomalous transport, offering a path to control nonequilibrium quasiparticle flow through environmental tuning.

</details>

*Nicholas Kamp,Louis Deslauriers,Carlos A. Argüelles,Gonzalo Herrera,William G. Thompson*

Main category: physics.ed-ph

TL;DR: 在一为期一周的中微子与天体粒子物理研究生暑期学校中，六位缺乏主动学习经验的讲师经约四小时专家培训后，将同伴教学式主动学习引入讲座；学生与讲师均给予高度正面评价，且多数讲师计划继续使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习（active learning）已在北美本科STEM教育中成为常规，但物理研究生暑期学校仍几乎完全采用传统讲授（traditional lecturing）；本研究检验新手讲师能否在短期培训后成功实施主动学习。

Method: 研究在一周制研究生暑期学校开展，38名来自8个国家的学生参加；六位讲师经物理教育专家约四小时培训后引入同伴教学（Peer Instruction）式主动学习。三条主要讲座系列各以传统与主动两种形式分别授课一天，并在前两日课后及周末对学生问卷调研，同时调研全部六位讲师。

Result: 在覆盖三位讲师、三个主题的每次主动讲座中，82%–100%的受访者认为互动环节有助于保持投入并检验理解；各讲座系列的难度匹配度在主动学习日均评价更佳。周末36名受访学生中33人希望未来研究生课程采用此类教学，无人反对；六位讲师均认为该方法有效并愿再次使用，但仅两人认为实施过程简单。

Conclusion: 经简短的专家主导培训，主动学习新手讲师可为异质听众成功实施主动学习，学生与讲师均希望增加此类教学；暑期学校不仅培养学生，也可促进讲师的教学发展。

Abstract: Active learning is becoming standard practice in undergraduate STEM instruction in North America and is slowly being adopted in graduate courses. Graduate summer schools, one- to two-week programs in which students from many institutions gather for intensive, specialized instruction in their field, are common in physics; they are taught by researchers chosen for their expertise, and their pedagogy remains almost entirely traditional lecturing. We report a study at a one-week graduate school in neutrino and astroparticle physics attended by 38 students from 8 countries. Six lecturers with limited or no prior experience of teaching with active learning introduced Peer Instruction-style active learning into their lectures after approximately four hours of preparation led by a physics-education specialist. Each of the three main lecture streams was taught traditionally on one day and in the active format on the other. Students were surveyed after each of the first two days and at the end of the week; all six instructors were surveyed as well. In every active lecture, spanning three instructors and three topics, 82-100% of respondents reported that the interactive segments helped them stay engaged and check their understanding, and in every stream the difficulty was rated as better matched on the active day. At the end of the week, 33 of 36 wanted such teaching in future graduate courses, and not one respondent disagreed. All six instructors judged the approach productive and intend to use it again; only two found implementation straightforward. With brief expert-led preparation, lecturers new to active learning implemented it successfully for a heterogeneous audience, and both students and instructors want more. The school was also the first time any of the six instructors implemented structured active learning: a summer school can develop its lecturers as well as its students.

</details>

Motivation: 人马座A*在视界尺度上呈现分钟至小时量级的宽带亮度波动，其成因涉及引力、等离子体与光传播的复杂耦合；现有动力学与流体模拟将这种变化归于湍流及局部磁化等离子体结构的形成，但光源内禀涨落如何映射到观测屏幕、光传播效应留下何种可辨识印记尚需厘清。

Conclusion: 文中强调的物理效应预期会体现在吸积流变化的时变特征（timing signature）中，可被现有仪器及未来测光与干涉观测设施探测；但其结论能否推广至真实吸积盘构型仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: The compact radio source at the centre of the Milky Way, Sagittarius A*, exhibits temporal broadband brightness variability. The observed timescales of such fluctuations, ranging from minutes to hours on horizon scales, arise from the complex interplay of gravitation, plasma, and light propagation processes in the immediate vicinity of the supermassive black hole. Kinetic and fluid simulations associate this variability with turbulence and the formation of magnetised, localised plasma structures.

Our aim is to identify distinct imprints of light propagation effects, which encode information both about the source and the underlying space-time geometry.
  We simulate image time series by propagating the emitted light to a distant observer's sky plane in Minkowski, Schwarzschild, and Kerr space-times. We then compute the corresponding observed light curves and power spectra, both for the full observed images and for selected angular sectors of the observer's sky plane.
  We derive a semi-analytical model for the power spectrum of a fluctuating hot spot in flat space-time, reproducing all qualitative features of curved-space-time spectra at moderate inclinations. Moreover, we describe how such power spectra are affected by space-time geometry, inclination, and source velocity, and show that orthogonal two-sector angular decompositions of the sky plane provide information on system orientation and lensing effects.
  The physical effects highlighted in this paper are expected to be present in the timing signature of accretion-flow variability, as observed by current instruments and future photometric and interferometric facilities. Further work is needed to investigate if our conclusions hold for realistic disc configurations.

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究用解析外解匹配有效场论，提取非旋转、具成分分层/弹性壳/相变中子星的动态潮汐响应极点与留数，并以谐振子耦合相对论有效单体动力学，给出模式激发解析振幅与时间依赖的壳层应变。在1.20+1.50 M☉代表双星中，潮汐模式仅贡献≲10⁻² rad的引力波相位移动，但引力模式可在壳层底部造成过度应变，最低剪切模量则导致大范围过度应变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潮汐激发将引力波辐射与中子星内部动力学联系起来，但具成分分层、弹性壳层及可能一阶相变的非旋转星的动态潮汐响应及其对星壳应变的影响尚需系统刻画。

Method: 采用解析的Mano–Suzuki–Takasugi外解将星体微扰与有效场论（effective field theory）匹配，提取相对论潮汐响应的极点与留数；将响应近似为按频率ω_α与模式潮汐形变率λ_α（等价潮汐重叠I_α）表征的谐振子之和，并耦合到含引力红移与参考系拖曳的相对论有效单体（effective-one-body）动力学，分解为受驱与自由振荡。

Result: 受驱-自由振荡分解得到的解析模式振幅在旋近（inspiral）阶段精度达百分之几；可重建时间依赖的壳层应变（crustal strain）。在1.20+1.50 M☉强子—混杂双星中，引力、界面与剪切模式引起≲10⁻² rad的引力波相位移动（phase shift）。

Conclusion: 尽管引力波相位移动很小，引力模式激发仍可在壳层底部附近造成过度应变（overstrain），而最低剪切模式则产生大范围过度应变，表明潮汐模式对中子星壳层形变可能具有观测相关性。

Abstract: Tidal excitation links gravitational-wave emission to the internal dynamics of neutron stars. We study the dynamical tidal response of nonrotating stars with composition stratification, elastic crusts and possible first-order phase transitions. Using analytical Mano--Suzuki--Takasugi exterior solutions, we match stellar perturbations to an effective field theory and extract the poles and residues of the relativistic tidal response. Over the inspiral band, this response is accurately approximated by a sum of harmonic oscillators, as in Newtonian theory, each specified by a frequency $ω_α$ and a mode-specific tidal deformability $λ_α$, or equivalently a tidal overlap $I_α$. We couple these oscillators to relativistic effective-one-body dynamics, including gravitational redshift and frame dragging, to follow mode excitation and gravitational-wave emission. A decomposition into driven and free oscillations gives analytical mode amplitudes accurate to a few percent during inspiral. We also reconstruct the time-dependent crustal strain. Despite this small phase shift, gravity-mode excitation can overstrain the crust near its base, while the lowest shear mode produces widespread overstrain.

Conclusion: 20与30 μm厚BNL LGAD在电学特性与时间分辨率方面表现良好，质子辐照实验为评估其在EIC子探测器真实条件下的运行寿命与辐照鲁棒性提供了基础。

Abstract: The 20 and 30 micrometer thick Low-Gain Avalanche Detectors (LGADs) produced at Brookhaven National Laboratory (BNL) were evaluated. ### [28] [Angle-Resolved Reflectance Measurements in the Vacuum Ultraviolet for Metallic Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14447)
*H. Amar,J. Álvarez,X. Arrue,C. Benitez,A. Cervera-Villanueva,J. Martín-Albo,L. Molina-Bueno,A. Roche,M. Sorel,J. Soto-Oton,S. Teruel-Pardo,N. Yahlali*

TL;DR: 本文介绍在IFIC搭建的旋转式角度分辨反射率测量装置，在气态氩环境中覆盖128–500 nm真空紫外波段测量材料双向反射分布函数，并用GGX微表面模型提取折射率与粗糙度参数以推算半球反射率。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惰性元素闪烁探测器（noble-element scintillation detectors）的光产额强烈依赖材料在真空紫外（VUV）波段的反射特性，而该波段光学测量数据稀缺，缺乏可靠的反射率表征。

Result: 对粗糙铝与不锈钢样品的角分布拟合可提取复折射率（refractive index）的实部与虚部以及表面粗糙度参数；据此将平面内测量外推至整个半球，并由气态氩外推至液氩与液氙介质，从而推断气态氩、液氩和液氙中的半球反射率（hemispherical reflectance）。

Conclusion: 该装置与方法可实现VUV波段材料角度分辨反射特性的系统测量，并借助GGX模型将有限角度测量外推至半球及液相介质，为惰性元素闪烁探测器的光学设计与光产额评估提供数据支持。

Abstract: The light yield of noble-element scintillation detectors depends strongly on the reflective properties of detector materials in the vacuum ultraviolet (VUV), where optical measurements are scarce. For this reason, we built a custom rotary setup at IFIC, equipped with a monochromator and operated in a gaseous argon atmosphere, to perform angle-resolved reflectance measurements in the specular reflection plane. We describe the setup and its extensive characterization, together with the procedure used to measure the bidirectional reflectance distribution function (BRDF) of materials at various angles of incidence, for wavelengths from 128 to 500 nm, covering both the argon (128 nm) and xenon (175 nm) scintillation wavelengths. To illustrate the capabilities of the setup, we present results for rough aluminum and stainless-steel surfaces as reference materials. Fitting the measured angular profiles with the analytical GGX microfacet model allows us to extract the real and imaginary parts of the refractive index and the surface roughness parameter of each sample. These parameters are then used to extrapolate the in-plane measurements to the full hemisphere and from gaseous argon to liquid media, enabling the inference of the hemispherical reflectance in gaseous argon, liquid argon and liquid xenon.

*Manisha Rajput,Farhan Ahmed Siddique,Chung-Che Huang,Ioannis Zeimpekis,Sayani Majumdar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作报道了晶圆级（wafer scale）二维半导体基FeFET，采用2-3层MoS2沟道与20 nm HZO栅栈，呈现异常p型输运与稳定多比特存储特性，存储窗口达4.4 V、ION/IOFF约10^5，并展示无损读出、零栅偏压非易失保持、低工作电压、高写耐久与超快开关速度等优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统浮栅晶体管（floating-gate transistors）在下一代低功耗存储应用中面临局限，而具备低功耗、非易失数据保持（non-volatile data retention）与稳定铁电极化翻转（ferroelectric polarization switching）的FeFET被视为潜在替代方案；二维沟道可提供更好的栅极静电控制（electrostatic gate control）与超越硅基的可微缩性。

Method: 制备晶圆级二维半导体基FeFET，沟道为2-3单层二硫化钼（MoS2），背栅氧化物为20 nm Hf0.5Zr0.5O2（HZO）栅栈，并在+3.8 V至-3.8 V工作电压范围内表征其滞回（hysteresis）与存储特性；通过分析极化电荷（polarization charge）对肖特基势垒宽度（Schottky barrier width）的调控来解释空穴输运与存储行为。

Result: 器件表现出异常p型电荷输运与稳定多比特存储特性，在±3.8 V工作电压范围内具有4.4 V存储窗口（memory window）及10^5的ION/IOFF比；极化电荷诱导的肖特基势垒宽度调制被证实强烈影响MoS2-HZO FeFET的空穴输运与存储性能。

Conclusion: 该FeFET具备无损数据读出（nondestructive data readout）、零栅偏压非易失保持、低工作电压、高写耐久（write endurance）与超快开关速度；其1T存储单元可简化密集三维集成阵列架构，有望用于CMOS后段制程（BEOL）兼容电路及面向AI负载的三维单片集成（3D monolithic integration）架构。

Abstract: Ferroelectric field-effect transistors (FeFETs) with low-power operation and non-volatile data retention enabled by stable ferroelectric polarization switching, have emerged as promising alternatives to conventional floating-gate transistors for next-generation low-power memory applications. A FeFET with two-dimensional (2D) channel provides the opportunity for better electrostatic gate control and scalability beyond silicon. In this work, we report wafer scale 2D semiconductor-based FeFETs with Hf0.5Zr0.5O2 (HZO) gate stack showing unusual p-type charge transport and stable multibit memory properties. FeFETs based on 2-3 monolayer Molybdenum disulfide (MoS2) channel and a 20 nm HZO back gate oxide layer exhibit a large hysteresis with a memory window spanning 4.4 V under a operating voltage range of +3.8 to -3.8 V, and an ION/IOFF ratio of 10^5. We demonstrate that the polarization charge-induced modification of Schottky barrier width strongly affects the hole transport and memory properties of MoS2-HZO FeFETs. We further demonstrate several advantages and unique features of the FeFETs such as nondestructive data readout, non-volatile data retention under zero gate bias, low operation voltage, high write endurance and ultra-fast switching speed. These 1T memory cells can simplify the dense 3D integrated array architecture and offer additional practical functionalities, such as fast programming and erasing of large data arrays that are absent for many conventional memories. The demonstrated MoS2 and HZO-based FeFET shows great potential for on-chip complementary metal-oxide semiconductor (CMOS)-back-end-of-line (BEOL) compatible circuits for multifunctional devices, especially for 3D monolithically integrated architectures for AI workload.

*Ayobami Daramola,Giovanni Bonny,Gilles Adjanor,Christophe Domain,Ghiath Monnet,Anna Fraczkiewicz,Duc Nguyen-Manh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用短程硬化 EAM-21 势的分子动力学方法，系统比较五种无钴 Fe-Ni-Cr-Mn 高熵合金与纯 Ni、Fe60Ni40、316L 模型合金及无锰参照合金的辐照损伤初级过程，揭示 Mn 对缺陷能量、级联产物与团簇化学的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无钴（Co-free）Fe-Ni-Cr-Mn 高熵合金（high-entropy alloys, HEAs）被视为核结构候选材料，但 Mn 成分变化如何影响其辐照损伤初级过程缺乏系统认识，需与已有实验辐照合金及传统 Fe-Ni-Cr 合金对照研究。

Method: 使用短程硬化处理的 EAM-21 势开展分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，计算五种 HEA（ES1、P35、Y3、Kumar、C30）及纯 Ni、Fe60Ni40、Fe70Ni10Cr20（316L 模型合金）、Fe50Ni34Cr16 的层错能（stacking fault energy）、阈位移能（threshold displacement energy）、点缺陷形成与迁移能，并在 300 K 与 823 K 下模拟 5–40 keV 共 10^15 个位移级联（displacement cascades）。

Conclusion: 结果支持 Fe60Ni40 与 Fe70Ni10Cr20 中级联内直接形成位错环（loop），而 ES1 中该机制较弱；Mn 的引入通过降低缺陷能量、改变团簇化学与层错特征调控辐照损伤演化。

Abstract: Co-free Fe-Ni-Cr-Mn high-entropy alloys (HEAs) are candidates for nuclear structural applications. We used molecular dynamics with the EAM-21 potential, stiffened at short range, to study five HEAs that have been irradiated experimentally: Fe37Ni34Cr16Mn13 (ES1), Fe35Ni35Cr15Mn15 (P35), Fe46Ni22Cr15Mn17 (Y3), Fe27Ni28Cr18Mn27 (Kumar) and Fe30Ni30Cr10Mn30 (C30). They were compared with pure Ni, Fe60Ni40, the Fe70Ni10Cr20 model alloy of 316L, and Fe50Ni34Cr16, which is ES1 with Mn replaced by Fe. We calculated stacking fault energies, threshold displacement energies, point-defect formation and migration energies, and 1015 displacement cascades from 5 to 40 keV at 300 and 823 K. The stacking fault energy follows the Ni content: 19-44 mJ/m^2 in the alloys and 113 mJ/m^2 in Ni. Compared with the Mn-free alloys, the HEAs have lower threshold displacement energies (48-56 against 64-74 eV) and lower dumbbell formation energies, and Mn is the element most transported by interstitials. All the concentrated alloys leave about the same number of Frenkel pairs, 56-72 at 40 keV, half the number in Ni. At 40 keV, 54-58% of the interstitials are in clusters in the HEAs, against 67-71% in Fe60Ni40, Fe70Ni10Cr20 and Fe50Ni34Cr16 and 82% in Ni. At the same cluster size, clusters of 4-12 interstitials are faulted about half as often in the HEAs, and this does not follow the stacking fault energy. The surviving interstitials are enriched in Mn in the HEAs and in Ni in the Mn-free alloys, and depleted in Cr. The results support direct loop formation in cascades in Fe60Ni40 and Fe70Ni10Cr20 and less of it in ES1.

</details>


### [31] [Hybridization-brightened interlayer excitons in multilayer WSe2 controlled by sliding ferroelectricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13264)
*Youngki Yeo,Idan Kizel,Dror Hershkovitz,Omri Meron,Iva Plutnarova,Zdenek Sofer,Watanabe Kenji,Takashi Taniguchi,Elad Katash,Dan Oron,Haim Suchowski,Jing-Yang You,Moshe Ben Shalom*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 少层WSe2中观测到约2.23 eV的明亮窄线宽高能层间激子（h-IX），其发光随层数与滑移铁电极化演化，多层杂化与滑移铁电可共同调控其能量、偶极与发光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层间激子（interlayer excitons, IXs）虽具备空间分离的电子-空穴波函数与电可调偶极矩，但波函数重叠度低限制了发光，尤其对高能态而言尤为明显。

Method: 在少层WSe2中观测近2.23 eV的h-IX，比较不同层数及平行堆叠3L/3L滑移铁电界面下的光谱，并结合GW-BSE计算分析激子构型与光学权重。

Result: 原生2H-WSe2中高能激子发光随厚度增亮，计算显示其具有层内-层间混合构型与增强光学权重；滑移铁电样品中h-IX能量与斯塔克（Stark）响应随极化取向（Pup/Pdown）分化，电场驱动畴壁移动引起两支间谱重迟滞转移。

Conclusion: 多层杂化可为高能层间激子提供光学活性途径，滑移铁电性则作为互补自由度调控其能量、偶极与发光，并实现铁电翻转的光学追踪。

Abstract: Interlayer excitons (IXs) combine spatially separated electron and hole wavefunctions with electrically tunable dipole moments, but their reduced wavefunction overlap generally limits optical emission, particularly for high-energy states. Here we report a bright, spectrally narrow high-energy interlayer exciton (h-IX) near 2.23 eV in few-layer WSe2 and investigate how its optical activity evolves with layer number and sliding-ferroelectric polarization. In pristine 2H-WSe2, we observe thickness-dependent brightening of high-energy exciton emission. GW-BSE calculations reveal mixed intralayer and interlayer configurations with enhanced optical weight, suggesting that multilayer hybridization contributes to the observed brightening. In parallel-stacked 3L/3L WSe2 containing a sliding-ferroelectric interface, the h-IX exhibits polarization-dependent energies and Stark responses that distinguish Pup and Pdown domains. Electric-field-driven domain-wall motion produces hysteretic transfer of spectral weight between the two h-IX branches, enabling optical tracking of ferroelectric switching. These results establish multilayer hybridization as a route to optically active high-energy interlayer excitons and sliding ferroelectricity as a complementary degree of freedom for controlling their energy, dipole and emission.

### [34] [TASCopilot: A provenance-preserving assistant for triple-axis neutron scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13374)
*Carina Jacobson,Aditya Purohit,Srikar Kovvali,Lekhnath Poudel,Jeffrey W. Lynn,William D. Ratcliff*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 TAS Co-pilot，一个面向三轴光谱（triple-axis spectroscopy, TAS）的科学助手框架，通过保留来源追踪（provenance）、结构化工具通信与人类审查，将文献、数据处理与测量证据连接为可核查的科学主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验决策常依赖分散在论文、仪器日志、数据文件和专业软件中的证据，科学助手需在不把合理解释变成无依据结论的前提下整合证据。TAS（用中子探测材料结构与激发）领域正存在这一需求。

Method: 框架保留来源信息（来源、处理步骤、审查决定），独立维护的科学工具以结构化请求与结果通信；验证规则与人工审查约束证据支持主张的方式；语言模型仅做信息抽取与结果解释，不批准科学主张也不控制仪器；并采用依赖感知的规约（dependency-aware reduction）执行既有科学流程并复用未变化的中间结果。

Result: 回顾性 SrMn$_2$Sb$_2$ 研究将相关材料文献与历史对准记录连接至 20 次实测扫描的磁激发建模；在 BT-7 的实时研究使用专用仪器接口完成精细对准与 40 次自适应测量。与同一晶体在相同条件下常规测量的一致性支持了该采集与分析链条。

Conclusion: 两个案例展示了一种在用户设施中跨文献、数值分析与测量维护可核查证据的方法论。

Abstract: Experimental decisions often depend on evidence scattered across papers, instrument logs, data files, and specialist software. A scientific assistant must connect that evidence without turning a plausible interpretation into an unsupported conclusion. TAS Co-pilot addresses this problem for triple-axis spectroscopy (TAS), which uses neutrons to probe material structure and excitations. It preserves provenance: the sources, processing steps, and review decisions behind each result. Independently maintained scientific tools communicate through structured requests and results; validation rules and human review govern how evidence can support a claim. Language models extract information and explain outcomes but do not approve scientific claims or control the instrument. Dependency-aware reduction executes existing scientific routines and reuses unchanged intermediate results. A retrospective SrMn$_2$Sb$_2$ study connects literature on a related material and historical alignment records to modeling of magnetic excitations in 20 measured scans. A live BT-7 study uses a dedicated instrument interface for fine alignment and 40 adaptively selected measurements. Agreement with a conventional measurement of the same crystal at shared conditions supports the acquisition and analysis chain. These cases demonstrate a methodology for maintaining inspectable evidence across literature, numerical analysis, and measurement at a user facility.

</details>


### [35] [Hidden interfacial magnetism in RuO$_2$ heterostructures revealed by a superconducting spin switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13579)
*Tetsuma Mandokoro,Hisakazu Matsuki,Shutaro Karube,Rodrigo de las Heras,Andrei Mazanik,Akihiro Yamada,Fugo Tokoro,Susumu Hirata,Ryusuke Hisatomi,Yoichi Shiota,Vitaly N. Golovach,F. Sebastian Bergeret,Teruo Ono*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用GdN/Nb/RuO$_2$超导自旋开关（superconducting spin switch）探测Nb/RuO$_2$界面处的隐藏磁性，发现其源于Nb夺取RuO$_2$氧原子形成的薄RuO$_x$氧化还原层，该层表现为弱且磁硬的铁磁体，并受空位诱导各向异性调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuO$_2$曾被视为交替磁性（altermagnetism）的代表候选，但现认为其体相无磁性，仅在表面与界面出现弱、静态且空间不均匀的磁性。该磁性的来源、稳定机制及其与界面态的关联仍属开放问题，且常规磁测量难以探测埋藏界面磁性。

Method: 构建GdN/Nb/RuO$_2$三层结构的超导自旋开关，通过界面交换场（exchange field）取向调节Nb的超导转变温度，从而探测传统磁强计无法企及的界面磁性；并利用超电流原位调节探测灵敏度。

Result: 开关效应揭示出一个隐藏的磁子系统，归因于Nb从RuO$_2$中夺取氧而形成的薄RuO$_x$氧化还原（redox）层；该层为弱且磁硬的铁磁体（ferromagnet），具空位诱导各向异性（vacancy-induced anisotropy）。此外，超电流可放大开关效率，实现探测灵敏度的原位可调。

Conclusion: Nb/RuO$_2$界面磁性源于界面化学反应生成的RuO$_x$层而非本征交替磁性；超导自旋开关可作为埋藏界面隐藏磁性的定量低温探测手段。

Abstract: RuO$_2$, once the flagship altermagnetic candidate, is now believed to be nonmagnetic in the bulk, yet weak, static and spatially inhomogeneous magnetism emerges at its surfaces and interfaces. Where this magnetism resides, what stabilises it, and whether it is connected to these states remain open questions. Here we address these questions at the Nb/RuO$_2$ interface using a superconducting spin switch. A GdN/Nb/RuO$_2$ trilayer allows interfacial exchange-field alignment to tune the superconducting transition temperature of Nb, probing interfacial magnetism inaccessible to conventional magnetometry. The switch reveals a hidden magnetic subsystem, which we attribute to a thin RuO$_x$ redox layer formed as Nb scavenges oxygen from RuO$_2$. This layer behaves as a weak and magnetically hard ferromagnet with vacancy-induced anisotropy. Furthermore, supercurrent amplifies the switching efficiency, making the probe sensitivity tunable in situ. Superconducting spin switches thus emerge as a quantitative low-temperature probe of hidden magnetism at buried interfaces.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过晶体结构预测与基于机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials）的分子动力学模拟，揭示混合阴离子氯氧化物（mixed-anion oxychlorides）中氧含量如何调控框架维度与锂离子输运，并筛选出若干室温自扩散率约 10^-6 cm²/s 的稳定候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合阴离子氯氧化锂（lithium oxychlorides）具有高锂电导率，但氧含量、结构框架与锂离子输运之间的关系尚不清晰，缺乏对成分设计的定量指导。

Result: 随氧含量增加，结构由孤立富氯多面体演变为一维链，进而形成连通度更高的二维与三维框架；第 5 族体系中 x ≈ 0.67–1.5 区间锂位构成三维渗透网络并出现快扩散。快速输运需低维框架支持经欠配位锂位（undercoordinated Li sites）的长程迁移、低势垒及各锂位能量相近。细网格搜索识别出 LiNbO_{0.67}Cl_{4.67}、LiTaO_{0.75}Cl_{4.5}、LiNbO_{1.5}Cl_3 与 LiTaO_{1.5}Cl_3 为稳定候选，预测室温自扩散率最高约 10^-6 cm²/s。

Conclusion: 研究确立了氧含量对结构框架与锂离子输运的控制规律，为锂氯氧化物电解质（lithium oxyhalide electrolytes）的成分设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Mixed-anion lithium oxychlorides exhibit high Li conductivity, but the relationship between oxygen content, structural framework, and Li-ion transport remains unclear. Here, crystal structure prediction and molecular dynamics simulations with machine-learning interatomic potentials are used to examine $\mathrm{Li}_yM\mathrm{O}_x\mathrm{Cl}_{6-2x}$ ($M$ = Nb and Ta [Group 5] and $M$ = Ti, Zr, and Hf [Group 4]). Increasing oxygen content changes isolated chloride-rich polyhedra into one-dimensional chains and subsequently more highly connected two- and three-dimensional frameworks. In the Group 5 series, fast Li diffusion occurs over $x \approx 0.67$--1.5, where the Li sites form a three-dimensional percolating network. Potential of mean force maps and nudged elastic band calculations reveal that fast Li-ion transport requires a low-dimensional framework supporting long-range migration through undercoordinated Li sites with low barriers, together with similar energies across Li sites. Finally, we perform fine-grid compositional search, which identifies $\mathrm{LiNbO}_{0.67}\mathrm{Cl}_{4.67}$, $\mathrm{LiTaO}_{0.75}\mathrm{Cl}_{4.5}$, $\mathrm{LiNbO}_{1.5}\mathrm{Cl}_3$, and $\mathrm{LiTaO}_{1.5}\mathrm{Cl}_3$ as stable candidates with predicted room-temperature self-diffusivities of up to $\sim 10^{-6}\,\mathrm{cm}^2/\mathrm{s}$. These findings establish how oxygen content controls framework and Li-ion transport, guiding the design of lithium oxyhalide electrolytes.

</details>


### [37] [An infrared divacancy color center in bulk hexagonal diamond for quantum communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13736)
*Xuanxuan Zhang,Chen-Min Dai,Cong Li,Chunlan Ma,Zenghua Cai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在寻找体相六方金刚石中的本征色心（intrinsic color center），作为光纤量子通信（fiber-based quantum communication）的潜在发射源，填补该材料体系内色心候选者缺乏系统性比较的空白。

Method: 采用杂化密度泛函理论（hybrid density functional theory）与约束占据ΔSCF（constrained-occupation delta SCF）计算，识别出五种具有稳定光学激发态的碳空位与双空位构型；并在三点位形坐标模型（three-point configuration-coordinate model）下比较其辐射与非辐射性质。

Result: 带正电的轴向双空位 VVa1+ 在五种候选中表现最优：ZPL 发射速率 43.9 MHz、ZPL 量子效率 11.8%，Huang-Rhys 因子为 0.395（结构弛豫弱），多模光致发光（multimode photoluminescence）计算给出的 Debye-Waller 因子为 69.37%，表明零声子贡献占主导；其预测 ZPL 为 0.603 eV（2057.3 nm），与 1310 nm 和 1550 nm 电信目标的光子能量间距在候选中最小。

Conclusion: VVa1+ 被认定为面向光纤量子通信的有前景本征色心；借助量子频率上转换（quantum frequency upconversion），可将其强红外 ZPL 转换至电信 O 波段与 C 波段。

Abstract: Intrinsic color centers are investigated in bulk hexagonal diamond as potential emitters for fiber-based quantum communication. Hybrid density functional theory and constrained-occupation delta SCF calculations identify five carbon-vacancy and divacancy configurations with stable optical excited states. A comparison of their radiative and nonradiative properties within a three-point configuration-coordinate model identifies the positively charged axial divacancy, VVa1+, as the most promising candidate. It exhibits the highest calculated zero-phonon-line (ZPL) emission rate of 43.9 MHz and ZPL quantum efficiency of 11.8% among the five candidates. Its weak structural relaxation yields a Huang-Rhys factor of 0.395, while a multimode photoluminescence calculation gives a Debye-Waller factor of 69.37%, indicating a dominant zero-phonon contribution. The predicted ZPL at 0.603 eV (2057.3 nm) also has the smallest photon-energy separation from the 1310 and 1550 nm telecom targets among the candidates. These results identify VVa1+ as a promising intrinsic color center for fiber-based quantum communication, with quantum frequency upconversion offering a route from its strong infrared ZPL to the telecom O and C bands.

</details>


### [38] [Electronic Origin of Contrasting Li and Na Clustering on Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13798)
*G. Kuderowicz,O. I. Malyi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用石墨烯（graphene）的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），揭示锂（Li）与钠（Na）吸附原子成簇倾向差异的电子结构起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负极材料中金属离子（如Li、Na）在碳表面的存储性能受离子键与金属键（ionic and metallic bonding）相互作用的调控，且Li与Na虽化学性质相近，却在石墨烯表面表现出不同的团簇（cluster）形成倾向，其电子机制尚需阐明。

Method: 采用石墨烯体系的第一性原理计算，比较孤立吸附原子与金属团簇形成前后电子向石墨烯的转移、费米能级（Fermi level）位移及新形成的金属—金属键合轨道能级。

Result: 孤立吸附原子向石墨烯转移电子并使费米能级升高；金属团簇形成则产生与金属—金属键合相关的低能轨道，从而降低费米能级与总能量。Li与Na过程相似，但新形成金属态的能级位置决定了二者团簇形成行为的差异。

Conclusion: Li与Na在石墨烯上团簇形成倾向的差异源于金属态能级（metallic state energy levels）的不同，为理解碳基负极中离子存储与金属析出提供电子层面解释。

Abstract: The electrochemical performance of anode materials is often defined by the ability of metal ions (e.g., Li and Na) to be effectively stored within the host material over a specific range of chemical potentials. This is particularly true for adsorbed Li and Na atoms on carbon surfaces (e.g., graphite or pore surfaces in hard carbon), where the interplay between ionic and metallic bonding often takes place - while metal atoms act as ionic species, in a finite concentration limit, they tend to form metallic bonding when they approach each other. What makes the case special is that, despite similar chemical identities, Li and Na atoms exhibit distinct tendencies for cluster formation; for example, the formation of Li metallic clusters on a graphene surface is only thermodynamically favorable after reaching a finite metal-ion concentration. Motivated by this, herein, we use first-principles calculations on graphene to examine the electronic origin of Li and Na clustering. Specifically, we demonstrate that the separated adsorbed atoms donate electrons to graphene, shifting the Fermi level to higher energies; however, the formation of metal clusters can result in the formation of lower-energy orbitals that are specifically associated with metal-metal bonding, thus lowering the Fermi level (and hence total energies). Importantly, while the process is somewhat similar for Na and Li atoms, the energy levels of the newly formed metallic states govern the difference in metallic cluster formation between Li and Na.

</details>


### [39] [Elastic Landau states as martensite nuclei in austenite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13816)
*Mohd Tahseen,Vivekanand Dabade*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文研究奥氏体—马氏体界面（austenite–martensite interfaces）的几何封闭性，指出经典相容条件与余因子条件均不能保证界面几何闭合，进而识别出可实现闭合、无应力马氏体核的两参数马氏体变形族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典相容条件（$λ_2=1$）仅允许平面界面，余因子条件（cofactor conditions）虽允许曲面界面，但二者均无法保证界面的几何闭合，因而无法解释嵌入奥氏体基体中的闭合无应力马氏体核的形成。

These correspond to cubic to monoclinic-II and cubic to orthorhombic transformations, respectively. We show that these states can be assembled to form arbitrarily shaped, stress-free martensitic islands surrounded by austenite. Such states can arise even in conventional shape memory alloys that do not satisfy supercompatibility, provided hierarchical twinning produces an average deformation that is a simple shear. The theory provides a geometric mechanism for self-accommodation. Its predicted deformation gradients agree with those reconstructed from experimentally observed microstructures. This agreement suggests that these microstructures originate from simple shear.

Abstract: Understanding thermal transport in structurally disordered carbon materials remains a fundamental challenge because conventional models based on a single coherence contribution cannot accurately describe heat conduction over a broad temperature range. Depending on the structural state, two or three coherence channels are required to accurately describe the thermal conductivity.

While the channel amplitudes increase with material density, the activation energies remain nearly invariant, indicating universal vibrational energy scales. ### [45] [Chemotactic recruitment offsets congestion in honeybee trophallaxis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13314)
*William Magrogan,Orit Peleg,Zachary P. Kilpatrick*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个随机框架（stochastic framework），将依赖化学信号的velocity-jump搜索与供体聚集处的随机顺序服务（random-order service）耦合，以群体喂食所需时间度量交哺效率（trophallactic efficiency），揭示效率在中间放弃率（abandonment rate）处最优，并说明服务与信号反馈可自组织地实现与局部服务能力匹配的招募。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜜蜂群体通过交哺（trophallaxis）分配食物，高效转移既需要搜索者找到供体并被服务，但化学信号吸引形成的聚集（aggregation）可能造成阻塞。论文旨在解释招募（recruitment）与服务拥塞（congestion）之间的权衡如何决定交哺效率。

Method: 建立随机建模框架，将依赖化学信号的velocity-jump搜索过程与供体聚集处的随机顺序服务相耦合，并以群体喂饱既定比例成员所需时间来量化交哺效率。

Result: 定向招募源于已喂食蜜蜂在供体附近逗留并增强局部化学线索，但逗留也会减慢服务；效率在平衡招募与拥塞的中间放弃率处达到最大。研究还刻画了位移线索（displaced cues）何时仍有用，以及竞争供体如何分配搜索者：在两个服务速率不等的供体情形下，较快者更早产生被喂食蜜蜂从而信号更强，把招募引向吞吐量更高的供体、远离易拥塞者。

Conclusion: 服务与信号之间的反馈（feedback）可在无集中协调（centralized coordination）的情况下自组织招募，使其匹配局部服务能力。

Abstract: Honeybee colonies distribute food by trophallaxis, a sequence of local interactions in which nectar donors transfer resources to hungry nestmates. Efficient transfer requires a searcher to find a donor and then be served. Chemical signals may draw searchers to a donor, though the crowd they form may block the way. We develop a stochastic framework coupling velocity-jump search that depends on chemical signaling to random-order service at donor aggregations, and measure trophallactic efficiency by the time the colony needs to feed a prescribed fraction of its members. Directed recruitment emerges because fed bees linger near donors and strengthen local chemical cues, but lingering also slows service. Trophallactic efficiency is therefore maximized at an intermediate abandonment rate that balances recruitment against congestion. We determine when displaced cues remain useful and how competing donors partition searchers. With two donors of unequal service rate, the faster one produces fed bees sooner and so advertises more strongly, drawing recruitment toward the donor that can sustain more throughput and away from the one that would congest. Together, these results show how feedback between service and signaling can self-organize recruitment to match local service capacity without centralized coordination.

</details>

### [46] [Tail Asymptotics for Gaps in Unitary Ensembles and the Airy Point Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13666)
*Junwen Liu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文推导幺正系综（unitary ensembles）与Airy点过程（Airy point process）中第k与第k+1大粒子间间隙的尾部渐近，给出右尾对数渐近（精确到常数项）与左尾立方渐近（系数k/6）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多关注最大粒子或首间隙，而固定指标k的相邻次序统计量之间的间隙在双侧尾部（极大与极小）的精细渐近及其对领头粒子的条件效应尚缺系统刻画。

Method: 对有限N系综考察重标度间隙大于a_N与小于b_N的概率，取联合极限N→∞、a_N→∞且a_N=o(N^{2/3})，以及N→∞、b_N↓0；对Airy点过程分别分析间隙趋于无穷的右尾与趋于零的左尾，并由此导出条件极限律。

Result: 两模型均得到右尾对数渐近至常数项、左尾立方渐近且系数为k/6；并以之导出给定异常大或小间隙下领头粒子的极限条件分布，k=1时大间隙条件化使最大粒子涨落由Tracy–Widom转为Gaussian（类似BBP相变）。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了固定数目的领头间隙中哪一个取得大极大或小极小的极限概率，表明间隙稀有事件会显著改变领头粒子的波动统计。

Abstract: We derive tail asymptotics for the gap between the $k$th and $(k+1)$st largest particles, with $k$ fixed, in unitary ensembles and in the Airy point process. For the finite-$N$ ensembles, we consider the probabilities of a rescaled gap larger than $a_N$ and of a rescaled gap smaller than $b_N$, in the joint limits $N\to\infty$, $a_N\to\infty$ with $a_N=o(N^{2/3})$, and $N\to\infty$, $b_N\downarrow0$, respectively. For the Airy point process, the right and left tails are analyzed as the gap size tends to infinity and zero, respectively. More precisely, in both models, we obtain logarithmic right-tail asymptotics up to the constant term and cubic left-tail asymptotics with coefficient $k/6$. As an application, we also derive the limiting conditional laws of the leading particles given the existence of an abnormally large or small gap. Abstract: We develop a rigorous framework for the description of general local composite observables in Euclidean quantum field theories. Via renormalisation dictated by sufficiently precise approximate solution theory of corresponding flow equations, we construct various local observables of the sine-Gordon model on the full space up to $β^2<6π$, including highly singular ones such as vertex fields $\exp(iαφ(y))$ in the optimal Wick regime $|\operatorname{Re}(α)|<4π/β$ and derivatives of arbitrarily high order, i.e. $\partial^μφ(y)$ for any multiindex $μ\in \mathbb{N}^2_0$. ### [49] [Phase transitions in the hard-wall large deviations for the Gaussian free field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14448)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临界高度附近，两种机制相互竞争：场整体上移的代价由容量（capacity）控制，剩余局部违规被抑制的代价由其期望数量控制，两者在该窗口内同为 $N^{d-2}/\log N$ 阶，因此需要刻画竞争的精确结果。

Method: 在临界窗口 $h_N(\beta)^2=g_0(4\log N+\log\log N+2\beta)$ 内，通过一个结合 Dirichlet 能量（Dirichlet energy）与指数势（exponential potential）的变分问题（variational problem）来表达该项概率的渐近性。

Result: 在临界窗口内得到精确的渐近速率；窗口外以相应的移动速度得到渐近结果：当 $\beta\to-\infty$ 时仅容量决定代价，当 $\beta\to+\infty$ 时由违规的期望数量决定代价。

Conclusion: 作为应用，给出随机交织（random interlacements）对宏观体覆盖水平（cover level）的下偏差界（lower-deviation bounds），并确定其在转变点处的衰减阶。

Abstract: We study the probability that the Gaussian free field on $\mathbb{Z}^d$, $d\ge3$, stays above a level $-h_N$ throughout the lattice $N$-blow-up of a bounded Lipschitz domain, when $h_N$ is close to the entropic-repulsion height $\sqrt{4g_0\log N}$. Two mechanisms compete: a collective upward displacement of the field, whose cost is governed by capacity, and the suppression of the remaining local violations, whose cost is their expected number, both of which are of order $N^{d-2}/\log N$ exactly in the window $h_N(β)^2=g_0(4\log N+\log\log N+2β)$. We obtain a sharp asymptotic rate expressed through a variational problem combining a Dirichlet energy with an exponential potential inside this critical window. Outside this window we obtain the asymptotics at the appropriate moving speeds: capacity alone governs the cost as $β\to-\infty$, and the expected number of violations as $β\to+\infty$. As an application, we derive lower-deviation bounds for the cover level of a macroscopic body by random interlacements, which determine the order of decay at the transition.

</details>

### [50] [After equilibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13148)
*Jorge Kurchan*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该摘要提出，"复杂度（complexity）"刻画的是动力学在比常规广延可观测量（extensive observables）平衡所需更精细层面上对状态空间（state space）的探索，因而在这些可观测量平衡后仍持续增长；这种探索可通过大偏差（large deviations）或"稀有"可观测量（rare observables）的平衡时间来量化，并自然映射到玻璃态动力学的"陷阱模型（Trap Model）"上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准广延可观测量平衡后系统的动力学行为仍是未解问题，需要一个超越常规平衡判据的量来刻画更精细层次的相空间探索；摘要由此动机出发，指出已有工作中提出的复杂度概念及其量化方式。

Method: 以动力学在时间上达到的大偏差（large deviations）来量化探索程度，等价地以"稀有"可观测量（如磁化强度的指数）达到平衡所需时间来度量；并将复杂度的演化映射到玻璃态动力学的标准模型——"陷阱模型（Trap Model）"上。

Result: 在该映射下，复杂度对应陷阱模型中不断发现更深的极小值（progressively deeper minima），而陷阱模型本身展现出老化（aging）与有效温度（effective temperatures）等玻璃态特征。

Conclusion: 复杂度可与玻璃态动力学模型建立自然对应关系，从而为理解平衡后持续进行的相空间精细探索提供统一框架，并将复杂度的增长解释为向更深极小值的逐级发现。

Abstract: `Complexity' measures the exploration of state space by the dynamics at a finer level of detail than that required for the equilibration of standard extensive observables , and therefore continues to grow long after these observables have equilibrated. In a recent paper, we proposed that this exploration may be quantified by the large deviations attained over time, or, equivalently, by the time taken for `rare' observables -- such as, for example the exponential of the magnetization -- to equilibrate.
  It turns out that the evolution of complexity can then be naturally mapped onto a standard model of glassy dynamics, the "Trap Model" , which exhibits aging and effective temperatures. Under this mapping, the exploration of increasingly fine details of phase-space corresponds to the discovery
  of progressively deeper minima in the glassy model.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: AlphaHits 是一种基于 Transformer 的 LHC 物理“击中级”（hit-level）基础模型，直接作用于探测器击中（detector hits），实现全事件带电粒子重建与多任务重建分类；其 TrackFormer 变体在完整 TrackML 事件上取得 0.959 的 TrackML 分数（据报道为全探测器包容性带电粒子重建的最高值），并在 Higgs 事件分类等任务上达到与全重建输入基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖重建后的高层输入或对目标径迹施加横向动量（transverse momentum, $p_T$）选择，限制了从低层探测器信息直接学习可迁移表示的能力；本文旨在探索面向 LHC 物理的可扩展、击中级通用基础模型（foundation models），以覆盖完整带电粒子内容并支持多任务学习。

Result: AlphaHits-TrackFormer 在所有可重建径迹上取得 0.959 的 TrackML 分数，且覆盖低至约 $150\,\mathrm{MeV}$ 的低 $p_T$ 粒子群；Higgs 事件分类 AUC 为 0.944，与使用全重建输入的 Particle Transformer（ParT）基线相当；喷注重建、喷注味分类与缺失横向动量（missing transverse momentum）回归性能与基于 Delphes 的基线相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，直接从探测器击中开展可扩展的全事件径迹重建与多任务学习具有潜力，是迈向可迁移击中级表示与 LHC 物理通用基础模型（general-purpose foundation models）的有前景的一步。

Abstract: We introduce AlphaHits, a Transformer-based approach towards hit-level foundation models for LHC physics, building on the TrackFormer and HiggsFormer architectures. AlphaHits operates directly on detector hits and demonstrates scalable full-event charged-particle reconstruction and multi-task event reconstruction and classification from low-level detector information. AlphaHits-TrackFormer reconstructs the full charged-particle content of complete TrackML events without imposing a transverse-momentum selection on the target tracks. It processes both pixel and strip hits in events containing up to $1.5\times10^5$ hits under HL-LHC-like pileup conditions and assigns hits to individual reconstructed trajectories across its kinematic range. This includes the challenging low-$p_T$ population down to approximately $150\,\mathrm{MeV}$ in TrackML samples. It achieves a TrackML score of 0.959 over all reconstructable tracks, representing, to our knowledge, the highest reported TrackML score for full-detector, inclusive charged-particle reconstruction. Building on the TrackFormer architecture, AlphaHits is pretrained on charged-particle tracking and fine-tuned for multi-task reconstruction and classification. From a shared hit-level representation, it predicts tracks, jets, jet flavour, and event-level observables. AlphaHits achieves an AUC of 0.944 for Higgs event classification, with performance comparable to a Particle Transformer (ParT) baseline using fully reconstructed inputs. It also achieves performance comparable to Delphes-based baselines for jet reconstruction, jet flavour classification, and missing transverse momentum regression. These results highlight the potential for scalable full-event tracking and multi-task learning directly from detector hits, representing a promising step towards transferable hit-level representations and general-purpose foundation models for LHC physics.

Abstract: Recent studies from various experiments at the LHC and RHIC suggest that small collision systems exhibit quark-gluon plasma (QGP) like signatures that were typically observed in large systems. The correlations between hard and soft particle production in $pp$ collisions are studied in events with $Υ$ mesons, which provide a valuable hard probe to study such effects. The multiplicity and distributions of kinematic variables of charged particles produced in association with an $Υ$ meson are measured using 139 fb$^{-1}$ of data at $\ensuremath{\sqrt{s}}\xspace = 13$~TeV collected by the ATLAS experiment at the LHC. The measurement is performed separately for the first three $Υ$(nS) states in intervals of their transverse momentum. At low transverse momentum, the associated charged-particle multiplicity is observed to be systematically lower in collisions where $Υ$(2S) or $Υ$(3S) is produced compared to $Υ$(1S), with the difference decreasing as the $Υ$ transverse momentum increases.

*Camilia Ben Messaouda,Theo Reymermier,Paul Swallow,Oleg Brandt for the ANUBIS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文在 ANUBIS 实验框架下，将此前针对 BC6（电子耦合）与 BC7（μ子耦合）基准场景的重味中性轻子（HNL）灵敏度研究扩展至 BC8 场景，即 HNL 仅与 τ 轻子耦合，并完成从信号产生到灵敏度提取的完整分析。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重味中性轻子（Heavy Neutral Leptons, HNLs）是中微子部分最小且动机充分的扩展，小混合角下自然长寿命。已有 ANUBIS 研究仅覆盖单一代 Majorana HNL 与电子或 μ 子专属耦合的 BC6、BC7 场景，τ 耦合的 BC8 场景尚属空白，需补充以形成完整的味空间覆盖。

Method: 采用 SET-ANUBIS v1.0.0 框架开展端到端（end-to-end）分析：从信号样本产生（signal-sample generation）、接收度评估（acceptance evaluation）到灵敏度提取（sensitivity extraction）。由于 τ 轻子本身不稳定，且带电流（charged-current）HNL 衰变模式仅在运动学 τ 产生阈值以上开放，该场景的产生、衰变与事件选择特征无法通过味重标度（rescaling）电子或 μ 子耦合结果获得，须独立模拟。

Result: 摘要未给出具体灵敏度数值或排除界限，仅说明完成了 BC8 场景下的投影灵敏度（projected sensitivity）分析流程与结果的提取。

Conclusion: BC8 场景构成 BC6/BC7 的互补补充，使 ANUBIS 对单代 Majorana HNL 的味耦合覆盖趋于完整；同时表明 τ 耦合情形因τ不稳定性与阈值效应，必须独立建模而非依赖味重标度，SET-ANUBIS v1.0.0 为这类分析提供了可复用的端到端框架。

Abstract: Heavy Neutral Leptons (HNLs) provide a minimal and well-motivated extension of the Standard Model neutrino sector, and are naturally long-lived for small mixing angles between SM neutrinos and HNLs. A previous ANUBIS study determined the projected sensitivity to a single Majorana HNL coupled exclusively to the electron or to the muon, corresponding to the BC6 and BC7 benchmark scenarios of the Physics Beyond Colliders initiative. The present article extends that work to the complementary BC8 scenario, in which the HNL couples exclusively to the tau lepton. As the tau lepton is itself unstable and because charged-current HNL decay modes become available only above the kinematic tau lepton production threshold, the production, decay and event-selection characteristics of this scenario cannot be obtained by rescaling the electron- or muon-coupled results in flavour. The analysis is carried out end to end, from signal-sample generation through acceptance evaluation to sensitivity extraction, with the released SET-ANUBIS~v1.0.0 framework.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量型夸克（vector-like quark）X$_{5/3}$是顶夸克的重费米子伴态，其存在可缓解标准模型（standard model, SM）的规范层级等问题，因此需通过实验搜寻检验其可探测性。

Abstract: A search for the pair production of a vector-like quark, X$_{5/3}$, a heavy fermionic partner of the top quark with an electric charge of 5/3, is presented. The search is performed in the final state with one lepton, an electron or a muon. Proton-proton collision data collected by the CMS experiment at the LHC from 2016 to 2018, at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, are used. Two decay modes are considered. In the conventional decay mode, the X$_{5/3}$ is assumed to decay exclusively into a top quark and a W boson. In an alternative, exotic scenario, it is assumed to decay exclusively into a top quark and a charged Higgs boson, assumed to subsequently decay exclusively into top and bottom quarks. No excess of data events over the standard model (SM) expectation is observed. Expected and observed upper limits on the pair production cross section of the X$_{5/3}$ are set at 95% confidence level as functions of the X$_{5/3}$ mass. These are the most stringent limits on the X$_{5/3}$ decaying to a top quark and a W boson, and the first limits on the X$_{5/3}$ decay into a top quark and a charged Higgs boson.

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文解析地刻画了振荡高斯型（oscillatory Gaussian）扰动对史瓦西黑洞准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）激发系数 $C_n$ 的依赖关系，覆盖脉冲宽度 $σ$ 与载波频率 $ν$ 的整个参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有直觉通常认为高效激发准正规模需要载波频率与模式频率匹配，本文旨在检验并超越"频率匹配"这一唯象判据，给出跨越脉宽与载频的系统性解析图像。

Method: 将激发系数分解为内禀激发因子与源相关重叠（overlap）之积，后者在复 QNM 频率处取值为源正、负频支的相干叠加，其振幅精确因子化为高斯谱包络（Gaussian spectral envelope）与干涉因子（interference factor）的乘积；并解析分离出相位结构，最后以时域数值模拟（time-domain numerical simulations）检验。

Result: 激发振幅仅在窄带（narrowband）源下于 $ν=ω_n^{\rm Re}$ 达峰；对足够宽带（broadband）脉冲，峰值移至 $ν=0$，故高效激发不必然要求载频与模式频率匹配。支间干涉仅在 $ν\neq0$ 时调制振幅，其显著程度由 $ω_n^{\rm Re}/ω_n^{\rm Im}$ 控制；相位分为仅依赖模式、仅依赖脉宽与依赖 $ν$ 三项，最后一项源于同一干涉，且在固定脉宽下以 $ν=0$ 为唯一极大。数值验证给出 $|C_0|$ 的相对中位误差约 4%；$|C_1|$ 在 $σ\leq5$ 时约 16%，全参数空间约 36%；相位中位差 $n=0$ 约 0.14 rad，$n=1$、$σ\leq5$ 约 0.34 rad。

Conclusion: 结果表明 QNM 激发无法仅凭频率匹配来理解，源的光谱宽度与正负频支干涉共同决定激发效率与相位；该解析框架可为更贴近实际的天体物理（astrophysical）情形提供定性启示。

Abstract: We map analytically the excitation of Schwarzschild quasinormal modes (QNMs) $C_n$ by oscillatory Gaussian perturbations across the pulse width $σ$ and carrier frequency $ν$. The excitation coefficient of each mode is determined by the product of an intrinsic excitation factor and a source-dependent overlap evaluated at the complex QNM frequency. For oscillatory Gaussian perturbations, this overlap is the coherent sum of the positive- and negative-frequency branches of the source, with its amplitude factorizing exactly into a Gaussian spectral envelope and an interference factor. We observe that the excitation amplitude peaks at $ν=ω_n^{\rm Re}$ only for narrowband sources. For sufficiently broadband pulses, the maximum lies at $ν=0$, so efficient excitation does not necessarily require matching the carrier and the mode frequency. We observe that the interference between branches, modulates the amplitude only for nonzero $ν$, a feature generic to real oscillatory sources. The prominence of the interference-induced modulation is controlled by the ratio $ω_n^{\rm Re}/ω_n^{\rm Im}$. The phase separates into a mode-only, a width-only and a $ν$-dependent term, the last generated by the same interference, with $ν=0$ being its unique maximum at fixed width. The analytical predictions are validated against time-domain numerical simulations, showing a relative median error of $\sim 4\%$ for $|C_0|$. For $|C_1|$, the median error is $\sim 16\%$ when $σ\leq 5$, increasing to $\sim 36\%$ across the full parameter space. The median phase difference for $n=0$ is $\sim0.14 \,\rm rad $ and for $n=1$, $σ\leq5$ it is $0.34\,\rm rad$. Our results demonstrate that QNM excitation cannot be understood through frequency matching alone and may provide qualitative insights into more realistic astrophysical scenarios.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质过密区域（暗物质尖峰）可使中等/极端质量比旋进（IMRIs/EMRIs）辐射的引力波退相位，从而间接探测暗物质，但该通道要求尖峰在宇宙学时间尺度上存续，故需评估宿主黑洞并合历史对尖峰的破坏。
