Conclusion: 结合此前关于 EMRI 诱导耗损的研究，能将可承载可探测暗物质尖峰的黑洞质量范围限定为 10^6 M_⊙–5×10^7 M_⊙（z ≤ 3）。

Abstract: Dark matter over-densities around massive black holes, known as dark matter spikes, can dephase gravitational waves emitted by intermediate- and extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (IMRIs/EMRIs), offering a promising way to indirectly detect dark matter. This detection channel, however, requires the spike to survive over cosmological time-scales. We investigate the impact that a host black hole's merger history has on its dark-matter spike. We focus on how the inspiral of a massive-black-hole (MBH) binary modifies and disrupts these spikes within the central $\sim 100$ Schwarzschild radii of the black hole. We find that a single merger with mass ratio $q = 0.03$ is capable of reducing a spike's density by around 3 (5) orders of magnitude at 100 (5) Schwarzschild radii across all polar angles relative to the binary's plane, independent of the primary MBH's mass, with larger mass ratios inducing even stronger depletion. In fact, a single past merger with mass ratio as small as $q = 0.005$ reduces central densities by 2--3 orders of magnitude, making gravitational-wave dephasing difficult to detect. We relate these results to the MBH merger history by analysing cosmological simulation data from Horizon-AGN. We find that mergers become increasingly common as the MBH mass increases, with mergers of mass ratio q > 0.03 having occurred in 50% of MBHs of mass $3\times10^7M_\odot$ and 95% of mass $10^8M_\odot$ at z=0. These fractions are reached at even lighter masses ($2\times10^7M_\odot$ and $5\times10^7M_\odot$, respectively) at z=3. We find that accretion-induced adiabatic MBH growth is unable to cause appreciable spike re-growth between such mergers in {\tt Horizon-AGN}. Thus, in combination with previous studies of EMRI-induced depletion, this work constrains the mass range over which massive black holes can host detectable DM spikes to $10^6M_\odot$--$5 \times 10^7M_\odot$ for $z \leq 3$.

### [58] [Eccentricity greatly enhances the detectability of line-of-sight acceleration in gravitational wave signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13197)

TL;DR: 该文指出，双星引力波信号中的偏心率（eccentricity）会显著提升对视线加速度（line-of-sight acceleration, LSA）等环境效应的探测灵敏度。关键在于从首个可探测的偏心谐波进入探测频段时开始分析，而非沿用 n=2 谐波的常规起点，从而覆盖更长的早期旋进信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学形成的致密双星系统（compact object binaries）具有偏心率分布尾部，并可能受环境扰动影响；如何提高对这类环境效应（如 LSA）的探测能力是核心动机。

Method: 采用 Fisher 分析（Fisher analysis）进行预测，并在最先进的偏心波形模型下开展完整的贝叶斯参数估计（Bayesian parameter estimation）；分析起点改为首个可探测偏心谐波进入探测频段的时刻。

Result: 在信噪比（SNR）足够时，20 Hz 处偏心率由 e20=0 增至约 0.1 可使 LSA 约束提升超过一个量级；对低 SNR 信号也预测有约 5 倍放大。

Conclusion: 充分利用该效应需持续改进高效偏心波形模型与似然评估（likelihood evaluation），以高效分析持续数千秒且含大量谐波的信号。

Abstract: Compact object binaries formed dynamically are characterized by a tail of eccentric sources and by the potential presence of environmental perturbations. Here we show that the presence of eccentricity in a gravitational-wave (GW) signal strongly enhances the sensitivity to environmental effects, focusing in particular on line-of-sight acceleration (LSA). We demonstrate that this enhancement is recovered in Fisher-analysis forecasts and, crucially, persists in full Bayesian parameter estimation using state-of-the-art eccentric waveform models. The key insight is that the signal should be analyzed from the moment the first detectable eccentric harmonic enters the detector band, rather than from the customary entry of the $n=2$ harmonic: this allows to probe the early inspiral and thus a longer signal, over which the GW dephasing introduced by LSA becomes substantially larger. We show that with this approach constraints on LSA can increase by more than an order of magnitude between eccentricities at 20~Hz $e_{20}=0$ and $e_{20}\sim0.1$, for signals with sufficient signal-to-noise-ratio (SNR), while we predict a factor $5$ amplification even for low-SNR ones. Fully exploiting this effect necessitates the continued improvement of efficient eccentric waveform models and likelihood evaluations, enabling the efficient analysis of signals lasting thousands of seconds and containing a large number of harmonics.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 强标量化Kerr黑洞的观测特征未必仅由n=1像主导；n≥2像也可能对观测到的偏振辐射有显著贡献，其相对重要性取决于吸积盘的径向发射率分布（radial emissivity profile）与观测者倾角。

Abstract: We investigate the intensity polarization properties of the indirect images of a geometrically and optically thin accretion disk around fully self-consistent models of Kerr black holes with synchronized scalar hair. The presence of a massive, self-gravitating scalar field can dramatically alter the lensing properties of the spacetime, resulting in morphologically complex indirect images. We show that for a representative high scalar charge model, this results in the presence of high order central ring-like images at low observer inclinations. These images are not exponentially demagnified, and their polarized intensity, relative to the direct images, is significant. We therefore conclude that the observational signature of strongly scalarized Kerr black holes may not be dominated solely by the $n=1$ image, as images with $n\geq2$ may also contribute significantly to the observed polarized emission, depending on the radial emissivity profile of the disk and the observer inclination.

</details>


### [60] [Challenging the hoop conjecture with spinning pancakes and baguettes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13266)
*Robin Croft,Gerardo García-Moreno,Paolo Pani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文通过构建轴对称旋转尘埃初值族，检验"环猜想（hoop conjecture）"在含转速情形下的表述：只要质量取包含转动能的准局域质量（quasilocal mass），猜想在14%系数误差内成立；若改用视界不可约质量（irreducible mass）或ADM质量，则随自旋出现显著偏离甚至达约25%的表观违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞唯一性定理与奇点定理均无法给出视界形成的一般判据，而环猜想（hoop conjecture）通过将包围质量与物质分布在各个方向上的致密度相联系来填补这一空白，但其在旋转情形下的适用性需要定量检验。

Result: 当准局域质量计入转动能时，猜想对旋转构型成立，系数与 unity 的偏差在14%以内，与非旋转情形一致；若改用视界不可约质量（irreducible mass），偏差随自旋增大，与 Kerr 黑洞情形类似。对无视界构型，将质量限制为物质面内包围能量时猜想成立，而使用含外部转动场能的 ADM 质量会导致最高约25%的表观违反。

Conclusion: 环猜想的成立与否敏感依赖于质量定义的选择；作者进一步讨论了无视界紧致构型的可能演化、其与弱宇宙监督假设（weak cosmic censorship）的关系及对无视界紧致天体的启示。

Abstract: Understanding when gravitational collapse produces a black hole remains a central challenge in general relativity. Black-hole uniqueness results characterize the stationary endpoint under suitable assumptions, while singularity theorems establish geodesic incompleteness in the presence of trapped surfaces and appropriate energy and global conditions. Neither, however, provides a general criterion for horizon formation. The hoop conjecture offers such a criterion by relating the enclosed mass to the compactness of a matter distribution in every direction. We construct axisymmetric families of initial data describing rotating dust and test a formulation of the conjecture adapted to this setting. We explore highly prolate and oblate configurations with both constant and differential rescaled angular-velocity profiles, including configurations near the threshold for apparent-horizon formation. We find that the conjecture holds for rotating configurations, with a coefficient within 14% of unity as in the nonrotating case, provided the quasilocal mass includes the rotational energy. If the irreducible mass of the apparent horizon is used instead, the deviations grow with spin, as they do for Kerr black holes. For horizonless configurations, the conjecture likewise holds when the mass is restricted to the energy enclosed by the matter surface, whereas using the ADM mass, which also includes rotational field energy outside the matter, leads to apparent violations of up to $\approx$25%. We also discuss the possible evolution of compact configurations without apparent horizons, their relation to weak cosmic censorship, and their implications for horizonless compact objects.

</details>


### [61] [Viscosity heats, diffusion drains: Open-system thermodynamics in unimodular cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13269)
*Miguel Cruz,Samuel Lepe*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该摘要论证广义相对论（general relativity, GR）中的体黏性（bulk viscosity）与幺正引力（unimodular gravity）中的能量扩散（energy diffusion）在物质非绝热性上存在精确对应关系（$Π\leftrightarrow \dot{Q}/3H$），但将该对应推广至可观测量与稳定性时因吸收能量扇区的温度 $T_D$ 而破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于幺正宇宙学（unimodular cosmology）是否绝热的争议，源于将熵（entropy）仅归属于物质还是归属于总有效流体（total effective fluid）的分歧；本文旨在厘清两种非绝热描述（体黏性与能量扩散）的对应条件及其适用范围。

Method: 以共动熵产生率（comoving entropy production）$\dot{Q}(1/T_D-1/T_m)$ 刻画能量扩散，考察 $T_D\rightarrow\infty$ 与 $T_D\rightarrow T_m$ 的极限；并用宇宙学运动学量（deceleration $q$、jerk $j$）与有效声速（effective sound speed）偏移 $c_s^2-\omega$ 对比两种模型的观测含义。

Result: 有限 $T_D$ 时扩散等价于与外部热库（external reservoir）的热交换，可冷却开放流体，从而在可观测量与稳定性上破坏黏性—扩散对应；$Π$ 由减速参数 $q$ 确定而扩散由 jerk $j$ 确定，故 $Λ$CDM 是幺正宇宙学的绝热极限，但以黏性建模时仍表现为耗散。若暗物质（dark matter）为守恒静止质量的气体，一致扩散仅移除热能、禁止 phantom 行为，并以有限热库约束宇宙学项的 growth；生成守恒粒子数的 logotropic 暗流体需在 $z\simeq12$ 存在相对论性暗物质。采用 Hayward–Kodama 视界温度时，幺正引力中的广义第二定律（generalized second law）可由局域版本推出，而体黏性情形须另行施加。

Conclusion: 黏性与扩散的对应仅在熵归属与 $T_D$ 取极限的特定条件下成立；幺正宇宙学的绝热性质应被视为 $Λ$CDM 的极限，而非与耗散模型等价，且其热力学稳定性与视界第二定律具有更自然的结构。

Abstract: In GR with bulk viscosity and in unimodular gravity with energy diffusion, the matter sector is non-adiabatic, and their balance, entropy, and temperature equations match if $Π\leftrightarrow \dot{Q}/3H$. Disagreement over whether unimodular cosmology is adiabatic comes from assigning entropy to matter only or to the total effective fluid. The key is the temperature $T_D$ of the sector absorbing the diffused energy: comoving entropy production is $\dot{Q}(1/T_D-1/T_m)$, yielding earlier limits for $T_D \rightarrow \infty$ and $T_D \rightarrow T_m$. For finite $T_D$, diffusion acts like heat exchange with an external reservoir: unlike viscosity, which only heats a closed fluid, diffusion into a colder bath can cool an open one, breaking the viscosity-diffusion correspondence for observables and stability. Cosmographically, $Π$ is fixed by the deceleration $q$, while diffusion is fixed by the jerk $j$; thus $Λ$CDM is the adiabatic limit of unimodular cosmology but remains dissipative when modeled via viscosity. The diffusive analogue of $|Π|/ρ$ is the shift $c_s^2-ω$ in effective sound speed, set by perturbative stability rather than acceleration. If dark matter is a gas with conserved rest mass, consistent diffusion removes only thermal energy, forbids phantom behavior, and bounds the growth of the cosmological term by the finite thermal reservoir; producing a logotropic dark fluid with conserved particle number would require relativistic dark matter at $z\simeq12$. With the Hayward-Kodama apparent-horizon temperature, the generalized second law in unimodular gravity follows from the local one, whereas for bulk viscosity it must be imposed separately.

Result: 注入实验表明该流程性能可满足需求，采用该方法的搜索是可行的（viable）。

Conclusion: 该搜索方案可行，并将成为Gaia观测与连续引力波分析的首次结合。

Abstract: Recent results from the Gaia third data release have highlighted 21 potential neutron stars candidates in wide, highly-eccentric binary systems and provide estimates of their orbital parameters. We present a feasibility study for a directed search for continuous gravitational waves (CWs) tailored for these and similar systems. To this extent, we have computed a frequency metric to place templates in the parameter space accounting for uncertainties on the orbital parameters. We have built upon the FastTracks infrastructure to efficiently tackle the high-dimensional search space, leveraging a combination of downsampling and interpolation to reduce the computational cost of generating the binary Doppler correction. We performed an injection campaign to demonstrate the performance of this procedure and showed that a search using this approach is viable. Such a search would represent the first combination of Gaia observations and CW analysis.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在一阶标量–高斯–博内耦合下，对标量场方程做内部积分，得到以能量密度（energy density）加径向压力（radial pressure）为权重的单极荷表达式；该荷由 lapse 加权的空间高斯–博内曲率（Gauss–Bonnet curvature）确定。

Result: 在径向零能量条件（radial null energy condition）下，该单极荷的符号与耦合常数一致，且对任意物态方程（equation of state）成立；同阶下与 Schwarzschild–Tangherlini 黑洞的荷符号相反。当空间为三维时该曲率恒为零，故荷消失。

Conclusion: 高维（D>4）静态球对称恒星普遍携带一阶标量单极荷，其存在性与符号具有普适性，并与同阶黑洞呈符号反转；该效应本质依赖于空间维数高于三维。

Abstract: Above four dimensions every static, spherically symmetric star, isotropic or anisotropic, carries a scalar monopole at first order in a scalar--Gauss--Bonnet coupling, given by an interior integral weighted by the energy density plus radial pressure. Under the radial null energy condition its sign is that of the coupling for any equation of state, opposite to that of a Schwarzschild--Tangherlini black hole at the same order. The charge is fixed by the lapse-weighted Gauss--Bonnet curvature of space, which vanishes identically when space is three-dimensional.

</details>

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出基于频率轨迹（frequency tracks）与模拟推断（Simulation-Based Inference, SBI）的无波形（waveform-free）EMRI分析方法：正确谐波对（harmonic pair）在掩膜情况下仍以>0.99概率进入前2名，98%情形可直接判识；序贯SBI数小时内将内禀参数先验体积压缩至多10^13倍，结合探测统计量精化后再压缩约4个数量级。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 跨各掩膜水平（masking levels），正确谐波对以>0.99概率进入前2名，在限定物理合理候选时98%案例可直接判识；序贯SBI于数小时GPU时间内将内禀参数先验体积压缩至多10^13，探测统计量精化再压缩至多4个数量级，且在重度掩膜轨迹下仍有效。

Conclusion: 基于频率轨迹的SBI为EMRI谐波识别与紧缩先验体积提供了快速、无波形依赖的途径，可支撑定向后续分析（targeted follow-up），并显著减轻后续基于MCMC分析的计算负担。

Abstract: Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals (EMRIs) are key targets for the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna, but their long, multi-harmonic waveforms make search and parameter estimation computationally expensive. A promising alternative is to work directly with frequency tracks recovered by a time-frequency search pipeline. We investigate whether such tracks can be used to (i) identify which harmonic(s) a recovered track corresponds to, and (ii) constrain the intrinsic EMRI parameters, including when tracks are partially masked near plunge. We first train Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) networks on large banks of azimuthal and radial fundamental-frequency tracks. For harmonic identification, we combine pairs of candidate tracks under different harmonic-label assumptions and use the resulting posterior medians and network acceptance rates to classify the correct labelling. For parameter identification, we apply a sequential (truncated-prior) SBI scheme and refine the results by cross-checking posterior samples against a time-frequency detection statistic. The correct harmonic pair is among the top-2 ranked candidates with probability $>0.99$ across masking levels, and is identified outright in 98% of cases when restricted to physically plausible candidates. Sequential SBI shrinks the intrinsic-parameter prior volume by up to $10^{13}$ within a few hours of GPU time, and the detection-statistic refinement reduces it by up to four further orders of magnitude, even for heavily masked tracks. Frequency-track-based SBI thus provides a fast, waveform-free way to identify EMRI harmonics and obtain tight prior volumes for targeted follow-up, substantially reducing the burden on subsequent MCMC-based analyses.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study a modified teleparallel gravity model of the $f(T)$ type. We propose a new functional form for in order to obtain a geometric origin to the $ΛCDM$ behavior in an appropriate limit. A perturbative expansion around the $ΛCDM$ regime is carried out, and the model's cosmic dynamics are calculated inside the flat FLRW background. By employing a Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analysis using multiple observational datasets, such as Cosmic Chronometers, Type Ia Supernova compilations (DES-SN5YR, Pantheon+SH0ES, and Union 3.0), DESI DR2 BAO measurements, and Planck 2018 CMB distance priors, to constrain the model parameters. The findings show that the suggested $f(T)$ model accurately replicates the observed accelerated expansion and is statistically consistent with current cosmological observations. The model stays close to the $ΛCDM$ scenario while permitting tiny dynamical adjustments, as evidenced by the extra parameter $γ$, which quantifies deviations from the conventional cosmological model, being limited to values near zero.

</details>


### [73] [Images of Regular Black Holes with Hedgehog Scalar Hair Embedded in Radiatively Inefficient Accretion Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14306)
*Yuxuan Shi,Hao Wei*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文以M87*的质量与距离为基准，生成带刺猬标量毛（hedgehog scalar hair）的几何正则黑洞周围辐射低效吸积流（RIAF）的同步辐射总强度图像，考察观测倾角、盘厚度与频率的影响。结果表明正则核心使临界曲线与辐射环系统性收缩，但这类偏离史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）的效应需超出当前基线能力的角分辨率才能分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞几何临界尺度如何在吸积流图像中体现，受光子传播、等离子体辐射过程与仪器分辨率共同支配；需厘清正则黑洞标量毛参数对可观测图像特征的系统性影响及其可分辨性。

Method: 采用M87*的质量与距离，对几何正则、带刺猬标量毛黑洞周围的辐射低效吸积流（radiatively inefficient accretion flow, RIAF）进行同步辐射成像，系统扫描观测倾角、盘厚度与观测频率，并以L/M（标量毛参数）从0到0.9作对比。

Conclusion: 正则核心系统性地收缩辐射环并改变所需等离子体密度，但要将这些偏离与史瓦西黑洞区分，所需角分辨率高于现有基线所能达到的水平，故当前观测难以检验此类标量毛效应。

Abstract: How a black hole's geometric critical scale manifests in an accretion-flow image is governed by photon propagation, plasma radiative processes, and instrumental resolution. In this work, we investigate radiatively inefficient accretion flows (RIAF) surrounding geometrically regular black holes with hedgehog scalar hair. Adopting the mass and distance of $\mathrm{M87}^*$, we generate total-intensity synchrotron images and systematically explore the viewing inclination, disk thickness, and observing frequency. For the fiducial setup at $230\,\mathrm{GHz}$, raising $L/M$ from $0$ to $0.9$ contracts the critical curve diameter by $4.78\%$, shrinks the half-flux radius by $4.07\%$, and lowers the required electron-density scale by $9.15\%$. At $84\,\mathrm{GHz}$, the primary brightness peaks shift farther outside the critical curve due to enhanced synchrotron self-absorption, whereas the sharper emission rings at $230$ and $345\,\mathrm{GHz}$ closely trace the critical curve. Ultimately, we find that although the regular core systematically contracts the emission ring and alters the required plasma density, resolving these deviations from a Schwarzschild black hole demands angular resolution finer than that achievable with current baselines.

</details>


### [74] [Dynamical obstructions to primary Proca hair and its logarithmic Maxwell continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14344)
*Anirudh Pradhan,K. Ghaderi,Ulug'bek Eshkarayev,Sardor Sabirov,Javlon Rayimbaev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文检验 Gauss–Bonnet 引力正则化矢量理论中原初黑洞毛发（primary black hole hair）的动力学可容许性，发现即使径向条件满足，约束角向系统仍存在负平方角速度的纵向模，构成局部角向阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始常模（constant norm）非平凡解族无法同时满足正偶极动能与实且非退化的径向张量特征，因此需考察正则化后毛发解在动力学上是否真正可容许。

Method: 采用正则哈密顿约化（regular Hamiltonian reduction）处理退化边界；在非退化径向分支上为同一矢量加入 Maxwell 项，要求模（norm）随空间变化，并取正的对数延拓进行数值构造；通过独立规范约化与完整背景进行交叉验证。

Result: 正对数延拓在空间无穷远具有有限质量，并可数值构造出规则未来视界；所构造背景在采样点满足径向张量与横向矢量条件，但约束偶角向系统（constrained even angular system）存在负平方角速度的纵向模。

Conclusion: 推导出含矢量约束与四次相互作用（quartic interaction）的逆对数系数并经独立方法验证；该局部角向阻碍在径向条件相容的情况下依然存在，说明该毛发解不具备完全的动力学可容许性。

Abstract: We examine the dynamical admissibility of primary black hole hair in a regularized vector theory of Gauss--Bonnet gravity. The original nontrivial constant norm family cannot satisfy both positive dipole kinetic energy and real nondegenerate radial tensor characteristics. A regular Hamiltonian reduction treats the degenerate boundaries. For asymptotically flat exteriors on the nondegenerate radial branch, adding a Maxwell term for the same vector requires a varying norm. Its positive logarithmic continuation has finite mass at spatial infinity and numerically constructed regular future horizons. The constructed backgrounds satisfy the radial tensor and transverse vector conditions at the sampled points. However, the constrained even angular system has a longitudinal mode with negative squared angular speed. We derive its inverse logarithm coefficient, including both the vector constraint and quartic interaction, and verify it using independent gauge reductions and the full backgrounds. This local angular obstruction persists despite the compatible radial conditions.

</details>


### [75] [Black Holes beyond General Relativity: The Effective Metric Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14353)
*Manuel Del Piano,Stefan Hohenegger,Francesco Sannino*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该综述系统阐述有效度规描述（Effective Metric Description, EMD）：以近视界级数展开和物理坐标不变量为参数，构建模型无关的黑洞时空形变框架，并推广至阴影与准正规模等观测量计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强场引力观测使黑洞成为检验广义相对论（General Relativity, GR）偏离的窗口，但形变黑洞几何模型繁多，逐模型与观测对比不切实际，因而需要模型无关的统一描述框架。

Method: 对静态球对称黑洞时空，采用从事件视界出发的固有径向距离或自由下落观测者固有时间等坐标不变量作参数，在视界附近作级数展开；求解与几何对称性相容的自洽性条件通解，推导保证有限霍金温度与曲率不变量的普适正则性条件，并用帕德重求和（Padé resummation）将描述外推至远离视界区域。

Result: 框架证明不同物理量描述之间的等价性，并推广至带电、极端及低维黑洞；借助帕德重求和实现模型无关的黑洞阴影计算，以及在试探场近似与程函极限（eikonal limit）下的准正规模（quasi-normal mode）频率计算，并给出与其他模型无关参数化方案之间的显式变换映射。

Conclusion: EMD 作为超越 GR 形变的有效描述，在基础黑洞物理、量子引力启发的时空形变与天体物理观测量之间提供了自洽的桥梁；其在大量超越 GR 几何模型上得到检验。

Abstract: Black holes provide a privileged window on gravity in the strong-field regime, and deviations from the classical solutions of General Relativity (GR) may encode information about quantum gravity and other extensions of the theory. The abundance of proposed deformed black hole geometries, however, makes model-by-model comparison with observations impractical. This review presents a unified account of the Effective Metric Description (EMD), a model-independent framework in which deformations of static, spherically symmetric black hole spacetimes are parametrized by physical, coordinate-invariant quantities, such as the proper radial distance from the event horizon or the proper time of a free-falling observer. After reviewing the classical black hole solutions and the open issues that motivate going beyond GR, we develop the EMD as a series expansion near the horizon for a given physical quantity. We provide the general solution of the resulting self-consistency conditions compatible with the symmetries of the geometry, derive universal regularity conditions ensuring a finite Hawking temperature and curvature invariants, establish the equivalence of descriptions based on different physical quantities, and extend the framework to charged, extremal and lower-dimensional black holes. Padé resummation extends the description away from the horizon, enabling model-independent computations of the black hole shadow and, in the eikonal limit, of quasi-normal mode frequencies in the test-field approximation. The framework is tested on a broad set of geometries used to model physics beyond GR, and compared with other model-independent parametrizations through explicit transformation maps. As an effective description of deformations beyond GR, the EMD provides a coherent bridge between fundamental black hole physics, quantum-gravity-inspired deformations, and astrophysical observables.

</details>


### [76] [Gravitational radiation from a particle in circular orbit around a wormhole: the nonrotating case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14455)
*Shauvik Biswas,Sayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将Poisson（1993）关于黑洞周围圆轨道粒子引力辐射的计算推广至虫洞情形，在慢速假设下用Teukolsky形式得到无穷远能流解析结果，发现22模存在黑洞极限下消失的新颖0.5PN项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有后牛顿（post-Newtonian）波形研究主要针对黑洞，虫洞等奇特致密天体（exotic compact objects, ECOs）的引力波波形尚缺乏系统分析，故需将经典黑洞计算框架推广至虫洞以检验可分辨特征。

Method: 考虑试验粒子绕类Schwarzschild虫洞（Schwarzschild-like wormhole）的运动，采用Teukolsky形式（Teukolsky formalism），在小速度假设下解析计算无穷远处的引力波能流，并聚焦22模与数值结果对照。

Result: 在四极极（quadrupole order）之上发现一个新颖的0.5PN阶项，该项在黑洞极限（black hole limit）下消失；更高阶项亦被修正，但在黑洞极限下给出正确对应。数值研究表明，当粒子远离最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）时两种波形吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该工作是研究涉及虫洞（ECOs）的后牛顿波形的首批尝试之一，表明虫洞与黑洞的波形差异可作为潜在可分辨特征。

Abstract: In this paper, we have generalized Poisson's(1993) calculation for the case of gravitational radiation from a particle in a circular orbit around a black hole to the case of the wormhole. Considering the motion of a test particle around a Schwarzschild like wormhole and using Teukolsky formalism, we have obtained an analytical result of flux at infinity under the assumption of small velocities. Focusing on the 22-mode, we have found a novel 0.5PN order term beyond the quadrupole order, which vanishes in the black hole limit. On the other hand, all higher order terms are getting modified in such a way that they give correct correspondence in the black hole limit. We have further corroborated our results with the numerical investigations, which show that the two waveforms match well when the particle lies far from ISCO. Thus, our work provides one of the first attempts toward the study of the post-Newtonian waveforms involving wormholes (ECOs).

</details>


### [77] [Singustars: Between Black Holes and Compact Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14482)
*Pablo Laguna*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff（TOV）方程允许一类由负质量奇点（negative-mass singularity）与自引力流体壳层构成的奇异解，本文将其命名为“singustars”。对 Γ=2 的多方物态，稳定区为 M≲0.63、C≲0.25，虽比任何稳定规则多方星更致密，但不含光子环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有文献对 TOV 方程奇异解（负点质量外加自引力流体）缺乏系统性刻画。本文旨在给出这类解（singustars）的参数结构、稳定性判据与非线性演化终态，明确其与规则多方星（regular polytropes）及 Buchdahl 界限的关系。

Method: 在 Γ-law 多方物态（polytropic equation of state）下求解精确平衡解并用径向模式（radial modes）做线性稳定性分析；在数值参数空间中考察不稳定模与能量束缚性；采用入射零坐标（ingoing null coordinates）进行非线性演化，并回溯出射零射线（outgoing null rays）以定位事件视界生成元（event-horizon generator）。

Result: Singustars 是围绕裸露排斥性负质量奇点、由两个零压面界定的厚壳层，外部为 Schwarzschild 时空，构成以 M 与紧致度 C≡M/R 标记的两参数族；当中心负质量趋于零时与规则多方星衔接，并可超越 Buchdahl 界限。Γ=2 时仅 M≲0.63 且 C≲0.25 稳定，该区域比任何稳定规则多方星更致密，但无光子环（light ring）。不稳定解只有单一不稳定径向模，许多能量上不束缚。非线性演化终态依赖扰动：在所研究坍缩模型中，首个俘获面（trapped surface）出现前已可识别出带奇点过去端点的事件视界生成元；向外扰动使壳层无束缚并散开、留下裸露奇点，而束缚壳层则迁移至同守恒质量的稳定位形附近振荡。

Conclusion: Singustars 构成 TOV 方程中一类独立于规则多方星的两参数奇异平衡族，具有超越 Buchdahl 界限的稳定子区；其非线性终态不唯一，取决于扰动方向与束缚性，可能出现裸奇点保留或向稳定位形的迁移振荡。

Abstract: The Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equations admit singular solutions in which a negative point mass is surrounded by a self-gravitating fluid. For a $Γ$-law polytropic equation of state, these solutions, which we call singustars, are thick shells bounded by two zero-pressure surfaces around a naked, repulsive, negative-mass singularity, with a Schwarzschild exterior. They form a two-parameter family, labeled by the mass $M$ and the compactness $C\equiv M/R$. Singustars join regular polytropes as the central negative mass vanishes and reach compactness beyond the Buchdahl bound. From exact equilibria and their radial modes, we find that singustars with $Γ= 2$ are stable only for $M\lesssim0.63$ and $C\lesssim0.25$, a region that nonetheless contains configurations more compact than any stable regular polytrope, although none with a light ring. In the numerically resolved parameter space, unstable singustars have a single unstable radial mode; many are energetically unbound. Nonlinear evolutions in ingoing null coordinates show that the end state depends on the perturbation. For the collapsing models studied, backward tracing of outgoing null rays identifies an event-horizon generator with a past endpoint on the singularity, before the first trapped surface appears. Outward perturbations disperse unbound shells and leave the singularity naked, while a bound shell migrates toward, and oscillates about, a stable configuration with the same conserved masses.

</details>

TL;DR: 该文研究最大扰乱（maximal scrambling）体系中的电荷失衡弛豫，发现在低温柔性区域失衡最慢衰减率为 Γ=2π(2Δ−d)k_BT/ℏ，确立了普朗克尺度（Planckian）电荷平衡，并表明最大扰乱本身不能固定其无量纲前因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普朗克时间（Planckian time）τ_Pl=ℏ/(k_BT) 被猜测为强相互作用量子物质中热弛豫时间的下限，且已有严格界限分别限制量子信息扰乱（scrambling）速率与流体力学（hydrodynamics）出现的时间，但扰乱速率与平衡速率之间的关系仍不清楚。论文旨在澄清这一关系。

Method: 以复 Sachdev–Ye–Kitaev（SYK）模型及其 Yukawa 推广为对象，研究两个子系统之间的电荷失衡（charge imbalance）弛豫；这些模型表现出最大扰乱。在低温柔性区域（conformal regime）采用线性响应分析（linear response analysis）提取失衡最慢衰减模式。

Result: 线性响应分析给出失衡最慢衰减率为 Γ=2π(2Δ−d)k_BT/ℏ，其中 Δ 为费米子标度维度（scaling dimension），d=0,1 为空间维度；即弛豫率与温度成正比，处于普朗克尺度。

Conclusion: 结果在最大扰乱体系中确立了普朗克尺度的电荷平衡（Planckian charge equilibration），同时表明仅凭最大扰乱性质不足以确定该弛豫率的无量纲前因子（dimensionless prefactor），其依赖标度维度与空间维度。

Abstract: The Planckian time, $τ_{\rm Pl}=\hbar/(k_BT)$, is conjectured to set a lower limit on thermal relaxation times in strongly interacting quantum matter. Rigorous bounds limit both how rapidly quantum information can scramble and how soon hydrodynamics can emerge, yet the relation between the scrambling and equilibration rates remains unclear. We address this question by studying the relaxation of a charge imbalance between two subsystems of the complex Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev model and its Yukawa generalizations, which exhibit maximal scrambling. In the low-temperature conformal regime, we find through a linear response analysis that the slowest decay of this imbalance occurs at rate $Γ=2π(2Δ-d)k_BT/\hbar$, where $Δ$ is the fermion scaling dimension and $d=0,1$ is the spatial dimension. These results establish Planckian charge equilibration in maximally scrambling systems and show that maximal scrambling alone does not fix its dimensionless prefactor.

</details>

TL;DR: 在π通量方格t-t' Hofstadter-Hubbard模型中，半满时存在整数量子霍尔（IQH）、手性自旋液体（CSL）与反铁磁（AFM）三相；掺杂任一母相后，几乎整个相图都被自旋陈数Cs=-2的单一手性拓扑超导体占据。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型被提出可在扭转莫尔双层（twisted moiré bilayers）中实现，研究目的是考察弱关联能带拓扑、内禀拓扑序与常规对称破缺三类基态能否共存于同一相图，以及掺杂后的配对行为是否依赖母相性质。

Conclusion: 掺杂该模型的拓扑能带可稳健地诱导出同一种配对态，与母相为IQH、自旋液体或弱涨落AFM及其磁涨落性质无关，说明母相的关联特征不改变掺杂诱导的配对结果。

Abstract: We study a $π$-flux square-lattice $t$-$t'$ Hofstadter-Hubbard model proposed to be realizable in twisted moiré bilayers. By tuning $U$ and $t'$ at half filling, ground-state density-matrix renormalization group simulations (DMRG) and a parton analysis identify an integer quantum Hall (IQH) phase, a chiral spin liquid (CSL), and an antiferromagnet (AFM) in the phase diagram, while charge and spin fluctuations from finite-temperature determinant quantum Monte Carlo (DQMC) suggest a closing charge gap but an open spin gap at the IQH-CSL boundary, consistent with the topological criticality expected there. This model therefore produces a phase diagram hosting weakly correlated band topology (IQH), intrinsic topological order (CSL), and conventional symmetry breaking (AFM) side by side. Upon doping any of these three parent phases, a single chiral topological superconductor with spin Chern number $C_s=-2$ occupies nearly the entire phase diagram. DMRG, DQMC, and weak-coupling perturbation theory agree on its pairing symmetry. This shows that doping the topological band of our model robustly induces the same paired state throughout the phase diagram regardless of the character of the underlying correlated half-filled state and its magnetic fluctuations, whether an IQH, a spin liquid or a weakly fluctuating AFM.

</details>


### [80] [Study of the frustrated SU(3) Heisenberg chain with SU(3)-symmetric matrix product states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13166)
*O. R. Hryniv,I. V. Lukin,A. G. Sotnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用非阿贝尔（non-Abelian）SU(3) 对称性 VUMPS 与有限纠缠标度（finite-entanglement scaling），将三聚化价键固体（trimerized valence-bond solid）相的上边界定为 α_c2 ≳ 3.5 且终止于 α ≈ 5 以下，排除 α ≈ 2 附近的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场论预测该链存在有限宽度的三聚化价键固体相，其后为无能隙的 SU(3)_1 相，但既有数值估计的上边界分歧显著（精确对角化（exact diagonalization）约 2.1，iDMRG 约 3.8），需更可靠的判定。

Method: 使用变分均匀矩阵乘积态（variational uniform matrix product state, VUMPS）算法，在虚拟键上施加完整非阿贝尔 SU(3) 对称性，键维（bond dimension）最高约 D ≈ 8000，并通过有限纠缠标度定位相边界；同时按 SU(3) 不可约表示（irreducible representation）分解转移矩阵谱（transfer-matrix spectrum）与纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）。

Result: 三聚化相延伸至 α_c2 ≳ 3.5 并在 α ≈ 5 以下终止，从而排除 α ≈ 2 附近的边界；在更大 α 区域，有效中心荷（effective central charge）的数值分析与所预测的 SU(3)_2 到 SU(3)_1 的无质量流动（massless flow）一致。

Conclusion: 高键维非阿贝尔 VUMPS 结合有限纠缠标度支持较宽的 三聚化相区间，并与 SU(3)_2 → SU(3)_1 无质量流动的场论图像相符，为先前数值分歧提供了倾向性判断。

Abstract: We study the SU(3) Heisenberg chain with nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor antiferromagnetic exchange $J_1$ and $J_2$. Field theory predicts that the trimerized valence-bond solid of this chain has a finite extent in $α=J_2/J_1$, followed by a gapless SU(3)$_1$ phase, but earlier numerical estimates of the upper boundary range from $α\approx2.1$ (exact diagonalization) to $α\approx3.8$ (iDMRG). We use the variational uniform matrix product state (VUMPS) algorithm with the full non-Abelian SU(3) symmetry imposed on the virtual bonds, reaching bond dimensions up to $D\approx8000$, and locate the phase boundaries by finite-entanglement scaling. Resolving the transfer-matrix spectrum and the entanglement spectrum by SU(3) irreducible representation, we find that the trimerized phase extends to $α_{c2}\gtrsim3.5$ and end below $α\approx5$, excluding a boundary near $α\approx2$. At larger $α$ our numerical analysis of the effective central charge is consistent with the predicted massless flow from SU(3)$_2$ to SU(3)$_1$.

</details>


### [81] [A Perspective on Topological Charge Density Wave Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13360)
*Chun-Tung Lien,Nidhish Thiruthukkal Puthenveettil,Alexander A. Balandin,Md Shafayat Hossain*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文是一篇视角（Perspective）文章，旨在厘清“拓扑电荷密度波（topological charge density wave）”这一术语所涵盖的多种物理上彼此不同的现象，并将其区分为三种含义：序参量及其缺陷的拓扑、电荷密度波重构电子结构及其边界态的拓扑、以及密度波序产生的类轴子电磁响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷密度波（charge density wave）是量子材料中最早已知的有序态之一，近来在拓扑、对称性破缺与集体动力学讨论中重新成为焦点；不同材料体系中观测到的能带重构、边界态、缺陷网络与电磁响应均引发拓扑解读。然而“拓扑电荷密度波”一词被用于描述多种物理上不同的现象，术语存在混淆，实验与理论支持程度差异明显，因此需要系统澄清。

Method: 采用视角（Perspective）综述方式：梳理该领域思想发展脉络；将“拓扑电荷密度波”拆解并界定为三种含义——(i) 序参量（order parameter）及其缺陷的拓扑、(ii) 电荷密度波重构电子结构（reconstructed electronic structure）及其边界模（boundary modes）的拓扑、(iii) 密度波序产生的类轴子（axion-like）电磁响应；并比较各类含义背后的实验与理论证据。

Result: 文中指出：实空间成像显示非公度（incommensurate）序可形成相位缺陷网络而非均匀调制；边界分辨谱学揭示的带隙内模式（in-gap modes）与电荷密度波序相关联，但观测仍局限于单一材料家族；在Weyl材料中密度波能隙与类轴子磁电响应相联系；在kagome金属中电荷序与贝里曲率（Berry curvature）、非常规对称性破缺及超导性交织。三种含义所获实验与理论支持差异显著。

Conclusion: 文章厘清了三类“拓扑电荷密度波”的证据基础，指出尚存争议与未解问题，并提出用于在电荷密度波态中确立拓扑性的实验判据（experimental criteria）。

Abstract: Charge density waves are among the earliest known ordered states in quantum materials, yet they have re-emerged at the center of modern discussions of topology, symmetry breaking, and collective dynamics. In several material classes, charge-density-wave order reshapes electronic bands, boundary states, defect structures, and electromagnetic responses in ways that invite a topological interpretation. Real-space imaging has shown that incommensurate order can form networks of phase defects rather than uniform modulations. Boundary-resolved spectroscopy has revealed in-gap modes whose existence and spatial structure track the charge-density-wave order, although such observations remain confined to a single material family. In Weyl materials, density-wave gaps have been linked to axion-like magnetoelectric response, placing the charge-density-wave phase within a dynamical-field description of the effective action. In kagome metals, charge order is intertwined with Berry curvature, unconventional symmetry breaking, and superconductivity. These developments have led to the term "topological charge density wave," but the phrase describes several physically distinct phenomena. We distinguish three meanings: (i) topology of the order parameter and its defects, (ii) topology of the charge-density-wave-reconstructed electronic structure and its boundary modes, and (iii) axion-like electromagnetic response generated by density-wave order. The experimental and theoretical support for these three meanings differs substantially. This Perspective traces the development of these ideas, clarifies the evidence behind each class, identifies unresolved questions and points of debate, and outlines experimental criteria for establishing topology in charge-density-wave states.

</details>


### [82] [Field-induced magnetic quantum phase transitions in a bilayer triangular lattice antiferromagnet K$_2$Co$_2$(HPO$_3$)$_2$(C$_2$O$_4$)(H$_2$O)$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13532)
*Yiqiu Liu,Chengcheng Yin,Xinyu Shi,Xiaohui Bo,Kefan Du,Zhanlong Wu,Rui Bian,Shuo Li,Jun Luo,Jie Yang,Yanhong Wang,Weiqiang Yu,Rui Zhou,Jinshan Zhang,Yi Cui,Hongcheng Lu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻挫双层三角晶格反铁磁体（frustrated bilayer triangular-lattice antiferromagnet）是研究场诱导量子相变与新奇异物态的重要平台，但此类材料的微观磁性相图仍需实验刻画，本文旨在通过局域探针手段厘清其磁场-温度相图。

Result: 在0.5–4 T，低温$1/T_1$出现峰值，标志磁有序起始，且有序相内$1/T_1$呈无能隙（gapless）行为，暗示自旋超固态（spin supersolid），该有序在约4 T被抑制；6–18 T出现明显NMR谱线劈裂，表明第二个有序磁相；低温$K_{\rm n}$的场导数在约10 T、15.5 T和18 T处斜率改变，前两者分别对应饱和磁矩的1/3与2/3，后者为第二有序相到完全极化态（fully polarized state）的量子相变。

Conclusion: 该化合物可作为研究阻挫双层三角晶格反铁磁体中场诱导量子相变（quantum phase transition）与涌现物相的平台；所观测到的1/3、2/3磁化平台特征与无能隙有序相为后续中子散射与理论计算提供实验依据。

Abstract: We present a $^{31}$P nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) study on the spin-$1/2$ bilayer triangular-lattice antiferromagnet K$_2$Co$_2$(HPO$_3$)$_2$(C$_2$O$_4$)(H$_2$O)$_2$. Measurements were performed at temperatures down to 0.06 K and magnetic fields up to 26 T. In the field range of 0.5 T to 4 T, the low-temperature spin-lattice relaxation rate $1/T_1$ exhibits a peak, signaling the onset of a magnetically ordered state. Within this ordered phase, $1/T_1$ shows gapless behavior, suggesting a spin supersolid phase. This ordering is suppressed around 4 T. Between 6 T and 18 T, clear splittings of the NMR spectra are observed, indicating the emergence of a second ordered magnetic phase. The field derivative of the low-temperature Knight shift $K_{\rm n}$ reveals changes in slope at approximately 10 T, 15.5 T, and 18 T. The first two features correspond to bulk magnetizations of $1/3$ and $2/3$ of the fully saturated moment, respectively, while the last one marks a quantum phase transition from the second ordered phase to the fully polarized state. These results establish the studied compound as a platform for investigating field-induced quantum phase transitions and emergent phases in frustrated bilayer triangular-lattice antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [83] [Emergent Quantized Zak Phases without Dimerization in a Multicomponent Fermionic Extended Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13695)
*F. X. Liu,Z. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作指出，在一维多组分费米子扩展Hubbard模型中，当在位与最近邻相互作用满足共振条件时，奇数个费米子组成的团簇会因相互作用产生等效二聚化，从而在没有显式晶格二聚化的体系中实现量子化Zak相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子化Zak相位通常与二聚化体系（如无相互作用的Su–Schrieffer–Heeger（SSH）模型）相联系；作者希望探索相互作用能否在缺少显式晶格二聚化的多组分费米子体系中诱导出等效二聚化与量子化Zak相位。

Method: 研究对象为一维多组分费米子的扩展Hubbard模型（extended Hubbard model），并设定在位相互作用与最近邻相互作用之间的共振条件；通过分析有效哈密顿量（effective Hamiltonian）的反演对称性（inversion symmetry）来讨论Zak相位的保护机制，并考虑外加倾斜场（tilted field）下费米子团簇的Bloch振荡（Bloch oscillations）行为。

Result: 发现当费米子数为奇数且n≥3时出现量子化Zak相位，其由有效哈密顿量的反演对称性保护；在倾斜场中费米子团簇呈现Bloch振荡，且可在一个Bloch周期内通过高斯波包（Gaussian wave packet）的演化提取其Zak相位。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无需显式晶格二聚化，多组分费米子体系即可通过相互作用机制实现量子化Zak相位，并提供了借助Bloch振荡中波包演化来探测该相位的方法。

Abstract: Quantized Zak phases are commonly associated with dimerized systems, such as the noninteracting Su--Schrieffer--Heeger (SSH) model. In interacting systems, however, interactions can induce effective dimerization of particle clusters even in the absence of explicit dimerization in the underlying lattice. In this work, we study a one-dimensional multicomponent fermionic extended Hubbard model under a resonance condition between the on-site and nearest-neighbor interactions. We find that quantized Zak phases emerge for odd fermion numbers $n\geqslant 3$, protected by the inversion symmetry of the effective Hamiltonian. Furthermore, in the presence of a tilted field, fermion clusters exhibit Bloch oscillations, during which their Zak phases can be extracted from the evolution of a Gaussian wave packet over one Bloch period. Our results reveal an interaction-induced mechanism for realizing quantized Zak phases in multicomponent fermionic systems without explicit lattice dimerization.

</details>


### [84] [Thermodynamic Properties of the S=1/2 Zigzag Spin Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13895)
*Nobuya Maeshima,Toshiya Hikihara,Kouichi Okunishi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究含各向异性非对角Γ相互作用的自旋1/2锯齿形海森堡梯子（zigzag Heisenberg ladder）的有限温度性质。结合无限时间演化块抽取（infinite time-evolving block decimation, iTEBD）与纯化方法计算热态，并用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）分析低能激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画Γ相互作用下锯齿形梯子模型的有限温度热力学行为，特别是熵与比热在基态相区内部及相边界附近的表现。

Method: 采用无限时间演化块抽取（iTEBD）结合纯化（purification）方法计算有限温热态，并以精确对角化（exact diagonalization）分析低能激发；对J₂/J₁=0的Néel相，通过自旋旋转映射到可精确求解的XYZ链（XYZ chain）。

Result: 在存在能隙的相内部，低温熵被强烈抑制，而在若干基态相边界附近熵保持增强。对J₂/J₁=0的Néel相，由低温比热提取的热能隙与精确的单自旋子能隙（one-spinon gap）一致，而精确对角化得到的有限尺寸激发能隙与双自旋子激发（two-spinon excitation）相符。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了Γ相互作用锯齿形梯子中熵与能隙随相区的显著差异，并表明低温比热反映单自旋子能隙而有限尺寸谱隙对应双自旋子激发。

Abstract: We investigate finite-temperature properties of the spin-1/2 zigzag Heisenberg ladder with an anisotropic off-diagonal $Γ$ interaction. Thermal states are calculated by an infinite time-evolving block decimation method combined with purification, while exact diagonalization is used to analyze low-lying excitations. The entropy is strongly suppressed at low temperatures inside the gapped phases, whereas it remains enhanced near several ground-state phase boundaries. For the Néel phase at $J_2/J_1=0$, the model can be mapped by spin rotations onto the exactly solvable XYZ chain. The thermal gap extracted from the low-temperature specific heat agrees with the exact one-spinon gap, while the finite-size excitation gap obtained by exact diagonalization is consistent with a two-spinon excitation.

</details>

*M. M. Ferreira-Carvalho,S. H. Chen,S. Rößler,Fanjun Xu,A. T. M. N. Islam,B. Lake,C. F. Chang,C. Y. Kuo,S. M. Valvidares,P. Gargiani,Z. Hu,A. C. Komarek,M. W. Haverkort,L. H. Tjeng*

Abstract: We performed temperature-dependent X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) and X-ray magnetic circular dichroism (XMCD) measurements on the disordered phase of Ba$_3$CoNb$_2$O$_9$ to determine the local electronic structure of the Co$^{2+}$ ions. We employed the line-fitting inverse partial fluorescence yield (iPFY) method to obtain reliable spectra from this insulating material. From the comparison between the experimental spectra and full multiplet cluster calculations we were able to constrain the local crystal-field environment and assess the spin-orbit-entangled nature of the Co$^{2+}$ ground state. In this chemically disordered system, the Co$^{2+}$ ions retain on average an effectively cubic crystal-field environment that supports a $J_\mathrm{eff} = 1/2$ ground state. These results further establish that Ba$_3$CoNb$_2$O$_9$ is a promising platform for investigating the effect of disorder on $J_\mathrm{eff} = 1/2$ magnetism.

### [86] [Particle-hole asymmetry and a fluctuating pair-density-wave state in a lightly doped Mott insulator on the square lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14101)
*Hao-Xin Wang,Zheng-Yuan Yue,Wen-Yuan Liu,Zheng-Cheng Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合大规模密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）与有限尺寸投影纠缠对态（PEPS），研究方形晶格 t-J 模型在低掺杂（δ≤0.1）下随次近邻跃迁 t' 从电子型到空穴型的演化，揭示粒子-空穴不对称性是竞争配对倾向的核心组织原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻掺杂方形晶格 Mott 绝缘体（Mott insulator）是否支持稳健的 d 波超导（d-wave superconductivity），是强关联物理与铜氧化物超导微观理论的核心问题；实验与数值研究显示的显著粒子-空穴不对称（particle-hole asymmetry）暗示 d 波超导、配对密度波（pair-density wave, PDW）、条纹（stripe）、电荷密度波（charge-density wave, CDW）与反铁磁关联之间存在微妙竞争。

Method: 采用大规模密度矩阵重整化群（density matrix renormalization group, DMRG）与有限尺寸投影纠缠对态（projected entangled pair states, PEPS），在低掺杂 δ≤0.1 下研究方形晶格 t-J 模型，并将次近邻跃迁 t' 从电子型（t'>0）调节至空穴型（t'<0）；计算涵盖开放边界条带与方形体系，PEPS 计算规模最大至 16×16 格点。

Result: 电子型一侧支持稳健且近乎均匀的 d 波配对；减小 t' 会诱导周期为二的格点（plaquette）调制，并在空穴型一侧形成电荷主导、静态配对振幅被强烈抑制的态。在具有 C4 对称哈密顿量的方形体系上，两种方法均发现空穴型态的配对关联选择 plaquette 动量 (π,0) 与 (0,π)，且在数个晶格间距内衰减，而主导的电荷密度调制呈双向棋盘格（checkerboard）样式，其周期外推约为 4a0，接近轻掺杂 Bi-2201 的实验观测。

Conclusion: 上述结果确立了轻掺杂空穴型区域存在涨落的 plaquette PDW：局域配对携带动量但缺乏相位相干（phase coherence）。研究由此将粒子-空穴不对称性确立为轻掺杂 t-J 模型中竞争配对倾向的中心组织原则。

Abstract: Whether a lightly doped Mott insulator on the square lattice supports robust $d$-wave superconductivity remains a central question in strongly correlated physics and in the microscopic theory of cuprate superconductivity. Experiments and numerical studies reveal a pronounced particle-hole asymmetry, pointing to a delicate competition among $d$-wave superconductivity, pair-density wave (PDW), stripe, charge-density wave (CDW), and antiferromagnetic correlations. Here we combine large-scale DMRG and finite-size PEPS to study the square-lattice $t$-$J$ model at low doping, $δ\leq 0.1$, while tuning the next-nearest-neighbor hopping $t'$ from the electron-like ($t'>0$) to the hole-like ($t'<0$) regime. The electron-like side supports robust, nearly uniform $d$-wave pairing, whereas decreasing $t'$ induces a period-two plaquette modulation and ultimately produces, on the hole-like side, a charge-dominated state with a strongly suppressed static pairing amplitude. DMRG calculations on open strips and squares, together with PEPS calculations on open squares up to $16\times16$ sites, support this evolution. On squares with $C_4$-symmetric Hamiltonians, both methods find that the pair correlations of the hole-like state select the plaquette momenta $(π,0)$ and $(0,π)$ and decay within a few lattice spacings, while the dominant charge-density modulation forms a bidirectional checkerboard whose period extrapolates to about $4a_0$, close to that observed in lightly doped Bi-2201. These observations establish a fluctuating plaquette PDW in the lightly doped hole-like regime, in which local pairs carry finite momentum but lack phase coherence. Our results firmly establish particle-hole asymmetry as a central organizing principle for competing pairing tendencies in the lightly doped $t$-$J$ model.

</details>


### [87] [Thermodynamic signatures of a nearby quantum critical endpoint in UTe$_2$ under intense magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14185)
*M. Gen,K. Somesh,A. Miyake,D. Li,Z. Yang,A. Miyata,Y. Kohama,M. Kondo,M. Tokunaga,D. Aoki,W. Knafo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过磁场角度倾斜下的磁化与磁致伸缩测量，定位了UTe₂中终止变磁转变线的量子临界端点，并讨论了其与场诱导超导的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重费米子顺磁体UTe₂在场诱导变磁附近出现多种非常规超导相，理解其机制的关键在于刻画变磁转变处的量子临界磁性质。

Method: 在晶体学b-c平面内、相对b向c倾斜θ≲42°的磁场构型下，于最高60 T磁场中进行磁化（magnetization）与磁致伸缩（magnetostriction）实验，研究UTe₂的热力学性质。

Result: 实验支持存在一个量子临界端点（quantum critical endpoint），位于θ_QCEP≈(70±10)°、μ₀H_QCEP≈(75±10) T，该端点终止了变磁转变线；同时在θ<θ_QCEP时观测到磁化率中的赝能隙（pseudo-gap），并与变磁转变相关联。

Conclusion: 量子临界端点被确定为变磁转变线的终点，作者进一步讨论了该端点与场诱导超导（magnetic-field-induced superconductivity）发展之间可能的关系。

Abstract: Several unconventional superconducting phases have been discovered in the vicinity of metamagnetism in the heavy-fermion paramagnet UTe$_2$ under magnetic field. To describe the mechanism of magnetic-field-induced superconductivity, a challenge is to characterize the quantum critical magnetic properties at the metamagnetic transition. In this work, we investigate the thermodynamic properties of UTe$_2$ under magnetic fields up to 60~T applied in the crystallographic $\mathbf{b}-\mathbf{c}$ plane. Magnetization and magnetostriction experiments have been performed under magnetic fields tilted by an angle $θ\lesssim42^\circ$ from $\mathbf{b}$ toward $\mathbf{c}$. They support that a quantum critical endpoint at the field angle $θ_{QCEP}\simeq(70\pm10)^\circ$ and magnetic field $μ_0H_{QCEP}\simeq(75\pm10)$~T terminates the metamagnetic transition line. A pseudo-gap in the magnetic susceptibility is related with the metamagnetic transition observed in magnetic fields for $θ<θ_{QCEP}$. The possible relation between the quantum critical endpoint and the development of magnetic-field-induced superconductivity is discussed.

</details>


### [88] [Dimensional Crossover and Correlated Spin Dynamics in the Chain-Like van der Waals Antiferromagnet AgCrP$_2$S$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14444)
*C. S. Saramgi,Y. Shemerliuk,S. Selter,S. Aswartham,B. Buechner,H. -J. Grafe,K. M. Ranjith*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过磁化率与³¹P核磁共振（NMR）系统研究链状范德华反铁磁体AgCrP₂S₆的自旋动力学，确认其为准一维S=3/2反铁磁体，并揭示从链内短程关联到三维各向异性有序的维度交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维范德华反铁磁体中的维度交叉（dimensional crossover）与关联自旋动力学尚缺乏微观层面的直接证据，需要借助局域探针加以厘清。

Method: 采用磁化强度、磁化率测量，并结合³¹P核磁共振（nuclear magnetic resonance, NMR），包括K–χ关系、自旋-晶格弛豫率1/T₁与内场临界行为分析；并用经典Fisher链、量子S=3/2最近邻链及受约束AF-FM锯齿链模型拟合磁化率。

Result: 磁化率在约250 K出现宽极大，给出链内交换能标J/k_B≈140 K（Fisher链）、≈114 K（量子S=3/2链），锯齿链计算亦相容；K–χ在T*≈250 K附近呈两段线性区；T_N以下1/T₁∝T⁵，内场给出β≈0.36，符合三维普适类。

Conclusion: AgCrP₂S₆被确立为嵌于层状范德华基质中的准一维S=3/2反铁磁体，局域探针可分辨从相关链内自旋动力学到各向异性三维磁序的交叉，为低维反铁磁体研究提供通用平台。

Abstract: We report a comprehensive investigation of the magnetic properties and spin dynamics of the chain-like van der Waals antiferromagnet AgCrP$_2$S$_6$ using magnetization, magnetic susceptibility, and $^{31}$P nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR). The magnetic susceptibility exhibits a broad maximum near 250 K, characteristic of strong short-range antiferromagnetic correlations in a low-dimensional system. A classical Fisher-chain analysis gives an effective intrachain exchange scale $J/k_B\approx 140$ K, while a quantum $S=3/2$ nearest-neighbor-chain calculation yields $J/k_B\approx 114$ K. A constrained AF-FM zig-zag-chain calculation is also compatible with the susceptibility, supporting a dominant quasi one dimensional antiferromagnetic exchange scale of order 100 K.
  $^{31}$P NMR measurements provide microscopic insight into both static and dynamic spin correlations. The $K-χ$ relation displays two distinct linear regimes separated near $T^\ast\approx 250$ K, with different slopes indicating a change in the effective transferred hyperfine couplings. Below $T_{N}$, the relaxation follows $1/T_1\propto T^5$, consistent with magnon-mediated relaxation in the ordered state. The temperature dependence of the internal hyperfine field yields a critical exponent $β\approx 0.36$, consistent with three-dimensional universality.
  Together, these results establish AgCrP$_2$S$_6$ as a quasi-one-dimensional $S=3/2$ antiferromagnet embedded in a layered van der Waals host, in which local probes directly resolve the crossover from correlated intrachain spin dynamics to anisotropic three-dimensional magnetic order. More broadly, AgCrP$_2$S$_6$ provides a useful van der Waals platform for studying dimensional crossover, correlated spin dynamics, and hyperfine-coupling evolution in low-dimensional antiferromagnets.

</details>

### [89] [Nuclear medium and final state interaction effects in $η$ production induced by $ν_μ(\barν_μ)$ at SBN and DUNE energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13602)
*A. Fatima,S. K. Noor Alam,M. Sajjad Athar,S. K. Singh*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文研究碳-12与氩-40核上中微子（ν_μ/反ν_μ）诱导η介子产生过程中的核介质修正与末态相互作用，发现二者共同导致产生截面显著压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: η介子产生主要由N*(1535)共振经其Nη衰变道主导，但在核环境中的数据解释需厘清核介质效应与末态相互作用的贡献，为当前及未来中微子实验提供理论依据。

Method: 采用局域密度近似（local density approximation）描述核介质并纳入N*(1535)共振的介质内修正；η介子在核内的传播与吸收则通过蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）方法模拟。

Result: 核介质修正与末态相互作用共同造成产生截面的显著压低（substantial suppression），表明核效应在η产生数据分析中不可忽略。

Conclusion: 研究结果为正在运行的SBND、MicroBooNE实验及即将开展的DUNE实验中η产生分析提供有价值的理论输入。

Abstract: We investigate nuclear-medium modification and final-state interaction in charged- and neutral-current $ν_μ(\barν_μ)$-induced $η$-meson production on $^{12}\mathrm{C}$ and $^{40}\mathrm{Ar}$. The nuclear medium is described within a local density approximation, incorporating in-medium modifications of the $N^*(1535)$ resonance, which is known to play the dominant role in $η$ production through its $N η$ decay channel. The subsequent propagation and absorption of the $η$ meson in the nucleus are simulated using a Monte Carlo approach. We find that nuclear-medium modifications and final state interaction lead to substantial suppression of the production cross section, highlighting the importance of nuclear effects in interpreting $η$ production data. Our results provide valuable theoretical input for the analysis of $η$-production at the ongoing SBND and MicroBooNE experiments and the forthcoming DUNE experiment.

</details>
