### [90] [Borrowed Planckian dissipation in magic-angle twisted graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12947)
*Dino Škrgić,Dejan Stojkovic*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 魔角扭转三层石墨烯（magic-angle twisted trilayer graphene）中，位移场 D 可作为调节电子-散射体耦合的旋钮；D 只作用于散射率而不改变载流子数，且在对称分布于费米能附近的平带权重下，"借用"耗散会带来两倍于平带自身电子的单位耦合熵，给出可判别的场微分比值 2π³/21ζ(3)≈2.46。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异金属（strange metals）中线性温阻系数与对数比热之比与电子-散射体耦合无关，但既有材料的该耦合由化学组分固定，无法独立调节，因而缺少检验该普适性及耦合熵关系的可控实验平台。

Method: 利用魔角扭转三层石墨烯的位移场 D 打破镜面对称，使轻狄拉克锥（Dirac cone）与平带（flat band）耦合，狄拉克载流子以正比于 D²+D₀² 的权重"借用"平带的普朗克自能（Planckian self-energy）；在八个场值下测量电阻曲线，并结合热平均 Kubo 公式（Kubo formula）与数值搜索分析。

Result: 电阻曲线符合场作用于散射率而非载流子数的图像；温度斜率随场按 a(1+D²/D₀²) 变化且不含耗尽狄拉克口袋所带来的四次项；弹性部分在 0.20–0.30 V/nm 间变化小于 5%，而非弹性部分在至 0.38 V/nm 时增长 2.5 倍。理论给出共形平带下比值 2π³/21ζ(3)≈2.46，对无高能速率地板的单调浴谱数值上最多为 2.73。

Conclusion: 借用耗散保留平带自身电子的浴指数（bath exponent）与输运前因子，但在浴具有自身动力学时，每单位借用耦合并入的熵恰为其两倍；判别性实验为以 D 为步长测量熵。

Abstract: In strange metals, the ratio of the coefficients producing signature linear-in-$T$ resistivity and the logarithmic specific heat is free of the coupling between the electrons and whatever scatters them. In the materials studied so far that coupling is fixed by chemistry. Magic-angle twisted trilayer graphene provides a dial with a displacement field $D$ that breaks the mirror symmetry separating a light Dirac cone from the flat bands, and the Dirac carriers borrow the Planckian self-energy of the flat band with a weight proportional to $D^2+D_0^2$. Resistance curves taken at eight fields are consistent with the field acting on the scattering rate rather than on the number of carriers. The slope in temperature follows $a(1+D^2/D_0^2)$ without the quartic term that a depleting Dirac pocket adds, and the elastic part varies by less than 5\% between 0.20 and 0.30~V/nm while the inelastic part grows 2.5-fold up to 0.38~V/nm. The thermally averaged Kubo formula favors flat-band weight placed symmetrically about the Fermi level over a single level. Borrowed dissipation keeps the bath exponent and transport prefactor of the flat band's own electrons but carries exactly twice their entropy per unit of borrowed coupling whenever the bath has dynamics of its own, so the field-differential ratio is $2π^3/21ζ(3)\approx2.46$ for a conformal flat band and, in a numerical search, at most 2.73 for monotonic bath spectra without a high-energy rate floor. The discriminating test is the entropy measured in steps of $D$.

</details>


### [91] [Hidden Spin-Supercurrent Counterflow in Conventional Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13076)
*Johanne Bratland Tjernshaugen,Jacob Linder*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在自旋劈裂场下的常规超导体中，自旋注入通过自旋混合界面激活自旋超流分量，实现阻性自旋电流（resistive spin current）空间的重新分布而总自旋流守恒，形成总自旋流与电荷流均为零的隐藏自旋超流反向流（spin-supercurrent counterflow）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上阻性自旋电流（resistive spin current）在空间中的变化依赖自旋弛豫（spin relaxation）或力矩作用，二者都会移除所输运自旋的角动量。论文旨在探索是否存在不依赖角动量耗散的、性质不同的空间变化机制。

Method: 采用理论分析框架，考虑处于自旋劈裂场（spin-splitting field）中的常规超导体，并引入自旋混合界面（spin-mixing interface）实现自旋注入，将阻性自旋电流与凝聚体（condensate）自旋超流分量结合分析。

Result: 即使超导体两端无净自旋偏压，也能产生有限且方向相反的阻性自旋流与凝聚体自旋流，构成总自旋流与电荷流皆为零的隐藏反向流；该机制在偏离精确补偿条件时仍存在，且在较宽参数范围内保持可观量级。

Conclusion: 凝聚体介导的自旋电流补偿（condensate-mediated spin-current compensation）是此前被忽视的自旋弛豫替代机制；其效应可通过自旋-电荷转换（spin-to-charge conversion）探测，表现为自旋霍尔注入器（spin-Hall injector）电学响应的改变或局域准粒子逆自旋霍尔（inverse spin-Hall）信号。

Abstract: Resistive spin currents conventionally vary in space through spin relaxation or torque, which removes angular momentum from the transported spin. We here demonstrate a qualitatively different mechanism in a conventional superconductor exposed to a spin-splitting field. Spin injection across a spin-mixing interface activates a spin-supercurrent contribution, allowing the resistive spin current to vary spatially while the total spin current remains conserved. Even without a net spin-bias across the superconductor, finite and oppositely directed resistive and condensate spin currents arise. This produces a hidden spin-supercurrent counterflow with vanishing total spin and charge currents. The mechanism persists away from exact compensation and remains sizable over broad parameter ranges. The counterflow may be accessible through spin-to-charge conversion, either as a modification of the electrical response of spin-Hall injectors or through a local quasiparticle inverse spin-Hall signal. Our results establish condensate-mediated spin-current compensation as a previously overlooked alternative to spin relaxation.

</details>


### [92] [Neutron Scattering studies of the density dependence of One-dimensional Quantum Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13235)
*Sutirtha Paul,Rahul Soni,Garfield Warren,Adrian Del Maestro,Paul E. Sokol*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在模板化多孔材料中用惰性气体预镀层实现维度约化后，受限⁴He呈现一维行为；随填充比例变化，一维液态核始终保持固定密度，其长度随填充增加而增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作已表明，经惰性气体（noble gas）预镀层增强维度约化（dimensional reduction）的模板化多孔材料中受限⁴He表现出一维行为，其中子激发（neutron scattering）与二维、三维超流氦（superfluid helium）定性不同，并与量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）计算一致。本文旨在考察填充比例（filling fraction）变化时一维核的行为。

Method: 采用中子散射（neutron scattering）测量，系统改变一维核（one-dimensional core）的填充比例，观测受限⁴He的激发与密度响应。

Result: 实验观测到一维液态（one-dimensional liquid）始终以固定密度（fixed density）形成，即使孔道填充增加亦然。

Conclusion: 作者推测，填充增加导致一维液态的一维延展范围（one-dimensional extent）增长，而密度保持不变；结合量子蒙特卡洛图像，即三层固态层形成、一维液态位于孔道中心。

Abstract: In previous work, we demonstrated that $^4$He confined within a templated porous material preplated with a noble gas to enhance dimensional reduction exhibits one-dimensional behavior. Neutron scattering revealed that the excitations in confined $^4$He are qualitatively distinct from those in two- and three-dimensional superfluid helium, aligning with quantum Monte Carlo calculations. These simulations suggest that three solid layers form, with a one-dimensional liquid located at the center of the pore. Here, we present neutron scattering results as the filling fraction of the one-dimensional core is varied. We observe that the one-dimensional liquid always forms with a fixed density, even as the pore filling increases. We posit that this is due to the growth of the one dimensional extent of the liquid as the filling is increased, but its density remains unchanged.

</details>


### [93] [Optically modulated field-dependent electrical response in a non-collinear antiferromagnet/ferromagnet heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13351)
*Adrielson Dias,André José,José Lucas,Rodrigo Cristian,José Holanda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究 MgO/IrMn₃/Py/Au 异质结构在射频（radio-frequency, RF）、白光照明与直流偏置（DC bias）联合作用下的场依赖电学响应，测得暗态数十纳伏、光照下约 0.95 μV 的差分电压，并在三种激励同时施加时提升至约 1.8 μV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用光、电、磁多刺激的相互作用调控自旋电子学异质结构（spintronic heterostructures）的功能响应，并探索基于非共线反铁磁体（non-collinear antiferromagnet）与铁磁层复合体系中的耦合效应。

Method: 在 20 MHz、标称功率 2 W 的射频激励下，对 MgO/IrMn₃/Py/Au 样品施加约 1 W 白光照明与 1 mA 直流电流，测量其场依赖电压响应；同时进行磁性与电阻表征，考察交换偏置（exchange bias）与磁阻的各向异性。

Result: 暗态电压维持在数十纳伏；光照后在 315–320 Oe 附近出现显著场依赖特征，达 0.9–1.0 μV，光照相关差分电压约 0.95 μV；1 mA 直流电流产生约 1 μV 响应，三者同时施加时电压增至约 1.8 μV。磁性表征显示阻塞温度（blocking temperature）约 380 K 的交换偏置，电阻对磁场取向呈明显依赖。

Conclusion: 结果表明场依赖电学响应可通过光与电的联合激励进行调制，涉及磁光相互作用（magneto-optical interactions）与自旋依赖输运（spin-dependent transport）现象，可能包含自旋霍尔相关贡献及磁性自旋霍尔效应（magnetic spin Hall effect）；该工作为非共线反铁磁体与铁磁层异质结构中的光-电-磁耦合效应提供了见解。

Abstract: The interplay among optical, electrical, and magnetic stimuli offers opportunities to control the functional response of spintronic heterostructures. Here, we investigate the field-dependent electrical response of MgO/IrMn$_3$/Py/Au under radio-frequency (RF) excitation, white-light illumination, and direct-current (DC) bias. Under a 20 MHz RF signal with a nominal power of 2 W, the measured voltage in the dark remains at several tens of nanovolts. Upon illumination with approximately 1 W of white light, a pronounced field-dependent feature emerges around 315--320 Oe, reaching 0.9--1.0 $μ$V. The illumination-associated differential voltage is approximately 0.95 $μ$V. A DC current of 1 mA produces a response of approximately 1 $μ$V, while the simultaneous application of RF, DC, and optical excitation increases the voltage to approximately 1.8 $μ$V. Magnetic characterization reveals exchange bias with a blocking temperature near 380 K, while electrical resistance measurements show a pronounced dependence on magnetic-field orientation, consistent with the anisotropic response of the heterostructure. These results demonstrate that the field-dependent electrical response can be modulated through combined optical and electrical excitation, involving magneto-optical interactions and spin-dependent transport phenomena, potentially including spin Hall-related contributions and the magnetic spin Hall effect. This work provides insights into coupled optical, electrical, and magnetic-field effects in heterostructures based on non-collinear antiferromagnets and ferromagnetic layers.

</details>


### [94] [Geometry-Selective Higher-order topological insulators induced by $d_{xy}$ altermagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13438)
*Siyuan Cheng,Yuee Xie,Xiaosen Yang,Yuanping Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文表明，与 \(d_{xy}\) 波交错磁体（altermagnet）近邻耦合可有效实现依赖边界几何的高阶拓扑相（higher-order topological phases），轴对齐边界保持无能隙边缘态，而对角边界因交替符号的有效质量产生四个角模（corner modes）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索通过近邻效应构造高阶拓扑相的有效途径，并厘清边界几何在拓扑性质判定中的作用，同时引入 \(d_{xy}\) 波交错磁体作为调控手段。

Method: 通过投影到边缘态子空间（edge-state subspace），推导不同边界几何下的有效边缘哈密顿量（effective edge Hamiltonians）；并在周期边界条件下引入 \(\pi\) 通量缺陷（\(\pi\)-flux defects）作为无物理边界时的补充诊断手段。

Result: 轴对齐边缘态保持无能隙，对角边缘获得符号交替的有效质量并在质量畴壁（mass domain walls）处局域出四个角模；\(\pi\) 通量缺陷在通量核心处产生零能束缚态（zero-energy bound states）。此外，塞曼场（Zeeman field）与交错磁序的相互作用通过边缘能隙的闭合与重开驱动拓扑相变（topological transition），而体能隙（bulk gap）始终不闭合。

Conclusion: 边界几何在高阶拓扑中起关键作用，近邻诱导的 \(d_{xy}\) 波交错磁性可作为一种设计高阶拓扑相的可行路径。

Abstract: We show that proximity to a $d_{xy}$-wave altermagnet provides an effective route to engineering boundary-geometry-dependent higher-order topological phases. The boundary geometry plays a crucial role in determining the topological properties: axis-aligned edge states remain gapless, whereas diagonal edges acquire effective masses with alternating signs, giving rise to four corner modes localized at the mass domain walls. By projecting onto the edge-state subspace, we derive the effective edge Hamiltonians for different boundary geometries. Furthermore, $π$-flux defects introduced under periodic boundary conditions yield zero-energy bound states localized at the flux cores, providing complementary evidence for nontrivial topology without physical boundaries. The interplay between the Zeeman field and the altermagnetic order drives a topological transition through the closing and reopening of the edge gap, while the bulk gap remains open. These findings highlight the crucial role of boundary geometry in higher-order topology and establish proximity-induced $d_{xy}$-wave altermagnetism as a route to engineering higher-order topological phases.

</details>


### [95] [Facilitated Relaxation Overrides Energetic Pathways in a Multiphase Square-Kagome Artificial Spin Ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13500)
*Wen-Cheng Yue,Zixiong Yuan,Peiyuan Huang,Ziao Wang,Hangyu Liu,Tianyu Li,Nuo-Zhou Yang,Jingyue Gao,Chen Ku,Shuo Song,Jiyao Shang,Yang-Yang Lyu,Yihuang Xiong,Xuecou Tu,Liang He,Xun Cao,Ying Dong,Huabing Wang,Peiheng Wu,Cristiano Nisoli,Yong-Lei Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用可几何调控的方-卡戈米人工自旋冰（square-kagome artificial spin ice）平台，证明跃迁网络拓扑（transition-network topology）可压过能量驱动的弛豫，导致畴壁激发出现非玻尔兹曼（non-Boltzmann）分布与动力学隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体系统的弛豫通常被假定由能量最小化主导，但在受助弛豫（facilitated relaxation）中，局部动力学规则决定哪些微观路径可达。天然材料中能量学、无序与动力学相互纠缠，难以分离这类规则，因此需要干净、可设计的实验平台。

Method: 构建几何可调的方-卡戈米人工自旋冰（由光刻纳米磁体阵列构成），其具备两种可调最近邻相互作用并支持自旋晶体（spin crystal）、自旋液体（spin liquid）与自旋泡沫（spin foam）等多种低能相；结合实验与蒙特卡罗模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）比较不同相中的弛豫行为。

Result: 在自旋泡沫相中，充足的助使者（facilitator）构型使弛豫遵循玻尔兹曼统计（Boltzmann statistics）；而在自旋晶体相中，尽管存在能量上更优的弛豫通道，畴壁激发仍被囚于非玻尔兹曼分布，原因是有序晶体背景缺乏连接拓扑上不同畴壁类所需的助使者，使这些类在动力学上被隔离（kinetically isolated）。

Conclusion: 跃迁网络拓扑可压过能量驱动的弛豫，人工自旋冰由此成为研究受约束动力学（constrained dynamics）与非遍历弛豫（nonergodic relaxation）的可编程平台。

Abstract: Relaxation in interacting many-body systems is typically assumed to follow energy minimization, yet local dynamical rules can determine which microscopic pathways are accessible. In facilitated relaxation, these pathways are enabled or blocked by neighboring configurations. However, isolating such rules in natural materials is difficult because energetics, disorder and dynamics are usually entangled. Here we realize a geometrically tunable square-kagome artificial spin ice-a lithographic nanomagnet array-that provides a clean, designable platform for facilitated relaxation. The array hosts two tunable nearest-neighbor interactions and supports multiple low-energy phases: spin crystal, spin liquid and spin foam. In the spin-foam phase, abundant facilitator configurations allow relaxation to follow Boltzmann statistics. In the spin-crystal phase, by contrast, domain-wall excitations are trapped in non-Boltzmann distributions, although energetically preferred relaxation channels exist. The ordered crystal background lacks the facilitators needed to connect topologically distinct domain-wall classes. These classes are therefore kinetically isolated, as observed in both experiment and Monte Carlo simulation. Our results show that transition-network topology can override energetic relaxation, establishing artificial spin ice as a programmable platform for constrained dynamics and nonergodic relaxation.

</details>


### [96] [Magnetization-free quantum anomalous Hall effect in altermagnetic weak topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13508)
*Yanmiao Han,Yu-Hao Wan,Peng-Yi Liu,Rundong Zhao,Qing-Feng Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出在弱拓扑绝缘体（weak topological insulator, WTI）薄膜上利用交错磁（altermagnetic）交换作用实现无净磁化（net magnetization）的量子反常霍尔（quantum anomalous Hall, QAH）相，获得 Chern 数 C = 1 的单手性边缘通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规 QAH 效应依赖铁磁性或磁性掺杂，限制材料选择，并常导致低迁移率、对无序敏感和工作温度低；因此需要一种无需磁性掺杂或全局磁化的实现途径。

Method: 构建 WTI 薄膜的最小模型（minimal thin-film model）与 slab 几何，引入交错磁交换作用使 Z 点处 Dirac 锥（Dirac cone）选择性打开能隙，同时利用薄膜上下表面杂化在 Γ 点打开平庸能隙，并计算相应的霍尔响应（Hall response）。

Result: 交错磁序在 Z 点打开能隙、表面杂化在 Γ 点打开平庸能隙，二者共同产生 Chern 数 C = 1 的相，具有单一手性边缘通道（single chiral edge channel）；拓扑转变（topological transition）在最小模型与 slab 几何中均得到验证。

Conclusion: 由于交错磁体（altermagnets）与层状 WTI 已在实验上存在，该机制为无需磁性掺杂或全局磁化实现 QAH 相提供了实验可行的路径，并表明交错磁表面工程（altermagnetic surface engineering）可为层状量子材料中的无耗散边缘输运（dissipationless edge transport）提供干净可控的方案。

Abstract: The quantum anomalous Hall (QAH) effect is usually realized through ferromagnetism or magnetic dop ing, which limits material choices and often results in low mobility, disorder sensitivity, and low operating temperatures. Here, we propose a mechanism to achieve the QAH phase without net magnetization, using altermagnetic exchange on a weak topological insulator (WTI). The altermagnetic order selectively gaps the Dirac cone at the Z point, while hybridization between the top and bottom surfaces of the WTI thin film opens a trivial gap at the point, generating a Chern number C = 1 phase with a single chiral edge channel. We verify the topological transition through both a minimal thin-film model and a slab geometry, and compute the associated Hall response. As altermagnets and layered WTIs already exist experimentally, this mechanism offers a promising and experimentally accessible route to realizing QAH phases without magnetic doping or global magnetization. The results indicate that altermagnetic surface engineering may provide a clean and controllable pathway for realizing dissipationless edge transport in layered quantum materials.

</details>


### [97] [Topological flat bands from nonlinear periodic motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13591)
*Urei Miura,Hisao Hayakawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用经典可积系统的Lax表述刻画非线性周期轨道的拓扑，将周期轨道映射为手征对称紧束缚模型，揭示谐振子对应完全二聚化SSH模型、分界线上缠绕数改变、并连接超对称量子力学的Witten指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典可积系统的周期轨道缺乏拓扑刻画工具，本文试图借助Lax表述把周期运动的频率结构转化为具有手征对称性的晶格模型，从而用拓扑不变量（缠绕数）描述非线性轨道的性质。

Method: 对单自由度哈密顿系统，将手征对称Lax算子沿周期轨道在频率基下展开，得到紧束缚模型；非线性通过高次谐波产生长程跃迁。将该构造应用于对称双势阱与行波约化下的周期非线性波，并做Lax算子的正则量子化。

Result: 能量守恒使模型的两条能带严格平坦；谐振子对应完全二聚化的SSH模型。在对称双势阱中，随阱内轨道过渡为跨双阱轨道，缠绕数改变，半无限链的零能边界模消失；导出边界模轮廓，其局域长度在趋近分界线时对数发散。分界线上周期与跃迁衰减长度发散而带隙有限，使无限频率链的缠绕数得以改变。对有简单转折点的束缚系统，所有周期轨道的手征缠绕数之和等于Witten指数；孤立波极限下有限周期缠绕数变为未定义。

Conclusion: Lax表述提供了连接经典非线性周期轨道拓扑与量子拓扑不变量（缠绕数、Witten指数）的统一框架，分界线处的奇异极限是缠绕数改变的机制，该框架可推广至周期非线性波但于孤立波极限失效。

Abstract: We use the Lax formulation of classical integrable systems to characterize the topology of nonlinear periodic orbits. For a Hamiltonian system with one degree of freedom, we express the chiral-symmetric Lax operator along a periodic orbit in a frequency basis. Energy conservation makes both bands of the resulting tight-binding model exactly flat. The harmonic oscillator corresponds to a fully dimerized topological Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model, while nonlinearity generates long-range hopping through higher harmonics. In the symmetric double well, the winding number changes as intrawell periodic orbits give way to an orbit spanning both wells, and the zero-energy edge mode of the semi-infinite chain disappears. We derive the edge-mode profile and show that its localization length diverges logarithmically as the separatrix is approached. At the separatrix, the period and the hopping decay length diverge while the band gap remains finite; this singular limit of the periodic motion allows the winding number of the infinite frequency chain to change. Canonical quantization of the Lax operator also connects the chiral winding numbers to supersymmetric quantum mechanics: for a bound system on the real line with simple turning points, their sum over all periodic orbits at a common positive energy equals the Witten index. We also apply the construction to periodic nonlinear waves through traveling-wave reductions. The finite-period winding number becomes undefined in their solitary-wave limits.

</details>


### [98] [Plasmons in bilayer graphene with O-type Kekulé distortion: Electron-hole asymmetry of the effective interlayer tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13716)
*Mingyu Wang,Guodong Yu,Shengjun Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 结合紧束缚模型与随机相位近似，研究了含O型Kekulé畸变双层石墨烯中等离激元的层间隧穿行为，发现电子与空穴掺杂下EIT呈现强不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效层间隧穿（effective interlayer tunneling, EIT）是决定双层结构反相等离激元（out-of-phase plasmons）的关键量，但其在Kekulé畸变双层石墨烯中的行为尚不明确。

Method: 采用紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）与随机相位近似（random phase approximation, RPA），对O/O和G/O两种构型的Kekulé畸变双层石墨烯中的等离激元进行计算。

Result: EIT在O/O与G/O双层中均表现出强电子-空穴不对称性；O/O双层反相等离激元的能隙随空穴浓度快速增大而随电子浓度近乎不变，且空穴掺杂引起更强的反常色散；G/O双层中增大Kekulé畸变Δ0可削弱EIT，在电子掺杂下Δ0=0.3时EIT近乎关闭。

Conclusion: 电子与空穴掺杂类型的切换可通过调制EIT来调节基于反相等离激元的器件性能。

Abstract: The effective interlayer tunneling (EIT) is a key quantity in determining the out-of-phase plasmons of a bilayer structure. Using the tight-binding model and the random phase approximation, we investigate plasmons in bilayer graphene with O-type Kekulé distortion. Both O/O and G/O bilayers exhibit strong electron-hole asymmetry in the EIT. For the O/O bilayer, the gap of the out-of-phase plasmon remains almost constant as the electron concentration varies, whereas it increases rapidly with increasing hole concentration; hole doping can also lead to stronger anomalous dispersion than electron doping. For the G/O bilayer, enhancing the Kekulé distortion $Δ_0$ can weaken the EIT, as reflected by the reduced interlayer hybridization caused by the lifted layer degeneracy, especially for the electron-doped case. For the electron-doped G/O bilayer, the EIT is almost turned off when $Δ_0=0.3$ from the perspective of the dispersion. Our results imply that switching the doping type between electron and hole can tune the performance of out-of-phase plasmon-based devices by modulating the EIT.

</details>


### [99] [Floquet first- and higher-order topological superconductors: A review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13804)
*Debashish Mondal,Arnob Kumar Ghosh,Arijit Saha,Tanay Nag*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了从一阶到三阶拓扑超导（FOTSC/TOTSC）中马约拉纳零模空间维度递减的相图景，并论证周期驱动可实现所有此类相（包括Floquet高阶拓扑超导相，FHOTSC），同时讨论区分常规马约拉纳零模与异常马约拉纳π模的拓扑不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算依赖马约拉纳费米子及其拓扑保护，而高阶拓扑超导（HOTSC）可将马约拉纳模的空间局域化调控扩展到传统一阶框架之外，因此需系统梳理拓扑超导谱系及驱动调控手段。

Method: 综述性梳理：从一阶到三阶拓扑超导的相图景，分析周期驱动协议下的Floquet工程（Floquet engineering），并考察自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）、磁场、磁性杂质与对称破缺质量项之间的相互作用。

Result: 周期驱动协议能够实现上述各类拓扑超导相，即使对应的未驱动系统在拓扑上是平庸的；不同驱动方案可在自旋轨道耦合、磁场、磁性杂质与对称破缺质量项的共同作用下产生具有相应拓扑不变量的Floquet高阶拓扑超导相（FHOTSC）。

Conclusion: 综述给出可区分常规马约拉纳零模（MZM）与异常马约拉纳π模（appearing at two distinct quasienergies，无静态对应物）的拓扑不变量讨论，为拓扑量子计算平台提供理论参考。

Abstract: The current developments in quantum technologies are deeply rooted in quantum computation, with Majorana fermions and their topological protection expected to play a central role. In this context, topological superconductors (TSCs) provide a promising platform for investigating Majorana modes and their potential applications in topological quantum computation. In dimensions $d > 1$, the spatial localization of topological Majorana modes can be controlled in higher-order topological superconductors (HOTSCs), extending beyond the conventional framework of first-order topological superconductors (FOTSCs). In this review, we systematically survey the diverse landscape of topological superconductivity, ranging from FOTSCs to third-order topological superconductors (TOTSCs), in which the spatial dimensionality of Majorana zero modes (MZMs) is progressively reduced. We demonstrate how periodic driving protocols enable Floquet engineering of all these phases, even when the corresponding undriven systems are topologically trivial. Furthermore, we investigate various driving schemes and explore the interplay among spin-orbit coupling, magnetic fields, magnetic impurities, and symmetry-breaking mass terms in realizing Floquet higher-order topological superconducting (FHOTSC) phases, characterized by appropriate topological invariants. We also provide an extensive discussion of topological invariants capable of distinguishing conventional MZMs from anomalous Majorana $pi$-modes, which emerge at two distinct quasienergies and have no static counterparts.

</details>


### [100] [Hierarchical Topological Protection and Subsymmetry-Driven Edge-State Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13839)
*Shu Long,Rijia Lin,Hui-Qiang Liang,Linhu Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作揭示了一种拓扑保护层级：多个对称性及其约化形式为拓扑边缘态提供彼此区分的保护机制，并可通过约化对称性约束实现边缘态构型的可控切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究通常将拓扑边缘态保护归于某一对称性，缺乏对多对称性共存及其约化形式如何分层提供保护的统一刻画，本文旨在建立这种层级框架。

Method: 通过将手性非厄米系统（chiral non-Hermitian systems）加倍构造厄米哈密顿量（Hermitian Hamiltonians），识别出结构手性对称性（structural chiral symmetry）与继承手性对称性（inherited chiral symmetry），并选择性破缺其中任一对称性以比较其保护作用。

Result: 两类手性对称性构成互补的拓扑保护通道，对边缘零模（edge zero modes）的保护角色不同，从而在对称性约化下形成不同保护层级；即使两个完整手性对称性均被破缺，固定的子对称性约束（subsymmetry constraints）仍使受保护零模构型在相反边缘间切换，并在未破缺系统的拓扑相边界处改变数目。

Conclusion: 结果确立了拓扑保护的层级框架，并表明约化对称性约束可作为调控边缘态构型的途径。

Abstract: We reveal a hierarchy of topological protection in which multiple symmetries and their reduced forms provide distinct mechanisms for protecting topological edge states. In Hermitian Hamiltonians obtained by doubling chiral non-Hermitian systems, a structural and an inherited chiral symmetry provide complementary channels of topological protection. By selectively breaking either symmetry, we demonstrate their distinct roles in protecting edge zero modes, establishing different levels of protection under symmetry reduction. Remarkably, even when both full chiral symmetries are broken, fixed subsymmetry constraints allow the protected zero-mode configurations to switch between opposite edges and change in number across the topological phase boundaries of the symmetry-unbroken system. Our results establish a hierarchical framework for topological protection, revealing a route to controlling edge-state configurations via reduced symmetry constraints.

</details>


### [101] [Chiral magnon Kerr interaction from linear and nonlinear Meissner screening in ferromagnet--superconductor bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13937)
*Hanchen Wang,Tao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出用超导栅调控铁磁绝缘体中磁振子Kerr相互作用，区分线性与非线性Meissner屏蔽两种贡献，并在YIG/脏铌体系中预言手性Kerr效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子Kerr相互作用（magnon Kerr interaction）的强度与符号依赖磁各向异性、偏置与模式几何，缺乏可调手段，需寻找有效调控途径以服务非线性与量子磁子学（magnonics）。

Method: 引入超导栅（superconducting gate）；先用线性响应分析反射偶极场随纵向磁化变化产生的Kerr贡献，再推广至有限激发幅度下Meissner屏蔽电流降低超流刚度（superfluid stiffness）的非线性情形；以脏铌（dirty niobium）栅控钇铁石榴石（yttrium iron garnet, YIG）为对象进行理论预言。

Result: 线性Meissner屏蔽给出随进动幅度增大而降低纵向磁化所产生的Kerr贡献；超流诱导的破对（superflow-induced pair breaking）叠加第二个Kerr贡献，由界面超流相对非线性屏蔽尺度控制；YIG/脏铌体系中Kerr效应被显著重整化，且在Damon–Eshbach构型下Kerr贡献呈强手性。

Conclusion: 线性与非线性Meissner屏蔽对Kerr相互作用的贡献可通过变温加以区分，由此建立磁振子非线性相互作用的方向性调控途径。

Abstract: Magnon Kerr interaction showing a dependence of magnon dispersion frequency on its population is a useful functionality for nonlinear and quantum magnonics; its strength and sign depend on magnetic anisotropy, bias, and mode geometry. We here propose to use a superconducting gate to tune the Kerr interaction of magnons in ferromagnetic insulators. Even when the superconductor responds linearly, the frequency shift induced by its reflected dipolar field depends on the longitudinal magnetization. The decrease of this magnetization with increasing precession amplitude therefore produces a Kerr contribution. We go beyond this linear Meissner-screening regime by showing that at finite excitation amplitude, the significant Meissner screening currents reduce the superfluid stiffness, thus enhancing the effective magnetic-field penetration and rendering the Meissner backaction amplitude dependent. This superflow-induced pair breaking adds a second Kerr contribution, controlled by the interfacial superflow relative to the nonlinear screening scale. For yttrium iron garnet gated by dirty niobium, we predict a significant renormalization of the Kerr effect by superconductors, with the resulting Kerr contribution strongly chiral in the Damon--Eshbach configuration. By showing that the linear and nonlinear Meissner screening to the Kerr interaction can be distinguished by varying temperature, our work establishes a route to directional control of magnon nonlinear interactions.

</details>


### [102] [Frequency Comb as a Probe of Fluctuation Mechanisms in Nanomechanical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13979)
*Maria Kallergi,Daniel K. J. Boneß,Maximilian Seitner,Wolfgang Belzig,Mark I. Dykman,Eva M. Weig*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该摘要表明，频率梳（frequency comb）除用于计量外，还可作为探测系统频率涨落（frequency fluctuations）的探针；在纳米机械谐振器（nanomechanical resonator）中观测到指数关联的 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 频率噪声及其导致的异常相位扩散（anomalous phase diffusion）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频率梳在计量学（metrology）中不可或缺，但其是否也能反映系统自身的频率涨落尚待阐明；该工作旨在将频率梳用作频率噪声的诊断工具。

Method: 研究对象为纳米机械谐振器（nanomechanical resonator），其中的频率梳源于共振驱动的非线性模式（nonlinear mode）的自持振动（self-sustained vibrations）；通过分析梳齿线形（comb lineshapes）提取频率噪声特性，并与独立测得的振动弛豫时间（relaxation time）进行比对。

Result: 梳齿线形显示出指数关联的 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 型频率噪声，其关联时间（correlation time）与独立测得的振动弛豫时间一致；该噪声进一步导致异常相位扩散（anomalous phase diffusion）。

Conclusion: 该噪声机制具有普适性：与频率梳本身相同，它源于自持振动的强非线性（strong nonlinearity）。

Abstract: Frequency combs are indispensable in metrology. We show that a frequency comb also probes frequency fluctuations of the system. In our nanomechanical resonator, the comb arises from self-sustained vibrations of a resonantly driven nonlinear mode. The comb lineshapes reveal exponentially correlated (Ornstein--Uhlenbeck) frequency noise. Its correlation time agrees with the independently measured relaxation time of the vibrations. The noise leads to anomalous phase diffusion. Its mechanism is generic: like the comb itself, it stems from the strong nonlinearity of the self-sustained vibrations.

</details>


### [103] [Evidence of anomalous Hall crystals in moiré rhombohedral graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14067)
*Hongyuan Li,Zuhan Geng,Annika Weber,Chenxi Xu,Yifan Jiang,Jiabin Xie,Lujin Min,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Yasuda,Xiaomeng Liu,Jie Shan,Kin Fai Mak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在空穴掺杂的莫尔菱面石墨烯（moiré rhombohedral graphene, mRG）中，通过输运与噪声测量首次给出反常霍尔晶体（anomalous Hall crystal, AHC）的直接实验证据，表明自发电子晶化可在无磁场下产生量子化霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AHC 概念此前被用于解释莫尔菱面石墨烯中观测到的部分陈绝缘体（Chern insulator），但一直缺乏直接的实验证据；该研究旨在检验强库仑相互作用与大贝里曲率（Berry curvature）体系中自发晶化是否真实存在。

Method: 采用电输运与噪声测量相结合的手段，对空穴掺杂 mRG 进行系统表征，并开展磁场、温度与栅压依赖性研究。

Result: 观测到一系列陈绝缘体，其掺杂密度跨越与莫尔晶格不公度的宽广连续区间；这些态表现出电流诱导的宽带噪声、记忆效应（memory effects）以及显著的栅极滞后（gate hysteresis），且邻近整数填充处不出现陈绝缘体。

Conclusion: 结果支持一个自发电荷有序态作为所观察陈绝缘体的母态（parental state），确立 mRG 中 AHC 的实验证据，并将其与已报道的重入或扩展量子（反常）霍尔态区分开来，为研究该新物态的集体动力学（collective dynamics）开辟途径。

Abstract: An anomalous Hall crystal (AHC) is a quantum state of matter, in which spontaneous electron crystallization leads to a quantized Hall effect without a magnetic field. The state can emerge in materials with strong Coulomb interaction and large Berry curvatures, such as few-layer rhombohedral graphene. Although the idea of AHCs has been invoked to explain some of the observed Chern insulators in moiré rhombohedral graphene (mRG), direct experimental evidence of AHCs has been missing so far. Here, we report experimental evidence of AHCs in hole-doped mRG through combined electrical transport and noise measurements. We identify a series of distinct Chern insulators spanning a broad and continuous range of doping densities incommensurate with the underlying moiré lattice; the states exhibit current-induced broadband noise and memory effects as well as pronounced gate hysteresis. Systematic magnetic field, temperature and gate dependence studies further support the emergence of a spontaneously charge-ordered state that acts as a parental state for the observed Chern insulators. The absence of Chern insulators at nearby integer fillings also distinguishes the observed state from the reported reentrant or extended quantum (anomalous) Hall states. Altogether, our results establish experimental evidence of AHCs in mRG and open the door for further exploration of the collective dynamics in this new state of matter.

</details>


### [104] [End-state-assisted tunneling at a topological insulator STM tip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14155)
*Rui Chen,Jie Zhou,Bin Zhou,Dong-Hui Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在量子自旋霍尔（quantum spin Hall）针尖框架下，揭示拓扑端态（topological end states）介导的共振隧穿机制，说明针尖几何与非对称终止如何控制自旋极化电流的输运与干涉对比度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期使用拓扑绝缘体针尖的自旋敏感扫描隧道实验，需要从微观层面理解针尖几何如何决定电流输运（current delivery），而现有认识不足。

Method: 采用量子自旋霍尔针尖模型，考虑边缘间杂化（interedge hybridization）打开的能隙，分析端态介导的共振隧穿；在独立路径（independent-path）描述下比较两条自旋相反的螺旋路径（helical paths）的有效长度与共振宽度，并将方向性通道与d波交错磁体（d-wave altermagnet）耦合。

Result: 非对称终止使两条螺旋路径的有效长度与共振宽度不等，其透射电流不平衡在共振匹配处消失、偏离匹配时变为有限；与d波交错磁体耦合产生自旋相关的干涉对比度；阶梯状终止中的杂化端态给出多个隧穿共振。

Conclusion: 结果确立端态介导隧穿（end-state-mediated tunneling）为强受限拓扑针尖扫描隧道显微镜（STM）的一种输运机制。

Abstract: Recent spin-sensitive scanning tunneling experiments with topological-insulator tips motivate a microscopic understanding of how tip geometry controls current delivery. We show that topological end states mediate resonant tunneling through the gap opened by interedge hybridization in a quantum spin Hall tip. An asymmetric termination gives oppositely spin-polarized helical paths unequal effective lengths and resonance widths. Within an independent-path description, their transmitted-current imbalance vanishes at a matched resonance and becomes finite away from it. Coupling these directional channels to a $d$-wave altermagnet yields a spin-dependent interference contrast. Hybridized end states along a stepped termination provide multiple tunneling resonances. Our results establish end-state-mediated tunneling as a transport mechanism for STM with strongly confined topological tips.

</details>


### [105] [Edge quantum weight of finite Chern flakes and its topological floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14271)
*Rohit Narula*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限陈数薄片（Chern flake）的边缘对量子权重（quantum weight）的贡献，证明手征边缘的低频跃迁给出单位面积下界 $K_\mathrm{edge}\geq|C|$，并指出测量结果取决于薄片尺寸与电路频率哪个先满足条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体量子权重（bulk quantum weight）$K_\mathrm{bulk}$ 被陈数（Chern number）$C$ 从下方以 $|C|$ 界定，但有限尺寸薄片存在边缘，边缘项是否随薄片增大而消失尚不明确，需澄清电路零点能测量究竟反映体还是边缘贡献。

Method: 以手征边缘（chiral edge）低频跃迁的偶极权重（dipole weight，即跃迁偶极矩阵元平方和）为对象，结合两种圆偏振均正吸收与边缘—体圆二色性（circular dichroism）相消的已知结论推导下界；并用格点薄片的精确自由费米子（free-fermion）计算加以验证。

Result: 边缘权重满足 $K_\mathrm{edge}\geq|C|$ 且不随薄片增大而消失；弱耦合下慢速电路测得双场向平均的 $K_\mathrm{bulk}+K_\mathrm{edge}$，而低于体能隙的快速电路趋于 $K_\mathrm{bulk}$，格点计算证实该下界与频率依赖。下界之上，单通道边缘权重由边界形状与边缘速度确定，方形与矩形薄片符合精度达 0.003。

Conclusion: 体与边缘量子权重的可测性取决于“先增大薄片还是先放慢电路”的顺序；结果仅在弱耦合成立，固定耦合下不断增大的薄片最终会离开该区域。额外边缘通道各自叠加独立数值，而边缘圆二色性只计净手征性（net chirality）。

Abstract: The zero-point energy of a circuit measures the polarization fluctuation of an insulator coupled to it. In a crystal this is the bulk quantum weight $K_\mathrm{bulk}$, which the Chern number $C$ bounds below by $|C|$. We show that a finite Chern flake adds an edge term that survives as the flake grows. The low-frequency transitions of its chiral edge carry a dipole weight, the sum of their squared dipole matrix elements, $K_\mathrm{edge}\geq|C|$ per unit area, a floor that follows from the positive absorption of both circular polarizations by every transition and the known cancellation of circular dichroism between edge and bulk. A weakly coupled circuit slower than the edge modes therefore reads $K_\mathrm{bulk}+K_\mathrm{edge}$, averaged over two field directions, while a faster one below the bulk gap approaches $K_\mathrm{bulk}$, so which quantum weight is measured depends on whether the flake is made large or the circuit slow first. Exact free-fermion calculations on lattice flakes bear out the floor and this dependence on the circuit frequency. The result holds at weak coupling, which a growing flake at fixed coupling eventually leaves. Above the floor, the edge weight of one channel is a number fixed by the shape of the boundary and the edge velocities, which square and rectangular lattice flakes follow to within 0.003. Each further edge channel adds its own such number, while the circular dichroism of the edge counts only the net chirality.

</details>


### [106] [Good Practices for Measuring the Interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya Interaction by Brillouin Light Scattering and Magneto-Optical Kerr Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14405)
*Marco Madami,Silvia Tacchi,Adriano Di Pietro,Alessandro Magni,Giovanni Gandini,Christian Rinaldi,Giovanni Carlotti,Gianfranco Durin,Johannes Hertel,Maximilian Lederer,Christopher H. Marrows,Vito Puliafito,Michaela Kuepferling*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文针对界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（iDMI）常数测量结果差异显著的问题，以布里渊光散射（BLS）和磁光克尔效应（MOKE）两种技术为基础，提出一套iDMI测量的规范实践与不确定性分析路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: iDMI是稳定手性磁态（如skyrmions与手性畴壁）的关键因素，对下一代自旋电子学（spintronics）技术至关重要；但其常数D的定量测定常存在显著不一致，亟需统一、可复现的测量规范。

Method: 以Co/Pt基异质结构（heterostructures）为研究对象，基于BLS与MOKE两种主要技术，系统梳理测量流程、数据评估与不确定性分析（uncertainty analysis），并强调用于提取iDMI常数的磁学参数及其不确定度传播的作用，以及测量模型对可靠性的影响。

Result: 分析表明，提取iDMI常数所依赖的磁学参数及其不确定度传播具有关键作用，合理的测量模型可提升测量可靠性；研究给出了涵盖测量程序、数据评估与不确定度分析的综合性路线图。

Conclusion: 所提方法学为提升不同实验室与不同实验技术间iDMI测量的一致性与可复现性（reproducibility）提供了实用框架。

Abstract: The interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (iDMI) is a key ingredient for stabilizing chiral magnetic states, such as skyrmions and chiral domain walls, which are key building blocks for next-generation spintronic technologies. Despite its importance, the quantitative determination of the iDMI constant, $D$, is often plagued by significant discrepancies. This paper establishes a set of good practices for measuring iDMI using two primary techniques: Brillouin Light Scattering (BLS) and the Magneto-Optical Kerr Effect (MOKE). Through the analysis of Co/Pt-based heterostructures, this work provides a comprehensive roadmap for measurement procedures, data evaluation, and uncertainty analysis. Our analysis highlights the crucial role of magnetic parameters used to extract the iDMI constant and propagation of their uncertainties, and the measurement model for enhancing measurement reliability. The proposed methodology provides a practical framework for improving the consistency and reproducibility of iDMI measurements across different laboratories and experimental techniques.

</details>


### [107] [Tuning and Mapping of Two-Tone Optomechanical Instability up to the Physical Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14427)
*Marco Dicosta,Alexandre Delattre,Louise Banniard,Laure Mercier de Lépinay,Andrew Fefferman,Eddy Collin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出完整理论描述光机械反作用规避（back-action-evading）实验中的双音不稳定性（two-tone instability），涵盖所有失谐情形、双泵浦功率失衡及器件非理想因素。模型在接近100的高协同性（cooperativity）下与实验高度吻合，并将极限因素归因于腔频噪声，同时在高驱动功率下发现具有机械梳状频谱（mechanical comb spectrum）的自驱动新态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反作用规避双音方案的实验实现需要准确理解双音不稳定性及其对失谐、功率失衡与器件缺陷的依赖，而已有工作对功率失谐、边带分辨（sideband resolution）及高协同性下的行为描述不足。

Method: 构建覆盖任意失谐（含双泵浦功率不平衡）与器件缺陷（有限机械品质因子、有限边带分辨）的双音不稳定性理论模型，并配套提出精细调谐（fine-tuning）流程，将模型与高达近100协同性的实验进行对比。

Result: 理论与实验吻合良好；功率失谐与边带分辨对寻找反作用规避"甜点"（sweet spot）尤为关键；实验极限因素被确定为腔频噪声（cavity frequency noise）。此外，在很高驱动功率下发现新的自驱动态，其共同特征为机械梳状频谱。

Conclusion: 该工作为反作用规避实验及相关双音方案的实现提供了完整理论工具与调谐方法，并揭示高功率下同步被非线性效应破坏、出现梳状自驱动态的现象。

Abstract: We report on the compete theory describing the two-tone instability proper to optomechanical back-action-evading experiments. We address all possible mistunings (including power imbalance of the two pumps), as well as the device imperfections (finite mechanical quality factor, and sideband resolution). We illustrate the importance of power mistuning and sideband resolution, the latter being particularly relevant in finding the back-action-evading "sweet spot". The model is compared to experiments performed up to an extremely high cooperativity, of almost a 100. This has been made possible thanks to a fine-tuning procedure, which is presented. The agreement theory-experiment is very good, and the limiting factor is unraveled: cavity frequency noise. Besides, we discover at very high drive powers new self-driven states, which all share the characteristic feature of presenting a mechanical comb spectrum; as if the synchronization of the responses to the two drives (which occurs at lower powers) was destroyed by nonlinear effects. We believe that our work is extremely relevant to the implementation of back-action-evading experiments, as well as other related two-tone schemes.

</details>


<div id='nlin.SI'></div>

# nlin.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Bi-Hamiltonian structure and conserved quantities of the Yajima-Oikawa-Newell system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14404)
*Marcos Caso-Huerta*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 该研究刻画了Yajima-Oikawa-Newell（YON）系统的Hamilton与双Hamilton结构：证明在自然分次下不存在局域且关于β多项式的Hamilton算子，但借助基于长波势的非局域规范变换，可将已知算子化为sl(2)流代数的Lie-Poisson结构，并构造出与之相容的第二弱非局域结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YON系统是含两个实参数α、β的长波-短波相互作用可积模型，可分别退化为Yajima-Oikawa系统（α=0）与Newell系统（β=0）。已有研究仅对Newell系统给出两个非局域Hamilton算子，而一般α≠0情形下的Hamilton结构尚不清楚，故有必要系统研究其Hamilton与双Hamilton描述。

Method: 对α≠0情形，通过长波常数平移将系统映射到Newell系统；证明不存在自然分次下齐次、关于β多项式的局域Hamilton算子；利用基于长波势的非局域规范变换（gauge transformation）使第一个已知算子局域化，得到sl(2)流代数（current algebra）的Lie-Poisson结构；再由显式弱非局域形式的递推算子（recursion operator）构造第二Hamilton结构并验证相容性；此外结合Noether定理与Lax对的渐近对角化。

Result: 对任意α≠0、β，变换后的系统关于一个局域非退化Poisson算子为Hamilton系统；第二弱非局域结构的非局域尾部由系统的三个自伴Lie点对称性生成，且两结构相容。递推算子无平方根且第一结构无Casimir，故Lenard-Magri链分裂为两个族，分别由两个最简单的守恒量播种，两族所有流均局域且Hamilton量互相内对合（in involution）。附带得到Lie点对称性与守恒量的Noether对应，并通过Lax对渐近对角化给出守恒密度的闭合形式。

Conclusion: YON系统在α≠0时虽不具备自然的局域多项式Hamilton算子，但经非局域规范变换后具有局域Lie-Poisson结构，并可构造相容的弱非局域第二结构；其Lenard-Magri链分裂为两个全局域、相互对合的守恒流族，同时建立了对称性与守恒量的对应及守恒密度的显式表达。

Abstract: We study the Hamiltonian and bi-Hamiltonian structure of the Yajima-Oikawa-Newell (YON) system, an integrable model of long wave-short wave interaction depending on two real parameters $α$ and $β$, which reduces to the Yajima-Oikawa system for $α=0$ and to the Newell system for $β=0$. For $α\neq0$, a constant shift of the long wave maps the YON system into the Newell system, for which two nonlocal Hamiltonian operators are known. We show that, for $α\neq0$, the system admits no local Hamiltonian operator homogeneous in its natural grading and polynomial in $β$. A nonlocal gauge transformation built on the potential of the long wave makes the first of the known operators local for a single value of its parameter: the transformed system is Hamiltonian, for every $α\neq0$ and every $β$, with respect to a local, non-degenerate Poisson operator, which is the Lie-Poisson structure of the $\mathfrak{sl}(2)$ current algebra. From a recursion operator advancing the hierarchy by two steps, given in explicit weakly nonlocal form, we construct a second, weakly nonlocal Hamiltonian structure, whose nonlocal tail is generated by the three autonomous Lie point symmetries of the system, and prove that both structures are compatible. Since the recursion operator admits no square root and the first structure has no Casimirs, the Lenard-Magri chain splits into two families, seeded by the two simplest conserved quantities. Every flow of both families is local, and all the corresponding Hamiltonians are in involution. As by-products, we relate the Lie point symmetries of the system to its conserved quantities via Noether's theorem, and obtain the conserved densities in closed form by asymptotic diagonalisation of the Lax pair.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [A Beltrami Mode in Extreme Fractional Hydrodynamics under Stochastic Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12965)
*José I. H. López*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文在三维环面上构造了自适应分数阶 Navier–Stokes（AFNS）流体在极端阶端点处的精确随机解：选取螺旋 Beltrami 本征场并将白噪声强迫对齐该场后，非线性向量方程退化为标量 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 过程，从而得到显式的不变高斯律及能量、涨落、时间关联、环量与螺旋度的解析公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察 AFNS 在极端阶端点所保留的有限分数阶耗散（fractional dissipation）的统计效应，并在不依赖级串假设（cascade hypothesis）的前提下，对比经典 Navier–Stokes 与 Euler 极限在相同强迫下的统计行为差异。

Method: 方法上采用精确随机解构造：利用螺旋 Beltrami 本征场（helical Beltrami eigenfield）将非线性向量方程约化为标量 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 过程，且要求时间白噪声强迫与该本征场对齐；由此获得不变高斯分布律（invariant Gaussian law）。

Result: 结果给出平稳能量、能量涨落、时间关联、环量（circulation）与螺旋度（helicity）的显式公式；相同强迫下经典 Navier–Stokes 模态的平稳能量在雷诺数（Reynolds number）趋于无穷时发散，而 Euler 极限的平均能量线性增长且不存在不变概率律。

Conclusion: 结论表明，该构造在不引入级串假设的情况下分离出 AFNS 极端阶端点处有限分数阶耗散所导致的统计效应，并清晰刻画了其与经典 Navier–Stokes 及 Euler 极限在不变测度存在性上的本质差别。

Abstract: We construct an exact stochastic solution of the extreme order endpoint of adaptive fractional Navier Stokes (AFNS) hydrodynamics on the three-dimensional periodic torus. A helical Beltrami eigenfield reduces the nonlinear vector equation to a scalar Ornstein Uhlenbeck process when the white-in-time forcing is aligned with that eigenfield. The resulting invariant Gaussian law yields explicit formulas for the stationary energy, its fluctuations, temporal correlations, circulation, and helicity. Under the same forcing, the corresponding classical Navier Stokes mode has a stationary energy that diverges as the Reynolds number tends to infinity, while the Euler limit exhibits linear growth of the mean energy and has no invariant probability law. The construction isolates, without a cascade hypothesis, the statistical effect of the finite fractional dissipation retained at the extreme endpoint of AFNS.

</details>


### [110] [Purely elastic centre-mode instability in channel and pipe flows of a simplified Phan-Thien-Tanner fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14104)
*Calum S. Skene,Jeffrey S. Oishi,Keaton J. Burns,Moritz Linkmann,Geoffrey M. Vasil,Alexander Morozov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文通过数值线性稳定性分析，在简化Phan-Thien-Tanner（sPTT）流体管道与槽道流动中发现宽泛聚合物浓度、较低Weissenberg数下的纯弹性中心模态不稳定性，表明该不稳定性存在与否由流体的瞬态流变响应决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将粘弹性压力驱动平行剪切流中的空间局域中面应力结构视为混沌行为的构成单元，并认为其源于层流态的线性中心模态不稳定性；但无惯性时Oldroyd-B与FENE-P模型预测该不稳定性仅限超稀溶液与极大Weissenberg数的槽道流，管道流中亦无纯弹性不稳定性报道，故需考察其他本构模型的预测。

Method: 对简化Phan-Thien-Tanner（sPTT）流体的槽道流与管道流进行数值线性稳定性分析，考察不同聚合物浓度与Weissenberg数下的纯弹性中心模态不稳定性。

Result: 在两种几何构型中均发现纯弹性中心模态不稳定性，其可跨越广泛的聚合物浓度范围，且出现在远低于FENE-P预测的Weissenberg数下；由于sPTT与FENE-P具有相同的稳态流变学与层流剖面，二者预测的差异指向瞬态流变响应。

Conclusion: 纯弹性中心模态不稳定性应在实际流动条件下可观测，该结果同时提供了区分不同本构模型的手段。

Abstract: Recent work identified spatially localised midplane stress structures as the building blocks of chaotic behaviour in pressure-driven parallel shear flows of viscoelastic fluids. Such coherent structures are thought to originate from a linear centre-mode instability of the laminar state. In the absence of inertia, however, the Oldroyd-B and FENE-P constitutive models predict that this instability is confined to ultra-dilute polymer solutions and very large Weissenberg numbers in channel flow, while no purely elastic instability has been reported in pipe flow. Here, we study numerically the linear stability of channel and pipe flows of a simplified Phan-Thien-Tanner (sPTT) fluid and find a purely elastic centre-mode instability in both geometries across a wide range of polymer concentrations and at much lower Weissenberg numbers. Since the sPTT and FENE-P models have identical steady rheology and laminar profiles, their contrasting predictions indicate that the existence of the centre-mode instability is governed by the transient rheological response of the fluid. Our results suggest that the purely elastic centre-mode instability should be observable under realistic flow conditions. They also offer a means of discriminating between constitutive models.

</details>


<div id='physics.geo-ph'></div>
