### [111] [First results from a borehole network for the seismic characterization of the Einstein Telescope Euregio Meuse-Rhine candidate site](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12917)
*L. D'Onofrio,S. Koley,P. Barneo,S. Bentvelsen,S. Danilishin,G. Degrande,S. Francois,S. Kadmiel,M. Kiehn,H. Michel,F. Nguyen,T. Niggemann,N. Nippe,J. Pretz,V. Tempel,B. Vink,W. Walk*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次给出Euregio Meuse-Rhine候选站点基于钻孔台阵（2026年前七个月、四个地下台站及同址地表传感器）的地震表征结果，并介绍为此开发的Python处理框架SiteChar。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）的低频灵敏度取决于站点地震环境，其影响路径包括地面运动与检验质量的机械耦合以及牛顿噪声（Newtonian noise），因此需对候选站点开展地震表征。

Method: 基于近期布设的钻孔观测网络，覆盖2026年前七个月、四个地下台站及其同址地表传感器；其中两个钻孔刻意布设在铁路基础设施附近，以描述人为瞬态（anthropogenic transients）在不同源距下向深部传播的行为。分析采用新开发的Python框架SiteChar，实现跨传感器、跨台网、跨观测时段的统一处理流程，按顺序分块处理数据并分离噪声基底（noise floor）与瞬态贡献。

Result: 给出了观测瞬态目录（catalogue of observed transients）的表征及初步分类，并报告了不同源距下人为瞬态向深部传播的水平。

Conclusion: 这些测量属于综合站点表征（integrated site characterization）工作的一部分，将观测地震场与三维地质及波传播模型结合，用于预测牛顿噪声并最终指导该候选站点的噪声缓解（mitigation）。

Abstract: The low-frequency sensitivity of the Einstein Telescope depends on the seismic environment of its site, both through the mechanical coupling of ground motion to the test masses and through Newtonian noise. We present the first results of the seismic characterization of the Euregio Meuse-Rhine candidate site based on a network of recently instrumented boreholes covering the first seven months of 2026 at four underground stations and their co-located surface sensors. Two of these boreholes were deliberately sited next to railway infrastructure, so that the levels reported here describe how anthropogenic transients propagate to depth at different distances from the source. The analysis was carried out with \texttt{SiteChar}, a Python framework developed for this work to provide a common processing pipeline across different sensors, networks and observation periods. It processes the data in sequential chunks and separates the noise floor from the contribution of transients. We present a characterization of the catalogue of observed transients and a tentative classification. These measurements form part of an integrated site characterization approach that combines the observed seismic field with three-dimensional geological and wave-propagation models to predict Newtonian noise and ultimately guide its mitigation at the Euregio Meuse-Rhine candidate site.

</details>


### [112] [An Idealized Model of Buoyant Gas Spreading and Silt Release beneath Rough Cave Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12895)
*Sandy Hardian Susanto Herho,Dasapta Erwin Irawan,Rusmawan Suwarman,Deny Juanda Puradimaja*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 该研究建立理想化模型，将自仿射粗糙顶板下的浮力气体视为准静态水洼序列，模拟洞穴潜水“渗流（percolation）”过程中气体驱动的泥沙剥落与沉降，并分析其可见度后果及团队效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放回路潜水员呼出的气体在洞顶聚集、沿岩壁迁移并震落淤泥，形成潜水员称的渗流现象，可能严重降低能见度，但缺乏定量描述该过程的模型。

Method: 将自仿射（self-affine）粗糙顶板下的气体建模为填充至溢流点、逐级爬升并相互合并的准静态水洼序列（quasi-static pools）；移动接触线（moving contact lines）剥落淤泥，释放的泥沙按粒径谱沉降；模型以凹陷填充算法验证并检验气体守恒与侵入渗流（invasion percolation）毛细极限指数。

Result: 计算与独立凹陷填充算法精确一致，气体体积守恒至舍入误差，并重现侵入渗流已知指数；顶板起伏与倾角的平衡定义池尺度（pool scale）并组织气体聚集位置，单源气体覆盖面积随供给体积呈慢于线性的增长，且截断较软使大池在更陡顶板上仍存在；参考巷道中领头潜水员释放泥沙远多于后方潜水员，团队总量远小于成员单独作用之和，该排序在所有测试的脱附效率与顶板几何下成立；团队通过后眼部高度朝出口方向的能见度仍持续下降一小时以上。

Conclusion: 绝对泥沙质量与可视距离依赖洞穴中尚未测量的量，结果应主要作为标度关系（scaling relations）和情景比较来解读。

Abstract: Gas exhaled by open-circuit divers collects under the ceilings of submerged caves, migrates along the rock, and dislodges loose silt that then falls through the passage, a process that cave divers call percolation. This study develops an idealized model of the process. Buoyant gas under a self-affine rough ceiling is treated as a quasi-static sequence of pools that fill to their spill points, release gas that climbs to the next high point, and merge where their levels meet. Moving contact lines strip silt from the rock, and the released silt settles as a spectrum of grain sizes that reduces the distance at which a diver can see. The computation agrees exactly with an independent depression-filling algorithm, conserves gas volume to round-off, and reproduces the known exponents of invasion percolation in its capillary limit. A balance between the relief of the ceiling and its dip defines a pool scale that organizes where gas collects, and the area covered by gas from a single source grows more slowly than the volume supplied, as predicted by the self-affine geometry. The cutoff at the pool scale is soft, so large pools persist on steeper ceilings than the scale alone would suggest. In a reference passage, the lead diver of a team releases far more silt than the diver behind, and a team releases far less than the sum of its members acting alone, because the lead diver fills the pools and strips the paths that later divers reuse. This ordering holds across every detachment efficiency and ceiling geometry tested. Visibility toward the exit at eye level continues to decline for an hour or more after a team has passed, as fine silt descends from a nearly opaque layer beneath the ceiling. Absolute silt masses and sighting ranges depend on quantities that have not been measured in caves, and the results are best read as scaling relations and comparisons between scenarios.

</details>


### [113] [EoSAlign: An Open-Source Software for Calculating, Comparing, and Aligning Pressure Under Extreme Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12897)
*Allison Pease,Heidi N. Krauss,Kassandra Amezcua,Sang-Heon Shim*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要介绍开源软件 EoSAlign，可从体积、拉曼位移或发光峰位等实验测量值计算压力，并对不同已发表压力标定（pressure scales）进行比对与对齐，以缓解高压实验中压力标度不一致的问题；作者以布里奇曼石（bridgmanite）与后钙钛矿（post-perovskite）状态方程（equation of state, EoS）的拟合对齐作为示例应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压实验中的压力确定至关重要，但不同压力标定之间存在不一致，导致相界、熔融条件与热力学模型等测量属性存在不确定度，并使研究间难以直接比较；在 100 GPa 以上，现有标度的分歧超过 10 GPa，问题尤为突出。

Method: 作者开发了开源软件 EoSAlign，可基于一系列已发表的标定计算压力，并支持从实验测量量（如体积（volume, Å³）、拉曼位移（Raman shift, cm⁻¹）或发光峰位（luminescence peak position, nm））得到压力值；该工具还能比较不同压力标准给出的压力，并将布里奇曼石与后钙钛矿的状态方程拟合并对齐至同一标度。

Result: 利用 EoSAlign 对布里奇曼石和后钙钛矿状态方程进行拟合并对齐到共同标度后，可减小与压力标度相关的差异。

Conclusion: EoSAlign 可应用于任何高压数据分析，用于比较实验结果、提升不同研究之间的一致性，并有助于推进对行星内部（planetary interiors）与高压物理（high pressure physics）的认识。

Abstract: Accurate pressure determination is essential in high pressure experiments. Inconsistencies among pressure scales limit direct comparisons and contribute to uncertainty in measured properties, such as, phase boundaries, melting conditions, and thermodynamic models, especially above 100 GPa where existing scales diverge by >10 GPa. EoSAlign, an open-source software, can calculate, compare, and align pressures using a range of published calibrations. EoSAlign calculates pressure from experimental measurements (such as volume (A^3), Raman shift (cm^-1), or luminescence peak position (nm)) and it can be used to compare pressure values from different pressure standards. Using EoSAlign, we fit and align the bridgmanite and post-perovskite equations of state to a common scale, enabling us to reduce pressure scale related differences. EoSAlign can be applied to any high pressure data analysis to compare experimental results, improve consistency between studies, and advance our understanding of planetary interiors and high pressure physics.

</details>


### [114] [Leveraging Large Language Models to Predict Atomic Oxygen Erosion of Polymers in Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12910)
*Jared Solovei,Sakshi Agarwal,Aubrey Toland,Jeffrey G. Ethier,Vikas Varshney,Lisa M. Hall,Wolfgang Windl,Rampi Ramprasad*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 该研究基于 GPT-4o 微调大语言模型（large language model, LLM），利用 201 条 NASA 低地球轨道（low Earth orbit, LEO）暴露任务数据预测聚合物原子氧（atomic oxygen, AO）侵蚀产率（erosion yield, $E_y$），在少量数据条件下优于传统机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO 面临凯斯勒综合征（Kessler Syndrome）引发的碎片级联风险，延长卫星在轨寿命亟需抗原子氧侵蚀的下一代聚合物材料；而真实 LEO 暴露实验成本高昂且地面难以复现，故需数据驱动方法加速材料发现。

Method: 构建由多次 NASA LEO 暴露任务整理而成的 201 条测量数据集，基于 OpenAI GPT-4o 微调大语言模型，直接以聚合物信息预测 AO 侵蚀产率，并与传统机器学习方法比较，同时检验薄膜厚度、分层等宏观描述符的作用。

Result: 模型取得 0.154 的数量级误差（order of magnitude error, OME）与 0.719 的对数尺度决定系数（log-$R^2$），优于传统机器学习方法且无需大量特征工程；加入宏观描述符未提升预测性能。

Conclusion: AO 侵蚀主要受化学与环境变量支配，而非体相结构特征，凸显化学导向的聚合物设计的重要性；该框架可作为快速筛选候选聚合物的可扩展平台，减少对昂贵航天实验的依赖，并显示 LLM 在有限数据下提取化学有意义关系的能力。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO), home to most active satellites, is projected to undergo a debris-fragmentation cascade known as Kessler Syndrome. In light of this eventuality, it is imperative to maximize the operational lifetime of LEO satellites. Instrumental to this goal is the development of next-generation polymeric materials with improved resistance to atomic oxygen (AO) erosion. Because genuine LEO exposure experiments are prohibitively expensive and LEO conditions are difficult to reproduce in the laboratory, data-driven approaches offer a promising alternative for accelerating materials discovery. Here, we develop a fine-tuned large language model (LLM) based on OpenAI's GPT-4o to predict the AO erosion yield ($E_y$) of polymers using a curated dataset of 201 measurements compiled from multiple NASA LEO exposure missions. The model achieves an order of magnitude error (OME) of 0.154 and a log-scaled coefficient of determination (log-$R^2$) of 0.719, outperforming conventional machine learning approaches while eliminating the need for extensive feature engineering. Notably, incorporating macroscopic descriptors such as film thickness and layering did not improve predictive performance, suggesting that AO erosion is governed primarily by chemical and environmental variables rather than bulk structural characteristics. This finding underscores the importance of chemically informed polymer design in developing materials with improved AO resistance, while demonstrating the ability of modern LLMs to effectively extract chemically meaningful relationships even when data is limited. Beyond its predictive accuracy, the proposed framework provides a scalable platform for rapidly screening candidate polymers, reducing reliance on costly spaceflight experiments.

</details>


### [115] [A domain-specific atomistic model for Earth's core materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12919)
*Tianqi Wan,Liangrui Wei,Zepeng Wu,Renata M. Wentzcovitch,Yang Sun*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 该研究构建了地球核心原子模型（Earth's Core Atomistic Model, ECAM），用于描述极端条件下Fe-Ni-S-Si-O-C-H多组分铁合金，为地球核心的化学分配与结晶模拟提供高效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解地球核心（Earth's core）需要准确描述极端条件下的多组分铁合金，而现有基础原子模型（foundational atomistic model）对核心材料的描述存在偏差。

Method: 通过基础原子模型采样、针对性第一性原理（ab initio）模拟与迭代探索构建ECAM；从近50万个构型（configurations）中筛选紧凑数据集，以高精度第一性原理计算统一标注，训练轻量模型，并显式纳入热电子贡献（thermal electronic contributions）。

Result: 该模型运行速度比原始基础模型快近两个数量级，修正了基础模型描述核心材料时的伪影（artifacts），并重现了Fe及二元合金的密度、地震波速（seismic velocity）、吉布斯自由能（Gibbs free energy）与相图，与既有第一性原理计算结果一致。

Conclusion: ECAM有望支持对地球核心化学分配（chemical partitioning）与结晶（crystallization）的更真实建模；该工作流程也为从基础模型采样走向高效领域专用模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Understanding Earth's core requires accurate descriptions of multicomponent iron alloys under extreme conditions. Here, we develop the Earth's Core Atomistic Model (ECAM) for Fe-Ni-S-Si-O-C-H alloys through sampling with a foundational atomistic model, targeted ab initio simulations, and iterative exploration. A compact dataset selected from nearly half a million configurations is consistently labeled using high-accuracy first-principles calculations and used to train a lightweight model. The model explicitly includes thermal electronic contributions and runs nearly two orders of magnitude faster than the original foundational model. ECAM corrects artifacts in the foundational model's description of core materials and reproduces the density, seismic velocity, Gibbs free energy, and phase diagrams of Fe and binary alloys in agreement with previous ab initio calculations. ECAM is expected to enable more realistic modeling of chemical partitioning and crystallization in Earth's core. Our workflow also provides a practical route from foundational-model sampling to efficient domain-specific simulation.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Second-level termination of the PPT entanglement-cost hierarchy for two qutrits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12993)
*Wei Song,Xiao-Lan Zong,Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对任意两 qutrit 量子态，PPT 纠缠成本（PPT entanglement cost）的半定规划（semidefinite programming, SDP）下界层级在第二层即终止，从而零误差渐近纠缠成本可由仅含三个 Hermitian 矩阵变量的 SDP 求得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零误差渐近纠缠成本在保持正部分转置（positive partial transpose, PPT）的操作下难以精确计算；已有的 SDP 下界层级是否在有限层终止、终止层数能否对所有态统一，尚缺乏结论。

Method: 核心是“缩短一层”可行对偶链（feasible dual chain）：结合精确有理算术（exact rational arithmetic）验证的有限证书（finite certificates）与参数域边界附近的解析估计（analytic estimates）。

Result: 对任意两 qutrit 态，层级在第二层终止，零误差渐近纠缠成本等价于一个含三个 Hermitian 矩阵变量的 SDP；同时表明两 qutrit 系统存在统一终止层，而所有维度上不存在统一终止层。

Conclusion: 两 qutrit 情形下 PPT 纠缠成本具有可判定、可计算的有限层刻画，但该统一终止性质不能推广到任意维度。

Abstract: We prove that for arbitrary two-qutrit quantum states, the semidefinite programming lower-bound hierarchy for the PPT entanglement cost terminates at the second level. Therefore, under operations preserving positive partial transpose, its zero-error asymptotic entanglement cost can be obtained by a semidefinite program involving only three Hermitian matrix variables. The key to the proof is to shorten the feasible dual chain by one level; to this end, we combine finite certificates verified by exact rational arithmetic with analytic estimates near the boundary of the parameter domain. This result shows that a unified terminating level exists for two-qutrit systems, whereas no unified terminating level exists for all dimensions.

</details>


### [117] [The wave equation and two-electron atomic wavefunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13320)
*John Lekner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要指出，三维空间中经典波动方程（classical wave equation）的轴对称解（axisymmetric solutions）与在相对固定原子核坐标下表示的双电子动能算符（two-electron kinetic energy operator）的零化函数（annihilating functions）之间存在一一对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅呈现一项数学对应关系的结论性陈述。

Method: 方法为解析性的数学构造：将经典波方程限制到轴对称情形，并与以固定原子核为原点的相对坐标（relative coordinates）下表达的双电子动能算符进行对应比较。

Result: 得到的主要结果是两类数学对象之间的一一对应（one-to-one correspondence）：三维经典波动方程的轴对称解集与双电子动能算符的零化函数集在结构上等价。

Conclusion: 该对应关系建立了经典波传播问题与量子化学中双电子动能算符零空间（null space）之间的形式联系；摘要未展开其进一步推论或应用。

Abstract: Axisymmetric solutions of the classical wave equation in three spatial dimensions are shown to be in one-to-one correspondence to functions which annihilate the two-electron kinetic energy operator expressed in coordinates relative to a fixed nucleus.

</details>


### [118] [Sample complexity of learning fermionic and bosonic Gaussian states with single-copy and entangled measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13428)
*Ron Rubin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明单拷贝（single-copy）测量下费米子高斯态（fermionic Gaussian state）学习的最优样本复杂度上界 Θ(m³/ε²)，解决 arXiv:2609.23189 的猜想 10.1，并基本补全费米子与玻色子高斯态在不同测量方案下的样本复杂度表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体测量（collective measurements）已有 Θ(m²/ε²) 的已知结果，而单拷贝测量仅知下界，其最优上界及与集体测量的差距未定；同时费米子与玻色子高斯态在单拷贝、k 拷贝与集体测量下的样本复杂度表存在大量空白。

Method: 构造单拷贝测量方案，使其噪声在各占据数尺度上与所用量度匹配；对 k 拷贝分块情形，采用协变最高权测量（covariant highest-weight measurement）并经 Weyl 位移估计器（Weyl-shift estimator）读出；玻色子情形给出以位移、压缩与占据数刻画的自适应单拷贝测量数量。

Result: 单拷贝测量以 Θ(m³/ε²) 份拷贝达到迹距离（trace distance）ε，无对数因子，为紧界；k 拷贝分块在 Frobenius 范数下为 Θ(max{m³/k, m²}/ε²)，在纯态边界固定距离处给出迹距离的 1/k 增益；玻色子有界协方差情形达到二元区间类上的极小极大率（minimax rate），无需谱隙或冷模计数假设；无能量或压缩限制时，非高斯测量以 O(ε⁻²)、O(m²/ε²)、O(m³/ε²) 的单拷贝分别学习单模、纯多模与一般 m 模态，去除 log log E 项。

Conclusion: 单拷贝与集体测量间的样本复杂度差距被确定为 Θ(m)，该文给出了相应的最优上界与非高斯测量策略，统一刻画了费米子与玻色子高斯态学习问题的复杂度图景。

Abstract: With single-copy measurements, every mixed fermionic Gaussian state on $m$ modes, pure modes included, is learned to trace distance $ε$ from $Θ(m^3/ε^2)$ copies, with no logarithmic factor, against the known $Θ(m^2/ε^2)$ for collective measurements. We prove the upper bound, resolving Conjecture 10.1 of arXiv:2609.23189 (lower bound for $ε\le1/3600$), and fill most open entries of the sample-complexity table of fermionic and bosonic Gaussian states, for single-copy, $k$-copy and collective measurements. The single-copy measurement's noise is matched to the metric at every occupation scale. With blocks of $k$ copies, the covariant highest-weight measurement of arXiv:2611.07271, read out through the Weyl-shift estimator, is exactly unbiased for every state; it gives $Θ(\max\{m^3/k,m^2\}/ε^2)$ in Frobenius norm on all states and in trace distance on every class at a fixed distance from the pure boundary. The gain $1/k$ ($k\le m$) away from the pure boundary, against $1/\sqrt k$ on cold number-conserving states, is therefore attained. For bosonic Gaussian states with bounded covariance, any displacement and squeezing, and occupations $n_1\le\dots\le n_m$, $O((m^2+r^3+\sum_{i,j>r}1/(n_i+n_j))/ε^2)$ single-copy measurements suffice, $r$ the largest index with $r\,n_r\le2$; this is the minimax rate on dyadic-interval classes, with no spectral gap or cold-mode count assumed, and holds with no covariance bound for displaced passive states. With no bound on energy or squeezing, non-Gaussian measurements learn centred one-mode states, mixed included, from $O(ε^{-2})$ and centred pure $m$-mode states from $O(m^2/ε^2)$ single copies, free of the $\log\log E$ term of Gaussian measurements, and $m$-mode states from $O(m^3/ε^2)$ copies, entangled only in a first round on blocks of $4m+6$.

</details>


### [119] [Maximal Violation of Mermin Inequality for Commuting von Neumann Algebras and Multipartite Vacuum Entanglement in QFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13981)
*Kan He,Jinchuan Hou,Shuyuan Yang,Bingke Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 Summers–Werner 关于两体 CHSH 不等式在真空态中被最大违背的判据推广至三体及 n 体情形，以多体 Mermin 不等式为对象，给出"每个正规态下最大违背"的等价刻画与充分、必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Summers 与 Werner 已证明相对论量子场论真空会最大且普遍地违背 Bell 不等式，但其判据仅针对两体情形（一个可观测量代数及其交换子（commutant）），对应的最大 CHSH 值判据为代数的强稳定性（strong stability）。多体情形（三个及以上相互交换的冯·诺依曼代数（von Neumann algebras））尚无对应结论，本文即研究该问题。

Method: 以多体 Mermin 不等式（Mermin inequalities）替代 CHSH 不等式，研究三个及以上相互交换的冯·诺依曼代数。对三体 Mermin 泛函（Mermin functional），通过渐近 GHZ 稳定子序列（asymptotic GHZ stabilizers）、局部因子的强稳定性以及无限 GHZ 张量积（GHZ tensor product）的联合嵌入等工具进行刻画，并构造反例检验各条件的独立性，结果进一步推广到 n≥3 体。

Result: 三体 Mermin 泛函在每个正规态（normal state）下的最大违背，等价于存在公共的渐近 GHZ 稳定子序列。每个局部因子的强稳定性是最大违背的必要条件，无限 GHZ 张量积的联合嵌入则是充分条件。反例表明：即便各代数分别强稳定且为 type III_1 型，并满足三体对偶性（tripartite duality），也不足以达到最大违背。

Conclusion: 两体 CHSH 判据不能简单延伸至多体情形；多体最大违背需由 GHZ 型渐近结构刻画，强稳定性与 type III_1 性质本身并不充分。相关结论可推广至 n≥3 体的一般情形。

Abstract: Summers and Werner proved that Bell inequalities are violated maximally and generically in the vacuum of a relativistic quantum field theory in [S. J. Summers and R. Werner, Commun. Math. Phys. 110 (1987), 247-259]. Their criterion concerns two-party case, that is, there are two algebras of quantum observables: one algebra and its commutant. In this case, the maximal CHSH value is converged to in every normal state exactly when the algebra is strongly stable. In the paper, we study the corresponding question for three and more mutually commuting von Neumann algebras. We focus on the multipartite Mermin inequalities instead of CHSH ones. For the tripartite Mermin functional we show that maximal violation in every normal state is equivalent to the existence of a common sequence of asymptotic GHZ stabilizers. Strong stability of each local factor is an necessary condition for maximal violation, and a joint embedding of an infinite GHZ tensor product is sufficient. Counterexamples show that strong stability and type III_1 of the individual algebras, even together with tripartite duality, do not suffice to arrive at the maximal violation. The results are also extended to n>=3 parties.

</details>


### [120] [CV-TF-QKD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14044)
*Stefano Pirandola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出连续变量版本的孪生场量子密钥分发（twin-field quantum key distribution, TF-QKD），采用独立高斯调制相干态与不可信单光子中继，实现抗集体攻击的渐近近等密钥率，并证明相位公开协议的可组合安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孪生场优势（twin-field advantage）此前主要限于离散变量体系，本文旨在将其扩展至高斯调制连续变量量子密码（Gaussian-modulated continuous-variable quantum cryptography），以突破中继无中继器（repeaterless）损耗界。

Method: 采用独立高斯调制相干态（independent Gaussian-modulated coherent states）与不可信单光子中继（untrusted single-photon relay），结合全记录协商（full-record reconciliation）与二元相位公开（binary phase-disclosure）协议；相位公开方案借助制备强度有界测试与符号奇偶互补性（sign-parity complementarity）建立针对一般相干攻击（general coherent attacks）的可组合安全性（composable security）。

Result: 在每条臂固定弱输入参考过噪声（input-referred excess noise）下，两种协议的中继单次使用密钥率均按 $\sqrt{\eta}$ 缩放（$\eta$ 为端到端透射率）。含探测器低效、暗计数、相位涨落与参考开销的有限密钥（finite-key）计算表明，在给定装置参数下密钥率超过纯损耗及匹配热损耗的中继无中继器界。

Conclusion: 结果将孪生场优势推广至高斯调制连续变量量子密码体系，表明连续变量方案在集体攻击与一般相干攻击下均可获得超越中继无中继器界的密钥率。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous-variable version of twin-field quantum key distribution, using independent Gaussian-modulated coherent states and an untrusted single-photon relay. Full-record reconciliation and a binary phase-disclosure protocol achieve nearly equal asymptotic key rates against collective attacks. At fixed weak input-referred excess noise per arm, both rates per relay use scale as $\sqrtη$, where $η$ is the end-to-end transmissivity. For phase disclosure, we establish composable security against general coherent attacks using a bounded test of preparation intensities and sign-parity complementarity. For specified apparatus parameters, finite-key calculations incorporating detector inefficiency, dark counts, phase fluctuations, and reference overhead predict rates exceeding the pure-loss and matched thermal-loss repeaterless bounds. These results extend the twin-field advantage to Gaussian-modulated continuous-variable quantum cryptography.

</details>


### [121] [Time Running Backwards, and Where It Stops: Superoscillations of Proper Time and the Memory of the Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12971)
*Yakir Aharonov,Avshalom C. Elitzur*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究“量子时间平移机”（quantum time-translation machine）即通过固有时（proper time）叠加与后选择实现弱值（weak value）放大的方案，能在多大程度上逆转不可逆过程。结论是：可逆性的障碍不是熵，而是带宽（bandwidth）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明，固有时叠加经后选择可产生时间膨胀因子的超振荡（superoscillation），使有效演化时长为 α_w t（α_w 为弱值，可大于 1、为零或为负）。本文追问这一机制能把不可逆过程“倒回”多远，并厘清限制逆转的物理根源。

Method: 构造一个封闭测试体系——处于激发态的原子及其辐射场与吸收壁作为单一量子客体整体处理——并采用投影式判据（witness）：检查后选择子系综中原本已开始衰变的原子是否比插入时更激发。理论结果仅依赖原子的存活振幅（survival amplitude），并分别分析两分支与多分支情形。

Result: 两分支情形下，判据仅在衰变时长小于环境记忆时间（memory time）时成立，此后辐射变为马尔可夫型（Markovian）；越过该界限后，后选择只能放大两分支间细胞内容的微小差异（由原子与场的全部能量展宽而非线宽决定），判据失效。要逆转超过记忆时间的衰变，需多分支、严格有界的环境谱，以及随待抵消环境振荡数指数下降的后选择概率。

Conclusion: 逆转的障碍并非熵（entropy），而是带宽（bandwidth）——正是使辐射最初不可逆的同一带宽。文末给出四种叠加时钟速率（clock rate）的方案，从空心大质量壳层到惯性速度。

Abstract: A quantum system prepared in a superposition of proper times - a clock whose worldline is superposed, or a clock at rest in a superposed gravitational potential - evolves, in each branch, by the same dynamics for a different duration. Post-selecting the control that sets the proper time turns the superposition into a superoscillation of the time-dilation factor: the system is found to have evolved for a proper time $α_w t$, where the weak value $α_w$ may exceed unity, vanish, or be negative - the quantum time-translation machine of Aharonov, Anandan, Popescu and Vaidman. We ask how far back such a machine can run an irreversible process. The test system is a sealed cell - excited atoms, the radiation field they emit into, and the absorbing walls, carried as one quantum object - and the witness is projective: atoms that had begun to decay before the run are found, in the post-selected sub-ensemble, more excited than when inserted. The answer is exact and depends only on the atoms' survival amplitude. With two branches the witness holds only while the decay is younger than the memory time of the environment, after which the emission becomes Markovian. Beyond it, the post-selection amplifies the minute difference between the contents of the cell in the two branches, set by the full energy spread of the atoms and their field rather than by the linewidth, and the witness fails. Reversing a decay past the memory time requires many branches, an environment with a strictly bounded spectrum, and a post-selection probability that falls exponentially with the number of environmental oscillations to be undone. The obstacle to reversal is therefore not entropy but bandwidth - the same bandwidth that makes the emission irreversible in the first place. We give four ways to superpose a clock rate, from hollow massive shells to inertial velocities.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum simulation of real-time bosonic dynamics with binary and unary position encodings on superconducting and trapped-ion processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13079)
*Masanori Hanada,Jiwoo Seo,Seok-Kyun Son,Junggi Yoon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作比较了位置格点的二进制（binary）与一元（unary）编码在超导（IBM）与离子阱（IonQ）硬件上模拟玻色自由度实时动力学的表现。约两百个两比特门的一元线路即可分辨对称双势阱中的隧穿，而需数千两比特门的二进制乘积公式线路无法复现该转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子比特硬件上模拟玻色自由度需离散化其位置空间，而所得格点的量子比特编码方式决定了实现实时动力学的线路结构，因此需系统比较不同编码的实用代价与可执行性。

Method: 在IBM超导与IonQ离子阱处理器上对比位置格点的二进制与一元编码：二进制编码借助量子傅里叶变换（quantum Fourier transform）实现动能项；一元编码每格点一个量子比特，势能用单比特旋转、动能用保激发交换门（excitation-conserving exchange gates）。单自由度的一元动力学属自由费米子（free-fermion）问题，其乘积公式可精确压缩为固定规模的近邻线路，并结合单激发子空间后选择（post-selection）去除泄漏。

Result: 约两百个两比特门的一元线路在两台处理器上均分辨出对称双势阱的隧穿：右阱概率在IBM上由0.97降至0.06，在IonQ上由0.96降至0.12；而需数千两比特门的二进制乘积公式线路未能复现该转移。无噪声仿真表明差异既源于编码也源于编译：缺少固定规模优化时，能捕捉该转移的一元乘积公式同样需数千两比特门。此外，由于对称格点的每个能量本征态在两阱中布居相同，实测的布居反转可为能量本征态间相干性提供下界。

Conclusion: 一元编码配合精确压缩在单自由度实时动力学模拟中显著优于二进制编码，但该优势依赖可压缩性；在寄存器间相互作用阻碍精确压缩的耦合振子（coupled-oscillator）基准中，优选编码取决于格点规模与演化时间。

Abstract: Simulating a bosonic degree of freedom on qubit hardware requires discretizing its position space, and the qubit encoding of the resulting lattice determines the circuits that realize its real-time dynamics. We compare binary and unary encodings of a position lattice on superconducting (IBM) and trapped-ion (IonQ) processors. Binary encoding applies the kinetic term through the quantum Fourier transform; unary encoding uses one qubit per site, single-qubit rotations for the potential and excitation-conserving exchange gates for the kinetic term. For a single degree of freedom the unary dynamics is a free-fermion problem, and its product formula compresses exactly into a nearest-neighbor circuit of fixed size. With about two hundred two-qubit gates, these circuits resolve tunneling in a symmetric double well on both processors, with the right-well probability falling from 0.97 to 0.06 on IBM and from 0.96 to 0.12 on IonQ, whereas binary product-formula circuits with several thousand two-qubit gates do not reproduce the transfer. Noiseless emulation indicates that this contrast reflects the compilation as well as the encoding: without the fixed-size optimization, a unary product formula that captures the transfer also requires several thousand two-qubit gates. Post-selection on the single-excitation subspace removes leakage, and because every energy eigenstate of the symmetric grid populates both wells equally, the measured population reversal bounds the coherence between energy eigenstates from below. Coupled-oscillator benchmarks, where inter-register interactions prevent this exact compression, show that the preferred encoding depends on the grid size and the evolution time.

</details>


### [123] [Time Direction Entanglement Entropy of Free Fermion Ground States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13085)
*Xinyu Sun,Bowei Liu,Joshua Yi,Biao Lian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于 Toeplitz 矩阵的 Fisher–Hartwig 猜想，为自由费米子量子态的时间方向纠缠熵（time direction entanglement entropy, TDEE）建立了解析框架，揭示其在时间长度上呈现面积律、对数律或体积律等多种标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间子区域的纠缠熵已被广泛研究，但时间方向区间内量子态的良定义纠缠熵仍缺乏探讨，其标度规律尚不清楚。

Method: 以支持时间区间内所有时间演化算符的最小子希尔伯特空间的约化密度矩阵定义 TDEE；借助 Toeplitz 矩阵的 Fisher–Hartwig 猜想构建离散时间步 τ 下的解析框架，并对一维安德森绝缘体进行数值计算。

Result: 无无序一维系统（无能隙费米子或能带绝缘体）在不同能隙、带宽与 τ 区间下，TDEE 随时间长分别呈面积律（非零能隙）、对数律或体积律；一维安德森绝缘体的 TDEE 呈类 Page 曲线行为，短时增长、超过由局域化决定的交叉时间后按幂律衰减。连续极限 τ→0 下，无能隙费米子、能带绝缘体与安德森绝缘体分别呈对数律、面积律与亚面积律。

Conclusion: 时间方向区间的子希尔伯特空间在 τ→0 极限下趋于稳定，可理解为 Lieb–Robinson 因果钻石（causal diamond）希尔伯特空间的一个子空间，为 TDEE 的几何解释提供了依据。

Abstract: While the entanglement entropy of a spatial subregion has been extensively studied, a properly defined entanglement entropy of quantum states in a time direction interval remains underexplored. We investigate the scaling behaviors of the recently defined time direction entanglement entropy (TDEE) for free fermion quantum states, which is computed from the reduced density matrix of a sub-Hilbert space that minimally supports all the time-evolved operators in the time direction interval. Based on the Fisher--Hartwig conjecture for Toeplitz matrices, we develop an analytical framework for understanding the TDEE in a time interval with discrete time step $τ$. For disorderless one-dimensional (1D) systems (gapless fermions or band insulators), in different regimes of gap, bandwidth and $τ$, we find the TDEE can exhibit area law (for nonzero gap), logarithmic law or volume law in the length of time. For 1D Anderson insulators, we numerically find the TDEE shows a Page-curve like behavior, growing at short time, and decaying in power-law at long time exceeding the crossover time determined by localization. In the continuum time limit $τ\rightarrow 0$, the TDEE of disorderless gapless fermions, band insulators and Anderson insulators exhibit logarithmic law, area law and sub-area law, respectively. We also show that the sub-Hilbert space of a time-direction interval stabilizes in the $τ\rightarrow 0$ limit, which is understandable as a subspace of the Hilbert space of the Lieb-Robinson causal diamond.

</details>


### [124] [Kinematic Quantum Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13089)
*Bernardo Barrera,Nishchhal Verma,Raquel Queiroz,Anatoli Polkovnikov,Anushya Chandran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"运动学量子几何（kinematic quantum geometry）"框架，用以描述随慢自由度坐标与速度绝热演化的快自由度，并在磁织构中的自旋粒子与石墨烯-LC电路两个体系中验证其预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Born-Oppenheimer几何（Born-Oppenheimer geometry）仅依赖坐标，无法涵盖快自由度随慢自由度速度变化的情形，因此需要扩展框架以刻画速度依赖的几何效应及其对慢自由度有效理论的修正。

Method: 引入依赖于坐标与速度的运动学量子态，构造混合Berry曲率（mixed Berry curvature），推导修正后的慢自由度有效理论；并在两个具体设置中检验：磁织构中运动的自旋粒子，以及石墨烯中狄拉克电子与LC电路的耦合。

Result: 在第一个设置中，该几何产生速度依赖的有效质量张量（effective mass tensor），并生成对速度非线性的类洛伦兹力（Lorentz-like force），即使在共面织构中也可出现；在第二个设置中，它重整化电路电容并产生对电场非线性的霍尔电流（Hall current）。小速度极限下可重现外电磁场与赝自旋织构中电子动力学的近期结果。

Conclusion: 运动学量子几何统一并推广了既有微扰结果，且只要运动学态变化足够缓慢，该框架在大速度、超出微扰区域时仍然有效。

Abstract: Kinematic quantum geometry describes fast degrees of freedom (DOFs) that adiabatically follow states parametrized by both the coordinates and the velocities of slow DOFs. It extends Born-Oppenheimer geometry, which depends on coordinates alone, introduces mixed Berry curvatures, and modifies the effective theory governing the slow DOFs. We test its predictions in two setups: spinful particles moving in magnetic textures, and Dirac electrons in graphene coupled to an LC circuit. In the first, kinematic quantum geometry produces a velocity-dependent effective mass tensor and generates a Lorentz-like force nonlinear in velocity that can arise even in coplanar textures. In the second, it renormalizes the circuit capacitance and generates a Hall current nonlinear in the electric field. For small velocities, it reproduces recent results for electronic dynamics in external electromagnetic fields and in pseudospin textures. However, the framework can remain valid at large velocities beyond the perturbative regime, provided the kinematic states are sufficiently slowly varying.

</details>


### [125] [Surface codes with coherent errors: optimal decoding and finite-size crossovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13098)
*Xu-Dong Dai,Zhou-Quan Wan,Xi Dai,Guo-Yi Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究均匀相干 Z 旋转下表面码（surface code）的有限尺寸行为，识别出弹道交叉尺度与重整化群尺度两个特征长度，并据此说明渐近解码阈值的提取困难，最终支持 θ_c=π/4 的泡利解码阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀相干 Z 旋转加完美 Syndrome 测量（syndrome measurement）的设定存在强烈有限尺寸效应，使渐近解码阈值（decoding threshold）难以可靠提取，需要澄清其标度结构。

Method: 通过识别两个特征长度刻画有限尺寸行为，并在复制 Majorana 理论（replicated Majorana theory）中导出量级为 β_R^2 的味间四体相互作用（inter-flavor quartic interaction），进而联系到 class-D 非线性 sigma 模型（nonlinear sigma model）及其边缘重整化群流（marginal RG flow）。

Result: 泡利解码度量（Pauli decoding measures）与相干信息（coherent information）呈现两个尺度：弹道交叉尺度 d_ba ~ 1/β_R^α 与更大的 d_RG ~ exp(C/β_R^4)；在 d_ba ≪ d ≪ d_RG 的中间区间出现显著有限尺寸漂移与近似平台。自由费米子模拟在标度变量 X_4=β_R^4 ln d 下显示解码度量的近似坍缩。

Conclusion: 结果与热力学极限下 θ_c=π/4 的泡利解码阈值一致；并证明对任意偶数 d，最大似然泡利解码（maximum-likelihood Pauli decoding）在所有确定性 Syndrome 条件量子恢复中最大化纠缠保真度（entanglement fidelity），其阈值达到量子最优极限并与相干信息所定义的阈值重合。

Abstract: We study the surface code under uniform coherent $Z$ rotations $\exp{(iθZ)}$ followed by perfect syndrome measurements, a setting known to exhibit strong finite-size effects that complicate the extraction of the asymptotic decoding threshold. We identify finite-size behavior of both Pauli decoding measures and the coherent information with two characteristic lengths: an algebraic ballistic-crossover scale $d_{\mathrm{ba}}\sim 1/β_R^α$ ($α=1$ for odd $d$ and $α=2$ for even $d$) and a much larger renormalization-group scale $d_\mathrm{RG}\sim \exp{(C/β^{4}_R)}$, where $β_R=-\tfrac{1}{2}\ln\tanθ$ and $d$ is the system size. As $θ$ approaches the ballistic SWAP point $π/4$, the two length scales diverge at very different rates, leaving a broad intermediate regime $d_{\mathrm{ba}}\ll d\ll d_\mathrm{RG}$ in which these quantities show substantial finite-size drift and approximate plateaus. To establish the microscopic basis of this crossover picture, we derive a leading inter-flavor quartic interaction of order $β_R^2$ in the replicated Majorana theory, which provides a route to the class-D nonlinear sigma model. The resulting marginal RG flow motivates the scaling variable $X_4=β_R^4\ln d$, in which free-fermion simulations show approximate collapse of decoding measures. These results are consistent with a Pauli-decoding threshold at $θ_c=π/4$ in the thermodynamic limit. For any even $d$, we prove that maximum-likelihood Pauli decoding maximizes entanglement fidelity over all deterministic syndrome-conditioned quantum recoveries, and that its decoding threshold attains the quantum-optimal limit, coinciding with the one defined by coherent information.

</details>


### [126] [Finite Measured Entropy Production Does Not Imply Finite Microscopic Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13110)
*Hieu Nguyen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文构建驱动量子系统，证明有限效率探测器在真空浴下观测到有界且严格为正的熵产生，而微观熵产生发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗粒化测量只给出熵产生的下界，但被隐藏的不可逆性上限尚不清楚，本文追问多少微观不可逆性可被有限测量掩盖。

Method: 采用阈下简并参量振荡器（degenerate parametric oscillator below threshold），经与玻色辅助子（bosonic ancillas）重复相互作用耦合热库；以有限收集效率、门控时间窗与独立暗计数的光子计数器定义对齐轨迹律的共同粗粒化，并解析证明相对熵的有界性。

Result: 对固定观测时长与探测器参数，观测相对熵在浴温趋零时一致有界，且在真空下严格为正；隐藏贡献满足 Σ_hid(T,T_b)=T Q̇_0/(k_B T_b)+O(1)。

Conclusion: 有限且具信息量的熵产生测量可与无限微观不可逆性共存；假阳性掩盖了支撑集失配，却保留了可测的时间不对称性。

Abstract: Coarse-grained measurements provide a lower bound on entropy production, but how much microscopic irreversibility can they conceal? We construct a driven quantum system in which an imperfect counter registers a finite, strictly positive arrow of time while the microscopic entropy production is infinite. The system is a degenerate parametric oscillator below threshold, coupled to a thermal reservoir through repeated interactions with bosonic ancillas. The oscillator sustains a finite, positive heat current into the vacuum bath, where absorption is forbidden and forward and reverse trajectory laws have different supports. A photon counter with finite collection efficiency, gated time bins, and independent dark counts defines a common coarse-graining of the aligned trajectory laws. For fixed observation duration $\mathcal T$ and detector parameters, we prove that the observed relative entropy remains uniformly bounded as the bath temperature $T_b$ vanishes and is strictly positive at vacuum. The hidden contribution obeys $Σ_{\mathrm{hid}}(\mathcal T,T_b)=\mathcal T\dot Q_0/(k_BT_b)+O(1)$, where $\dot Q_0>0$ is the stationary vacuum heat current. False positives conceal the support mismatch while preserving measurable time asymmetry. Thus, finite and informative measurements of entropy production can coexist with infinite microscopic irreversibility.

</details>


### [127] [How wide must an eavesdropper be? Leakage of a toric-code logical bit to a strip and the Nishimori point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13174)
*Konstantinos Karagiannis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明：对任意区域与任意泡利噪声，观察者获知逻辑比特的最优优势恰为 1 − 2P_fail（P_fail 为该区域受限最大似然解码的失败概率），并据此给出容错量子计算中分区解码与复用读出线路的信息泄露几何标度规律与设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算机将每个逻辑量子比特的各个部分分发给独立解码器模块并共享读出线路，局部信息暴露的多少直接关系安全性。既有直觉认为噪声提供保护、宽度决定风险，但缺少严格的定量刻画。

Method: 建立观察者最优优势与区域受限最大似然解码失败概率之间的精确恒等式；以随机键 Ising 模型（random-bond Ising model）的 Nishimori 点刻画窄条的操作阈值，并用宽度达 16 的 Walsh-Hadamard 转移矩阵（transfer matrix）数值定位阈值；在 d = 3、5 的两台 IBM Heron 处理器上进行模拟条带攻击者实验。

Result: 保护来自几何而非噪声：缺失单列的区域在任意噪声下学不到任何信息；跨码区域仅在 d > 350（10^-3 翻转率单行）这类不现实距离下受噪声保护。宽度并非风险的良好指标：i.i.d. 噪声下两行条带与单行泄露相同，而阈下窃听者所需宽度仅按 ln d 增长。硬件上观察到宽度三处的预测跃变，非均匀比特错误使训练后的攻击者能从均匀噪声下无用的第二行获益。

Conclusion: 由恒等式将泄露问题转化为解码阈值与统计力学模型问题，所得规律被提炼为分区解码（partitioned decoding）与复用读出（multiplexed readout）的设计准则。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computers hand pieces of each logical qubit to separate decoder modules and shared readout lines. How much does a piece reveal? We prove that for any region and any Pauli noise, including correlated noise, an observer's optimal advantage in learning a logical bit is exactly 1 - 2 P_fail, where P_fail is the failure probability of maximum-likelihood decoding confined to that region. Two lessons follow. Geometry, not noise, provides the protection: a region that misses a single column of the code learns exactly nothing at any noise level, while a region that spans the code is protected by noise only at impractical distances (d > 350 for one row at a 10^-3 flip rate). And width is a poor guide to risk: under i.i.d. noise a two-row strip learns exactly as little as one row, yet below threshold an eavesdropper needs a width growing only as ln d. Through the identity, the strip's operational threshold is the Nishimori point of the random-bond Ising model, and a Walsh-Hadamard transfer matrix up to width 16 locates it to within a few thousandths. On two IBM Heron processors (d = 3, 5), an emulated strip adversary shows the predicted jump at width three, and non-uniform qubit errors let a trained attacker profit from the second row that uniform noise makes useless. We turn these results into design rules for partitioned decoding and multiplexed readout. Code and data are included.

</details>


### [128] [Dynamical Casimir emission from Floquet-driven Landau polaritons: Gauge invariance forbids a superradiant phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14339)
*S. Hughes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立周期驱动 Landau 极化激元的精确理论，证明磁调制不会产生 Floquet 超辐射相变（SRPT），真正的物理效应是动力学 Casimir 效应，可产生双模压缩光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有预测认为仅调制磁场即可增强周期平均耦合并诱发 Floquet SRPT，但该预测可能忽略了规范不变性与磁通调制感应电场的作用，需要严格检验。

Method: 计入磁通调制感应的电场（法拉第定律），借助 Kohn 定理将问题精确约化为电子质心模式与腔模耦合，任一时刻哈密顿量为平方和；用 Krein 定理及自包含证明给出稳定性的频率阈值（Krein 频率），并推导广义 TRK 求和规则与规范定理。

Result: 高于 Krein 频率时系统全阶稳定；低于它仅在共振舌（resonance tongues）内失稳，源于动力学 Casimir 效应；高频下电子感受到平均场加有质动力势阱（Fock–Darwin 问题）而非增强耦合；规范定理排除任何能带结构（含 Kane 非抛物性）下的 Floquet 光子凝聚。

Conclusion: 磁调制不产生 SRPT，而是产生动力学 Casimir 发射：低阈值下 Faraday 驱动的极化激元–磁等离激元对，以及具 5–10 dB 双模压缩的双色光子对；应以最小耦合哈密顿量本身而非其投影参数进行调制与平均。

Abstract: Ultrastrong light--matter coupling of linear systems, such as Landau polaritons formed by a two-dimensional electron gas in a terahertz cavity, is described by the bosonic Hopfield model, and gauge invariance forbids a superradiant phase transition (SRPT). Modulating the magnetic field alone has been predicted to enhance the cycle-averaged coupling and induce a Floquet SRPT. We develop an exact theory of periodically driven Landau polaritons. Including the electric field induced by the modulated flux (Faraday's law), the problem reduces exactly, by Kohn's theorem, to the electronic center-of-mass mode coupled to the cavity, whose Hamiltonian is a sum of squares at every instant. A theorem of Krein then guarantees stability to all orders above a computable drive frequency, the Krein frequency, for which we give a self-contained proof. Below it, the system is stable except within resonance tongues where the drive creates pairs from the vacuum, the dynamical Casimir effect. At high drive frequency the electrons experience the mean field plus a ponderomotive trap (the Fock--Darwin problem), not an enhanced coupling. A gauge theorem excludes a Floquet photon condensate for any band structure, including Kane nonparabolicity, for which we derive a generalized Thomas--Reiche--Kuhn (TRK) sum rule. The predicted transition arises from averaging the coupling in a fixed Landau-level basis, which yields an effective model that violates the TRK sum rule; under driving, gauge invariance also requires the induced Faraday field. The rule to modulate and average the minimal-coupling Hamiltonian, not the parameters of its projection, applies to any linear, minimally coupled system. Instead of an SRPT, magnetic modulation produces dynamical Casimir emission: Faraday-driven polariton--magnetoplasmon pairs at low threshold, and two-color photon pairs with up to 5--10~dB of two-mode squeezing.

</details>

### [129] [Everything in its right place: attention is a radio head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13146)
*Lluís Arola-Fernández,Aissam Khadraoui,Alex Arenas*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出注意力可从接收器与上下文标记的同步中涌现：将过去标记视为频率随年龄漂移的振荡器，当前标记通过内容对齐"天线"、通过位置设定"接收器频率"，求解Kuramoto问题得到闭式注意力层，含锁定与静默两种相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力被视为对标记的数字式计算（指数选择、除法归一化、位置编码排序），作者希望用振荡器同步这一动力学视角重新解释并重构注意力机制。

Method: 将每个过去标记建模为状态携带内容、频率随年龄漂移的振荡器；当前标记按内容（查询对齐天线）与位置（旋钮设定接收频率）选择它们，并对该接收器求解Kuramoto问题，得到闭式（closed form）的注意力层。

Result: 该层存在由上下文决定的临界耦合（critical coupling）：高于它时接收器锁定且权重归一化，低于它时保持静默且权重和小于一；训练后的网络利用两种相完成选择、弃权（abstain）与计数，并在理论预测处失效，且能在远超训练长度的范围内继续读取。

Conclusion: 注意力的各部分被统一到一个"无线电头（radio head）"装置中：调谐听到什么与何时听到，无相关内容时保持静默。

Abstract: Attention, the core of language models, is a digital calculation over tokens: an exponential selects them, a division normalizes and a positional code sets the order. We show that attention can instead emerge from the synchronization of a receiver with its context tokens. Each past token is an oscillator whose state carries its content and whose frequency drifts with its age. The present token selects them by content, aligning an antenna with its query, and by position, setting the frequency of a receiver with its dial. Solving Kuramoto's problem for this receiver yields an attention layer in closed form with two phases: above a critical coupling set by the context, the receiver locks and its weights normalize; below it, it stays silent and its weights sum to less than one. Trained networks use both phases to select, abstain and count, fail where the theory predicts and keep reading far beyond the training length. Each part of attention finds its place in one device, a radio head: it tunes in to what to hear and when, and stays silent when nothing relevant is on air.

</details>
