### [130] [Strong decays of $ψ(3770)$, $ψ(4040)$ and $ψ(4160)$ in four relativistic wave-function representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13915)
*Wen-Yuan Ke,Wan-Li Ju,Tianhong Wang,Qiang Li,Guo-Li Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在 Salpeter 方程框架下，采用四种相对论波函数表示与修正的 ${}^3P_0$ 模型，研究 $ψ(3770)$、$ψ(4040)$、$ψ(4160)$ 的 OZI 允许强衰变；仅用 $ψ(3770)\to D\bar D$ 宽度定标夸克对产生强度，$\varphi_2$ 表示对两个更高态的预言与实验一致，表明波函数需独立包含 $S$、$P$、$D$ 分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强衰变可直接检验重夸克偶素（heavy quarkonium）波函数的相对论结构，因此需要一个能区分不同相对论波函数表示的定量判据。

Method: 采用 Salpeter 方程给出四种相对论波函数表示 $\varphi_1$–$\varphi_4$（在独立保留的 $S$、$P$、$D$ 波分量上有所差别），配合修正的 ${}^3P_0$ 模型计算 OZI 允许强衰变；夸克对产生强度由 $ψ(3770)\to D\bar D$ 宽度一次性确定，此后不再调整。

Result: $\varphi_2$ 预言 $ψ(4040)$ 宽度 $94^{+34}_{-23}$ MeV、$ψ(4160)$ 宽度 $100^{+23}_{-20}$ MeV，与实验值 $84.5\pm12.3$ 与 $69\pm10$ MeV 相符，且是唯一给出与数据相容的宽度比的表示；$\varphi_1$ 对 $ψ(4040)$ 超出 $2.5σ$，$\varphi_3$ 给出最大宽度并在 $5σ$ 水平被排除，$\varphi_4$ 因缺少独立 $S$ 波分量而低估 $ψ(3770)$ 约 $2σ$ 且无法用于含矢量介子的末态。

Conclusion: 成功的波函数表示须独立包含 $S$、$P$、$D$ 分量，其中 $P$ 波项提供主要的相对论修正；波函数的节点结构（nodal structure）是预言大不确定度的来源。由 $\varphi_2$ 提取的混合角（mixing angle）$7.92^{\circ}$、$25.8^{\circ}$、$26.3^{\circ}$ 与耦合道（coupled-channel）模型结果一致。

Abstract: Strong decays provide a direct test of the relativistic structure of heavy quarkonium wave functions. We study the OZI-allowed hadronic decays of the vector charmonia $ψ(3770)$, $ψ(4040)$ and $ψ(4160)$ within the Salpeter equation, using four relativistic wave-function representations $\varphi_1$--$\varphi_4$ that differ in the $S$-, $P$- and $D$-wave components they retain independently, and the revised ${}^3P_0$ model. The quark-pair creation strength is fixed once, by reproducing the measured $ψ(3770)\to D\bar D$ width, and is not readjusted afterwards, so that the results for the two higher states are parameter-free predictions. The representation $\varphi_2$ predicts $94^{+34}_{-23}$~MeV for $ψ(4040)$ and $100^{+23}_{-20}$~MeV for $ψ(4160)$, in agreement with the measured $84.5\pm12.3$ and $69\pm10$~MeV, and it is the only one whose predicted width ratios are compatible with the data. By contrast, $\varphi_1$ overshoots $ψ(4040)$ by $2.5σ$, $\varphi_3$ gives the largest widths and is disfavoured at the $5σ$ level, and $\varphi_4$, which has no independent $S$-wave component, underestimates $ψ(3770)$ by about $2σ$ and cannot be applied to final states containing vector mesons. These results show that a successful representation must contain independent $S$, $P$ and $D$ components, the $P$-wave terms providing the dominant relativistic correction, while the nodal structure of the wave functions accounts for the large uncertainties of the predictions. The mixing angles extracted from $\varphi_2$, $7.92^{\circ}$, $25.8^{\circ}$ and $26.3^{\circ}$, agree with the values obtained in coupled-channel models.

</details>


### [131] [Thermal damping of spectator scalar dark matter: Relaxing the overclosure and isocurvature bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13080)
*Erwin H. Tanin,Sarunas Verner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 轻标量场在暴胀期间获得错位量并可充当暗物质，但会携带等曲率扰动；弱耦合至原初等离子体可通过热阻尼同时放松过闭合与等曲率限制，将暴胀能标上限放宽与丰度降低相同的因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最简单的"暴胀期轻标量错位暗物质"方案在高暴胀能标下被等曲率扰动（isocurvature perturbations）与过闭合（overclosure）两类限制联合排除，作者希望在不放弃该基本图像的前提下寻找可行出路。

Method: 引入标量场与原初等离子体（primordial plasma）的弱耦合，利用等离子体有限平衡时间（finite equilibration time）导致的热阻尼（thermal damping）耗散标量丰度；并分析振幅二次型阻尼率（damping rate quadratic in the amplitude）所带来的吸引子行为。

Result: 热阻尼降低标量丰度从而放松过闭合限制；当阻尼随振荡振幅增长时，不同初始振幅被驱动至仅依赖局部辐射温度的同一吸引子（attractor），使标量与绝热时钟（adiabatic clock）同步并抑制等曲率。最终暴胀能标（inflationary scale）的等曲率上限恰好按阻尼降低丰度的同一因子放宽，另加初始错位量增大带来的额外放宽。

Conclusion: 该机制对任何振幅二次型阻尼率均成立，且与其温度依赖性无关，说明弱等离子体耦合是挽救高能标错位暗物质方案的一般性途径。

Abstract: A light scalar present during inflation generically acquires a misalignment and can later constitute the dark matter. Since it fluctuates independently of the inflaton, however, it carries isocurvature perturbations. Isocurvature and overclosure bounds together rule out the simplest version of this scenario at high inflationary scales. We show that a weak coupling to the primordial plasma can relax both bounds simultaneously. The plasma depletes the scalar abundance through thermal damping, a dissipative effect arising from the plasma's finite equilibration time, thereby relaxing the overclosure bound. If the damping grows with the oscillation amplitude, it can also drive different initial amplitudes onto a common attractor that depends only on the local radiation temperature. This synchronizes the scalar with the adiabatic clock, thus suppressing its isocurvature. The resulting isocurvature bound on the inflationary scale relaxes by exactly the factor by which damping reduces the abundance, in addition to the relaxation from the larger initial misalignment now allowed. This result holds for any damping rate quadratic in the amplitude, regardless of its temperature dependence.

</details>


### [132] [Galaxy clusters as cosmic lenses for high frequency gravitational-waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13105)
*Francesca Lecce,Francisco R. Candòn,Alessandro Lella,Alessandro Mirizzi,Julia K. Vogel,Tiziano Zanzarella*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出利用星系团（Galaxy Clusters）延展磁场的“宇宙透镜（cosmic lenses）”效应增强引力子-光子转换（graviton-photon conversion），并基于室女座星系团（Virgo Cluster）的X射线与γ射线观测，给出高频引力波（HFGW）随机背景幅度在10^17–10^22 Hz频段的新上限，较银河系磁场给出的限制严格约1–2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频引力波（f ≳ 1 MHz）可探测新物理，但缺乏针对该频段的专用探测器，因此依赖天体物理磁场中引力子-光子转换的间接探测途径具有重要意义；已有基于银河系磁场的限制仍有提升空间。

Method: 将弥漫于星系团的延展磁场视为宇宙透镜，用以显著增强引力子-光子转换；并结合大视场仪器（如eROSITA与COMPTEL）对室女座星系团的X射线和γ射线观测来施加限制。

Result: 获得对10^17–10^22 Hz频段随机高频引力波背景幅度的新限制，相比由银河系磁场导出的约束改善约一到两个数量级。

Conclusion: 星系团磁场可作为增强引力子-光子转换的有效宇宙透镜，星系团的多波段观测为高频引力波背景提供了更强有力的间接限制。

Abstract: High-frequency Gravitational Waves (HFGW)($f \gtrsim 1$ MHz) offer a distinctive probe of exotic physics. In the absence of dedicated detectors targeting this frequency range, indirect probes based on graviton-photon conversions in astrophysical magnetic fields become particularly relevant. In this work we show that the extended magnetic fields permeating Galaxy Clusters may act as cosmic lenses, leading to a strong enhancement of graviton-photon conversions. In this context, X- and $γ$-ray observations of the Virgo Cluster through large field-of-view instruments such as eROSITA and COMPTEL yield novel limits on the amplitude of stochastic HFGW backgrounds in the frequency range $10^{17}-10^{22}$ Hz, improving upon constraints derived from the Galactic magnetic field by one to two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [133] [Comment to: "Rewording Theoretical Predictions at Colliders with Vacuum Amplitudes"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13087)
*Zeno Capatti,Valentin Hirschi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本评论（comment）指出 Ramírez-Uribe 等人发表于 Phys. Rev. Lett. 133, 211901 (2024) 的论文中两项核心结果存在缺陷：其一，微分散射截面（differential scattering cross-section）的被积函数不能仅由真空振幅（vacuum amplitude）的单一"留数（residue）"给出；其二，非夹挤阈（non-pinched threshold）在该微分表示中并非总在局部匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对原论文关于微分散射截面与真空振幅留数关系、以及阈值匹配性的论断提出反证，厘清初末态区分所需的数学结构。

Method: 以评论文体（comment）形式，对原论文两项论断逐一进行概念性反驳：指出须考虑两次迭代的不连续性（two iterated discontinuities）方能区分初态与末态；并否定非夹挤阈的局部匹配结论。

Result: 原论文的两项核心结论均被判定为不成立：单一留数不足以给出微分截面被积函数，非夹挤阈的局部匹配亦不普遍成立。

Conclusion: 该评论对原论文（arXiv:2404.05491）的核心论断提出实质性质疑，提示其微分表示（differential representation）框架需重新审视。

Abstract: This comment highlights flaws in two central results presented by Ramírez-Uribe et al. in the titular letter, Phys. Rev. Lett. 133, 211901 (2024) [arXiv:2404.05491]. Firstly, the authors incorrectly claim that integrands of differential scattering cross-sections are obtained as single "residues" of vacuum amplitudes, although a sharp distinction of initial and final states requires to consider two iterated discontinuities. Secondly, they wrongly state that non-pinched thresholds always locally match in such a differential representation.

</details>


### [134] [Off-shell continuation from on-shell recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13096)
*Wenqi Ke,Zhen Liu,Ishmam Mahbub*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文从在壳因子化与内部大质量矢量极化求和出发，给出横向与纵向贡献在任意虚度下的离壳延拓，并发展出分离纵向极化质量奇异项的方法，从而提供一种自下而上构造无质量奇异大质量振幅的系统途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有大质量矢量振幅描述（如费曼图规范及五分量表述）依赖图级别的高能幂次计数与场分解，缺乏基于在壳振幅的独立理解；纵向极化在无质量极限下的幂律奇异项也需系统处理。

Method: 基于在壳可构造性（on-shell constructibility）与三点离壳流约束 p·J = O(m)，对横向与纵向贡献做离壳延拓；针对含外线纵向矢量的振幅，构造分离纵向极化质量奇异项（mass-singular terms）的方法，并考察其在各因子化通道中以接触项形式出现及抵消的条件。

Result: 在研究所涉示例中，质量奇异项表现为各因子化通道内的接触贡献，并给出了其抵消条件；同时以四点实例说明更高点流约束如何由在壳可构造性与质量奇异抵消产生，并讨论光滑无质量极限施加的限制。

Conclusion: 该工作为构造无质量奇异项的大质量振幅提供了系统的自下而上方法，并给出基于大质量在壳振幅对极化分解与逐图高能幂次计数的独立理解，为连接大质量与无质量描述、在壳态与离壳描述提供路径。

Abstract: Starting from on-shell factorization and the polarization sum of an internal massive vector, we provide an off-shell continuation of the transverse and longitudinal contributions defined at arbitrary virtuality. The construction relies on on-shell constructibility and the three-point off-shell current constraint $p \cdot J = O(m)$. For amplitudes with external longitudinal vectors, we develop a method that isolates the mass-singular contributions associated with the longitudinal polarization, namely terms with power-law singularities in the massless limit. In the examples studied, these terms appear as contact contributions in individual factorization channels, and we identify the conditions under which they cancel. We give an independent understanding, based on massive on-shell amplitudes, of the polarization decomposition and diagram-by-diagram high-energy power counting underlying the Feynman-Diagram gauge and related five-component formulations. We further clarify how higher-point current constraints can emerge from on-shell constructibility and the cancellation of mass singularities. We illustrate the mechanism with explicit four-point examples and discuss the constraints imposed by a smooth massless limit. The work provides a systematic bottom-up method for building massive amplitudes free of mass-singular terms and opens a route toward a broader amplitude-based understanding of field decompositions, connecting massive and massless descriptions and on-shell states with off-shell descriptions.

</details>


### [135] [A dual-moment framework toward unification of the four fundamental interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13418)
*Zhiguang Tan,Shengjie Wang,Hua Zheng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一个基于“原始荷与运动诱导动力学矩”的最小唯象双矩框架，用统一的平方反比律形式描述引力、电磁与强相互作用，并通过普适耦合关系 T = Z/c² 连接各类耦合系数，同时给出三类可检验预言与实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四种基本相互作用（fundamental interactions）的统一仍是基础物理的核心未解问题；现有统一方案多依赖额外空间维度（extra spatial dimensions）、新对称结构或未被观测的自由度，因此本文寻求一种不依赖这些假设的最小化唯象替代路径。

Method: 构建以原始荷（primitive charges）与运动诱导动力学矩（motion-induced kinetic moments）为基础的双矩（dual-moment）唯象框架，在统一平方反比律（inverse-square-law）形式下自洽描述引力、电磁与强相互作用，并假设跨扇区（sector）的普适耦合关系 T = Z/c²；弱相互作用（weak interaction）仅作形式上的容纳，定量计算留待后续研究。

Result: 模型复现了行星轨道、原子系统与重夸克强子（heavy-quark hadrons）的既有低能动力学极限，并预言对快速自转致密天体（rapidly spinning compact objects）所致光线偏折（light deflection）的可区分修正；同时设计了三类独立检验方案：改进的扭秤（torsion-balance）测量、质量流实验室偏折实验与天文光线弯折观测。

Conclusion: 实验室测量面临严峻技术挑战，而针对致密自转天体的天文观测为约束或否证（falsify）该框架提供了可行途径；弱相互作用的完整定量处理被明确列为后续工作。

Abstract: The unification of the four fundamental interactions remains one of the central open challenges in fundamental physics. Most existing unification scenarios rely on extra spatial dimensions, novel symmetry structures, or unobserved dynamical degrees of freedom. In this work, we construct a minimal phenomenological dual-moment framework rooted in primitive charges and motion-induced kinetic moments. Gravitational, electromagnetic, and strong interactions are coherently described within a unified inverse-square-law formalism. We postulate a universal coupling relation $T = Z/c^2$ that connects source-charge coupling coefficients and moment-mediated coupling coefficients across different interaction sectors. While the framework can formally accommodate weak-charge degrees of freedom, full quantitative computations for weak-interaction dynamics are deferred to future follow-up studies. The model reproduces established low-energy dynamical limits for planetary orbits, atomic systems, and heavy-quark hadrons, and predicts distinguishable corrections to light deflection by rapidly spinning compact objects. We further design three independent test schemes: improved torsion-balance measurements, a mass-current laboratory deflection experiment, and astronomical light-bending observations. Although laboratory measurements impose formidable technical challenges, astronomical observations of compact spinning objects offer promising avenues to constrain or falsify the proposed framework.

</details>


### [136] [The KLN theorem meets collinear factorisation: Local Unitarity for hadronic cross-sections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13184)
*Zeno Capatti,Lucien Huber*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与共线因子化（collinear factorisation）相容的初态 KLN 定理（Kinoshita-Lee-Nauenberg theorem）实现方案，使部分子截面（partonic cross-sections）按构造红外有限（infrared finite），并可在四维下直接蒙特卡洛积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统做法依赖维度正规化（dimensional regularisation）作为红外调节器来获得有限截面；本文旨在构建不依赖该调节器、且与共线因子化相容的初态 KLN 方案。

Method: 将强子截面（hadronic cross-sections）表示为部分子分布函数（parton distribution functions）与对入射部分子数目求和的各阶部分子截面的卷积；借助 Local Unitarity 形式实现红外有限性的局域化，从而在四维中计算，并用 Reverse Unitarity 作解析计算。

Result: 在次领头阶（next-to-leading order）与领头幂次（leading power）下验证框架：用 Local Unitarity 数值计算 Drell-Yan 与 t\bar{t} 产生截面；用 Reverse Unitarity 解析得到深度非弹性散射（deep inelastic scattering）与 Drell-Yan 截面。所有结果均可通过普适的方案转换（scheme conversion）与 \overline{MS} 部分子模型结果对应。

Conclusion: 该初态 KLN 实现可在四维下直接得到红外有限的部分子截面，并与 \overline{MS} 部分子模型通过普适方案转换相联系，为无维度正规化的截面计算提供了可行框架。

Abstract: We present an initial-state implementation of the Kinoshita-Lee-Nauenberg (KLN) theorem that is compatible with collinear factorisation. In this formulation, hadronic cross-sections are expressed as convolutions of parton distribution functions with partonic cross-sections obtained by summing over the number of incoming partons. By virtue of the KLN theorem, the partonic cross-sections are infrared finite by construction, without relying on dimensional regularisation as an infrared regulator. The Local Unitarity formalism provides a local realisation of this infrared finiteness, allowing partonic cross-sections to be evaluated directly in four dimensions by Monte Carlo integration. We demonstrate the framework at next-to-leading order and leading power. Using Local Unitarity, we compute the Drell-Yan and $t\bar{t}$ production cross-sections numerically; using Reverse Unitarity, we obtain the deep inelastic scattering and Drell-Yan cross-sections analytically. In all cases, a universal scheme conversion relates our results to those obtained in the $\overline{\text{MS}}$ parton model.

</details>


### [137] [Thermal $CP$ dynamics in a four-fermion model: mean-field and functional renormalization group analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13405)
*Arthur F. Vieira,Rudnei O. Ramos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有限温度、零化学势下研究含标量、赝标量及混合标量-赝标量相互作用的一味四费米子模型（one-flavor four-fermion model），结合平均场近似（mean-field approximation）与泛函重整化群（functional renormalization group, FRG），阐明热涨落在抑制自发对称破缺与费米子不稳定性中的不同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清有限温度下赝标量凝聚（pseudoscalar condensate）所导致的自发 P/CP 对称破缺行为，以及混合标量-赝标量相互作用与费米子质量相（fermion mass phase）在标度演化中的表现。

Method: 采用平均场近似确定平衡态凝聚，并借助泛函重整化群描述相互作用与费米子质量相的标度依赖性（scale dependence）。

Result: 平均场层面，在无显式对称破缺源时，主导相互作用决定凝聚方向；赝标量凝聚自发破坏普通 P/CP 对称性，并通过连续热相变（continuous thermal phase transition）消失。正则高温 FRG 轨迹下，无量纲混合耦合在红外趋于零，而有量纲对应量保持有限；温度升高亦抑制费米子质量相的标度演化。

Conclusion: 结果表明，热涨落（thermal fluctuations）对自发对称破缺与费米子不稳定性的抑制具有不同角色，但并不必然消除显式对称破缺（explicit symmetry breaking）效应。

Abstract: We investigate a one-flavor four-fermion model with scalar, pseudoscalar, and mixed scalar-pseudoscalar interactions at finite temperature and vanishing chemical potential. Our analysis combines the mean-field approximation, which determines the equilibrium condensates, with the functional renormalization group (FRG), which describes the scale dependence of the interactions and the fermion mass phase. Within the mean-field approach, we find that, in the absence of explicit symmetry-breaking sources, the dominant interaction determines the direction of condensation. In particular, a pseudoscalar condensate spontaneously breaks the ordinary $P/CP$ symmetry and disappears through a continuous thermal phase transition. For regular high-temperature FRG trajectories, the dimensionless mixed coupling tends to zero in the infrared, while its dimensionful counterpart remains finite. Increasing temperature also suppresses the scale evolution of the fermion mass phase. Together, these results clarify the distinct roles of thermal fluctuations in suppressing spontaneous symmetry breaking and fermionic instabilities, without necessarily eliminating explicit symmetry breaking effects.

</details>


### [138] [Three Dimensional Structure of Pion with One Dynamical Gluon in the Light-Cone Quark Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13805)
*Satyajit Puhan,Shubham Sharma,Narinder Kumar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在光锥夸克模型（light-cone quark model）中引入动力学胶子（dynamical gluon），将π介子波函数扩展为|q q̄⟩+|q q̄g⟩两成分形式，系统计算了π介子的GPDs、TMDs与PDFs，并用Drell-Yan、J/ψ、Υ及HERA leading-neutron F₂^π数据检验。q q̄g成分对Drell-Yan贡献微弱，但对胶子敏感观测量（如J/ψ产生与小x处结构函数）十分重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越领头Fock态（leading Fock state）的π介子描述是理解其三维结构的关键，而仅含价夸克成分的模型无法刻画胶子敏感的过程，因此需要纳入动力学胶子自由度。

Method: 在光锥夸克模型框架下，通过光前波函数重叠（light-front wave function overlap）计算π介子GPDs、TMDs与PDFs；价夸克部分采用带Melosh-Wigner旋转的改进Brodsky-Huang-Lepage波函数，q q̄g部分采用光前夸克-胶子顶点。

Result: 无需额外约束即满足夸克数求和规则与动量求和规则；进一步得到电磁形状因子（electromagnetic form factor）与质量分解（mass decomposition）。经NLO演化后与π介子诱导的Drell-Yan、J/ψ、Υ及HERA leading-neutron F₂^π数据比较，q q̄g成分对Drell-Yan贡献很小，但对J/ψ产生和小x处π介子结构函数等胶子敏感观测量显著重要。

Conclusion: 在π介子波函数中显式包含动力学胶子成分是必要的，该成分虽对部分过程贡献有限，却对胶子敏感观测量起关键作用，为统一描述π介子三维结构提供了自洽框架。

Abstract: We study the three-dimensional structure of the pion beyond the leading Fock state by including a dynamical gluon, $|π\rangle=|q\bar q\rangle+|q\bar q g\rangle$. Within the light-cone quark model, we calculate the pion GPDs, TMDs, and PDFs from light-front wave function overlaps, using a modified Brodsky--Huang--Lepage wave function with the Melosh--Wigner rotation for the valence sector and the light-front quark--gluon vertex for the $q\bar q g$ sector. The quark-number and momentum sum rules are satisfied without additional constraints. We further obtain the electromagnetic form factor and the mass decomposition. After NLO evolution, we compare the PDFs with pion-induced Drell--Yan, $J/ψ$, $Υ$, and HERA leading-neutron $F_2^π$ data. The $q\bar q g$ sector contributes little to Drell--Yan but becomes important for gluon-sensitive observables such as $J/ψ$ production and the pion structure function at small $x$.

</details>


### [139] [The Higgs boson, naturalness, and the Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14370)
*Mikhail Shaposhnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该综述考察希格斯场（Higgs field）在宇宙从最早时刻到现今状态中的可能作用，横跨粒子物理与宇宙学两个领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯玻色子（Higgs boson）是唯一已知的基本标量粒子（fundamental scalar particle），可能对粒子物理与宇宙学产生深远影响，因此有必要系统梳理其在宇宙演化中的角色。

Method: 采用综述（review）方式，先讨论粒子物理疑难，再转向希格斯玻色子可能充当驱动力的宇宙学情景。

Result: 粒子物理方面涵盖自然性（naturalness）与等级问题（hierarchy problem），以及电弱真空（electroweak vacuum）可能存在的亚稳性（metastability）；宇宙学方面涵盖希格斯暴胀（Higgs inflation）、重加热（reheating）与热大爆炸（hot Big Bang）的开启、电弱相变（electroweak phase transition），以及通过电弱过程实现的量子生成（baryogenesis）。

Conclusion: 希格斯场被置于粒子物理疑难与早期宇宙演化的统一框架中加以审视，其性质对理解宇宙起源与现状具有关键意义。

Abstract: The Higgs boson -- the only known fundamental scalar particle -- may have profound implications for both particle physics and cosmology. This review examines how the Higgs field may have influenced the Universe from its earliest moments to its present state. We first discuss particle-physics puzzles, including the naturalness and hierarchy problems, as well as the possible metastability of the electroweak vacuum. We then turn to cosmological scenarios where the Higgs boson could be a driving force, including Higgs inflation, reheating and the onset of the hot Big Bang, the electroweak phase transition, and baryogenesis via electroweak processes.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Quantum Control Geometry as a Metric of Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13101)
*Mahmoud Abdelshafy,Joaquin F. Rodriguez-Nieva*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个量子几何框架（quantum geometric framework），通过量化多体本征态对物理允许的哈密顿量形变的敏感性，给出超越二元判据的混沌度量，并论证多种混沌特征实为同一底层几何结构的不同表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子混沌系统（quantum-chaotic systems）虽共享随机矩阵（random matrices）的粗粒化特征，如Wigner-Dyson能级间距统计，但在本征态结构与多体动力学上差异显著。核心问题是：这些更细粒度的特征究竟探测量子混沌的不同侧面，还是同一底层结构的不同表现；以及是否存在有意义的依据判定某系统"更混沌"。

Method: 引入量子几何框架，量化多体本征态对哈密顿量中物理允许形变的敏感性，该形变受空间局域性（spatial locality）、对称性（symmetries）与守恒律（conservation laws）约束。所得的几何敏感性（geometric susceptibility）具备定量性、内禀性（依赖允许形变流形而非探测方式）与统一性（连接本征态结构与动力学）。

Result: 在量子混沌的典型模型中，最小几何敏感性、最大本征态随机性、局域算符的宽谱响应以及非流体力学慢结构的抑制，均在哈密顿量控制空间的同一区域汇聚。

Conclusion: 这些看似不同的混沌标志可能是同一底层几何结构的不同表现。该框架为理解物理约束下弛豫（relaxation）与扰乱（scrambling）的极限提供路径，并可用于在控制受限的可编程量子系统中工程化强混沌区域。

Abstract: Quantum-chaotic systems share the same coarse-grained features characteristic of random matrices, such as Wigner-Dyson level-spacing statistics, yet can exhibit strikingly different eigenstate structure and many-body dynamics, including relaxation, transport, scrambling, and entanglement growth. This raises the fundamental question of whether these finer-grained features probe genuinely distinct aspects of quantum chaos or, instead, reflect different manifestations of a common underlying structure---and whether such structure provides a meaningful basis for regarding one many-body quantum system as "more chaotic" than another. Here, we address this question by introducing a quantum geometric framework that quantifies the susceptibility of many-body eigenstates to physically allowed deformations of the Hamiltonian, as constrained by spatial locality, symmetries, and conservation laws. The resulting geometric susceptibility is quantitative, providing a metric of chaos beyond traditional binary diagnostics; intrinsic, as it depends on the manifold of allowed Hamiltonian deformations rather than on the way the system is probed; and unifying, as it directly connects eigenstate structure with dynamics. For canonical models of quantum chaos, we find that minimal geometric susceptibility, maximal eigenstate randomness, broad spectral response of local operators, and the suppression of nonhydrodynamic slow structure coalesce in the same region of Hamiltonian control space. This suggests that these seemingly distinct signatures of chaos are different manifestations of a common underlying geometric structure. Our framework provides a route toward understanding the limits of relaxation and scrambling under physical constraints; in applications, it offers a means to engineer strongly chaotic regimes in programmable quantum systems with restricted controls.

</details>


### [141] [Dynamics of Rényi entanglement entropies and Eigenstate Thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13141)
*Riccardo Senese,Pasquale Calabrese*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究确定性混沌 Floquet（Floquet）模型中纠缠动力学如何编码于时间演化算符的本征态，借助复制希尔伯特空间（replicated Hilbert space）中的谱表示与 ETH 类拟设，给出 Rényi-2 纯度（purity）动力学的解析刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是纠缠动力学如何在时间演化算符的本征态中被编码；受本征态热化假说（eigenstate thermalization hypothesis, ETH）启发，试图为确定性混沌系统中的纠缠增长建立类似 ETH 的谱层面基础。

Method: 采用约化密度矩阵矩（纯度和高阶推广）的谱表示，其经谱退相位（spectral dephasing）导致非平衡纠缠增长；在复制希尔伯特空间中以子系统置换算符（Rényi-2 对应 swap 算符）替代可观测量。聚焦 Rényi-2，结合精确随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）计算、既有 ETH 结果与一维数值检验，构造复制本征基下 swap 矩阵元及其与初始态重叠统计交叉关联的 ETH 类拟设（ansätze）。

Result: 在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下，纯度动力学完全由这些统计产生的光滑缓变函数决定，类似单复制中的广义 ETH 表述；在大子系统极限下，局域性与体谱退相位使函数依赖简化为单一基频（fundamental frequency）。

Conclusion: 该形式体系提供了 ETH 类陈述对任意小与任意大子系统尺寸均可自然共存的框架，并讨论了向更高阶 Rényi 熵（Rényi entropies）的推广；结果澄清了纠缠动力学的谱基础，并为谱滤波方法（spectral filter methods）计算确定性系统中 Rényi 演化奠定基础。

Abstract: How is entanglement dynamics encoded in the eigenstates of the time-evolution operator? We address this question by focusing on Rényi entropy dynamics in deterministic chaotic Floquet models in arbitrary dimensions. Inspired by the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH), we employ spectral representations of reduced density matrix moments (purity and its higher-order generalizations) whose decay through spectral dephasing captures nonequilibrium entanglement growth. These representations closely resemble those governing local-observable dynamics, but involve eigenstates in a replicated Hilbert space and observables are replaced by subsystem permutation operators, such as the swap operator for Rényi-2. Focusing on Rényi-2, we combine exact random matrix theory calculations, established ETH results and numerical tests in 1D to formulate ETH-like ansätze for subsystem swap matrix elements in the replicated eigenbasis and their cross-correlations with initial-state overlap statistics. In the thermodynamic limit, purity dynamics is entirely determined by smooth slowly varying functions that emerge from such statistics, paralleling generalized ETH formulations in a single replica. In the limit of large subsystems, these functions exhibit a striking structural simplification: locality of the dynamics together with bulk spectral dephasing reduce their functional dependence to a single fundamental frequency. Our formalism provides a setting where ETH-like statements naturally coexist for both arbitrarily small and large subsystem sizes. We discuss extensions of our results to higher Rényi entropies. Beyond clarifying the spectral foundations of entanglement dynamics, these results lay some of the groundwork for future applications of spectral filter methods to the computation of Rényi evolution in deterministic systems.

</details>


### [142] [A new representation of effective diffusion in fluctuating periodic potentials through stochastic duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13526)
*Derek Frydel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将均匀化方法（homogenization）与随机对偶（stochastic duality）结合，重新研究涨落周期力场中的有效扩散问题，并用物理系统与对偶系统的稳态密度与概率流（probability current）给出有效扩散系数（effective diffusion coefficient）的新表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的有效扩散问题中，有效扩散系数通常需借助满足后向Kolmogorov方程（backward Kolmogorov equation）的修正子（corrector）求解，形式不直观且难以与稳态量直接关联。本文旨在消除修正子，建立仅依赖稳态密度与概率流的表达式。

Method: 考虑一类特定涨落：力场内部状态在泊松分布时刻（Poisson-distributed times）从固定分布独立重抽（redraw）。在此动力学下，将修正子的空间导数所满足的方程重新解释为反转力场（F→-F）所得对偶系统的稳态前向方程（stationary forward equation），从而消去修正子。

Result: 得到以物理系统与对偶系统的稳态密度和概率流表示的有效扩散系数；并引入辅助函数Q(x)，其空间变化由稳态电流决定。当稳态电流为零时，Q(x)与位置无关，有效扩散系数约化为局部关系 D_eff = L^2 Q。

Conclusion: 该结果将经典Lifson–Jackson关系推广至涨落周期力场。理论通过解析计算、稳态方程数值解以及长时间粒子位移的直接模拟得到验证，包括具有非零稳态电流的驱动系统。

Abstract: We revisit the classical problem of effective diffusion in fluctuating periodic force fields by combining homogenization with stochastic duality. We consider a specific class of fluctuations in which the internal state of the force field is independently redrawn from a fixed distribution at Poisson-distributed times. In the homogenization framework, the effective diffusion coefficient \(D_{\rm eff}\) is expressed in terms of a corrector function satisfying a backward Kolmogorov equation. For the redraw dynamics considered here, the equation governing the spatial derivative of the corrector can be reinterpreted as the stationary forward equation of a dual system obtained by reversing the force field, \(F\to-F\). This relation eliminates the corrector and yields a representation of \(D_{\rm eff}\) entirely in terms of stationary densities and probability currents of the physical and dual systems. We further introduce an auxiliary function \(Q(x)\) whose spatial variation is determined by the stationary current. When this current vanishes, \(Q(x)\) becomes independent of position and the effective diffusion coefficient reduces to the local relation \( D_{\rm eff}=L^2Q. \) This result generalizes the classical Lifson--Jackson relation to fluctuating periodic force fields. We verify the theory using analytical calculations, numerical solutions of the stationary equations, and direct simulations of the long-time particle displacement, including a driven system with nonzero stationary current.

</details>


### [143] [Stochastic evolution of interacting directed strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13622)
*S. S. Manna,Deepak Dhar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究通过模拟与解析方法，考察无限圆柱面上仅含排除体积相互作用的 N 个聚合物环（polymer rings）的随机动力学，给出阶跃初始条件下密度剖面的标度形式，并揭示其与无自旋费米子基态及 KPZ 普适类的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究受限几何中聚合物环的集体扩散行为，刻画从阶跃初始条件（step initial condition）出发的密度剖面在流体力学极限（hydrodynamic limit）下的标度结构，并建立与已知统计物理模型的联系。

Method: 采用模拟与解析相结合的方法；横向扩散由局域 Rouse 型移动（Rouse-like moves）实现，即上扭结（up-kink）与下扭结（down-kink）之间的转换，当翻转速率不等时运动带有偏置。初始条件为正 y 坐标区域空、y ≤ 0 区域均匀占据的阶跃分布。

Result: 无偏情形下密度剖面标度形式为 y/√t 的函数，偏置情形下为 y/t 的函数；无偏情形在环面（torus）上的稳态中，固定 x 坐标直线上各聚合物位置的边际概率分布与一维无自旋量子力学费米子（spin-less fermions）基态完全一致；偏置问题等价于 2+1 维生长表面（growing surface），预期属于 Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）普适类。

Conclusion: 聚合物环系统的随机动力学在标度层面可由简单普适形式描述：无偏时表现为扩散型 y/√t 标度并映射到自由费米子，偏置时表现为弹道型 y/t 标度并对应 KPZ 类生长界面，体现了该模型与量子多体及界面生长问题之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We study the stochastic dynamics of a system of $N$ polymer rings on an infinite cylinder with only excluded volume interaction, using simulations and also analytically. The strings can diffuse in the transverse direction by local Rouse-like moves where an up-kink changes to a down -kink, or vice versa. The motion is biased if the rates of flip are not equal. In the initial state, we start with a step initial condition: all the sites with a positive $y$ coordinate are empty, and the sites with $y \leq 0$ are occupied with a uniform density. We study the scaling form of the density profile in the hydrodynamic limit. The scaling form is a function of $y/\sqrt{t}$ in the unbiased case, and a function of $y/t$ in the biased case. In the unbiased case, in the steady state on a torus, the marginal probability distribution of the positions of the strings on a line with fixed $x$-coordinate is exactly same as that in the ground state of quantum-mechanical spin-less fermions on a line. The biased problem is equivalent to a growing surface in $2+1$ dimension, which is expected to be in the Kardar-Parisi-Zhang universality class.

</details>


### [144] [The active chiral random walk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14446)
*Nir Sherf,Joshua Uhlig,Jan Wójcik,Ralf Metzler,Yael Roichman,Erik Kalz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出一个极简的格子模型（lattice model），用于描述具有持续性（persistence）、反向（reversal）与手性（chirality）的活性手性粒子动力学，并通过粗粒化（coarse-graining）导出连续介质方程与闭合形式的输运量表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有活性物质（active matter）模型往往难以用少量透明微观参数同时刻画持续性、反向与手性，且平滑角扩散（angular diffusion）描述在强转向情形下失效；因此需要一个紧凑、可精确求解且易调的框架。

Method: 构建由少量微观参数完全定义的格子模型，通过粗粒化提取连续方程，并推导速度自关联（velocity autocorrelation）、非对角方向关联、均方位移（mean-squared displacement）以及长时间扩散与奇扩散（odd diffusion）系数的闭式表达式；以复参数 λ = q_p + i q_c 编码持续性（q_p）与手性（q_c）。

Result: 在弱转向极限（由稀疏重取向事件微观实现）下，连续描述可映射到成熟的活性物质模型；超出该极限时，离散模型表现出平滑角扩散描述无法捕捉的有限步动力学，如反持续性运动（anti-persistent motion）。

Conclusion: 该模型为手性活性输运（chiral active transport）提供了一个紧凑、精确可解且易于调节的框架，少量透明微观参数即可控制其宏观输运行为。

Abstract: We introduce a minimal and intuitive lattice model for an active chiral particle which exhibits persistence, reversal, and chirality in its dynamics. It is fully defined by a small set of microscopic parameters that provide transparent control over the resulting dynamics. By coarse-graining, we extract continuum equations and closed-form expressions for the velocity autocorrelation, off-diagonal directional correlation, mean-squared displacement, and long-time diffusion and odd diffusion coefficients. The complex parameter $λ=q_p+\mathrm{i}q_c$ thereby encodes the microscopic information on persistence ($q_p$) and chirality ($q_c$) of the walker. In a weak-turning limit, microscopically realized by infrequent reorientation events, the continuum description can be mapped onto well-established models of active matter. Outside this limit the discrete model exhibits finite-step dynamics, such as anti-persistent motion, that are not captured by a smooth angular-diffusion description. The model therefore provides a compact, exactly solvable, and readily tunable framework in which a small number of transparent microscopic parameters control chiral active transport.

</details>


### [145] [Non-Gaussian limit distributions of giant-component sizes in inhomogeneous networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13683)
*Tomoaki Nogawa,Takehisa Hasegawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在高异质网络上的渗流（percolation）与传染病传播（epidemic spreading）模型中，巨连通分量（giant component）的尺寸分布在热力学极限下往往不收敛于高斯分布，而在适当标度下收敛于非高斯极限分布（non-Gaussian limit distribution, NGLD）。该标度行为可由广义中心极限定理（generalized central limit theorem, GCLT）刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规假设认为巨连通分量尺寸分布在热力学极限下趋于高斯分布；该文旨在检验高异质网络（highly inhomogeneous networks）情形下这一假设是否成立，并刻画其真实极限分布形态。

Method: 通过在渗流与传染病传播模型上考察巨连通分量尺寸分布的标度行为，并将其与适用于重尾分布（heavy-tailed distribution）随机变量求和的广义中心极限定理（GCLT）进行对照分析；对比无标度网络（scale-free network）与确定性层级小世界网络 Farey 图两类网络结构。

Result: 在适当标度下，尺寸分布收敛于非高斯极限分布（NGLD），与 GCLT 的预期一致。对无标度网络，标度指数（scaling exponent）仅由网络度指数（degree exponent）决定，与控制参数无关；而在 Farey 图上，标度指数随控制参数连续变化。

Conclusion: 高异质网络上巨连通分量尺寸的非高斯涨落具有普适性结构，其标度指数依赖于网络拓扑异质性的来源：无标度网络中度指数单独决定标度指数，而确定性层级网络（Farey 图）中标度指数可由控制参数连续调节。

Abstract: We demonstrate that the size distribution of giant components in percolation and epidemic spreading models on highly inhomogeneous networks often fails to converge to a Gaussian distribution in the thermodynamic limit. Instead, when appropriately scaled, the distribution converges to a non-Gaussian limit distribution (NGLD). This scaling behavior aligns with the generalized central limit theorem (GCLT), which governs sums of random variables following heavy-tailed distributions. For scale-free networks, the scaling exponent is determined solely by the degree exponent of the network, independently of the control parameter. In contrast, on the Farey graph, a deterministic hierarchical small-world network, the scaling exponent varies continuously with the control parameter.

</details>


### [146] [Critical geometry of triconnected clusters in percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13783)
*Qi Wang,Youjin Deng,Ming Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究借助双连通分量分解与SPQR图分解算法，系统测定了渗流（percolation）中三连通集团（triconnected clusters）的分形维数（fractal dimension），发现其随空间维数增加而单调下降，并在 d≥6 时趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多关注双连通集团等高连通度子结构，而“去除任意两点仍保持连通”的三连通集团（triconnected clusters）的临界几何性质尚缺乏系统刻画，其分形维数与维数依赖关系不明确。

Method: 将双连通分量分解（biconnected-component decomposition）与图的SPQR分解（SPQR decomposition）相结合，设计出可在大型系统中高效识别三连通集团的算法，并开展大规模蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）。

Result: 测得 d=2、3、4 时三连通集团分形维数分别为 d₃=1.2308(3)、1.083(2)、0.77(2)，随维数单调下降；d=6 时最大三连通集团随系统尺寸对数增长，完全图（complete graphs，对应 d=∞）情形下趋于有限尺寸，二者均指示 d≥6 时 d₃=0。与三连通集团尺寸分布二阶矩相关的类磁化率量（susceptibility-like quantity）的奇异部分满足 χ∼L^{2d₃−d}，在 d≥3 时消失。

Conclusion: 三连通集团具有与标准渗流（standard percolation）不同的临界行为：其分形维数在上临界维数附近趋于零，类磁化率不再发散而趋于有限渐近值，表明高连通度子结构在高维下存在定性差异。

Abstract: We investigate the critical geometry of triconnected clusters in percolation, which remain connected after the removal of any two sites. By combining biconnected-component decomposition with the SPQR decomposition of graphs, we develop an efficient algorithm for identifying triconnected clusters in large systems. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations yield estimates of the fractal dimension $d_3=1.2308(3)$, $1.083(2)$, and $0.77(2)$ in dimensions $d=2$, $3$, and $4$, respectively, revealing a systematic decrease of $d_3$ with increasing $d$. In dimension $d=6$, the largest triconnected cluster grows logarithmically with system size, whereas on complete graphs, corresponding to $d=\infty$, it approaches a finite size. Both behaviors indicate a vanishing fractal dimension, $d_3=0$, for $d\geq6$. Furthermore, the singular contribution to the susceptibility-like quantity associated with the second moment of the triconnected-cluster size distribution scales as $χ\sim L^{2d_3-d}$. Consequently, it vanishes for $d\geq3$, leaving a finite asymptotic susceptibility, in contrast to the divergent behavior observed in standard percolation.

</details>


### [147] [Thermodynamics of words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14039)
*Klaus M. Frahm,Dima L. Shepelyansky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文提出“词语热化假说（word thermalization hypothesis）”，将文学作品中的词语视为经典振子（经典粒子），其词频对应于能级占据数，并在能量、总词数与总概率范数守恒下演化至类 Rayleigh-Jeans（RJ）热化与凝聚。对16部文学作品的分析显示有效系统能量极低，出现 RJ 凝聚，且理论对真实词频分布给出良好描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于为文学文本的词频分布（如 Zipf 律）提供一种基于统计物理（statistical mechanics）的统一解释框架，并建立词频统计与物理热化现象及其他领域（如财富热化）之间的联系。

Method: 将词语类比为经典振子（oscillators），词频/词元（tokens）类比为能级上的填充因子（filling factors），在总能量、总词数与总概率范数（即不同词数）守恒的约束下引入词语间有效相互作用导致的热化过程；并用 Lorenz 曲线、Pareto 曲线与 Zipf 曲线刻画词频分布。

Result: 对包括《圣经》、莎士比亚全集及美国国家语料库（American National Corpus）数据在内的16个文本案例，有效系统能量很低，呈现 RJ 凝聚（Rayleigh-Jeans condensation）：大量总概率集中于最低能态，对应大量仅出现一两次的罕见词；热化理论对真实数据给出相当好的描述。

Conclusion: 词语热化框架可将文学文本词频分布与多模光纤光传播等物理系统中的 RJ 热化/凝聚现象以及世界经济中的财富热化（wealth thermalization）相联系，为跨学科的词频统计建模提供统一视角。

Abstract: We introduce the word thermalization hypothesis according to which in a literary work there are effective interactions between words that drive them to thermalization where their word frequency distribution is the same as for the phenomenon of Rayleigh-Jeans (RJ) thermalization and condensation. In this concept, the different words play a role of classical oscillators (particles) and their word frequencies, or related tokens, are analogous to certain filling factors over energy state levels. The thermalization process is characterized by the conservation of total system energy, or total number of words/tokens, and total probability norm, or total number of different words. We apply this approach to analyze 16 literary works, including Bible, complete works of Shakespeare, and data from American National Corpus. We show that for these cases the effective system energy is very low and thus there is the RJ condensation phenomenon with a big fraction of total probability concentrated at the lowest energy states corresponding to a big fraction of rare words appearing only once or twice in the whole text. Such a RJ condensation is observed for light propagation in multimode optical fibers and other physical systems. We relate the thermalization of words to the recently proposed wealth thermalization for households in world countries. The economy concepts of Lorenz and Pareto curves, and also Zipf curves, are applied to the word frequency distributions of the selected 16 cases and it is shown that the thermalization theory gives a strikingly good description of real data.

</details>


### [148] [Memory of Initial States in Long-Time Dynamics Revealed by Non-Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14108)
*Julia Koczorowska,Lev Vidmar,Patrycja Łydżba*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在二次型量子多体系统中，初始态的信息通过广义吉布斯系综（generalized Gibbs ensemble）保留在长时间平均值中；该工作进一步表明这种记忆也延伸到平均值周围的时间涨落（temporal fluctuations），且涨落幅度是初始态非高斯性（non-Gaussianity）的光滑函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有认识认为少体可观测量在二次系统（quadratic systems）的量子动力学中，初始态信息仅体现在长时间平均值上，由广义吉布斯系综刻画；但平均值之外的时间涨落是否也携带初始态记忆尚不清楚。

Method: 生成一族非高斯初始态（non-Gaussian initial states），并以相应的熵（entropy）量化其非高斯性，进而考察时间涨落幅度与非高斯性之间的关系，用于解释量子淬火（quantum quench）后涨落标度行为的差异。

Result: 时间涨落的幅度是初始态非高斯性的光滑函数；这一关系解释了量子淬火后观察到的定性不同的涨落标度：非高斯初始态给出指数小（exponentially small）的涨落，而高斯初始态给出多项式小（polynomially small）的涨落。

Conclusion: 非高斯性（non-Gaussianity）被确立为揭示长时间平均值之外初始态记忆的关键量，将初始态记忆的讨论从平均值层面推广到时间涨落层面。

Abstract: In quantum dynamics of few-body observables in quadratic systems, information about the initial state is retained in long-time averages, as captured by the generalized Gibbs ensemble. Here, we show that this memory extends beyond the averages to temporal fluctuations around them. By generating a manifold of non-Gaussian initial states and quantifying their non-Gaussianity via the corresponding entropy, we find that the magnitude of temporal fluctuations is a smooth function of the initial-state non-Gaussianity. This relation explains the qualitatively different scaling of fluctuations observed after quantum quenches: exponentially small fluctuations for non-Gaussian initial states and polynomially small fluctuations for Gaussian initial states. Therefore, our results identify non-Gaussianity as a key to uncovering initial-state memory beyond long-time averages.

</details>


### [149] [The Multiscale Principle in phase transition: Geometric structure, thermodynamic entropy, and dynamical instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14230)
*Patricio Venegas-Aravena,Fernando A. Oliveira*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过将热力学分形维数D与关联长度及熵变联系起来，重新解释临界点附近的能量耗散，并将几何结构、热力学熵与动力学不稳定性（混沌）统一起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界现象中系统表现出临界涨落与长程关联，关联长度发散与自旋团簇分形几何的出现，对涨落耗散定理（FDT）等经典框架构成挑战，需要替代性理论框架。

Method: 将临界指数、分形维数与热力学形式体系Principium Luxuriae相联系，通过把热力学分形维数D与关联长度和熵变关联，以多尺度过程重新表述能量耗散。

Result: 该方法再现了二维Ising模型临界行为相关的有效标度趋势；并发现临界温度附近D的下降标志着系统向宏观相干性的转变，此时系统在整个尺度上耗散能量。

Conclusion: 分形网络使局部涨落全局传播，导致对扰动的高度敏感性，从而在几何结构、热力学熵与动力学不稳定性（混沌）的出现之间建立起直接联系。

Abstract: Scaling and multi-scaling are two guiding principles for understanding nature or even commerce and industrial outcome. In the realm of critical phenomena, scaling laws describe the behavior of physical quantities near a continuous phase transition. This is a complex phenomena where systems exhibit critical fluctuations and long-range correlations, challenging classical formulations such as the fluctuation-dissipation theorem (FDT). In particular, near critical points, the divergence of the correlation length and the emergence of fractal geometry in spin clusters suggest the need for alternative frameworks. In this work, we explore the connection between critical exponents, fractal dimensions, and the thermodynamic formalism known as the Principium Luxuriae. By linking the thermodynamic fractal dimension D to the correlation length and entropy variations, we reinterpret energy dissipation in terms of multiscale processes. This approach allows us to reproduce effective scaling tendencies associated with the critical behavior of the 2d Ising model and provides new insight into how systems redistribute energy macroscopically during phase transitions. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the decrease in D near the critical temperature marks a transition toward macroscopic coherence, where the system begins to dissipate energy across its entire scale. This unified behavior, however, induces a heightened sensitivity to perturbations, where local fluctuations are transmitted globally through the fractal network. This mechanism establishes a direct bridge between geometric structure, thermodynamic entropy, and the onset of dynamical instability (chaos) as the system approaches criticality.

</details>


### [150] [From Distribution of Extremes With Degradation to the Stress Redundancy Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14375)
*Frédéric Paquin-Lefebvre,Élisa Delbreil,David Holcman*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了降解（killing/degradation）源如何影响众多独立同分布随机粒子中最快到达窄目标者的首达统计，基于Fokker-Planck方程的时间依赖通量与混合边值问题，刻画了首达时间、逃逸位置及逃逸概率的分布，并揭示降解场对经典缩放律与最优轨迹的修正机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出，多个相同独立随机粒子中最先成功到达窄目标者的统计规律如何受降解源影响尚不明确，而该问题对界定细胞与分子时间尺度具有意义，因此需要系统刻画降解对首达统计的作用。

Method: 利用与相应Fokker-Planck方程（Fokker-Planck equation）关联的时间依赖通量（time-dependent fluxes）分析首达时间与逃逸位置分布，并通过求解混合边值问题（mixed boundary value problem）计算逃逸概率；在大初始种群极限下推导渐近公式，并采用最优控制（optimal control）方法求最优轨迹。

Result: 在大初始种群数$N_0$极限下得到最快粒子平均首达时间的渐近公式，显示杀伤场（killing field）修正经典$1/\log(N_0)$缩放；首次逃逸时刻的种群规模呈现与降解的特定依赖，称为"胁迫冗余原理"（stress redundancy principle）；窄子区域降解场下最优轨迹依杀伤强度规避降解区；并给出二维规则域中窄吸收弧上首达出口点分布的解析表达，以及降解子区与目标边界相切时几何参数对出口分布的影响。

Conclusion: 降解源能显著改变最快扩散粒子的统计特性，相关结论对界定细胞与分子时间尺度具有参考价值。

Abstract: How a degradation source affects the statistics of the first among many identical independent stochastic particles to successfully reach a narrow target remains unclear. Here, we investigate the impact of degradation on the distributions of first-passage times and exit locations by exploiting the time-dependent fluxes associated with the corresponding Fokker-Planck equation. We also compute the escape probability by solving a mixed boundary value problem. In the large initial population $N_0$ limit, we derive an asymptotic formula for the mean first passage time associated with the fastest particle, revealing how the killing field modifies the classical $1/\log(N_0)$ scaling. However, when considering the population size at the time of the first exit, we obtain a specific dependency with respect to degradation, a phenomenon that we call the stress redundancy principle. For a degradation field restricted to a narrow subregion, we use an optimal control approach to derive the optimal trajectory where the fastest escape particles will be concentrated: interestingly, we report here that the optimal trajectories avoid the degradation area depending on killing intensity. Finally, we obtain analytical expressions for the distribution of first exit points along a narrow absorbing arc in two-dimensional regular domains. In addition, for a killing subregion tangent to the target boundary, our computations reveal how geometrical parameters influence the exit distribution. In summary, a killing source can significantly affect the statistics of the fastest diffusing particles, relevant for defining cellular and molecular time scales.

</details>


### [151] [Mutual Linearity in Nonlinear Population Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14501)
*Shiling Liang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文指出广义 Lotka–Volterra 动力学（generalized Lotka–Volterra dynamics）构成一类新的互线性（mutual linearity）系统：即使物种会灭绝、吸引子可为混沌，改变单一物种方程后所有丰度仍保持仿射关系（affine relations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互线性此前仅在马尔可夫跳跃过程（Markov jump processes）与化学反应网络（chemical reaction networks）中已知，缺乏对生态型非线性动力学是否具备该性质的认识。

Method: 以广义 Lotka–Volterra 动力学为对象，考察对单一物种方程施加任意强度改动（即改变一个转变速率/参数）时其余物种的响应，并分析其在灭绝分段与混沌吸引子上的表现。

Result: 对某一物种方程的任意改动对其余物种等价于一个有效压制（effective press），并使其全部丰度保持仿射相关；该关系在灭绝处呈分段（piecewise）形式，在混沌吸引子（chaotic attractors）上以时间平均（time averages）形式成立。

Conclusion: 广义 Lotka–Volterra 动力学被确立为互线性系统的新类别，表明互线性可延拓至允许灭绝与混沌的非线性种群动力学（nonlinear population dynamics）。

Abstract: Mutual Linearity in Nonlinear Population DynamicsMutual linearity, the persistence of affine relations among stationary observables under arbitrarily strong changes of one transition rate, is known for Markov jump processes and chemical reaction networks. We show that generalized Lotka--Volterra dynamics form a new class of mutually linear systems, even though species can go extinct and attractors can be chaotic. Any change to one species' equation acts on the others as one effective press and keeps all abundances affinely related: piecewise across extinctions, and as time averages on chaotic attractors.

</details>
