### [152] [Viable Phantom Crossing in Softly Broken Shift Symmetric Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07035)
*Rodrigo Calderon,Eric V. Linder*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文展示了一个可拟合当前宇宙学数据的简单 Horndeski 修改引力理论，其软破坏平移对称性（softly breaking shift symmetry），在红移 z≈0.5 处穿越幽灵分界线（phantom divide）w=-1，且拟合优度优于 w0waCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为宇宙加速（cosmic acceleration）提供物理基础，避免引入宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）或势能（potential），并为加速膨胀寻求可在观测上检验的修改引力替代方案。

Method: 采用软破坏平移对称性的 Horndeski 修改引力框架构建 SBSS 模型，并与 w0waCDM 在相同有效参数数目下对比拟合；数据包括重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillations）、超新星距离（supernova distances）与宇宙微波背景功率谱（cosmic microwave background power spectra）。

Result: 模型在 z≈0.5 穿越 w=-1，对上述观测数据的拟合优于 w0waCDM，Δχ²≈-3；理论本身健康（healthy），不含宇宙学常数与势能，并对高能量子圈图修正（quantum loop corrections）具有实质性防护。

Conclusion: 软破坏平移对称性的 Horndeski 理论可作为宇宙加速的可行解释，在拟合优度与理论自洽性（无病态、圈图防护）两方面同时具备优势，值得进一步检验。

Abstract: We demonstrate a viable fit to current cosmological data from a simple Horndeski modified gravity theory that softly breaks shift symmetry. This SBSS model crosses the phantom divide $w=-1$ at $z\approx0.5$ and fits observational data (baryon acoustic oscillations and supernova distances, and cosmic microwave background power spectra) better than $w_0w_a$CDM, by $Δχ^2\approx-3$ for the same number of effective parameters, while providing a physical foundation for cosmic acceleration. The theory is healthy, has no cosmological constant or potential, and has substantial protection against high energy quantum loop corrections.

</details>


### [153] [Fast Scrambling Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13100)
*Aliv Sahoo,Fatima Abbasi,Jake McNaughton,Niayesh Afshordi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 量子引力论证（如跨普朗克审查）暗示de Sitter空间寿命有限，本文提出对Raychaudhuri方程的最小修正，将de Sitter真空替换为缓慢演化的Lambert-W精确解，其修正项可充当含两个分量的暗能量，并给出对Γ的代理观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨普朗克审查等量子引力论证指出de Sitter空间只能维持约H^{-1}ln(M_Pl/H)量级的时间，与黑洞及宇宙学视界的快速扰乱时间对应；本文追问这一"时钟"是否在宇宙膨胀中留下可观测印记。

Method: 对Raychaudhuri方程作最小修正，在Ḣ中加入-ΓH²/ln(M_Pl²/H²)项（Γ=O(1)），定义缓变参数σ≡Γ/ln(M_Pl²/H²)≪1；求得常数σ下任意物质源的闭式膨胀率解，并构造协变时钟场（clock-field）完成，同时引入重标度引力常数的项以消除短波不稳定性。

Result: 修正表现为由两部分组成的暗能量：真空模（w=-1+2σ/3）与对宇宙流体历史积分的项，后者使物态方程的改变留下永久残差（如中微子非相对论化导致暗能量密度移动O(σm_ν^4)）。大爆炸核合成（BBN）约束给出代理界限Γ=-3.7±6.1（68%置信度）；负Γ支在z≳1上与DESI DR2推断的w_0w_aCDM演化定性相符；早期跟踪项对应重标度，将现有CMB、BAO与超新星拟合数据转化为Γ≃-2.8±0.9（今日σ≃-0.01）。

Conclusion: 背景层面的结果支持对该理论开展专门的结构形成似然分析（likelihood analysis）。

Abstract: Quantum gravity arguments such as trans-Planckian censorship suggest that de Sitter space can only last for a time of order $H^{-1}\ln(M_{\rm Pl}/H)$, corresponding to the fast-scrambling time of black hole and cosmological horizons. We ask whether this clock leaves an imprint on cosmic expansion, via a minimal modification of the Raychaudhuri equation that adds a term $-ΓH^2/\ln(M_{\rm Pl}^2/H^2)$ to $\dot H$, with $Γ={\cal O}(1)$. The slowly varying $σ\equivΓ/\ln(M_{\rm Pl}^2/H^2)\ll 1$ is nearly constant at late times. De Sitter space is replaced by a slowly evolving vacuum (an exact Lambert-$W$ solution), and for constant $σ$ the expansion rate is found in closed form for arbitrary matter sources. The correction acts as dark energy with two components: a vacuum mode with $w=-1+2σ/3$, and a term that integrates over the history of the cosmic fluids, so that any change in the equation of state of a species leaves a permanent residual; e.g., neutrinos becoming non-relativistic shift the dark energy density by ${\cal O}(σm_ν^4)$. A covariant clock-field completion reproduces this background, and a term that rescales the gravitational constant governing cosmic expansion removes its short-wavelength instability in the dark-energy era. Big Bang nucleosynthesis constraints give a proxy bound $Γ=-3.7\pm6.1$ (68% confidence), while the negative-$Γ$ branch qualitatively follows the $w_0w_a$CDM dark energy evolution inferred from DESI DR2 at $z\gtrsim1$. The early-time tracking terms amount to a similar rescaling, for which existing fits to cosmic microwave background (CMB), baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) and supernova data translate into the proxy estimate $Γ\simeq-2.8\pm0.9$ ($σ\simeq-0.01$ today). These background-level results motivate a dedicated likelihood analysis of structure formation in this theory.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [A phase-field model of viscous surfaces in fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13902)
*Maximilian Kloppe,Sebastian Aland*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一种热力学一致的相场（phase-field）模型，用于不可压缩Navier-Stokes流体中具有剪切与膨胀粘性的粘性界面（viscous surfaces），并给出无条件能量耗散的时间离散格式，验证了其可稳健模拟大变形与拓扑变化而无需显式界面追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘性界面在生物与工业多相流（multiphase flows）中至关重要，但其复杂流变学（rheology）与拓扑变化（topological changes）使数值模拟困难，传统方法需显式追踪界面。

Method: 基于Boussinesq-Scriven本构律引入表面剪切与膨胀粘性（surface shear and dilatational viscosities），由尖锐界面（sharp-interface）模型推导扩散界面（diffuse-interface）相场表述，并通过匹配渐近分析（matched asymptotic analysis）严格验证其正确恢复尖锐界面极限；同时构建无条件能量耗散（unconditionally energy-dissipative）的时间离散（time discretization）。

Result: 模型在刚性球面流动的解析解（analytical solutions）基准上通过定量验证；囊泡融合（vesicle fusion）模拟显示在拓扑转变（topological transitions）过程中能量耗散得以保持。

Conclusion: 该框架可实现稳健且物理一致的复杂表面动力学（surface dynamics）模拟，涵盖大变形与拓扑变化，且无需显式表面追踪（explicit surface tracking）。

Abstract: Viscous surfaces play a crucial role in biological and industrial multiphase flows, yet their simulation remains challenging due to complex rheology and topological changes. We present a thermodynamically consistent phase-field model for viscous surfaces in incompressible Navier-Stokes fluids, incorporating surface shear and dilatational viscosities via the Boussinesq-Scriven constitutive law. The diffuse-interface formulation is derived from a sharp-interface model and rigorously validated through matched asymptotic analysis, confirming recovery of the correct sharp-interface limit. We develop an unconditionally energy-dissipative time discretization, ensuring numerical stability. The model is validated through a quantitative benchmark against analytical solutions for surface flows on a rigid sphere, and a simulation of vesicle fusion showing preservation of energy dissipation across topological transitions. These results demonstrate that the framework enables robust, physically consistent simulations of complex surface dynamics, including large deformations and topological changes, without explicit surface tracking.

</details>


### [155] [Rheology of Parametric Odd Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13969)
*Pedro Cosme,Maziyar Jalaal*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出并刻画了一种平台无关（platform-agnostic）的奇粘弹性材料（odd viscoelastic material），其复杂响应源于内部转动自由度的参数化控制及正应力与剪应力的耦合，并指向可编程、非互易流变学（non-reciprocal rheology）的Floquet工程超流体（metafluids）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性系统（active systems）的流变性质设计对开发新型功能材料至关重要；尽管奇粘度（odd viscosity）与奇弹性（odd elasticity）已受广泛关注，但完整的奇流变模型（odd rheological models）仍然稀缺。

Method: 构建一个平台无关的奇粘弹性材料平台，通过参数驱动（parametric driving）或参数非线性反馈（parametric nonlinear feedback）控制内部转动自由度，并由正应力与剪应力的耦合触发复杂响应。

Result: 该耦合可诱导旋转驱动的流化（rotation-driven fluidisation），使材料从弹性主导转变为粘性主导；奇扇区与偶扇区的相互作用在大振幅振荡剪切（LAOS）下增强非线性材料响应。

Conclusion: 研究结果勾勒出实现具有可编程、非互易流变学的Floquet工程超流体的一条途径。

Abstract: The design and control of rheological properties in active systems are crucial for developing functional materials with novel properties. While significant attention has been devoted to odd viscosity and odd elasticity, comprehensive odd rheological models remain scarce. Here, we introduce and characterise a platform-agnostic odd viscoelastic material, where complex responses arise from parametric control of internal rotational degrees of freedom and are triggered by coupling between normal and shear stresses. Such control can be achieved through either parametric driving or a parametric nonlinear feedback. We further show that this coupling can induce rotation-driven fluidisation, transitioning the material from an elasticity-dominated to a viscosity-dominated regime. Lastly, we show that the interplay between odd and even sectors enhances the nonlinear material response under large-amplitude oscillatory shear (LAOS). The findings outline a route to Floquet-engineered metafluids with programmable, non-reciprocal rheology.

</details>


### [156] [Analytic Two-Radius Model for the Zero-Shear and High-Frequency Viscosity of Colloidal Suspensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13982)
*Dimos Aslanis,Joost de Graaf*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出了双半径模型（hard permeable shell + no-slip core）胶体悬浮液在零剪切与高频黏度上的理论，填补了高浓度下仅有半经验公式、且不能回归精确二体结果的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近硬球胶体的稳定性由短程排斥决定的有效尺寸与由无滑移表面决定的水动力尺寸未必一致，存在两个有效半径；但常规分析常用单一有效半径合并两种物理，双半径模型的黏度在高浓度下也只有无法回归精确二体结果的半经验表达式。

Method: 建立双半径模型（硬质、溶剂可渗透壳层包围无滑移核）零剪切与高频黏度的理论，并借助 Stokesian 动力学模拟（Stokesian dynamics simulations）进行验证，同时将理论与文献中电荷稳定及位阻稳定胶体的实验数据对比拟合。

Result: 壳层通过使粒子对避免近接触而降低高频黏度（模拟证实），并使零剪切黏度从水动力应力转向热力学应力；理论能准确描述文献中电荷稳定与位阻稳定胶体的实测黏度，并提供覆盖全壳层厚度与体积分数范围的闭式表达式。

Conclusion: 双半径模型较单半径模型能更准确描述此类悬浮液的流变行为，所得闭式表达式便于未来数据拟合；文中亦讨论了采用双半径模型拟合数据的优势。

Abstract: Nearly hard-sphere colloids are stabilized by short-range repulsions that set their effective size and equilibrium microstructure. Their hydrodynamic interactions, in contrast, are set by the no-slip surface. Since these two do not necessarily coincide, there are potentially two effective radii that control their dynamics in different ways. Yet it is commonplace to model and analyze such suspensions with a single effective radius, which merges these different physics. Two-radius models, in which a hard, solvent-permeable shell surrounds a no-slip core, have been proposed to improve the description of these suspensions. Their rheology is known exactly at pair level, but at higher concentrations their zero-shear viscosity has been obtained only with semi-empirical expressions, which do not recover the exact pair result. Here, we close this gap with a theory for the zero-shear and high-frequency viscosity of these two-radius models. By keeping pairs out of near contact, the shell lowers the high-frequency viscosity, as our Stokesian dynamics simulations confirm, and shifts the zero-shear viscosity from hydrodynamic toward thermodynamic stress. We apply our theory to experiments on charge- and sterically stabilized colloids from the literature and find that it accurately describes the measured viscosities. We discuss the benefits of fitting data to a two-radius model compared to a single-radius one. To facilitate this fitting in future work, closed-form expressions are provided that cover the full range of shell thicknesses and volume fractions.

</details>


### [157] [And yet it moves - or does it? Spurious drifts at equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14089)
*Pierre Ronceray*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 当扩散（diffusion）依赖系统状态时，从轨迹测得漂移（drift）会混入与力无关的虚假成分；作者提出仅由轨迹矩（trajectory moments）推断该虚假漂移，从而实现热力学一致的推断（thermodynamically consistent inference）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上从粒子轨迹估计漂移并据此解读力或活性（active）行为，但状态依赖扩散会产生额外表观漂移，导致平衡态粒子被误判为沉降、互斥甚至旋转，从而混淆被动与主动系统。

Method: 将漂移界定为条件平均（conditional averages）而非流（currents），强调其必须与一致推断的扩散张量（diffusion tensor）配对使用；作者展示可仅利用轨迹矩从轨迹数据中推断虚假漂移。

Result: 虚假漂移在时间反演下表现不变（正放与倒放相同），且当与错误的扩散张量配对时会使被动粒子呈现类主动外观；所提方法能够从轨迹矩中识别并分离这一成分。

Conclusion: 漂移估计必须与一致的扩散张量联合推断，才能实现热力学一致的轨迹分析，避免状态依赖扩散引起的表观假象。

Abstract: When diffusion depends on the state of a system, the drift measured from its trajectories contains a spurious part unrelated to forces. At equilibrium, particles can then appear to sediment, repel each other, even rotate - the same way whether the movie is played forward or backward. Drifts are conditional averages, not currents: they carry meaning only together with a consistently inferred diffusion tensor. Paired with the wrong one, they make passive particles look active. I show how to infer spurious drifts from trajectory moments alone, enabling thermodynamically consistent inference.

</details>


### [158] [Mechanical pairing and self-assembled chains of non-convex granular rotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14120)
*Thomas Barois,Melanie Labiausse,Jean-Francois Boudet,Juho S. Lintuvuori,Hamid Kellay*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验与数值模拟展示了具有旋转活性的颗粒转子（granular rotors）自组装成链状结构，并揭示双转子间吸引力由旋转活性与噪声之比及几何形状共同调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究非凸刚性体（non-convex rigid bodies）在旋转活性（rotational activity）驱动下的相互作用与自组装行为，理解活性与噪声竞争如何产生可控的凝聚结构。

Method: 采用实验与数值（experimental and numerical）相结合的方法：先系统研究两个转子之间的相互作用，再考察转子对（spinner pairs）凝聚态向链状结构的扩展。

Result: 发现双转子间存在吸引力（attractive interaction），其强度受旋转活性与噪声之比（ratio of rotational activity to noise）调控，并依赖转子几何形状；转子对的凝聚态可形成稳健的链（robust chains）。

Conclusion: 链状结构既可通过初始设定获得，也可经由自组装（self-assembly）自发形成，表明旋转活性颗粒具备构建可控链状聚集体的能力。

Abstract: We experimentally and numerically demonstrate the self-assembly of granular rotors into chain-like structures. The rotors are non-convex rigid bodies with rotational activity. We first investigate the interaction between two rotors and we establish an attractive interaction controlled by the ratio of the rotational activity to the noise and also dependent of the rotors geometry. We then show that the cohesive state between spinner pairs leads to robust chains that can be either initially set or spontaneously obtained by self-assembly.

</details>


### [159] [Cohesive granular piles with controlled volume fraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14128)
*Pierre Jodlowski,Marie-Julie Dalbe,Nicolas Vandenberghe*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出一种在圆柱容器中制备大尺寸湿颗粒样品（wet granular media）的规范化流程，并通过定量实验验证其可获得不同体积分数（volume fraction）且复现性良好的样品，结合微CT（micro-CT）扫描证实样品在局部与整体尺度上均具有均匀性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湿颗粒介质的组织状态在不同测量间易发生变化，导致实验结果不可复现（non reproducible results）；因此制备过程的严格控制与对颗粒堆微观结构（microstructure）的深入理解，是抑制实验间结果离散的关键。

Method: 设计一套在圆柱容器中制备大尺寸湿颗粒样品的实验协议（protocol），并给出关于该协议有效性的定量结果，包括可获取不同体积分数 φ₀ 的多种颗粒材料及其在同实验条件下的密度复现性；另使用微CT扫描仪进行X射线扫描（X-ray scans）以考察样品微观结构。

Result: 整体密度（bulk density）φ₀ 与局部密度（local one）φ 之间差异较小，表明样品具有均匀性（homogeneity）；在同一介质不同高度进行的多次扫描进一步证实了单个样品沿高度方向的均匀性。

Conclusion: 该制备协议能够有效控制湿颗粒样品的密度并保证其可复现性，所制样品在整体与局部尺度上均表现出良好的均匀性，为减小湿颗粒实验结果的离散性提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Experimental procedures involving wet granular media expose the sample to variation of its organization across different measurements, leading to non reproducible results. Rigorous control over the preparation process and a thorough understanding of the microstructure of the granular heap are key parameters to manage results dispersion from one experiment to an other. In this study, we present a protocol to prepare large samples of wet grains in a cylindrical container. Quantitative results are presented showing the effectiveness of the protocol regarding : the accessibility of different granular materials with different volume fraction $φ_0$ and the reproducibility of this said density obtained experimentally in the same experimental conditions. In addition, X-ray scans were performed using a micro-CT scanner to dive into the microstructure of the carefully prepared piles. Results show a small variation between the bulk density $φ_0$ and the local one $φ$, underlining the homogeneity in the samples. On top of that, several scans made in the same medium at different heights confirm the homogeneity across a given sample.

</details>


### [160] [Banding instabilities in poroelastic media under flow-driven compaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14235)
*Joseph J. Webber*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 交联多孔弹性介质在水压差下被压缩并形成稳态渗透剖面，但该稳态并非必然稳定；本文提出一种基于孔隙度—渗透率关系的流体力学失稳机制，导致水平或垂直带状/通道化图案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典多孔弹性模型将介质视为自由弹性球，忽略了交联与抗剪切变形的作用；该效应会抵抗压缩致密化，并可能引发尚未被研究的失稳与通道化现象。

Method: 在描述交联多孔弹性介质（cross-linked poroelastic media）受压后稳态剖面（取决于渗透、剪切与输运性质）的基础上，对稳态解进行稳定性分析，并提出失稳形成机制；进一步量化压力水头（pressure head）、剪切刚度（shear stiffness）及渗透率对孔隙度依赖对失稳增长率与特征间距的影响。

Result: 稳态剖面不一定稳定，可出现局部溶胀与压缩交替的水平或垂直带状结构；垂直带在形态上类似高渗透性通道（channelisation），可比拟颗粒介质中的虫孔或隧道。失稳增长率与间距尺度可随压力水头变化而被定量刻画，并受剪切刚度与渗透率—孔隙度关系调控。

Conclusion: 该失稳本质为流体力学效应，机制源于孔隙度—渗透率关系（porosity–permeability relationship）而非弹性效应；相关认识可启示地球物理、人体生物学与工业中的多孔弹性体系通道化问题。

Abstract: When cross-linked poroelastic media are subjected to a hydrostatic pressure difference, they compact, remaining fully swollen on their uppermost surface and becoming increasingly compressed with depth. A critical unexplored effect of cross-linking and resistance to shear deformation is investigated, which is not seen in classical models of poroelastic media treated as free elastic spheres, and resists the kind of compressive compaction that characterises these systems. Eventually, a steady-state profile dependent on osmotic, shear and transport properties is reached, supporting a uniform interstitial flow wherein the medium acts like a passive filter. In this paper, we show how these steady-state profiles are not necessarily stable, and propose a mechanism for the formation of instabilities, wherein some regions of the medium swell and others compact, forming distinctive horizontal or vertical banding patterns. In the latter case, these bands appear qualitatively to be higher-permeability conduits or channels, similar to wormholes or tunnels seen in granular media. It is possible to quantify the effect of changing the pressure head on the growth rate of instabilities and the separation lengthscale in this channelisation case, and how material properties like shear stiffness and dependence of permeability on porosity affect the dynamics of instability. Critically, it is shown that these instabilities are a fluid-mechanical effect, with an entirely new mechanism based on the porosity--permeability relationship and not on elastic effects. With an understanding of these mechanisms, we can derive insight into channelisation in poroelastic systems in geophysics, human biology and industry.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Spin-glass order in a field requires magnetic-field chaos in XY gauge glasses for $d\leq 4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14109)
*Manaka Okuyama,Masayuki Ohzeki,Hidetoshi Nishimori*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究 XY 规范玻璃（gauge-glass）模型，证明在 d≤4 且存在零场自旋玻璃相时，任何有限温度下按瑞利分布（Rayleigh distribution）逐点涨落的定向磁场，要么在自旋玻璃相中诱发磁场混沌（magnetic-field chaos），要么完全破坏自旋玻璃序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XY 规范玻璃模型被认为在 d≥3 时存在自旋玻璃相，但有限维系统中自旋玻璃相在磁场下的行为缺乏严格结论；本文旨在给出严格的定量约束。

Method: 通过规范变换（gauge transformation）将重叠（overlap）统计等价于高斯随机场中具有关联键无序的 XY 规范玻璃的磁化统计，并结合 Dario、Harel、Peled 关于连续自旋系统中随机场抑制铁磁序的定量估计，将其推广到依赖键的相互作用。

Result: 证明对任意固定非零场强，零场与非零场平衡自旋构型间平方重叠的无序平均在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下趋于零，从而得到上述二择一结论。

Conclusion: 若自旋玻璃相在该场下持续存在（如复制对称破缺（RSB）图像所预言），则必然呈现磁场混沌。该结果不判定自旋玻璃序是否在场中存活，故不能区分液滴（droplet）图像与 RSB 图像；严格均匀场的情形仍未解决。

Abstract: The XY gauge-glass model is believed to exhibit a spin-glass phase in dimensions $d\geq 3$. In this paper, we prove that, in dimensions $d\leq 4$ and at any finite temperature, a magnetic field applied in a fixed direction, with amplitudes that fluctuate from site to site according to the Rayleigh distribution, must either induce magnetic-field chaos in the spin-glass phase or destroy spin-glass order altogether, whenever a spin-glass phase exists at zero field. In particular, if a spin-glass phase persists under such a field, as predicted by the replica-symmetry-breaking (RSB) picture, it must exhibit magnetic-field chaos. This conclusion follows from our proof that, for any fixed nonzero field strength, the disorder average of the squared overlap between equilibrium spin configurations at zero field and those at nonzero field vanishes in the thermodynamic limit. By gauge transformations, the overlap statistics are identical to the magnetization statistics of an XY gauge glass with correlated bond disorder in a Gaussian random field. Our proof combines this exact identity with quantitative estimates on the suppression of ferromagnetic order by random fields in continuous-spin systems, due to Dario, Harel, and Peled [Commun. Math. Phys. 405, 212 (2024)] and building on the work of Imry and Ma [Phys. Rev. Lett. 35, 1399 (1975)] and of Aizenman and Wehr [Phys. Rev. Lett. 62, 2503 (1989); Commun. Math. Phys. 130, 489 (1990)]. We extend these estimates to bond-dependent interactions. Although our result provides a rare rigorous constraint on the nature of spin-glass phases in finite-dimensional systems, it does not determine whether spin-glass order survives in a field, and therefore does not distinguish between the droplet and RSB pictures. The case of a strictly uniform field remains open.

</details>


### [162] [Disorder-Induced Logarithmic Spreading and Localization of Quantum Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14200)
*Ang Yang,Jingxuan Zhang,Lei Ying*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 通过对比Bose–Hubbard模型与Gross–Pitaevskii方程，研究发现弱相互作用无序玻色系统中量子涨落在中段时间呈现对数扩散（logarithmic spreading），而非无无序时的弹道扩散，并最终饱和；扩散速率随无序强度代数式下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序（disorder）可局域化量子粒子，即使存在相互作用亦然，但其对超越平均场（mean-field）动力学的量子涨落的影响仍缺乏理解，因此研究弱相互作用无序玻色系统中量子涨落的传播规律。

Method: 比较Bose–Hubbard模型与Gross–Pitaevskii方程，考察随机无序（random disorder）与准周期无序（quasiperiodic disorder）下量子涨落的传播；并采用含时Bogoliubov–de Gennes框架，将局域化平均场波函数识别为产生涨落的含时配对背景。

Result: 与无无序系统的弹道扩散（ballistic spreading）不同，量子涨落在中段时间呈对数扩散后饱和；扩散速率随无序强度增加呈代数式（algebraically）减小，且该行为在不同粒子数下持续存在并延伸至二维。涨落产生速率随距离的指数抑制为对数推进的涨落前沿提供了物理机制。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一种由无序控制的量子涨落动力学新区域，并确立无序可作为调控量子涨落扩散的手段。

Abstract: Disorder can localize quantum particles, even in the presence of interactions, but its influence on quantum fluctuations beyond mean--field dynamics remains poorly understood. Here, we investigate the propagation of quantum fluctuations in weakly interacting disordered bosonic systems by comparing the Bose--Hubbard model with the Gross--Pitaevskii equation. We find that, in contrast to ballistic spreading in the disorder--free system, quantum fluctuations exhibit logarithmic spreading at intermediate times under both random and quasiperiodic disorder, before eventually saturating. The spreading rate decreases algebraically with increasing disorder strength, and this behavior persists across different particle numbers and extends to two dimensions. Using a time--dependent Bogoliubov--de Gennes framework, we identify the localized mean--field wavefunction as a time--dependent pairing background that generates quantum fluctuations. The exponential suppression of fluctuation--generation rates with distance provides a physical mechanism for the logarithmically advancing fluctuation front. Our results reveal a distinct regime of disorder--controlled quantum--fluctuation dynamics and establish disorder as a means of controlling their spreading.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Tripartite nonlocal magic from reflected and negativity spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13071)
*Sriram Akella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文给出三分纯态（tripartite pure state）与稳定子态（stabilizer state）在局域酉等价（local-unitary equivalence）下的充要判据：边缘谱、反射谱与负性谱（marginal, reflected, negativity spectra，至多差符号）均为平坦。据此以“反平坦度（anti-flatness）”量化三分非局域魔法（tripartite nonlocal magic），并给出可计算、可实验测量的诊断量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有魔法（magic）资源度量多针对单分或双分情形，缺乏对三分非局域魔法的可计算且可实验检验的判据，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以谱平坦性作为稳定子态等价判据，引入反平坦度量（anti-flatness）量化偏离程度，并用随机测量（randomized measurements）实现该诊断量的实验可测性。

Result: 在非阿贝尔量子双模型（non-abelian quantum doubles）中，该诊断量仅在三分划分的三重点（triple points）位于面（plaquettes）上时出现；在随机张量网络（random tensor networks）中，其出现恰对应两个边界区域纠缠楔（entanglement wedge）相连。

Conclusion: 谱平坦性为三分非局域魔法提供了统一判据；反平坦度诊断量兼具可计算性与实验可测性，并在拓扑序与全息纠缠结构中具有明确的物理表征。

Abstract: A tripartite pure state is local-unitary equivalent to a stabilizer state if and only if its marginal, reflected, and negativity spectra (up to signs) are flat. These conditions are quantified using anti-flatness, giving a diagnostic of tripartite nonlocal magic that is computable and experimentally measurable using randomized measurements. In non-abelian quantum doubles, it appears only when the triple points of the tripartition lie on plaquettes. In random tensor networks, it appears exactly when the entanglement wedge of two boundary regions connects.

</details>


### [164] [Four-charge Rotating AdS Black Holes in Gauged STU Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13074)
*Liang Ma,H. Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出了四维规范STU超引力中含四个独立电荷的非极端旋转AdS黑洞的精确解，并验证其BPS极限下的熵与对偶共形场论的微观结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范化（gauging）引入的标量势破坏了无规范理论中解生成方法所依赖的隐藏对称性（hidden symmetry），使得此类解长期难以构造。

Method: 构造四维规范STU超引力中带四个独立电荷的非极端旋转AdS黑洞精确解，分析其整体性质并导出热力学量（thermodynamic quantities），进而取BPS极限。

Result: 得到了精确解及其整体性质与热力学量；在BPS极限下，所得熵（entropy）与对偶共形场论（conformal field theory）给出的已知微观结果相符。

Conclusion: 该精确解克服了规范理论中标量势破坏隐藏对称性带来的构造困难，且其BPS极限熵与微观计数一致，为规范STU超引力黑洞的热力学与全息检验提供了支持。

Abstract: We present an exact solutions for non-extremal rotating AdS black holes with four independent charges in four-dimensional gauged STU supergravity. The scalar potential introduced by gauging breaks the hidden symmetry underlying solution-generating methods in the ungauged theory, making the construction of such solutions a longstanding challenge. We analyse the global properties of the solution and derive its thermodynamic quantities. We further take the BPS limit and show that the resulting entropy agrees with the known microscopic result obtained from the dual conformal field theory.

</details>


### [165] [More physics at the entangling surface: boundary conditions and entanglement in 2d TQFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13078)
*Jackson R. Fliss,Alan Rios Fukelman,Stathis Vitouladitis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文系统研究二维拓扑量子场论（TQFT）中的纠缠，指出纠缠度量依赖于实现希尔伯特空间分解的切割映射（cutting maps），并构造等距（isometric）切割映射以给出良定义的纠缠计算方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠度量在TQFT中对实现希尔伯特空间分解的切割映射敏感，而仅基于受边界条件约束的路径积分的朴素切割映射对一般TQFT并非等距，故需澄清并修正纠缠的定义方式。

Method: 通过用适当的边算符（edge operators）修饰路径积分，为任意二维TQFT构造等距切割映射，从而可对任意态、任意二分（bipartition）与任意边界条件计算纠缠。

Result: 发现尽管所有边界条件均有能隙，仍出现类似Affleck-Ludwig的非整数“基态简并”；可由单一态的多区间Rényi熵（Rényi entropies）重构任意亏格（genus）的配分函数；对称基态的纠缠熵可区分具有相同对称性的不同能隙相，真空的纠缠不对称性（entanglement asymmetry）以熵方式探测对称性破缺。

Conclusion: 该框架统一处理二维TQFT纠缠问题，并应用于有限群规范理论（finite gauge theories）、Haagerup能隙相及伴随QCD₂（adjoint QCD₂）的红外相。

Abstract: We present a systematic treatment of entanglement in two-dimensional topological quantum field theories (TQFTs). Our approach clarifies the sensitivity of entanglement measures to the cutting maps that implement Hilbert space factorization. Such maps embed the TQFT Hilbert space into a description with potentially more degrees of freedom, and for entanglement to be well defined they must be isometric, i.e., they must preserve the norms of all states. We show that the naïve cutting map based solely upon path integration subject to boundary conditions is not isometric for generic TQFTs. For any 2d TQFT, we give a prescription to construct isometric cutting maps by dressing path integration with suitable edge operators. This allows us to compute entanglement for any state, any bipartition, and any boundary condition. As a consequence, we find a non-integer "ground-state degeneracy" à la Affleck-Ludwig, despite all boundary conditions being gapped. We further show how to reconstruct the partition function of the TQFT at arbitrary genus from the multi-interval Rényi entropies of a single state. For theories with (possibly non-invertible) symmetries, we show that the entanglement entropy of the symmetric ground state can distinguish gapped phases with the same symmetry, without any further knowledge of their vacua, while the entanglement asymmetry of the vacua entropically detects symmetry breaking. We illustrate our formalism with a range of applications, from finite gauge theories to Haagerup gapped phases and infrared phases of adjoint QCD$_2$.

</details>


### [166] [Type IIB instanton corrections from pure modular unfolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13086)
*Amineh Mohseni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用模对称性（modular symmetry），仅从低傅里叶模式（low Fourier modes）重建 IIB 型 higher-derivative 相互作用的瞬子修正，无需借助超对称微分方程，并对 D^6R^4 系数给出可与已知展开对照的弱耦合瞬子前因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖超对称微分方程来重构瞬子修正，本文希望仅凭模对称性从低模数据完成重建，从而为研究非 BPS 相互作用（non-BPS interactions）提供基于模系数函数的通用框架。

Method: 对 D^6R^4 系数，用 Rankin-Selberg 展开（Rankin-Selberg unfolding）由完整零模确定连续谱重叠；在假定偶尖点谱（even cusp spectrum）具有猜想性简单结构下，由完整第一模的 Mellin 留数提取尖点振幅，并结合 Hecke 关系重建全部高傅里叶模式；再用 Kuznetsov 迹公式（Kuznetsov trace formula）与谱尾界进行控制。

Result: 推导出每个固定正电荷下弱耦合瞬子前因子中 g_s^{-1/2} 项与常数项，其中首项由无穷尖点求和集体产生，两个系数均与已知展开一致；在低模数据不完整时构造模歧义（modular ambiguities）并给出高电荷首项系数稳定的充分条件，对 D^8R^4 数据识别出实现更高电荷预言所需的额外信息。

Conclusion: 模对称性可在不引入超对称微分方程的前提下重建 higher-derivative 相互作用的瞬子修正，并为非 BPS 相互作用的低模数据分析与高电荷预言提供可操作框架。

Abstract: We study the reconstruction of instanton corrections to type IIB higher-derivative interactions from their low Fourier modes using modular symmetry, without invoking supersymmetry differential equations. This provides a framework for studying non-BPS interactions through their modular coefficient functions. For the $D^6R^4$ coefficient, Rankin-Selberg unfolding determines the continuous spectral overlap from the complete zero mode. Assuming the conjectured simplicity of the even cusp spectrum, we extract the cusp amplitudes from Mellin residues of the complete first mode. We combine both contributions to reconstruct all higher Fourier modes, using Hecke relations for the cusp contribution. Using the Kuznetsov trace formula and bounds on the spectral tail, we derive the $g_s^{-1/2}$ and constant terms in the weak-coupling instanton prefactor at every fixed positive charge. The leading term arises collectively from the infinite cusp sum, and both coefficients agree with the known expansion. With incomplete low-mode data, we construct modular ambiguities and give sufficient conditions for the stability of leading higher-charge coefficients. Applied to the supplied $D^8R^4$ data, these results identify the additional information needed for higher-charge predictions.

</details>


### [167] [Holographic Drag Force and the Entanglement Membrane: Geometric Reconstruction and Information Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13088)
*Dmitry S. Ageev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了全息（holography）框架下运动重夸克所受耗散阻力（drag force）与纠缠膜（entanglement membrane）之间的精确几何对应，并据此建立起非线性耗散、蝴蝶速度（butterfly velocity）、极点跳跃（pole-skipping）与信息速度之间的全阶关系，进而实现体几何（bulk geometry）的重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息理论的核心问题之一是不同的边界可观测量如何编码并重构引力对偶的几何。作者试图沟通强耦合动力学中两个看似无关的方面——运动重粒子的耗散阻力与支配量子信息传播的纠缠膜。

Method: 通过构造阻力与膜张力之间的可逆变换，表明两者共享同一底层几何结构；并将非线性动量损失的各阶修正依次对应到蝴蝶速度、近视界（near-horizon）膜局域几何以及标量极点跳跃，同时利用随机动量展宽（stochastic momentum broadening）补充径向信息以重构外度量。

Result: 非线性修正的首阶确定蝴蝶速度，高阶项逐次探测近视界膜的局域几何，并给出非线性耗散与近视界谱数据的全阶关系；五次阻力系数决定信息速度对初始纠缠的首阶依赖，完整信息速度曲线可重构物理内部膜分支上的约化体几何，随机动量展宽则可重构完整外度量至整体标度。

Conclusion: 耗散、纠缠动力学与谱性质以互补方式提供对全息几何的访问，从而建立一个统一框架，用以理解不同边界可观测量所编码的几何信息。

Abstract: A central question in holography is how different boundary observables encode and reconstruct the geometry of the gravitational dual. In this work, we uncover an exact geometric correspondence between two seemingly distinct aspects of strongly coupled dynamics: the dissipative drag force experienced by a moving heavy particle(quark) and the entanglement membrane governing the propagation of quantum information. We show that both constructions retain the same underlying structure of the geometry, leading to an invertible transformation between the finite-velocity drag force and the geometric membrane tension. This correspondence reveals that nonlinear momentum loss encodes detailed information about entanglement dynamics: the leading nonlinear correction determines the butterfly velocity, while higher-order terms successively probe the local geometry of the membrane near the horizon. We further connect this hierarchy to scalar pole-skipping, establishing an all-order relation between nonlinear dissipation and near-horizon spectral data. The shared geometric structure also has implications for quantum information recovery, with the quintic drag coefficient determining the leading dependence of information velocity on initial entanglement. Conversely, the complete information-velocity curve provides a reconstruction of the reduced bulk geometry along the physical interior membrane branch, complementing the exterior reconstruction from drag. We show that stochastic momentum broadening supplies the additional radial information needed to reconstruct the complete exterior metric up to an overall scale. Our results thus reveal how dissipation, entanglement dynamics, and spectral properties provide complementary access to holographic geometry, establishing a unified framework for understanding the geometric information encoded in distinct boundary observables.

</details>


### [168] [Moduli spaces in CFT: vortex defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13092)
*Gabriel Cuomo,Adar Sharon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在自发破缺共形对称性的共形场论中构造了一类余维二共形缺陷，通过连续无界参数连接量子区与半经典区，并由此从破缺相的有效描述导出精确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有BPS缺陷（如Gukov-Witten面算子、ABJM涡旋环）依赖超对称，且缺乏不依赖超对称的统一构造；本文旨在给出更一般的余维二共形缺陷框架。

Method: 假设模空间上连续整体对称性亦破缺，将大荷算子鞍点进行解析延拓以构造缺陷；缺陷由控制模空间算子期望值的连续无界参数刻画，并在量子区与半经典区之间插值，借助破缺相有效描述求精确量。

Result: Gukov-Witten面算子结果与局域化（localization）计算一致；计算了ABJM理论中一大类1/2-BPS涡旋环的g函数（g-function）；并在适当标度极限下将缺陷算子谱与破缺真空中的大质量粒子谱相联系。

Conclusion: 该构造在不依赖超对称的前提下统一并推广了已知BPS缺陷，表明破缺相有效描述可给出缺陷物理的精确结果，并揭示缺陷算子谱与对称性破缺真空中粒子谱的对应关系。

Abstract: We construct and study a class of codimension-two conformal defects in CFTs with spontaneously broken conformal symmetry, assuming that a continuous global symmetry is also broken on the moduli space. Our construction can be viewed as an analytic continuation of the saddle point describing large charge operators and generalizes known BPS defects in supersymmetric theories, such as Gukov-Witten surface operators in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM and vortex loops in ABJM theory, but does not rely on supersymmetry. These defects are characterized by continuous, unbounded parameters controlling the expectation values of operators parametrizing the moduli space, and interpolate between a quantum regime and a semiclassical regime in which the local physics approaches that of the symmetry-breaking vacuum. This connection allows us to derive exact results from the effective description of the broken phase. In particular, our results for Gukov-Witten surface operators agree with localization, and we compute the $g$-function for a broad class of $1/2$-BPS vortex loops in ABJM theory. Finally, we relate the spectrum of defect operators, in a suitable scaling limit, to the spectrum of massive particles in the symmetry-breaking vacuum.

</details>


### [169] [Categorical RMT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13093)
*Chuanxin Cui,Kristan Jensen,Sridip Pal,Moshe Rozali*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出“范畴随机矩阵理论（categorical RMT）”，将随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory, RMT）推广到圆周上具有普通或非可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetries）的局域量子系统，并由混沌的 Fibonacci 任意子链与 S_3 链予以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 RMT 只能逐个对称性扇区（symmetry sector）描述混沌系统能级统计，无法处理对称性缺陷导致的扭曲希尔伯特空间（twisted Hilbert spaces）以及非可逆对称性下不同扭曲之间共享扇区的情形。

Method: 以对称性缺陷定义扭曲希尔伯特空间，取管代数（tube algebra）的不可约表示作为对称性扇区；对称性固定精确简并与扇区间等同关系，其余部分按普通 RMT 处理，互不相关的块视为统计独立。框架进一步推广到 1+1 维共形场论（CFT）中固定自旋的主态（primary states），并结合连续对称性的 Sugawara 构造与谱流（spectral flow）以及全息 CFT 中的环面虫洞（torus wormholes）进行讨论。

Result: 在混沌 Fibonacci 任意子链中得到确认，并在混沌 S_3 链中检验了该框架；对非可逆对称性，不同扭曲的希尔伯特空间可共享扇区从而共享部分谱，这是普通 RMT 无法描述的新现象。在 CFT 情形下，模协变性（modular covariance）要求额外关联；中性谱即使在玻色理论中也可呈现辛统计（symplectic statistics）；全息 CFT 中由 SymTFT 或 Chern–Simons 理论修饰的环面虫洞实现了预言的 ramp 及其模完备化（modular completion）。

Conclusion: 范畴 RMT 为含普通与非可逆对称性的混沌局域系统提供了统一的能级统计描述，并自然延伸至 1+1 维 CFT 与全息背景，预言可由虫洞构型实现。

Abstract: Random matrix theory (RMT) describes the level statistics of chaotic systems one symmetry sector at a time. We propose its analogue for local quantum systems on a circle with ordinary or non-invertible symmetries. Symmetry defects define twisted Hilbert spaces, and the symmetry sectors are irreducible representations of the tube algebra, which acts within and between these spaces. Under our proposal, the symmetry fixes exact degeneracies and identifications among sectors, and ordinary RMT describes the rest, with unrelated blocks treated as statistically independent. We call this categorical RMT. For a non-invertible symmetry it goes beyond ordinary RMT, since Hilbert spaces with different twists can share a sector, and so part of their spectrum, though no invertible symmetry relates them.
  We confirm this in a chaotic Fibonacci anyon chain, and check the framework in chaotic $S_3$ chains. We extend categorical RMT to primary states at fixed spin in 1+1-dimensional CFTs, where modular covariance requires additional correlations. For continuous symmetries, the Sugawara construction and spectral flow isolate a neutral spectrum, which can have symplectic statistics even in a bosonic theory. In holographic CFTs, torus wormholes decorated by the SymTFT or by Chern--Simons theory realize the predicted ramp and its modular completion.

</details>


### [170] [BPS Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13097)
*Yiming Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文将大 N 矩阵自举（large N matrix bootstrap）方法应用于约束对偶于经典几何的重型 BPS 态中简单算符的期望值，在弱耦合、大 N 的 N=4 SU(N) 超杨-米尔斯理论中检验了 1/2、1/4、1/8-BPS 扇区，并给出 1/16-BPS 扇区的初步结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于利用自举方法区分对偶于无视界几何（horizonless geometries）与黑洞（black holes）的重型 BPS 态：前者因显式 moduli 应产生较大的允许区域，后者则应收敛为自举岛（islands），从而为探测黑洞微观态提供手段。

Method: 采用大 N 矩阵自举技术，在弱耦合下研究大 N 的 N=4 SU(N) 超杨-米尔斯理论（super Yang-Mills theory），聚焦标度维数（scaling dimension）量级为 N^2 的 BPS 态，并对 1/16-BPS 扇区采用 BMN 截断（BMN truncation）。

Result: 在 1/2、1/4、1/8-BPS 扇区，自举给出较大的允许区域，与大电荷极限下这些态的本征值描述（eigenvalue description）一致；1/16-BPS 扇区的 BMN 截断包含对偶于无视界几何与黑洞的两类态。

Conclusion: 结果表明自举方法可用于刻画重型 BPS 态的几何对偶结构，但在自举中隔离黑洞态仍是一项挑战。

Abstract: We apply the large $N$ matrix bootstrap technique to constrain expectation values of simple operators in heavy BPS states that are dual to classical geometries. For states dual to horizonless geometries, their explicit moduli give rise to large allowable regions in the bootstrap, while for black holes, a converging bootstrap should find islands. We explore this method in $\mathcal{N}=4$ $SU(N)$ super Yang-Mills theory at large $N$, working at weak coupling and focusing on BPS states with scaling dimensions of order $N^2$. For the $1/2$, $1/4$, and $1/8$-BPS sectors, the bootstrap finds large regions, in agreement with an eigenvalue description of such states in the limit of large charges. We also present preliminary results for a BMN truncation of the $1/16$-BPS sector, which contains states dual to both horizonless geometries and black holes. Isolating the black hole states in the bootstrap remains a challenge.

</details>


### [171] [Effective scalar-fluid description of thermal first-order phase transitions and their spinodal instabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13102)
*Alessio Caddeo,Oscar Henriksson,Carlos Hoyos,Mikel Sanchez-Garitaonandia,Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出改进的标量-流体有效描述（scalar-fluid effective description），用于处理相对论性理论中的一级相变（first-order phase transition），并在全息模型（holographic model）的微观计算中得到验证；结果表明 spinodal 不稳定性的性质由比热（specific heat）的符号决定，并识别出一个控制气泡壁速度（bubble wall velocity）的摩擦系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯流体力学描述（hydrodynamic description）在描述一级相变附近的稳定性行为时存在不足，需要更精确的长波有效理论，并需与微观计算对照检验。

Method: 构建改进的标量-流体有效理论，分析 spinodal 不稳定性的模式结构及其与热力学稳定性（thermodynamic stability）的关联；同时在全息模型中作微观计算进行对比验证，并从全息关联函数（holographic correlator）中提取摩擦系数。

Result: 有效理论显示：比热为正时声学模式（sound modes）稳定、存在非流体力学不稳定性（non-hydrodynamic instability）；比热为负时某一 sound mode 变为不稳定。全息计算在长波长下确认了该有效描述的有效性，优于纯流体力学描述。

Conclusion: 标量-流体有效描述在长波极限下经全息验证成立，spinodal 不稳定性类型由比热符号决定；控制气泡壁速度的摩擦系数可由全息关联函数提取。

Abstract: We formulate an improved scalar-fluid effective description of first-order phase transitions in relativistic theories, and test it against a microscopic calculation in a holographic model. The effective theory shows that the nature of the spinodal instability is linked to thermodynamic stability, determined by the sign of the specific heat. When the specific heat is positive, the sound modes are stable and there is a non-hydrodynamic instability. When it is negative, a sound mode becomes unstable instead. The holographic calculation confirms the validity of the effective description at long wavelengths, improving on the purely hydrodynamic description. We also identify a friction coefficient that governs the bubble wall velocity and can be extracted from a holographic correlator.

</details>


### [172] [Resummations In Higher-Curvature Modifications Of Starobinsky Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13112)
*Marco Piva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文针对 Starobinsky 暴胀（Starobinsky inflation）的高曲率修正，通过重求和（resummation）α/γ 的特定级数构造“改进”慢滚展开（improved slow-roll expansion），使 γ→0 极限正则并与先按 γ 或先按 α 展开结果一致，且与最新观测数据相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维算符耦合 γ 的微扰处理会产生慢滚参数 α 的负幂，威胁慢滚展开的可靠性；而将 γ 保持精确时，展开在 γ→0 又无法平滑退化为 Starobinsky 模型。因此需要一种在两极限下均自洽的展开方案。

Method: 对 α/γ 比值的特定级数进行重求和（resummation），构造在 γ→0 极限下正则的“改进”慢滚展开；以 R^3 项为具体例子，计算改进展开次领头阶（next-to-leading order）的功率谱（power spectra）、谱指数（spectral indices）与张标比（tensor-to-scalar ratio）。

Result: 改进展开在 γ→0 时正则，且先按 γ 或先按 α 展开给出相同结果；近期文献结果可作为该结果的一阶泰勒展开被重现。与最新 ACT、Planck 与 SPT 数据比较，重求和结果与一阶结果仅在 n_s 处于偏好区域之外时差异明显。

Conclusion: 重求和方案解决了高曲率修正下慢滚展开的自洽性问题。文中还讨论了紫外完备理论（如可重整量子引力（renormalizable quantum gravity））中高曲率算符的起源，其中 Starobinsky 模型的预言是稳定的。

Abstract: We study higher-curvature corrections to Starobinsky inflation, which are expected to be generated at the quantum level. When treated perturbatively in the couplings $γ$ of the higher-dimensional operators, these terms produce inverse powers of the slow-roll parameter $α$, casting doubt on the reliability of the slow-roll expansion. If instead $γ$ is kept exact, the expansion fails to reproduce the Starobinsky model smoothly as $γ\to 0$. We show that this problem is solved by resumming certain series in the ratio $α/γ$. The resulting ``improved'' slow-roll expansion is regular in the limit $γ\to 0$ and gives the same result whether one expands first in $γ$ or in $α$. We illustrate this explicitly for an $R^3$ term, obtain the power spectra, spectral indices and tensor-to-scalar ratio at next-to-leading order in the improved expansion, and show that results in the recent literature are recovered as the first-order Taylor expansion of ours. Comparing with the latest ACT, Planck and SPT data, we find that the resummed and first-order results differ appreciably only for values of $n_s$ outside the favored regions. Finally, we comment on the origin of higher-curvature operators in UV-complete theories, such as renormalizable quantum gravity, where the predictions of the Starobinsky model are stable.

</details>


### [173] [Expiscating the black hole singularity in holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13261)
*Hewei Frederic Jia,Mukund Rangamani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本工作论证全息热两点函数在高频渐近下的主导指数率与算符的共形维数及（固定的）自旋无关，并借助精确WKB技术给出谱函数在微扰（OPE控制）与非微扰（WKB输运控制）部分的因子化结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清黑洞曲率奇点（curvature singularity）如何在对偶场论可观测量的平面展开（planar expansion）领头阶中被编码，并理解高频极限下全息热两点函数渐近行为的普适性来源。

Method: 分析主导的普适WKB谱曲线（WKB spectral curve），考察动量随频率线性及与维数相关的次线性标度两种区域；利用精确WKB技术（exact-WKB）推导边界与视界之间的连接矩阵（connection matrix），并在次线性区域将近奇点标度方程的精确解与外部WKB解匹配。

Result: 谱曲线的经典作用量主导三类时间域特征：弹跳测地线奇点（bouncing geodesic singularity）、强阻尼准正模（quasinormal modes）及多重应力张量OPE系数的渐近。谱函数因子化为OPE控制的微扰部分与WKB输运控制的非微扰部分；线性标度下控制物理输运的转折点与奇点分离，但奇点锚定的同伦类代表决定主导WKB作用量；次线性区转折点趋近奇点。

Conclusion: 高频渐近的领先指数率具有与算符维数和自旋无关的普适性；黑洞曲率奇点在领头平面阶通过对偶场论两点函数的WKB作用量与连接矩阵被系统编码，具体机制依赖于动量随频率的标度方式。

Abstract: We argue that the leading exponential rate in the high frequency asymptotics of holographic thermal two-point functions is independent of the conformal dimension and spin (held fixed) of the operator. We analyze the leading universal WKB spectral curve governing the asymptotics at non-vanishing momentum scaling with frequency. We show that its classical action governs time domain signatures of the bouncing geodesic singularity, highly damped quasinormal modes, and the asymptotics of multi-stress tensor OPE coefficients. Beyond the leading order, we examine linear and dimension-dependent sublinear scalings of momentum with frequency. In both regimes, we derive the connection matrix between the boundary and the horizon using exact-WKB techniques. The results show a factorization of the spectral function into a perturbative part controlled by the OPE and a non-perturbative part governed by WKB transport. For linear scaling at fixed $k/ω$ in the timelike regime, the turning points controlling physical transport remain separated from the curvature singularity. Nevertheless, a singularity-anchored representative of the same transport homology class determines the leading WKB action. In the sublinear regime, the relevant turning points approach the singularity. We match an exact solution of the near-singularity scaled equation to the exterior WKB solution to determine the connection matrix and spectral function. Our results clarify how the black hole curvature singularity is encoded in dual field theory observables at leading order in the planar expansion.

</details>


### [174] [Rotational instability and quadrupolar order in BFSS matrix theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13122)
*Jun Liu,Jorge E. Santos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究BFSS矩阵理论在引力极限与高温经典矩阵极限下，带单一非零角动量的热态行为：引力侧发现非轴对称四极变形黑洞分支更受热力学青睐，而经典矩阵侧未观测到该集体序，取而代之的是旋转促进单个D0膜分离并带走大量角动量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究角动量（angular momentum）对BFSS矩阵理论（BFSS matrix theory）热态的影响，并检验引力图像与经典矩阵图像在高角动量下的对应关系。

Method: 在引力侧，用单自旋D0黑洞（singly-spinning D0 black hole）描述旋转系综，对临界旋转以上的非轴对称四极微扰（non-axisymmetric quadrupolar perturbation）作线性稳定性分析与非线性演化；在经典矩阵极限下，对不同矩阵尺寸与角动量进行数值模拟，考察集体四极序与单膜分离行为。

Result: 引力侧发现超过临界旋转后单自旋D0黑洞对非轴对称四极微扰失稳，沿该模式非线性演化得到新的非轴对称旋转黑洞分支，其在微正则系综（microcanonical ensemble）与正则系综（canonical ensemble）中均优于轴对称分支；经典矩阵侧在所研究的矩阵尺寸与角动量范围内未发现此类集体四极序，而是旋转加速单个D0膜从膜团中分离，该膜以大半径轨道带走很大比例角动量，且其角动量份额与相对轨道半径随矩阵尺寸增大而增加。

Conclusion: 结果区分了引力极限下的集体四极变形与经典矩阵极限下的角动量转移至单膜两种机制，为检验矩阵理论/引力对偶在旋转热态中的一致性提供了可区分的判据。

Abstract: We study the effect of angular momentum on thermal states of the BFSS matrix theory in its gravitational and high-temperature classical regimes. We focus on ensembles with only one non-zero angular momentum. On the gravity side, the rotating ensemble is described by the singly-spinning D$0$ black hole. Beyond a critical rotation, we find that this solution becomes unstable to a non-axisymmetric quadrupolar perturbation. Following this mode non-linearly, we find a new branch of non-axisymmetric rotating black holes that is thermodynamically favoured over the axisymmetric branch in both the microcanonical and canonical ensembles. On the other hand, in the classical matrix limit, we find no evidence for such collective quadrupolar order over the matrix sizes and angular momenta studied. Instead, rotation accelerates the separation of a single D$0$-brane from the clump of branes initially prepared. The separated brane carries a large fraction of the angular momentum by orbiting the rest of the clump at a large in-plane radius. Both the angular momentum share and the orbital radius relative to the clump size increase with the matrix size. These results distinguish a collective quadrupolar deformation in the gravity limit from angular-momentum transfer to a single brane in the classical matrix limit.

</details>


### [175] [Scale separation and partial supersymmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13130)
*Niccolò Cribiori,Fotis Farakos,Alexandros Zarafonitis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出弦理论（string theory）新的候选真空解：在反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）空间中的低维有效理论里，部分超对称破缺（partial supersymmetry breaking）发生在参数化低于 Kaluza–Klein 能标的尺度上，并具备参数化的尺度分离（parametric scale separation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究中，部分超对称破缺与尺度分离通常难以在低于紧致化能标的能量尺度上同时实现，作者希望构造可在远低于紧致化能标处研究部分超对称破缺的显式弦理论框架。

Method: 采用质量为零的 IIA 型超引力（massless type IIA supergravity）在涂抹近似（smeared approximation）下求解运动方程；并将这些解识别为三维 $\mathcal N=2$ 规范超引力（gauged supergravity）的 $\mathcal N=1$ 超对称相。

Result: 所得真空解在弱耦合与强耦合下均表现出参数化尺度分离；同时作者对其先前提出的、具有尺度分离与扩展超对称（extended supersymmetry）的解作了进一步评述。

Conclusion: 若这些真空确为弦理论的解，则将提供可在参数化低于紧致化能标的能量范围内研究部分超对称破缺的显式构造。

Abstract: We propose new candidate string theory vacua in which partial supersymmetry breaking occurs within the lower-dimensional effective theory in anti-de Sitter space, parametrically below the Kaluza--Klein scale. These vacua solve the equations of motion of massless type IIA supergravity in the smeared approximation and exhibit parametric scale separation, at both weak and strong string coupling. They are reproduced as $\mathcal N=1$ supersymmetric phases of a three-dimensional $\mathcal N=2$ gauged supergravity. Should these vacua be solutions of string theory, they would provide explicit setups in which partial supersymmetry breaking can be studied at energies parametrically below the compactification scale. In addition, we further comment on our previously proposed solution with scale separation and extended supersymmetry.

</details>


### [176] [General non-extremal rotating charged AdS black hole solutions with four independent charge parameters in $D=4$, STU gauged supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13151)
*Di Wu,Shuang-Qing Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出四维 $\mathcal{N}=2, U(1)^4$ 规范超引力中带负宇宙学常数的非极端旋转带电黑洞的精确解，含七个独立参数，并研究其热力学性质与可分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Cvetič-Youm 黑洞解未覆盖规范超引力（gauged supergravity）情形，需要一个含宇宙学常数、旋转与电磁荷的普适非极端精确解作为研究 AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$ 对应与微观熵的新背景。

Method: 在四维 $\mathcal{N}=2, U(1)^4$ 规范超引力框架下构造精确解，含质量、转动、两个电场荷与两个磁荷及非零宇宙学常数共七个独立参数；分析其热力学性质，并给出保证 Hamilton-Jacobi 与无质量 Klein-Gordon 方程可分离的 Killing-Stäckel 张量（Killing-Stäckel tensor）。

Result: 得到推广 Cvetič-Youm 解至规范超引力情形的普适非极端解；作者预期这是四维规范 STU 模型中唯一无 Misner 弦奇异性（Misner string singularity）、且对称轴上无 C-metric 型锥形缺陷的正则黑洞解；并讨论若干已知特例与 BPS 极限。

Conclusion: 该解为检验 AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$ 对应、以及通过 BPS 指标（BPS index）极值化研究微观熵提供了新背景。

Abstract: We present an exact solution for generic non-extremal rotating, charged black holes with a negative cosmological constant in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2, U(1)^4$ gauged supergravity. This general non-extremal solution contains seven independent parameters related to the mass, rotation, two electric and two magnetic charges, as well as a nonzero cosmological constant. It generalizes the four-dimensional Cvetič-Youm black hole solution to the gauged supergravity case. We anticipate that this is the only regular black hole solution in four-dimensional gauged STU model without any Misner string singularity and/or C-metric-like conical defect on the symmetry axes. We also investigate its thermodynamic property and give the Killing-Stäckel tensor responsible for the separability of Hamilton-Jacobi and massless Klein-Gordon equations. Various special cases related to the known solution are discussed, and its BPS limit is also analyzed. Our solution provides a new background to test the AdS$_4$/CFT$_3$ correspondence and to study the microscopic entropy via extremization of the BPS index.

</details>


### [177] [Nuclear geometry under external magnetic field in the Skyrme-Maxwell model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13152)
*Christoph Adam,Andrés R. Anadón,Carlos Naya,Thomas Winyard*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作求解了恒定外磁场中U(1)规范Skyrme-Maxwell模型的静态解（重子数B≤6），发现规范skyrmion比非规范情形能量更低、束缚更强，且随磁场增强其几何形态由轻微变形演化为环面链与轴对称环面结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究外磁场作为同位旋破缺源（isospin-breaking source）如何影响规范skyrmion（gauged skyrmion）的能量与形状，并考察不同重子数扇区下的解结构。

Method: 在恒定外磁场下求解U(1)规范Skyrme-Maxwell模型（U(1) gauged Skyrme-Maxwell model）的静态解，覆盖重子数B≤6；采用基于GPU实现的arrested Newton flow（ANF）算法获取能量极小解（energy minimizers）。

Result: 磁场沿其方向拉伸skyrmion，其尺寸随磁场强度先增大后减小；规范skyrmion较非规范对应解能量更低、束缚更强。弱磁场下解为非规范skyrmion的小变形，B=3、5、6扇区出现负重子密度（negative baryon density）区域；中等磁场出现新几何，尤以(B-1)环面链（toroidal chain）为著；强磁场下所有B均为轴对称环面（axially symmetric toroidal）结构。

Conclusion: 外磁场显著改变规范skyrmion的能量、束缚性质与拓扑几何形态，且形态随磁场强度呈现分阶段的定性转变。

Abstract: We find static solutions of the U(1) gauged Skyrme-Maxwell model in a constant external magnetic field, for baryon numbers $B \leq 6$. The field acts as an isospin-breaking source, stretching the skyrmion along its direction, while its size grows and then shrinks as the induction increases. We obtain the energy minimizers with a GPU implementation of the arrested Newton flow (ANF) algorithm. Gauged skyrmions have less energy and are more strongly bound than their ungauged counterpart. At weak magnetic field, the solutions are small deformations of the ungauged skyrmions, including regions of negative baryon density in sectors $B = 3, 5, 6$. For intermediate field, novel geometries appear, notably a $(B-1)$-toroidal chain. High field values lead to axially symmetric toroidal structures for all $B$.

</details>


### [178] [Quantum Langlands correspondence for Painlevé II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13639)
*Sibasish Banerjee,Babak Haghighat,Jie Sun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从秩三 Wakimoto-Whittaker 模出发，构造了量子 Painlevé II 的非规则 KZ-BPZ 对应，给出矩阵积分形式的解，并借助精确量子 Langlands 对偶实现仿射解的重构与经典/NS 极限的互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为量子 Painlevé II 建立非规则 KZ 方程与 BPZ 方程之间的对应关系（irregular KZ-BPZ correspondence），并厘清仿射系统与标量共形块方程解之间的关联机制。

Method: 基于秩三 Wakimoto-Whittaker 模（rank-three Wakimoto-Whittaker module）构造非规则 KZ 方程与量子等单值方程（quantum isomonodromy equation）组成的联合仿射系统，经 Drinfeld-Sokolov 约化（Drinfeld-Sokolov reduction）得到标量 BPZ 方程；对一个基本插入（fundamental insertion）求矩阵积分解，并利用精确量子 Langlands 对偶（quantum Langlands duality）完成解的重构。

Result: 得到含单个基本插入时该联合系统的矩阵积分解（matrix-integral solutions）；证明精确量子 Langlands 对偶将基本插入与对偶 Whittaker 微分之零点互换，从而由 BPZ 方程重构仿射解。

Conclusion: 该对偶在经典极限（classical limit）与 Nekrasov-Shatashvili 极限（Nekrasov-Shatashvili limit）之间互换，分别给出 Painlevé II 的 Lax 联络（Lax connection）与非规则 Gaudin oper，表明 KZ-BPZ 对应与量子 Langlands 对偶在量子 Painlevé 系统中统一成立。

Abstract: We construct an irregular KZ-BPZ correspondence for quantum Painlevé II from a rank-three Wakimoto-Whittaker module. The irregular KZ equation and the quantum isomonodromy equation form a joint affine system whose Drinfeld-Sokolov reduction yields scalar BPZ equations. We obtain matrix-integral solutions of this system for one fundamental insertion. An exact quantum Langlands duality exchanges the fundamental insertion with the zero of the dual Whittaker differential and allows affine solutions to be reconstructed from the BPZ equations. This duality interchanges the classical and Nekrasov-Shatashvili limits, which yield the Painlevé II Lax connection and an irregular Gaudin oper, respectively.

</details>


### [179] [Giant Graviton Correlators: From Non-maximal to Dual, Bootstrap and Hidden Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13444)
*Qi Chen,Zhongjie Huang,Yunfeng Jiang,Xinan Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在大 $N$ 与无穷 't Hooft 耦合下计算了 $\mathcal{N}=4$ 超杨–米尔斯理论（super Yang–Mills theory）中两个轻 $\tfrac12$-BPS 算符与两个巨引力子算符的四点函数，发现其具有隐藏的十维共形对称性（ten-dimensional conformal symmetry）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻算符与重巨引力子（giant graviton）混合的四点函数此前缺乏对任意轻算符维度与任意允许巨引力子尺寸的一般结果，本文旨在将其推广并揭示其对称性结构。

Method: 采用自举技术（bootstrap techniques），在嵌入空间（embedding space）框架下处理非极大球面巨引力子与对偶巨引力子（dual giant graviton）的任意允许尺寸。

Result: 得到对任意轻算符维度和任意允许巨引力子尺寸成立的四点函数，显示它们可被组织为紧凑的生成函数（generating functions），体现了隐藏的十维共形对称性；两类巨引力子的生成函数均可由极大巨引力子情形经 $SO(2,10)$ 旋转得到。

Conclusion: 巨引力子四点函数受十维共形对称性组织，极大巨引力子情形可作为统一出发点，通过嵌入空间的 $SO(2,10)$ 旋转生成非极大情形。

Abstract: We compute four-point functions of two light $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS operators and two heavy giant graviton operators in $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills theory at large $N$ and infinite 't Hooft coupling. Using bootstrap techniques, we determine the correlators for arbitrary light-operator dimensions and arbitrary allowed sizes of non-maximal sphere and dual giant gravitons. We show that these correlators exhibit a hidden ten-dimensional conformal symmetry, allowing them to be organized into compact generating functions. Remarkably, the generating functions for both types of giant gravitons can be obtained from the maximal giant case through $SO(2,10)$ rotations in the embedding space.

</details>


### [180] [Boundary dualities of symplectic Chern-Simons theories and minimal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13484)
*Yu Nakayama,Tadashi Okazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出 3d N=2 USp(2n) Chern-Simons 理论（能级 −(n+3/2)，含基本手征多重态）的 Neumann 型边界条件对偶于 Abelian rank-0 理论的 Dirichlet 型边界条件，并在拓扑扭曲下对应于仿射顶点算子代数（VOA）L_n(osp(1|2))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在建立三维 Chern-Simons 物质理论中边界条件之间的对偶关系，并将其与顶点算子代数及线缺陷、线算子结构联系起来，以构造边界条件与线算子的对偶网络。

Method: 采用拓扑扭曲（topological twist）与全纯扭曲（holomorphic twist）分析边界 VOA；通过取 fugacity 的特殊化，计算反对称 Wilson 线（antisymmetric Wilson lines）的线缺陷半指数（half-index），并与 Virasoro 极小模型 M(2,2n+3) 的特征标比较；结合 Giveon-Kutasov 型对偶与镜像对称（mirror symmetry）推广对偶关系。

Result: 在拓扑扭曲下边界 VOA 被提出为 L_n(osp(1|2))；线缺陷半指数在相差单项式意义下给出其所有模的超特征标（supercharacters），与 Virasoro 极小模型 M(2,2n+3) 的特征标仅相差符号。对偶被推广到能级 −(n+ℓ+1/2)，边界 VOA 为 L_n(osp(1|2ℓ))。对实物质的其他规范群，全纯扭曲下边界 VOA 为自由实费米子中规范流（gauge currents）的交换子（commutant），如 Spin(7) 加旋量对应 Ising 模型，G_2 加 7 表示对应 N=1 超共形极小模型 SM(3,5)。

Conclusion: 所提出的对偶与 Giveon-Kutasov 型对偶、镜像对称共同构成边界条件与线算子的对偶网络（web of dualities），并将 Chern-Simons 物质理论的边界条件与已知极小模型及 VOA 结构联系起来。

Abstract: We propose that a Neumann-type boundary condition of the 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ $USp(2n)$ Chern-Simons theory at the level $-(n+\frac32)$ with a fundamental chiral multiplet is dual to a Dirichlet-type boundary condition of an Abelian rank-$0$ theory. In a topological twist we propose that it supports the affine vertex operator algebra (VOA) $L_n(\mathfrak{osp}(1|2))$. With the corresponding specialization of the fugacities, the line defect half-indices of the antisymmetric Wilson lines give, up to monomials, the supercharacters of all its modules, which coincide up to signs with the characters of the Virasoro minimal model $M(2,2n+3)$. We extend the duality to the level $-(n+\ell+\frac12)$ and propose that the boundary VOA is $L_n(\mathfrak{osp}(1|2\ell))$. Together with the Giveon-Kutasov type duality and the mirror symmetry, the proposed dualities form a web of dualities of the boundary conditions and the line operators. For other gauge groups with real matter we propose that the boundary VOA in the holomorphic twist is the commutant of the gauge currents in free real fermions, which is the Ising model for $Spin(7)$ with the spinor and the $\mathcal N=1$ superconformal minimal model $SM(3,5)$ for $G_2$ with $\mathbf7$.

</details>


### [181] [Geometry of Gauge-invariant Quantum Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13670)
*Andrea Quadri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文指出无质量规范场（massless gauge fields）的规范不变代表元通常含逆微分算子，提出将这类非局域性（non-locality）替换为局域映射（local map），并讨论其BRST完备化（BRST completion）与向非阿贝尔（non-Abelian）情形的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无质量规范场的规范不变表达一般依赖逆微分算子，导致非局域表述；本文旨在追溯该非局域性的来源并寻求局域化的等价描述。

Method: 将非局域性归因于自由规范复形（free gauge complex）的同伦收缩（homotopy contracting），在场层面用引入独立标量场（independent scalar）与微分约束（differential constraint）的局域映射替代，并借助BRST完备化保持物理内容，最后通过L∞同伦转移（L∞ homotopy transfer）将方法推广至相互作用与非阿贝尔规范群（non-Abelian gauge groups）。

Result: 给出了用独立标量与微分约束构造的局域映射以替代逆微分算子，并说明BRST完备化可保持物理内容；同时指出该方法与Stückelberg理论（Stückelberg theory）的关系。

Conclusion: 规范不变代表元的非局域性可被局域映射重述，且该框架有望经L∞同伦转移扩展至非阿贝尔规范理论与相互作用情形。

Abstract: Gauge-invariant representatives of massless gauge fields generally involve inverse differential operators. We trace this non-locality to the homotopy contracting the free gauge complex and replace it, at field level, by a local map using an independent scalar and a differential constraint. We discuss the BRST completion needed to preserve the physical content and the relation to the Stückelberg theory. We finally comment on the extension of this approach to non-Abelian gauge groups via the extension to interactions through $L_\infty$ homotopy transfer.

</details>


### [182] [Bions and infrared renormalons in Kähler sigma models with $U(1)$ isotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13554)
*Chao-Hsiang Sheu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{R}\times S^1$ 上带扭曲边界条件的二维靶空间 Kähler sigma 模型的半经典 bion 贡献，在变形 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ 模型中显式构造实与复 bion 解，并给出双重整化参数下的递次（resurgent）结构推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅在一维量子力学约化与单一耦合的 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ 模型中讨论 bion 引起的非微扰效应与递次结构；本文旨在将这一图像推广至具有两个重整化参数的 Kähler sigma 模型，并考察更一般的 Kähler 靶空间。

Method: 在变形 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ 模型中类比量子力学约化的方式构造实与复 bion 解，单圈计算玻色与费米涨落并重整化耦合常数与伸长参数（elongation parameter），再用 Lefschetz thimbles 对 bion 准模空间（quasi-moduli space）积分；随后推广至具 $U(1)$ 对称性的一复维 Kähler 靶。

Result: 在超对称点上实与复 bion 贡献相互抵消，偏离超对称点时出现首个虚歧义（imaginary ambiguity）；要求其与微扰歧义抵消可推断相应的红外重整子（infrared renormalon）极点。推广情形下靶依赖仅通过两不动点处的矩映射差（moment-map difference）与度规归一化进入，歧义结构与 bion 权重的红外标度行为对各靶共通，而极点位置依赖靶度规。

Conclusion: 该计算将 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ 模型的递次结构由单一耦合显式推广至两个重整化参数，并表明 bion 非微扰贡献的歧义结构与红外标度具有跨靶空间的普适性，而重整子极点位置携带靶度规信息。

Abstract: We study semiclassical bion contributions to the vacuum energy of Kähler sigma models with two-dimensional target spaces on $\mathbb{R} \times S^1$ with twisted boundary conditions. This is first illustrated in the deformed $\mathbb{CP}^1$ model, where explicit real and complex bion solutions are constructed in a manner analogous to that used in its quantum-mechanical reduction [1]. Evaluating the bosonic and fermionic fluctuations at one loop, we obtain the effective action with both the coupling constant and the elongation parameter properly renormalized. Integration over the bion quasi-moduli space using Lefschetz thimbles then gives the leading nonperturbative contribution, which the real and complex bion contributions cancel at the supersymmetric point, while the first imaginary ambiguity appears when deviating from the supersymmetric point. Requiring its cancellation against the perturbative ambiguity allows us to infer the corresponding infrared-renormalon pole. This is an explicit example generalizing the resurgent structure of the $\mathbb{CP}^1$ model in [2] from one coupling to two renormalized parameters. The calculation further extends to Kähler targets of one complex dimension with a $U(1)$ symmetry. At leading dilute one-loop order, the target dependence enters through the moment-map difference and the metric normalizations at the two fixed points. The ambiguity structure and infrared scaling of the bion weight are common to these targets, while the inferred pole position depends on the target metric.

</details>


### [183] [Gravitational Particle Production and Locality of the Wavefunction-Squared](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13864)
*Emine Şeyma Kutluk*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出，对于膨胀宇宙学（expanding cosmologies）中的自由大质量场，背景几何不产生粒子（no particle production）这一性质蕴含波函数模平方（wavefunction-squared）的局域性；二者源自出模（out modes）在远未来的行为，并在多数膨胀宇宙中几乎总是同时成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是厘清两个看似独立的现象——宇宙学背景下的粒子产生（particle production）与波函数模平方的局域性（locality）——之间是否存在内在联系，并澄清文献中与之看似矛盾的既有结果。

Method: 作者通过比较全局德西特时空（global de Sitter）与庞加莱坐标片（Poincare patch）等具体情形，考察出模在远未来（far future）的渐近行为，分析当相位中时间与动量依赖可分离时上述两现象的共同起源，并讨论其与格林函数（Green's functions）的联系。

Result: 奇数维全局德西特时空中，粒子不产生与波函数模平方局域性源自同一恒等式（identity）；而在庞加莱坐标片中二者在任何条件下均不出现。对于出模在远未来具有时间与动量依赖可分离相位的系统，这两种现象几乎总是同时出现，作者认为该条件对多数膨胀宇宙成立。

Conclusion: 结论是粒子不产生与波函数模平方局域性在多数膨胀宇宙学中相互关联、近乎等价地出现，其共同判据在于出模的远未来行为；文中还说明了该讨论与格林函数及若干既有文献结果的衔接方式。

Abstract: We show that no particle production due to the background geometry implies the locality of the wavefunction-squared for free heavy masses on expanding cosmologies. On global de Sitter of odd dimensions these two stem from the same identity, whereas in the Poincare patch neither occur under any condition. These two phenomena both depend on the behaviour of the out modes at far future and we show that they almost always come together for systems with an out mode that has a phase with separated time and momentum dependency at far future. This will be true for most expanding cosmologies. We comment on how the discussion can be connected to Green's functions and on some previous results in the literature that are seemingly contrary to our conclusions.

</details>


### [184] [Tunneling from an oscillating state in quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14022)
*Oliver Janssen,Matthew Kleban,Mehrdad Mirbabayi,Cameron Norton*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该摘要研究围绕亚稳极小值（metastable minimum）振荡的标量场（scalar field）如何衰变，利用欧几里得源构造（Euclidean source construction）给出两类极限下的衰变率结果：低频率时衰变等价于冻结势（frozen potential）中的 Coleman 气泡，小振幅时衰变指数随振荡呈线性修正且薄壁情形修正指数级放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有衰变率（如 Coleman 气泡）多针对静态势，而实际场可能围绕亚稳极小值相干振荡；振荡是否以及如何改变隧穿/衰变率缺乏清晰答案，尤其薄壁与小振幅、低频等极限下的行为需要澄清。

Method: 考虑相干态（coherent state）⟨q(t)⟩=a cos mt，采用欧几里得源构造（Euclidean source construction）计算衰变。分别考察 mR≪1（m 为质量、R 为气泡半径）与任意 mR 下的小振幅展开，并推广至一个方向缓慢滚动、另一方向亚稳的场。

Result: 当 mR≪1 时，任意振幅下时刻 t 的衰变均为冻结势 U_t(η)=U(⟨q⟩+η)−U(⟨q⟩)−U′(⟨q⟩)η 中的 Coleman 气泡（Coleman bubble）。小振幅、任意 mR 时 B(t)=B_0−aℳcos mt+O(a²)，ℳ 由真空反弹（vacuum bounce）尾部决定；薄壁下 ℳ∝e^{mR}，故指数小的振荡也显著改变衰变率。

Conclusion: 振荡场的衰变可由欧几里得源构造统一描述：低频极限归结为冻结势中的 Coleman 气泡，小振幅极限给出由真空反弹尾部控制的线性修正，薄壁时修正被指数增强；同一方法亦适用于慢滚动与亚稳方向并存的场。

Abstract: How does a scalar field oscillating about a metastable minimum decay? For a coherent state with $\langle q(t) \rangle = a \cos mt$ we find two results. When $mR \ll 1$, with $m$ the mass and $R$ the bubble radius, the decay at time $t$ is a Coleman bubble in the frozen potential $U_t(η) = U(\langle q \rangle +η)-U(\langle q \rangle)-U'(\langle q \rangle)η$, for any amplitude. At small amplitude but any $mR$, $B(t)=B_0 - a \mathcal{M} \cos mt+O(a^2)$, with $\mathcal{M}$ set by the tail of the vacuum bounce; for a thin wall $\mathcal{M} \propto e^{mR}$, so exponentially small oscillations change the rate significantly. Both results follow from a Euclidean source construction. The same method computes the tunneling rate of a field slowly rolling in one direction and metastable in another.

</details>


### [185] [Landau Theory of Black-Brane Phase Transitions from Kasner Singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14075)
*Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用黑洞内部几何（black-hole interior）构建连续黑膜相变（black-brane phase transition）的朗道理论（Landau theory），以内禀 Kasner 标量速度（Kasner scalar velocity）为序参量（order parameter），其系数完全由终端奇点（terminal singularity）确定，并预言与数值解一致的临界标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有黑膜/标量化相变研究多依赖边界视角或数值方法，缺乏从黑洞内部几何出发、可解析刻画临界行为的有效场论框架。本文旨在以内部终端奇点为基础建立朗道型描述，从而直接比较不同相并解析提取临界性质。

Method: 以守恒径向荷（conserved radial charge）从两类端点（Reissner–Nordström 柯西视界（Cauchy horizon）与 Kasner 奇点（Kasner singularity））确定巨势（grand potential）以进行相的直接比较；解析证明临界点处守恒径向荷的二次修正为零；以 Kasner 标量速度的倒数作为内部序参量构造朗道势（Landau potential），系数由终端奇点完全确定，并用 Iyer–Wald 第一定律（Iyer–Wald first law）与边界朗道理论联系并固定四次项系数。

Result: 对两个具有不同标量自相互作用（scalar self-interaction）的模型，该理论给出一致的结果：它预言 Kasner 序参量的临界标度（critical scaling）以及两相间的巨势差，且与完全包含反作用（fully backreacted）的数值解吻合。

Conclusion: 研究表明白洞内部几何可作为黑膜连续相变的有效朗道描述基础：终端奇点足以确定朗道势系数，且内禀序参量框架与常规边界朗道理论相容。该构造为解析刻画标量化相变的临界行为提供了可检验的途径。

Abstract: We develop a Landau theory of a continuous black-brane phase transition using the geometry of the black-hole interior. The normal phase has a Reissner--Nordström Cauchy horizon, whereas the scalarized phase ends at a Kasner singularity. A conserved radial charge determines the grand potential from either endpoint and permits a direct phase comparison. At the critical point, we show analytically that the quadratic correction to the conserved radial charge vanishes. Using the inverse Kasner scalar velocity as an interior order parameter, we then construct a Landau potential whose coefficients are determined entirely from the terminal singularity. The Iyer--Wald first law relates this potential to the conventional boundary Landau theory and fixes its quartic coefficient. For two models with different scalar self-interactions, the resulting theory predicts the critical scaling of the Kasner order parameter and the grand-potential difference between the phases, in agreement with fully backreacted numerical solutions.

</details>


### [186] [Generalizations of Courant brackets in the T-dual description of a weakly curved string background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14172)
*Ilija Ivanišević*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究弱弯曲背景下闭玻色弦的T-对偶（T-duality）如何在Courant括号层面实现，构造出弱弯曲推广的Courant括号及其与标准Courant代数体的显式同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当T-对偶被视为正则变换（canonical transformation）时，标准Courant括号T-对偶不变，而B-扭曲与θ-扭曲Courant括号相互交换；此前的这一图象仅限平坦背景，需考察其在弱弯曲背景（常度规加线性依赖Kalb-Ramond场）下如何被修正。

Method: 在常场强（constant field strength）一阶近似下，构造参数依赖辅助世界面规范场线积分的广义荷（generalized charges），利用T-对偶变换律与初始理论的泊松括号（Poisson bracket）计算其代数，并对对偶流施加B-与θ-平移以得到扭曲括号的推广。

Result: 所得代数给出弱弯曲推广的Courant括号及其关联的Courant代数体（Courant algebroid），并通过对括号、配对（pairing）与锚（anchor）的搬运建立与标准Courant代数体的显式同构；由B-与θ-平移得到的扭曲括号分别是初始θ-与B-扭曲Courant括号的T-对偶实现，并识别出相应的代数体同构。

Conclusion: 结论表明T-对偶下的括号交换结构在弱弯曲背景中依然成立，但需以非局域（nonlocal）的对偶描述和广义荷代数为基础，并可通过Courant代数体同构精确刻画。

Abstract: When T-duality is realized as a canonical transformation, the standard Courant bracket is T-dual invariant, while the $B$- and $θ$-twisted Courant brackets are exchanged. We determine how these relations are modified for a closed bosonic string moving in a weakly curved background with a constant metric and a Kalb-Ramond field with linear coordinate dependence. We work to first order in its constant field strength. The T-dual background fields depend on a line integral of auxiliary worldsheet gauge fields, making the dual description nonlocal. We construct generalized charges whose parameters depend on this line integral and calculate their Poisson bracket algebra using the T-duality transformation laws and the canonical brackets of the initial theory. The resulting algebra yields a weakly curved generalization of the Courant bracket and its associated Courant algebroid. We establish an explicit isomorphism with the standard Courant algebroid by transporting the bracket, pairing, and anchor. Applying $B$- and $θ$-shifts to the dual currents, we obtain weakly curved generalizations of the respective twisted Courant brackets. We show that these brackets provide the T-dual realizations of the initial $θ$- and $B$-twisted Courant brackets, respectively, and identify the corresponding Courant algebroid isomorphisms.

</details>


### [187] [Yang-Lee edge singularity at $\mathcal{O}(\partial^4)$ from the functional renormalization group: revised analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14206)
*Konrad Kockler,Jan M. Pawlowski,Vladimir V. Skokov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 使用函数重整化群（functional renormalization group）在导数展开（derivative expansion）的二阶与四阶保留全部算符，将 Yang-Lee 边缘奇异性（Yang-Lee edge singularity）的临界指数（critical exponents）求为维度的连续函数；四阶结果与六圈 ε 展开（ε expansion）一致，并给出 d=2 时 η 与 ω 接近精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在以函数重整化群系统确定 Yang-Lee 边缘奇异性临界指数随维度的连续变化，并厘清复 Yang-Lee 不动点（complex Yang-Lee fixed point）上以往工作中超标度关系（hyperscaling）被大幅违反的来源。

Method: 在导数展开的二阶和四阶保留所有算符进行截断；对复不动点比较以 φ=0 为参考点与以质量消失处流动虚场值为参考点的两种场展开（field expansion），并检验线性化流（linearized flow）时是否计入展开点移动。

Result: 四阶给出 η=-0.153(7)、-0.352(9)、-0.575(9)（分别对应 d=5、4、3），与六圈 ε 展开吻合；三维下得 σ=0.076、ν_c=0.359；边缘指数零点位于 d_σ=2.65(1)；继续追踪不动点至 d=2 得 η=-0.795(21)、ω=1.90(6)，与精确值 -4/5 和 2 相比。以 φ=0 为参考的展开在本研究所用光滑正规子（smooth regulator）下不收敛，而以质量消失处流动虚场值为参考的展开可收敛至 d=1.3，并在任意场截断阶数保持热本征值与场维度间的精确关系及超标度恒等式 θ_h+θ_t=d。

Conclusion: Yang-Lee 边缘奇异性的连续维度临界指数可由四阶函数重整化群可靠获得并与六圈结果一致；早期工作报告的超标度关系大幅偏离源于线性化流时忽略了展开点移动，而非截断本身。

Abstract: We determine the critical exponents of the Yang-Lee edge singularity as continuous functions of dimension using the functional renormalization group, retaining all operators at second and fourth order in the derivative expansion. At fourth order, we obtain $η=-0.153(7)$, $-0.352(9)$ and $-0.575(9)$ in $d=5$, $4$ and $3$, respectively, in agreement with the six-loop $ε$ expansion. In three dimensions, these results give $σ=0.076$ and $ν_c=0.359$. We locate the zero of the edge exponent at $d_σ=2.65(1)$, also consistent with the six-loop estimate. Continuing the fixed point through $d_σ$, we obtain $η=-0.795(21)$ and $ω=1.90(6)$ in $d=2$, compared with the exact values $-4/5$ and $2$.
  For the complex Yang-Lee fixed point, convergence of the field expansion depends crucially on its reference point. Expansions about $φ=0$ fail to converge with the smooth regulator used here, whereas expansions about the running imaginary field value at which the mass vanishes converge down to $d=1.3$. This representation preserves the exact relation between the thermal eigenvalue and the field dimension, and hence the hyperscaling identity $θ_h+θ_t=d$, at every field-truncation order. We trace the large violations reported in earlier works to the omission of the expansion-point motion when linearizing the flow, rather than to the truncation itself.

</details>


### [188] [Reducing Massive Scattering Amplitudes to Normal Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14270)
*Ming-Lei Xiao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将 Gröbner 基约化（Gröbner-basis reduction）推广到协变张量恒等式（covariant tensor identities），以实现含质量振幅（massive amplitudes）的局部振幅约化（local amplitude reduction），并给出可追溯的正规形约化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部振幅约化（local amplitude reduction）是有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）计算的关键工具，但多项式正规形（polynomial normal forms）仅能复现无质量 y-基（massless y-basis），含质量振幅需要保持小群张量缩并（little-group tensor contractions）的约化规则。

Method: 把 Gröbner 基约化推广至协变张量恒等式，在缩并语境中确定首项（leading monomials），并生成由质量行列式（mass determinants）诱导的高次规则；进而构建三点、四点与五点替换目录（replacement catalogs）。

Result: 利用三、四、五点替换目录，实现了符号质量（symbolic-mass）的振幅约化，并复现了独立的三个与四个矢量算符基（three- and four-vector operator bases）。

Conclusion: 该方法为局部 EFT 匹配（local EFT matching）与 syzygy 分析提供了可追溯的正规形约化（traceable normal-form reductions）。

Abstract: Local amplitude reduction is a crucial tool in effective field theory (EFT) calculations. While polynomial normal forms reproduce the massless y-basis, massive amplitudes require reduction rules that preserve little-group tensor contractions. We generalize Gröbner-basis reduction to covariant tensor identities, determining leading monomials in their contraction contexts and generating the higher-degree rules induced by mass determinants. Using three-, four-, and five-point replacement catalogs, we demonstrate symbolic-mass amplitude reductions and reproduce the independent three- and four-vector operator bases, providing traceable normal-form reductions for local EFT matching and syzygy analysis.

</details>


### [189] [Multiloop Unitarity from Airy Zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14359)
*Laurentiu Rodina*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文将“隐藏零点（hidden zeros）”及由其唯一确定振幅的唯一性结果，从树图与单圈曲面被积函数推广至二、三、四圈任意多重度，并指出树级零点与由Kontsevich矩阵Airy函数生成的新型圈级零点共同固定权重，从而使幺正性（unitarity）有可能在所有微扰阶涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明，隐藏零点可在不假设因子化（factorization）的前提下唯一确定$\Tr(\phi^3)$理论的树图振幅与单圈曲面被积函数；本文旨在把零点结构与唯一性结论推广到更高圈数，并理解圈级零点如何与幺正性所需的因子化及切割数据相联系。

Method: 在任意多重度下考察二、三、四圈情形，识别出两类自然进入全圈问题的零点：树级零点推广至全圈阶，用于固定附着于真空“圈核（loop cores）”的子图族内部相对权重；并利用Kontsevich矩阵Airy函数（matrix Airy function）生成丰富的圈级零点结构，以固定圈核自身的相对权重。

Result: 树级零点可延伸至所有圈阶并固定子图族内的相对权重；Airy圈零点则固定圈核之间的相对权重。两者结合给出幺正性所要求的相对因子化与切割数据。

Conclusion: 树级零点与Airy圈级零点的组合可导出幺正性所需的因子化与切割信息，使“幺正性在所有微扰阶涌现”这一目标变得可达。

Abstract: Hidden zeros are non-trivial cancellations that make amplitudes or integrands vanish on specific kinematic loci away from propagator poles. They have been shown to uniquely determine tree amplitudes and one-loop surface integrands in $\Tr(φ^3)$ theory, without assuming factorization. Here we extend both the zeros and the uniqueness results to two, three and four loops at arbitrary multiplicity. We show that two types of zeros naturally enter the all-loop problem. First, tree-level zeros extend to all loop orders and fix relative weights within families of subgraphs attached to vacuum ``loop cores.'' We then show that Kontsevich's matrix Airy function can be used to generate a rich structure of loop level zeros that fix the relative weights of the loop cores themselves. The relative factorization and cut data required by unitarity therefore follow from the combination of tree and Airy loop zeros. These results bring within reach the emergence of unitarity to all orders in perturbation theory.

</details>


### [190] [Solving the SU(2) BFSS model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14512)
*Kabir Bajaj,Henry W. Lin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作通过数值求解 SU(2) BFSS 矩阵量子力学的基态波函数，并利用 Lüscher 方法提取非微扰四点散射振幅，结果与矩阵 bootstrap、Herderschee–Maldacena 计算及 Born-Oppenheimer 近似一致，并发现正能束缚态与亚稳共振态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验 BFSS 矩阵量子力学（matrix quantum mechanics）中基态关联函数与非微扰散射振幅的多种独立计算途径是否一致，并探索其束缚态与共振谱结构。

Method: 对 SU(2) BFSS 基态波函数进行数值求解；将基态关联函数与矩阵 bootstrap（matrix bootstrap）得到的 islands 比较；由波函数幂律尾部提取三点振幅；并采用 Lüscher 方法（Lüscher's method）提取非微扰四点散射振幅。

Result: 基态关联函数与 bootstrap 结果吻合至五位有效数字；波函数幂律尾部给出的三點振幅与 Herderschee–Maldacena 的计算一致；四点振幅在适当区域与 Born-Oppenheimer 近似给出的 $v^4/r^7$ 相互作用相符；并在多个 SO(9) 不可约表示（irreps）中发现具有正能量的显式束缚态（bound states）及亚稳共振态（metastable resonances）。

Conclusion: 数值结果在基态关联、三点振幅与四点散射等多个层面与已有解析及 bootstrap 计算相互印证，并揭示了与 OpenAI 近期发现相关的正能束缚态及亚稳共振结构。

Abstract: We numerically solve the ground state wavefunction of SU(2) BFSS matrix quantum mechanics. We compare ground correlators against islands obtained using the matrix bootstrap and find agreement up to five significant figures. The power law tail of this wavefunction determines the three-point amplitude, which agrees with the computation of Herderschee and Maldacena. Beyond the ground state, we extract the non-perturbative four-point scattering amplitude using Lüscher's method. In the appropriate regime, we find agreement with the Born-Oppenheimer approximation that yields the well-known $v^4/r^7$ interaction. We also find explicit bound states with positive energies in various SO(9) irreps. These are closely related to the bound states whose existence was recently discovered by OpenAI. Finally, we identify metastable resonances in the spectrum.

</details>
