# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Reciprocal mirror map derivatives, euler-twisted Gromov-Witten invariants, and instanton counts via quantum Serre duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13126)
*Irit Huq-Kuruvilla,Michael T. Schultz*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用欧拉扭转（euler-twisted）理论的量子Serre对偶（quantum Serre duality）的非等变极限，建立反规范Calabi-Yau超曲面、局部Calabi-Yau与环绕Fano簇三类Gromov-Witten理论之间镜像映射导数的精确关系，并由此得到扭转不变量的整性与模形式结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的是把反规范Calabi-Yau超曲面的genus zero Gromov-Witten理论、局部Calabi-Yau理论以及环绕Fano簇的欧拉扭转理论联系起来，寻找它们之间通过镜像映射导数（mirror map derivatives）刻画的精确对应关系。

Method: 核心工具是Iritani、Mann与Mignon提出的欧拉扭转理论量子Serre对偶（quantum Serre duality）的非等变极限（non-equivariant limit），据此得到互反镜像映射导数（reciprocal mirror map derivatives）之间的关系，并将3点扭转不变量的生成级数表示为局部镜像映射对闭镜像映射的导数。

Result: 低维情形下该级数被证明为Eisenstein型模形式（modular form of Eisenstein type），并由此证明扭转不变量的整性（integrality）；Fano的正则化小量子D-模（regularized small quantum D-module）可约，且等价于由该生成级数支配的非齐次Picard-Fuchs方程。互反镜像映射导数则被证明为局部3点不变量的生成函数，恢复了局部Calabi-Yau三维已知结果并证明了关于局部四维的猜想。

Conclusion: 通过对P^2、P^3、P^4的显式计算，验证了上述关系，包括反规范椭圆曲线与K3曲面正次数普通genus-zero不变量的消失、五次三维Calabi-Yau瞬子数（instanton numbers）的恢复，以及局部射影空间genus-zero不变量的若干公式。

Abstract: We study mirror map derivatives as a means of relating the genus zero Gromov-Witten theories of an anticanonical Calabi-Yau hypersurface in a smooth, almost minimal Fano variety and the local Calabi-Yau formed from the total space of the Fano's canonical bundle with the euler-twisted theory of the ambient Fano. A precise relationship between reciprocal mirror map derivatives is obtained from the non-equivariant limit of quantum Serre duality for the euler-twisted theory of the ambient Fano as formulated by Iritani, Mann, & Mignon. This allows us to express the generating series of 3-point euler-twisted invariants as the derivative of the local mirror map with respect to the closed one. In low dimensions, this series is shown to be a modular form of Eisenstein type which has arisen before in this area. We prove integrality of the twisted invariants from this identification. The regularized small quantum $D$-module of the Fano is reducible, and we show it is equivalent to an inhomogeneous Picard-Fuchs equation governed by this generating series. The reciprocal mirror map derivative is then shown to be a generating function of local 3-point invariants, which recovers some known results for local Calabi-Yau threefolds and proves a conjecture of the second named author and A. Malmendier in previous work on local fourfolds. Explicit calculations for $\mathbb{P}^2,\mathbb{P}^3$ and $\mathbb{P}^4$ illustrate these relationships, which includes the vanishing of positive-degree ordinary genus-zero invariants for anticanonical elliptic curves and K3 surfaces, the recovery of the quintic threefold's instanton numbers, and finally some formulas for genus-zero invariants of local projective spaces.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [A decomposition theorem for minimal W-algebras of type A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13239)
*Thibault Juillard*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 对 A 型单李代数，本文证明极小仿射 W-代数（minimal affine W-algebra）同构于通用仿射顶点代数局部化中的交换子子代数，并给出 à la Losev 的张量分解定理，进而导出极小 W-代数的逆哈密顿约化（inverse Hamiltonian reduction）结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小仿射 W-代数由极小幂零轨道（minimal nilpotent orbit）数据经手征哈密顿约化（chiral Hamiltonian reduction）得到，是 Slodowy 切片的量子化；其结构及与通用仿射顶点代数（universal affine vertex algebra）的关系有待明确刻画。

Method: 在单李代数为 A 型时，利用 Slodowy 切片（Slodowy slice）的几何，将极小 W-代数实现为通用仿射顶点代数局部化中的交换子子代数（commutant subalgebra），并建立仿照 Losev 的分解定理；有限 W-代数情形采用类似论证。

Result: 证明了极小 W-代数与局部化中交换子子代数的同构；局部化的通用仿射顶点代数可分解为极小仿射 W-代数与另一顶点子代数的张量积；作为直接推论得到极小 W-代数的逆哈密顿约化结果，并在有限 W-代数情形获得相应类比结论。

Conclusion: A 型情形下极小 W-代数的交换子刻画与张量分解成立，二者均依赖于 Slodowy 切片的几何，并统一给出仿射与有限两种 W-代数的逆约化结论。

Abstract: To any simple Lie algebra, one can associate a minimal affine W-algebra. It is a vertex algebra which is obtained from the universal affine vertex algebra by a chiral Hamiltonian reduction procedure. This reduction is determined by data coming from the minimal nilpotent orbit. It is a chiral quantisation of the Slodowy slice associated to this minimal orbit.
  In this paper, when the simple Lie algebra is of type A, we prove that the minimal W-algebra is isomorphic to a commutant subalgebra in a localisation of the universal affine vertex algebra. Then, we establish a decomposition theorem à la Losev: the localised universal affine vertex algebra decomposes as the tensor product of the minimal affine W-algebra and another vertex subalgebra. As a direct corollary, we retrieve the celebrated result of inverse Hamiltonian reduction for the minimal W-algebra.
  We also prove an analogue of this result for the corresponding finite W-algebra. In both cases, our approach relies on the geometry of the associated Slodowy slice.

</details>


### [193] [Differential Monodromy, $q$-Opers, and Universal $(G,q)$-Wronskians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13446)
*Anton M. Zeitlin*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为 ADE 型单李代数对应的微分算子族给出 $q$DE/ODE/IM 对应（$q$-difference equation / ordinary differential equation / integrable model correspondence）的几何表述，借助扭 $(G,q)$-oper、Steinberg 形式与通用亚纯 $(G,q)$-Wronskian 刻画零与无穷远处解框架的中心联络，并得到满足 ADE QQ 关系的 $Q$-函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是建立代数 $q$DE/ODE/IM 对应在 ADE 型单李代数族情形下的几何框架，并厘清移位单值（shifted monodromy）、仿射 oper 与量子仿射 $q$-特征标之间的结构关系。

Method: 在解析性假设下，将由移位单值给出的 $q$-联络视为扭 $(G,q)$-oper，并在无穷远处扇区内以指定渐近行为归一化的解框架中呈现 Steinberg 形式；进一步借助通用亚纯 $(G,q)$-Wronskian 识别零与无穷远解框架间的中心联络，并对非单位根 $q$ 用移位 $Q$-比值上的量子仿射 $q$-特征标参数化移位单值。

Result: 通用 $(G,q)$-Wronskian 的广义极小（generalized minors）给出满足 ADE $QQ$ 关系的 $Q$-函数；当 $q$ 不是单位根时，移位单值可由在移位 $Q$-比值上求值的量子仿射 $q$-特征标参数化，且所用特征标公式与描述归一化转移矩阵本征值的公式一致。对一般的非扭 Feigin--Frenkel--Hernandez 仿射 oper 验证了解析性假设，从而将其与扭 $(G,q)$-oper 相联系。

Conclusion: 该几何表述将 $q$DE/ODE/IM 对应统一到扭 $(G,q)$-oper 与广义 Wronskian 的框架内，使 $Q$-函数、$QQ$ 关系与量子仿射 $q$-特征标表述彼此兼容，并覆盖一般非扭 FFH 仿射 oper。

Abstract: We give a geometric formulation of the $q$DE/ODE/IM correspondence for families of differential operators associated with simple Lie algebras of ADE type. Under analytic hypotheses, the $q$-connection associated with shifted monodromy defines a twisted $(G,q)$-oper and has Steinberg form in the frame of solutions normalized by prescribed asymptotics in a sector at infinity. We identify the central connection between the normalized solution frames at zero and infinity with a universal meromorphic $(G,q)$-Wronskian. Its generalized minors give $Q$-functions satisfying the ADE $QQ$-relations. When $q$ is not a root of unity, we parametrize shifted monodromy by quantum-affine $q$-characters evaluated on shifted $Q$-ratios, using the same character formulas that describe normalized transfer-matrix eigenvalues. We verify the analytic hypotheses for generic untwisted Feigin--Frenkel--Hernandez affine opers, thereby relating these affine opers to twisted $(G,q)$-opers.

</details>

### [194] [Chemotactic Horizons Regulate Threshold Dynamics in the Biological Carbon Pump](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13165)
*Rahul Chajwa,Manu Prakash*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过海上单细胞追踪测量海洋雪（marine snow）周围微生物的运动性，揭示趋化性（chemotaxis）与沉降流（sedimentation-induced flow）的相互作用机制。研究识别出"趋化地平线"（chemotactic horizon），并发现遭遇率（encounter rate）随沉降速度呈非单调依赖关系，最终建立相空间动力学框架以刻画海洋生物碳泵（biological carbon pump）中海洋雪的归趋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物与沉降海洋雪的相遇调控有机碳再矿化（remineralization），是海洋生物碳泵的关键环节；然而开放海洋中高度多样的微生物如何响应将沉降流与相遇耦合的天然趋化线索，仍属未解问题。

Method: 在船上对新鲜采集的海洋雪施加覆盖生态相关沉降速度的流场，利用高分辨率单细胞追踪（single cell tracking）测绘微生物运动性谱系，并基于实测的化学梯度重定向量化趋化地平线，进而将实测遭遇率纳入位置-速度相空间的动力学输运方程（kinetic transport equation）。

Result: 微生物运动性表现出高度多样的谱系，包括运动与非运动状态间的快速切换；遭遇率随聚合体沉降速度呈非单调依赖，在14.4 m/day处达峰，超过78.1 m/day后急剧下降并进入类示踪剂（tracer-like） regime；由速度依赖再矿化产生的有效非线性阻尼（effective nonlinear damping）导致阈值诱导的分界线（separatrix），将过阻尼与弹道微生物种群分离并引起速度分聚（velocity segregation）。

Conclusion: 研究将微生物运动性与沉降流的直接测量同海洋雪在生物泵中归趋的涌现相空间相联系，为研究海洋碳输出建立了定量多尺度（multiscale）框架。

Abstract: Microbial encounters with sinking marine snow regulate organic carbon remineralization, acting as a critical knob in the ocean's biological carbon pump. Yet how the vast diversity of microbes in the open ocean respond to natural chemotactic cues coupling sedimentation-induced flow to microbial encounters in the ocean remains unresolved. Here we report shipboard measurements of microbial motility around freshly collected marine snow under imposed flows spanning ecologically relevant sinking speeds. Via high resolution single cell tracking at sea, we map a highly diverse repertoire of microbial motility, including rapid switching between motile and non-motile states. We identify a chemotactic horizon from measured reorientation towards chemical gradients, defining a spatial region around an aggregate within which chemotactic alignment overcomes flow-induced rotation due to sedimentation. This interplay produces a non-monotonic dependence of encounter rates on aggregate sinking speed, with a peak at 14.4 m/day followed by a sharp decline beyond 78.1 m/day, with a tracer-like regime at high effective sinking speeds. We incorporate the measured encounter rates into a kinetic transport equation in position-velocity phase space, with an effective nonlinear damping arising from velocity-dependent remineralization. The resulting particle dynamics reveal a threshold-induced separatrix dividing overdamped and ballistic microbial populations, leading to velocity segregation. Our study connects direct measurements of microbial motility and sedimentation-induced flow to the emergent phase-space for fate of marine snow in the biological pump, establishing a quantitative multiscale framework for investigating ocean carbon export.

</details>


### [195] [Energetic costs of reaching the physical limits of sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14145)
*Albert Alonso,Julius B. Kirkegaard,Robert G. Endres*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该文推导了将梯度感知不确定度与吸收分子数及熵产生联系起来的闭式表达式，指出梯度感知需分辨吸收通量的角向不对称性，并发现细胞感知受计数噪声而非能量成本限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞通过吸收配体分子感知化学梯度，此非平衡过程必然产生熵；但细胞需吸收多少分子、耗散多少能量才能逼近感知的物理极限，尚不明确。

Method: 推导关联梯度感知不确定度（gradient sensing uncertainty）与吸收分子数的闭式表达式（closed-form expressions），并将其与熵产生（entropy production）相联系；引入由细胞吸收强度决定的有界偶极对比因子（dipole-contrast factor）。

Result: 与仅依赖总计数（total count）的浓度感知不同，梯度感知须分辨吸收通量的角向不对称性（angular asymmetry）；在强信号区（strong-signal regime），细胞以扩散限制摄取（diffusion-limited uptake）的小部分吸收量即可接近趋化排列（chemotactic alignment）峰值，所需摄取事件数由跨细胞的相对浓度变化决定。

Conclusion: 对盘基网柄菌（Dictyostelium）趋化性的再分析显示，细胞处于该极限的0.25%，代谢负荷很小，表明感知受计数噪声（counting noise）而非能量成本限制。

Abstract: Cells sense chemical gradients by absorbing ligand molecules, a nonequilibrium process that necessarily produces entropy. But how many molecules must a cell absorb, and therefore energy dissipate, to approach the physical limits of sensing? We derive closed-form expressions relating gradient sensing uncertainty to the number of absorbed molecules, which we link to entropy production. Unlike concentration sensing, which depends only on the total count, gradient sensing must resolve the angular asymmetry of the absorbed flux, adding a bounded dipole-contrast factor set by how strongly the cell absorbs. In the strong-signal regime, cells approach peak chemotactic alignment while absorbing a small fraction of the diffusion-limited uptake, with the required number of uptake events set by the relative concentration change across the cell. Re-analysis of Dictyostelium chemotaxis places the cell at 0.25% of that limit with a minor metabolic load, suggesting that sensing is limited by counting noise rather than by energetic cost.

</details>


### [196] [Geometric regulation of stress-limited growth on surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14159)
*Scott Weady*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究建立了表面生长型微生物群落（microbial communities）在集体生长压力抑制个体生长假设下的模型，结合球面渐近解、数值解与周期表面均匀化（homogenization）分析，证明表面几何（geometry）可调节生长速率，并给出使生长速率任意小或接近无应力生长速率的曲面类别与设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物在表面生长时，个体生长受集体生长诱导的压力（growth-induced pressure）抑制，但表面几何与形貌（topography）如何影响这种应力感知（stress-sensing）尚不清晰，需要建立定量模型加以刻画。

Method: 构建个体生长受集体压力抑制的表面微生物群落模型，在球面上求渐近解（asymptotic solutions）与数值解，并对周期表面进行均匀化（homogenization）处理，以解析几何对生长的影响。

Result: 分析表明表面几何既可减缓也可加速生长；据此识别出可使生长速率任意小、或任意接近无应力生长速率（stress-free growth rate）的曲面类别。

Conclusion: 应力感知与远大于个体尺度的表面曲率（curvature）和形貌相耦合，结果为调控应力敏感微生物群落的生长提供了设计原则（design principles）。

Abstract: We analyze a model for microbial communities growing on surfaces in which individual growth is inhibited by collective growth-induced pressure. Asymptotic and numerical solutions on the sphere and homogenization of periodic surfaces show surface geometry can be tuned to either slow or accelerate growth. We use these insights to identify classes of surfaces for which the growth rate can be made arbitrarily small or arbitrarily close to the stress-free growth rate. These results show stress-sensing is coupled to surface curvature and topography at scales much larger than the individual, and provide design principles for controlling the growth of stress-sensitive microbial communities.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [Goldstone-Mediated Chiral Superconductivity in Self-Doped Electron Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12946)
*Shuchen Zhang,Zhihuan Dong,Zhaoyu Han,Shubhayu Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文探讨菱方石墨烯中靠近电子晶相时由电子声子（Goldstone phonon）介导的配对机制，发现库仑排斥可被过屏蔽，导致具有无配对马约拉纳边缘模式的手性拓扑超导体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受菱方石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）在电子晶相附近观测到超导性的启发，研究电子声子介导配对的可能性，重点关注平庸与拓扑电子晶体相边界附近自掺杂产生的小费米海。

Method: 采用自洽方法计算自掺杂晶体（self-doped crystal, SDC）的Goldstone声子谱，进而导出费米面上巡游载流子之间由电子-声子介导的有效相互作用，并在λ-jellium模型与简化的菱方石墨烯模型中验证。

Result: 发现库仑排斥（Coulomb repulsion）可被Goldstone声子过屏蔽（overscreen），从而产生具有无配对马约拉纳边缘模式（Majorana edge mode）的手性拓扑超导体；结论在两种模型中均稳健，并提出特征非线性I-V信号作为检验预言的手段。

Conclusion: 该工作勾勒出一条电子关联与拓扑共同起关键作用的非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）新路径，并为相关预言提供了可实验检验的非线性输运信号。

Abstract: Motivated by the observation of superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene in the proximity of electronic crystalline phases, we study the possibility of pairing mediated by electronic phonons. We focus on the vicinity of the phase boundary between trivial and topological electron crystals, where self-doping generically gives rise to a small Fermi sea of carriers with Dirac dispersion. We compute the Goldstone phonon spectra of the self-doped crystal (SDC) using a self-consistent approach, and subsequently derive the effective electronic-phonon-mediated interaction between itinerant carriers at the Fermi surface. Remarkably, we find that the Coulomb repulsion can be overscreened by the Goldstone phonons, leading to a chiral topological superconductor with an unpaired Majorana edge mode. Our conclusions are robust across the $λ$-jellium model and a simplified model of rhombohedral graphene, and we propose characteristic non-linear I-V signals as a test of our prediction. More generally, our results outline an intriguing route for unconventional superconductivity where both electronic correlations and topology play key roles.

</details>


### [198] [Mimicking the zero-field Josephson diode effect with remanent magnetization and self-field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13157)
*Nathan Satchell,Arjun Sapkota,Pukar Sedai,Kurt Lorenzen,Juan Gomez*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在Nb/Pd89Ni11/Nb结中，偏置电流在电极中产生的自磁场与势垒剩磁共同作用，可在零场下产生10%–12%的类二极管不对称性，其表观行为与内禀约瑟夫森二极管效应（intrinsic Josephson diode effect）难以通过换向区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性势垒在零外加场下呈现的约瑟夫森二极管（Josephson diode）行为通常被归因于磁性势垒破坏时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）的内禀效应，本文旨在检验是否存在更普通的替代解释。

Method: 采用交叉型（cross-type）Nb/Pd89Ni11/Nb 结，分析电极中偏置电流的自磁场（self-field）对两种电流极性干涉图案的位移，以及面内剩磁（remanent magnetization）对图案的平移；并通过改变电流引线的接线方式（将引线移至电极另一臂）进行直流的对照测试。

Result: 自磁场使两种电流极性的干涉图案向相反方向位移；面内剩磁将图案平移并把不对称性带到零场，形成非易失性（nonvolatile）类二极管，不对称度为10%–12%，极性由磁化方向决定。反转电流或磁场无法区分该仿效现象与内禀效应，但改变接线可以：更换电流引线会在有场与零场下均使自场二极管反转，而内禀二极管应保持不变。

Conclusion: 所观察到的零场二极管特征可由自磁场与势垒剩磁这两种普通要素完全解释，因此不宜直接视作内禀约瑟夫森二极管效应；提出以更换电流引线的直流测试作为电流垂直于膜面（current-perpendicular-to-plane）几何下判别约瑟夫森二极管的简便方法。

Abstract: A Josephson junction whose critical current is larger in one direction than in the other is a diode. With a magnetized barrier, a junction can be a diode at zero applied field, and this is commonly attributed to an intrinsic Josephson diode effect, in which the magnetic barrier breaks time-reversal symmetry. Here we show that the same signatures arise from two ordinary ingredients: the magnetic field of the bias current in the electrodes and the remanent magnetization of the barrier. In cross-type Nb/Pd$_{89}$Ni$_{11}$/Nb junctions, the self-field displaces the interference patterns of the two current polarities in opposite directions. An in-plane remanent magnetization translates the pattern and brings the resulting asymmetry to zero field, where the junction mimics a nonvolatile diode with an asymmetry of 10 to 12% and a polarity set by the magnetization direction. Reversing the current or the field cannot distinguish this mimic from an intrinsic effect, but the wiring can: moving the current lead to the other arm of the electrode across the field reverses the self-field diode, in applied field and at zero field alike, whereas an intrinsic diode would be unchanged. This gives a simple dc test for Josephson diodes in the current-perpendicular-to-plane geometry.

</details>


### [199] [From Growth to Devices: Oxygen-Controlled Superconductivity in BSCCO Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14231)
*Sohini Guin,Ramesh Kumar Guin,Pankaj Bhardwaj,Laxmipriya Nanda,Rajib Sarkar,Dhavala Suri*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过独立调控退火温度与时间优化溅射Bi₂Sr₂Caₙ₋₁CuₙO₂ₙ₊₄₊δ（BSCCO）薄膜，确立退火—氧含量—超导输运的直接关联，并验证其微纳加工兼容性与空气稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现器件兼容的高临界温度（high-$T_c$）超导体需同时控制氧化学计量（oxygen stoichiometry）并耐受纳米加工（nanofabrication），而退火条件与氧掺入、超导输运之间的关联尚需明确。

Method: 采用溅射（sputtering）制备BSCCO薄膜，独立改变退火温度与时间；结合X射线衍射（XRD）、电子显微镜（electron microscopy）与X射线光电子能谱（XPS）表征晶格氧演化；并将优化薄膜制成微桥（micro-bridge）与平面约瑟夫森结（planar Josephson junction），分别用剥离（lift-off）与聚焦离子束（FIB）图形化。

Result: 最优退火条件为860 °C、3 h，获得最高超导转变温度与最窄转变宽度；晶格氧呈非单调演化，主导CuO₂面空穴掺杂（hole doping），产生$T_c$随掺杂的特征穹顶型（dome-like）依赖；器件在两种图形化方法下均可制备，并具备约2个月的空气稳定性（air-stability）。

Conclusion: 研究为铜氧化物（cuprate）薄膜集成至超导电子学与量子器件架构提供实用指导准则。

Abstract: Realizing device-compatible high-$T_c$ superconductors requires simultaneous control of oxygen stoichiometry and tolerance to nanofabrication. Here, we investigate sputtered Bi$_2$Sr$_2$Ca$_{n-1}$Cu$_n$O$_{2n+4+δ}$ (BSCCO) thin films and establish a direct link between annealing conditions, oxygen incorporation, and superconducting transport. By independently varying the annealing temperature and duration, we identify an optimal annealing condition of $860\,^{\circ}$C for 3\,h, which yields the highest superconducting transition temperature and the narrowest transition among the investigated samples. X-ray diffraction, electron microscopy, and X-ray photoemission spectroscopy reveal a non-monotonic evolution of lattice oxygen, which governs hole doping in the CuO$_2$ planes and produces the characteristic dome-like dependence of $T_c$ on doping concentration. We further translate these optimized films into micro-devices to assess fabrication-induced effects, via micro-bridges as well as a planar Josephson junction. We demonstrate devices fabricated via both lift-off and focused ion beam (FIB) patterning methods -- proving the fabrication compatibility of these films. Importantly, we demonstrate their air-stability for extended periods of time (2 months). The combined insights offer practical guidelines for integrating cuprate thin films into superconducting electronics and quantum device architectures.

</details>
